    • FILE: sql/etapa3_explorar_ocupacion_y_spots_publicos.sql

    -- ============================================================================
-- KINETIC OS v2.6 — ETAPA 3: OCUPACIÓN EN VIVO (cupo_actual) Y SPOTS 3D PÚBLICOS SIN PII
-- Proyecto Supabase: gcppxrlzxlgkuapktxvl | Idempotente (Seguro de re-ejecutar)
-- ============================================================================

-- 1. COLUMNAS E ÍNDICES DE ALTA FRECUENCIA EN public.servicios Y public.reservas
ALTER TABLE public.servicios
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cupo_actual INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS spots_locks JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb;

ALTER TABLE public.reservas
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS spot_number INTEGER DEFAULT NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS fecha_clase TEXT DEFAULT NULL;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_servicios_spots_locks_gin
  ON public.servicios USING GIN (spots_locks);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reservas_servicio_estado_fecha_spot
  ON public.reservas (servicio_id, estado, fecha_clase, spot_number);

-- Backfill inicial de cupo_actual sobre servicios activos
UPDATE public.servicios s
SET cupo_actual = COALESCE((
  SELECT COUNT(*)::integer
  FROM public.reservas r
  WHERE r.servicio_id = s.id
    AND LOWER(COALESCE(r.estado, 'activa')) IN ('pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada')
), 0);

-- 2. TRIGGER ATÓMICO: SINCRONIZACIÓN DE cupo_actual EN TIEMPO REAL
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fn_sync_servicios_cupo_actual()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_old_sid UUID := CASE WHEN TG_OP IN ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') THEN OLD.servicio_id ELSE NULL END;
  v_new_sid UUID := CASE WHEN TG_OP IN ('INSERT', 'UPDATE') THEN NEW.servicio_id ELSE NULL END;
BEGIN
  IF v_new_sid IS NOT NULL THEN
    UPDATE public.servicios s
    SET cupo_actual = (
      SELECT COUNT(*)::integer
      FROM public.reservas r
      WHERE r.servicio_id = v_new_sid
        AND LOWER(COALESCE(r.estado, 'activa')) IN ('pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada')
    )
    WHERE s.id = v_new_sid;
  END IF;

  IF v_old_sid IS NOT NULL AND v_old_sid IS DISTINCT FROM v_new_sid THEN
    UPDATE public.servicios s
    SET cupo_actual = (
      SELECT COUNT(*)::integer
      FROM public.reservas r
      WHERE r.servicio_id = v_old_sid
        AND LOWER(COALESCE(r.estado, 'activa')) IN ('pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada')
    )
    WHERE s.id = v_old_sid;
  END IF;

  RETURN COALESCE(NEW, OLD);
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_sync_servicios_cupo_actual ON public.reservas;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_sync_servicios_cupo_actual
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OF estado, servicio_id OR DELETE ON public.reservas
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION public.fn_sync_servicios_cupo_actual();

-- 3. TRIGGER ANTI-COLISIÓN: IMPEDIR DOBLE RESERVA DEL MISMO SPOT 3D (1..50)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fn_guard_reservas_spot_unico()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_conflict BOOLEAN := false;
BEGIN
  IF NEW.spot_number IS NULL
     OR NEW.spot_number < 1
     OR NEW.spot_number > 50
     OR LOWER(COALESCE(NEW.estado, 'activa')) NOT IN ('pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada')
  THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  IF NEW.servicio_id IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM public.reservas r
      WHERE r.servicio_id = NEW.servicio_id
        AND r.id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.id
        AND LOWER(COALESCE(r.estado, 'activa')) IN ('pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada')
        AND r.spot_number = NEW.spot_number
        AND (
          COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(COALESCE(NEW.fecha_clase, '')), ''), 'any') = 'any'
          OR COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(COALESCE(r.fecha_clase, '')), ''), 'any') = 'any'
          OR LEFT(TRIM(r.fecha_clase), 10) = LEFT(TRIM(NEW.fecha_clase), 10)
        )
    ) INTO v_conflict;

    IF v_conflict THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'KINETIC_SPOT_COLLISION: La estacion #% ya fue reservada por otro atleta.', NEW.spot_number
        USING ERRCODE = '23505';
    END IF;
  END IF;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_guard_reservas_spot_unico ON public.reservas;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_guard_reservas_spot_unico
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF spot_number, estado, fecha_clase, servicio_id ON public.reservas
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION public.fn_guard_reservas_spot_unico();

-- 4. RPC: BLOQUEO TÁCTICO TEMPORAL DE SPOT (90-120s) SIN UPDATE DIRECTO DEL CLIENTE
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.registrar_lock_spot_temporal(
  p_servicio_id UUID,
  p_spot_number INTEGER DEFAULT NULL,
  p_fecha_clase TEXT DEFAULT 'any',
  p_ttl_ms INTEGER DEFAULT 95000
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid UUID := (SELECT auth.uid());
  v_holder TEXT := COALESCE(v_uid::text, 'anon');
  v_date_tag TEXT := COALESCE(NULLIF(LEFT(TRIM(COALESCE(p_fecha_clase, '')), 10), ''), 'any');
  v_now_ms BIGINT := FLOOR(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM clock_timestamp()) * 1000)::bigint;
  v_safe_ttl BIGINT := LEAST(180000, GREATEST(5000, COALESCE(p_ttl_ms, 95000)));
  v_raw JSONB;
  v_cleaned JSONB := '{}'::jsonb;
  v_key TEXT;
  v_val JSONB;
  v_lock_key TEXT;
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL OR p_servicio_id IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', false);
  END IF;

  SELECT COALESCE(spots_locks, '{}'::jsonb)
    INTO v_raw
  FROM public.servicios
  WHERE id = p_servicio_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', false);
  END IF;

  FOR v_key, v_val IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_each(v_raw) LOOP
    IF COALESCE((v_val->>'expiresAt')::bigint, 0) > v_now_ms
       AND NOT (
         COALESCE(v_val->>'holder', '') = v_holder
         AND COALESCE(v_val->>'dateTag', 'any') = v_date_tag
       )
    THEN
      v_cleaned := v_cleaned || jsonb_build_object(v_key, v_val);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;

  IF p_spot_number IS NOT NULL AND p_spot_number BETWEEN 1 AND 50 THEN
    v_lock_key := v_date_tag || ':' || p_spot_number::text;
    IF NOT (v_cleaned ? v_lock_key) THEN
      v_cleaned := v_cleaned || jsonb_build_object(
        v_lock_key,
        jsonb_build_object(
          'spot', p_spot_number,
          'holder', v_holder,
          'dateTag', v_date_tag,
          'expiresAt', v_now_ms + v_safe_ttl
        )
      );
    END IF;
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.servicios
  SET spots_locks = v_cleaned
  WHERE id = p_servicio_id;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'spots_locks', v_cleaned);
END;
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.registrar_lock_spot_temporal(UUID, INTEGER, TEXT, INTEGER) TO authenticated;

-- 5. RPC PÚBLICO SIN PII: LECTURA DE OCUPACIÓN Y SPOTS 3D POST-RLS
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.obtener_ocupacion_spots_servicio(
  p_servicio_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
  p_servicio_nombre TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_fecha_clase TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_caller_holder TEXT := COALESCE((SELECT auth.uid())::text, '');
  v_date_filter TEXT := NULLIF(LEFT(TRIM(COALESCE(p_fecha_clase, '')), 10), '');
  v_now_ms BIGINT := FLOOR(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM clock_timestamp()) * 1000)::bigint;
  v_booked INTEGER[] := '{}'::integer[];
  v_locked_others INTEGER[] := '{}'::integer[];
  v_unassigned INTEGER := 0;
  v_active_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_row RECORD;
  v_spot INTEGER;
  v_qr_match TEXT[];
  v_raw_locks JSONB := '{}'::jsonb;
  v_k TEXT;
  v_v JSONB;
BEGIN
  IF p_servicio_id IS NULL AND LENGTH(TRIM(COALESCE(p_servicio_nombre, ''))) = 0 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
      'booked_spots', '[]'::jsonb,
      'locked_other_spots', '[]'::jsonb,
      'unassigned_count', 0,
      'active_count', 0
    );
  END IF;

  FOR v_row IN
    SELECT r.spot_number, r.qr_token, r.fecha_clase
    FROM public.reservas r
    WHERE LOWER(COALESCE(r.estado, 'activa')) IN ('pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada')
      AND (
        (p_servicio_id IS NOT NULL AND r.servicio_id = p_servicio_id)
        OR (p_servicio_id IS NULL AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(r.servicio, ''))) = LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p_servicio_nombre, ''))))
      )
      AND (
        v_date_filter IS NULL
        OR r.fecha_clase IS NULL
        OR LENGTH(TRIM(r.fecha_clase)) = 0
        OR LEFT(TRIM(r.fecha_clase), 10) = v_date_filter
      )
    LIMIT 100
  LOOP
    v_active_count := v_active_count + 1;
    v_spot := v_row.spot_number;
    IF v_spot IS NULL THEN
      v_qr_match := regexp_match(COALESCE(v_row.qr_token, ''), 'KNT-S([0-9]{1,2})-', 'i');
      IF v_qr_match IS NOT NULL THEN
        v_spot := v_qr_match[1]::integer;
      END IF;
    END IF;

    IF v_spot IS NOT NULL AND v_spot BETWEEN 1 AND 50 THEN
      IF NOT (v_spot = ANY(v_booked)) THEN
        v_booked := array_append(v_booked, v_spot);
      END IF;
    ELSE
      v_unassigned := v_unassigned + 1;
    END IF;
  END LOOP;

  IF p_servicio_id IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT COALESCE(spots_locks, '{}'::jsonb)
      INTO v_raw_locks
    FROM public.servicios
    WHERE id = p_servicio_id;

    FOR v_k, v_v IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_each(COALESCE(v_raw_locks, '{}'::jsonb)) LOOP
      v_spot := COALESCE((v_v->>'spot')::integer, 0);
      IF COALESCE((v_v->>'expiresAt')::bigint, 0) > v_now_ms
         AND v_spot BETWEEN 1 AND 50
         AND COALESCE(v_v->>'holder', '') <> ''
         AND COALESCE(v_v->>'holder', '') <> v_caller_holder
         AND (
           v_date_filter IS NULL
           OR COALESCE(v_v->>'dateTag', 'any') = 'any'
           OR COALESCE(v_v->>'dateTag', 'any') = v_date_filter
         )
      THEN
        IF NOT (v_spot = ANY(v_locked_others)) THEN
          v_locked_others := array_append(v_locked_others, v_spot);
        END IF;
      END IF;
    END LOOP;
  END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'booked_spots', to_jsonb(v_booked),
    'locked_other_spots', to_jsonb(v_locked_others),
    'unassigned_count', v_unassigned,
    'active_count', v_active_count
  );
END;
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.obtener_ocupacion_spots_servicio(UUID, TEXT, TEXT) TO anon, authenticated;

NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema';

    FILE: src/__tests__/adversarial/sprint3ExploreEventosTerminalV275.adversarial.test.ts

    // === src/__tests__/adversarial/sprint3ExploreEventosTerminalV275.adversarial.test.ts === //
import fc from 'fast-check';
import {
  formatStudioStationBadge,
  formatUseStudioStationCta,
  resolveStudioFrontStageLabel,
} from '../../services/studioLayoutGridHelper';
import {
  computeHaversineKm,
  hydrateServicesGpsWithClubCoords,
  resolveLosCabosZoneKey,
} from '../../services/exploreZoneHelpers';
import {
  isEventEditorFormValid,
  shouldShowLastSpotsBadge,
} from '../../components/eventos/eventDetailsPureHelpers';
import {
  extractVerifiedInstagramHandle,
  resolveAffiliateInstagramUrl,
} from '../../utils/externalLinks';
import { formatDistanceLabel } from '../../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';

const g = globalThis as Record<string, unknown>;
const describeSuite =
  typeof g.describe === 'function'
    ? (g.describe as (name: string, fn: () => void) => void)
    : (_name: string, fn: () => void) => fn();
const itCase =
  typeof g.it === 'function'
    ? (g.it as (name: string, fn: () => void) => void)
    : (_name: string, fn: () => void) => fn();

const assertTrue = (cond: boolean, msg: string) => {
  if (!cond) throw new Error(msg);
};

describeSuite('KINETIC OS v2.7.5 Sprint 3 — Explorar, Mapa, Terminal, Fichas & Sala 3D (fast-check 500x)', () => {
  itCase(
    'Propiedad 1: extractVerifiedInstagramHandle rechaza /explore/search/, vacíos, espacios e inyecciones y acepta solo handles válidos',
    () => {
      const badInputs = fc.oneof(
        fc.constant(''),
        fc.constant('   '),
        fc.constant('https://www.instagram.com/explore/search/keyword/?q=Kinetic'),
        fc.constant('https://instagram.com/explore/tags/fitness/'),
        fc.constant('https://instagram.com/p/C12345/'),
        fc.constant('https://instagram.com/reels/12345/'),
        fc.constant('javascript:alert(1)'),
        fc.constant('data:text/html,<script>alert(1)</script>'),
        fc.constant('__proto__'),
        fc.constant('constructor'),
        fc.constant('prototype'),
        fc.constant('tropicalfitnesssjc'),
        fc.constant('El Tule Fitness'),
        fc.constant('handle with spaces'),
        fc.string({ minLength: 1, maxLength: 24 }).map((s) => `${s} ${s}`),
        fc.string({ minLength: 1, maxLength: 24 }).map((s) => `https://instagram.com/explore/search/${encodeURIComponent(s)}`)
      );

      fc.assert(
        fc.property(badInputs, fc.string({ minLength: 1, maxLength: 30 }), (malformed, clubName) => {
          assertTrue(
            extractVerifiedInstagramHandle(malformed) === '',
            `Debió rechazar entrada inválida: ${malformed}`
          );
          const resolved = resolveAffiliateInstagramUrl({ handle: malformed, clubName, isVerified: true });
          assertTrue(
            !resolved.includes('/explore/search'),
            `URL no debe contener /explore/search: ${resolved}`
          );
          assertTrue(resolved === '', `Handle inválido debe devolver vacío y no fallback: ${resolved}`);
        }),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );

      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc
            .stringMatching(/^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9_]{2,20}[a-zA-Z0-9]$/)
            .filter((s) => !['tropicalfitnesssjc', '__proto__', 'constructor', 'prototype', 'instagram', 'explore', 'search'].includes(s.toLowerCase())),
          (validRaw) => {
            const expected = validRaw.toLowerCase();
            assertTrue(
              extractVerifiedInstagramHandle(`@${validRaw}`) === expected,
              `Debe aceptar @handle válido: ${validRaw}`
            );
            assertTrue(
              extractVerifiedInstagramHandle(`https://www.instagram.com/${validRaw}/`) === expected,
              `Debe extraer handle de URL válida: ${validRaw}`
            );
          }
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );
    }
  );

  itCase(
    'Propiedad 2: shouldShowLastSpotsBadge nunca activa con 0 ocupados o lleno, e isEventEditorFormValid exige título y múltiplo de 10 KC',
    () => {
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.integer({ min: -5, max: 60 }),
          fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 50 }),
          (occ, maxCupo) => {
            const shown = shouldShowLastSpotsBadge(occ, maxCupo);
            if (occ <= 0 || occ >= maxCupo) {
              assertTrue(!shown, `No debe mostrar ÚLTIMOS LUGARES cuando occ=${occ}, max=${maxCupo}`);
            } else {
              const rem = maxCupo - occ;
              assertTrue(
                shown === (rem > 0 && rem <= 3),
                `Inconsistencia badge: occ=${occ}, max=${maxCupo}, rem=${rem}, shown=${shown}`
              );
            }
          }
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );

      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 300 }),
          fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 9 }),
          fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 50 }),
          fc.string({ minLength: 1, maxLength: 30 }).filter((s) => s.trim().length > 0),
          (mult10, remMod, cupoVal, cleanTitle) => {
            const validPrice = mult10 * 10;
            assertTrue(
              isEventEditorFormValid({
                titleVal: cleanTitle,
                discVal: 'Pádel',
                dateVal: '2026-12-01',
                timeVal: '09:00',
                priceVal: validPrice,
                cupoVal,
              }),
              `Formulario válido rechazado: ${validPrice} KC`
            );
            assertTrue(
              !isEventEditorFormValid({
                titleVal: '   ',
                discVal: 'Pádel',
                dateVal: '2026-12-01',
                timeVal: '09:00',
                priceVal: validPrice,
                cupoVal,
              }),
              'Título vacío debe ser rechazado'
            );
            const invalidModPrice = validPrice + remMod;
            assertTrue(
              !isEventEditorFormValid({
                titleVal: cleanTitle,
                discVal: 'Pádel',
                dateVal: '2026-12-01',
                timeVal: '09:00',
                priceVal: invalidModPrice,
                cupoVal,
              }),
              `Precio no múltiplo de 10 KC (${invalidModPrice}) debe rechazarse`
            );
            assertTrue(
              !isEventEditorFormValid({
                titleVal: cleanTitle,
                discVal: 'Pádel',
                dateVal: '2026-12-01',
                timeVal: '09:00',
                priceVal: -10,
                cupoVal,
              }),
              'Precio negativo debe rechazarse'
            );
          }
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );
    }
  );

  itCase(
    'Propiedad 3: hydrateServicesGpsWithClubCoords iguala distancia con \\u00A0km por sede y El Tule resuelve a Corredor Turístico',
    () => {
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.double({ min: 22.86, max: 23.09, noNaN: true }),
          fc.double({ min: -109.94, max: -109.68, noNaN: true }),
          fc.double({ min: -0.02, max: 0.02, noNaN: true }),
          fc.double({ min: -0.02, max: 0.02, noNaN: true }),
          fc.constantFrom<'es' | 'en'>('es', 'en'),
          fc.constantFrom(
            'El Tule',
            'Puente El Tule, Cabo San Lucas',
            'El Tule Fitness & CrossFit',
            'Corredor Turístico Km 16, Los Cabos'
          ),
          (clubLat, clubLon, jitterA, jitterB, lang, tuleLabel) => {
            const club = {
              id: 'club-canonical-tule',
              nombre: 'Club Canónico Los Cabos',
              latitud: clubLat,
              longitud: clubLon,
              latitude: clubLat,
              longitude: clubLon,
            };
            const events = [
              { id: 'ev-1', negocio_nombre: club.nombre, latitud: clubLat + jitterA, longitud: clubLon - jitterA },
              { id: 'ev-2', negocio_nombre: club.nombre, latitud: clubLat - jitterB, longitud: clubLon + jitterB },
            ];
            const hydrated = hydrateServicesGpsWithClubCoords(events, [club]);
            assertTrue(
              hydrated[0].latitud === clubLat && hydrated[0].longitud === clubLon,
              'Evento 1 debe heredar coordenadas exactas del club anfitrión'
            );
            assertTrue(
              hydrated[1].latitud === clubLat && hydrated[1].longitud === clubLon,
              'Evento 2 debe heredar coordenadas exactas del club anfitrión'
            );
            const d1 = computeHaversineKm(22.8905, -109.9167, hydrated[0].latitud, hydrated[0].longitud);
            const d2 = computeHaversineKm(22.8905, -109.9167, hydrated[1].latitud, hydrated[1].longitud);
            const lbl1 = formatDistanceLabel(d1, lang, true);
            const lbl2 = formatDistanceLabel(d2, lang, true);
            assertTrue(lbl1 === lbl2, `Dos eventos del mismo club deben dar distancia idéntica: ${lbl1} vs ${lbl2}`);
            assertTrue(lbl1.includes('\u00A0km'), `Debe usar espacio duro \\u00A0km: ${lbl1}`);
            assertTrue(!lbl1.includes(' km'), `No debe contener espacio rompible ' km': ${lbl1}`);
            assertTrue(
              resolveLosCabosZoneKey(tuleLabel) === 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO',
              `El Tule (${tuleLabel}) debe mapear siempre a CORREDOR TURÍSTICO`
            );
          }
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );
    }
  );

  itCase(
    'Propiedad 4: resolveStudioFrontStageLabel jamás usa TARIMA/ESPEJO en deportes de cancha y estación usa CANCHA #N en Español',
    () => {
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.constantFrom('Pádel', 'padel', 'Tenis', 'Tennis', 'Pickleball', 'Voleibol', 'Volleyball', 'Bádminton', 'Squash'),
          fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 50 }),
          fc.boolean(),
          (courtDisc, spotNum, isEn) => {
            const stage = resolveStudioFrontStageLabel(courtDisc, 'court', isEn);
            const upper = stage.toUpperCase();
            assertTrue(
              !upper.includes('TARIMA') && !upper.includes('ESPEJO') && !upper.includes('COACH STAGE') && !upper.includes('MIRROR'),
              `Etiqueta inválida para disciplina de cancha ${courtDisc}: ${stage}`
            );
            assertTrue(
              stage === (isEn ? 'CENTER NET / COURT FRONT' : 'RED CENTRAL / FRENTE DE CANCHA'),
              `Etiqueta esperada de red/cancha no coincide: ${stage}`
            );
            const badgeEs = formatStudioStationBadge('court', spotNum, false);
            const ctaEs = formatUseStudioStationCta('court', spotNum, false);
            assertTrue(badgeEs === `CANCHA #${spotNum}`, `Badge ES esperado CANCHA #${spotNum}, recibido ${badgeEs}`);
            assertTrue(ctaEs === `USAR CANCHA #${spotNum}`, `CTA ES esperado USAR CANCHA #${spotNum}, recibido ${ctaEs}`);
            assertTrue(!badgeEs.includes('COURT') && !ctaEs.includes('COURT'), 'No debe mezclar COURT en Español');
          }
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );
    }
  );
});

    FILE: src/app/(tabs)/index.tsx

    // === src/app/(tabs)/index.tsx === //
import { useFocusEffect, usePathname, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, StatusBar, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { ScreenSkeletonScanner, triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import { ExploreHeaderControls } from '../../components/explorar/ExploreHeaderControls';
import { ExploreMapViewSection } from '../../components/explorar/ExploreMapViews';
import { ExploreOverlaysStack, ExplorePinCacheWarmer } from '../../components/explorar/ExploreOverlaysStack';
import { isClubStrictlyActiveUI } from '../../components/explorar/ExploreRadarMapPin';
import { ExploreVenuesListSection } from '../../components/explorar/ExploreVenuesListSection';
import {
  EXPLORE_ZONE_CAMERA_TARGETS,
  isNearMeExploreZone,
  resolveExploreZoneCamera,
} from '../../components/explorar/ExploreZoneAndSortModals';
import { resolveCategoryImage } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { useExploreBookingAndRpg } from '../../hooks/explorar/useExploreBookingAndRpg';
import { useExplorar } from '../../hooks/useExplorar';
import { globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { styles as baseStyles } from '../../styles/tabs/index.styles';
import { buildMapPreviewCardData } from '../../utils/exploreEnrichmentHelpers';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { computePinCameraFocusOffset } from '../../utils/exploreMapAndSearchHelpers';
import { KINETIC_RADAR_RADIUS_KM, padZoneCameraRegion, resolveZoneCameraDurationMs } from '../../services/exploreZoneHelpers';
import { subscribeKineticRootRefresh } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';

const pickValidCoord = (...vals: unknown[]): number | null => {
  for (const v of vals) {
    if (v == null || v === '') continue;
    const n = Number(v);
    if (Number.isFinite(n) && Math.abs(n) > 0.1) return n;
  }
  return null;
};

export default function ExplorarScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [isFocused, setIsFocused] = useState(true);
  const { user, session, profile, refreshProfile, activateResilientSession } = useAuth();
  const { themeColors, tx, td, tDisc, fmtDist, fmtDate, language, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, ['resetZoneBtn', 'resetZoneBtnText']), [resolvedTheme]);
  const mapRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const mainScrollRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);

  const {
    isLoading, hasLoadedInitial, refreshing, isAdminView, afiliadosRaw, afiliadosEnriquecidos, sectionsByDiscipline, categoryCountsMap, mapMarkers,
    favoriteIds, toggleFavorite, searchQuery, setSearchQuery, selectedCategories, setSelectedCategories,
    quickChip, setQuickChip, sortBy, setSortBy, viewMode, setViewMode, selectedZone, setSelectedZone,
    mapLayers, toggleMapLayer, resetMapLayers, userCoords, hasRealUserCoords, isAcquiringRealGps, syncUserGpsLocation, onRefresh, loadExploreData,
  } = useExplorar(user, profile, session);

  const [selectedClub, setSelectedClub] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [selectedEvent, setSelectedEvent] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [mapPinPreview, setMapPinPreview] = useState<{ pin: any; isEvent: boolean } | null>(null);
  const [showMyRpgModal, setShowMyRpgModal] = useState(false);
  const [catPickerOpen, setCatPickerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [sortPickerOpen, setSortPickerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [zonePickerOpen, setZonePickerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isMembershipOpen, setIsMembershipOpen] = useState(false);
  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<any>({ visible: false });
  const [webMapFocus, setWebMapFocus] = useState<{ lat: number; lng: number; zoom?: number; offset?: [number, number]; ts: number } | null>(null);
  const [nativeFocusRegion, setNativeFocusRegion] = useState<{
    latitude: number;
    longitude: number;
    latitudeDelta: number;
    longitudeDelta: number;
    ts: number;
  } | null>(null);

  const isFocusedRef = useRef(true);
  const uiTimersRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]>([]);
  const scheduleUi = useCallback((fn: () => void, ms: number) => {
    const id = setTimeout(fn, ms);
    uiTimersRef.current.push(id);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => () => { uiTimersRef.current.forEach(clearTimeout); uiTimersRef.current = []; }, []);

  const { myUserStats, myPrestigeInfo, mySeasonInfo, handleReserveEvent } = useExploreBookingAndRpg({
    user,
    profile,
    isAdminView,
    activateResilientSession,
    refreshProfile,
    loadExploreData,
    setSelectedEvent,
    setAlertConfig,
    setIsMembershipOpen,
    scheduleUi,
    tx,
  });

  const hasMountedFocusRef = useRef(false);
  useFocusEffect(useCallback(() => {
    isFocusedRef.current = true;
    setIsFocused(true);
    if (hasMountedFocusRef.current || hasLoadedInitial) {
      loadExploreData(true, true);
    }
    hasMountedFocusRef.current = true;
    return () => { isFocusedRef.current = false; setIsFocused(false); };
  }, [loadExploreData, hasLoadedInitial]));

  useEffect(() => {
    const isExploreRoute = pathname === '/' || pathname === '/index';
    if (!isFocused || !isExploreRoute) return;
    const gpsTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (isFocusedRef.current) {
        syncUserGpsLocation().catch(() => {});
      }
    }, 350);
    return () => clearTimeout(gpsTimer);
  }, [isFocused, pathname, syncUserGpsLocation]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return subscribeKineticRootRefresh(() => {
      if (isFocusedRef.current) loadExploreData(true, true);
    });
  }, [loadExploreData]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedClub((prev: any) => {
      if (!prev) return null;
      const pid = String(prev.id || '');
      const pName = String(prev.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const fresh = afiliadosEnriquecidos.find(
        (a: any) => (pid && String(a.id || '') === pid) || (pName && String(a.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === pName)
      );
      return fresh ? { ...prev, ...fresh } : prev;
    });
  }, [afiliadosEnriquecidos]);

  const handlePullToRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    setMapPinPreview(null);
    await onRefresh();
  }, [onRefresh]);

  const handleViewAllCaboZones = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Medium');
    setSelectedZone('todas');
    setSelectedCategories([]);
    setQuickChip('todas');
    setSearchQuery('');
    setMapPinPreview(null);
    const allCam = padZoneCameraRegion(EXPLORE_ZONE_CAMERA_TARGETS.TODAS, 1.18);
    const animMs = resolveZoneCameraDurationMs(resolveExploreZoneCamera(selectedZone, userCoords), allCam);
    setWebMapFocus({ lat: allCam.latitude, lng: allCam.longitude, zoom: allCam.webZoom, ts: Date.now() });
    setNativeFocusRegion({ ...allCam, ts: Date.now() });
    if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
      mapRef.current?.animateToRegion?.(allCam, animMs);
    }
  }, [setSelectedZone, setSelectedCategories, setQuickChip, setSearchQuery, selectedZone, userCoords]);

  const handleFocusClubOnMap = useCallback(
    (club: any) => {
      if (!club) return;
      if (!isAdminView && !isClubStrictlyActiveUI(club)) return;
      triggerHaptic('Medium');
      resetMapLayers();
      setQuickChip('todas');
      setSelectedCategories([]);
      if (isNearMeExploreZone(selectedZone) && Number(club.distanciaKm) > KINETIC_RADAR_RADIUS_KM) {
        setSelectedZone('todas');
      }
      const targetLat = pickValidCoord(club.latitude, club.latitud, club.coords?.latitude, userCoords.latitude) ?? 22.8905;
      const targetLng = pickValidCoord(club.longitude, club.longitud, club.coords?.longitude, userCoords.longitude) ?? -109.9167;
      const isSpecClub = Boolean(club.es_especialista || String(club.categoria || '').toLowerCase().includes('especialista'));
      const rawFocusDisc = String(club.disciplina || '').trim();
      const resolvedFocusDisc = isSpecClub && (!rawFocusDisc || rawFocusDisc.toLowerCase() === 'fuerza') ? 'Fisioterapia' : (rawFocusDisc || 'Fuerza');
      const fallbackImg = resolveCategoryImage(resolvedFocusDisc, club.categoria || club.nombre);
      setViewMode('mapa');
      setMapPinPreview({
        pin: {
          id: `af_${club.id}`,
          markerType: isSpecClub ? 'specialist' : 'club',
          latitude: targetLat,
          longitude: targetLng,
          title: club.nombre,
          disciplina: resolvedFocusDisc,
          avatar_url: club.avatar_url || club.portada_url || club.imagen || club.coverResolved || fallbackImg,
          rawAffiliate: club,
          scale: 1.35,
          haloColor: '#00F0FF',
        },
        isEvent: false,
      });
      const camOffset = computePinCameraFocusOffset(targetLat, targetLng, 0.0045);
      const streetRegion = {
        latitude: camOffset.latitude,
        longitude: camOffset.longitude,
        latitudeDelta: camOffset.latitudeDelta,
        longitudeDelta: camOffset.longitudeDelta,
        ts: Date.now(),
      };
      setNativeFocusRegion(streetRegion);
      setWebMapFocus({ lat: targetLat, lng: targetLng, zoom: 17, offset: camOffset.webOffset, ts: Date.now() });
      if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
        scheduleUi(() => {
          mapRef.current?.animateToRegion?.(
            {
              latitude: camOffset.latitude,
              longitude: camOffset.longitude,
              latitudeDelta: camOffset.latitudeDelta,
              longitudeDelta: camOffset.longitudeDelta,
            },
            450
          );
        }, 120);
      }
    },
    [isAdminView, resetMapLayers, selectedZone, setSelectedZone, setQuickChip, setSelectedCategories, setViewMode, userCoords, scheduleUi]
  );

  const centerOnUserGps = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    const lat = Number(userCoords.latitude || 22.8905);
    const lng = Number(userCoords.longitude || -109.9167);
    setWebMapFocus({ lat, lng, zoom: 14, ts: Date.now() });
    setNativeFocusRegion({ latitude: lat, longitude: lng, latitudeDelta: 0.045, longitudeDelta: 0.045, ts: Date.now() });
    if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
      mapRef.current?.animateToRegion?.(
        { latitude: lat, longitude: lng, latitudeDelta: 0.045, longitudeDelta: 0.045 },
        resolveZoneCameraDurationMs(resolveExploreZoneCamera(selectedZone, userCoords), { latitude: lat, longitude: lng })
      );
    }
    syncUserGpsLocation().catch(() => {});
  }, [syncUserGpsLocation, userCoords, selectedZone]);

  const openClaseModal = useCallback((clase: any, parentClub?: any) => {
    if (!clase) return;
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    const fallbackClubAv = resolveCategoryImage(
      parentClub?.disciplina || clase?.categoria || 'Fuerza',
      parentClub?.categoria || parentClub?.nombre
    );
    setSelectedEvent({
      ...clase,
      negocio_nombre: clase.negocio_nombre || parentClub?.nombre || 'Kinetic Oficial',
      ubicacion: clase.ubicacion || parentClub?.ubicacion || tx('Ubicación Verificada', 'Verified Location'),
      avatar_club: parentClub?.avatar_url || clase.avatar_club || fallbackClubAv,
    });
  }, [tx]);

  const handleSelectPin = useCallback((m: any, isEvent: boolean) => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setMapPinPreview({ pin: m, isEvent });
    const lat = pickValidCoord(m?.latitude, m?.rawAffiliate?.latitude, m?.rawAffiliate?.latitud);
    const lng = pickValidCoord(m?.longitude, m?.rawAffiliate?.longitude, m?.rawAffiliate?.longitud);
    if (lat !== null && lng !== null) {
      const camOffset = computePinCameraFocusOffset(lat, lng, 0.0045);
      setNativeFocusRegion({
        latitude: camOffset.latitude,
        longitude: camOffset.longitude,
        latitudeDelta: camOffset.latitudeDelta,
        longitudeDelta: camOffset.longitudeDelta,
        ts: Date.now(),
      });
      setWebMapFocus({ lat, lng, zoom: 17, offset: camOffset.webOffset, ts: Date.now() });
      if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
        mapRef.current?.animateToRegion?.(
          {
            latitude: camOffset.latitude,
            longitude: camOffset.longitude,
            latitudeDelta: camOffset.latitudeDelta,
            longitudeDelta: camOffset.longitudeDelta,
          },
          420
        );
      }
    }
  }, []);

  const handleOpenPreviewFullModal = useCallback(() => {
    if (!mapPinPreview) return;
    triggerHaptic('Medium');
    const { pin, isEvent } = mapPinPreview;
    if (isEvent) openClaseModal(pin.rawEvent || pin, pin.rawAffiliate);
    else setSelectedClub(pin.rawAffiliate || pin);
  }, [mapPinPreview, openClaseModal]);

  const handlePressMyUserPin = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Medium');
    setMapPinPreview(null);
    setShowMyRpgModal(true);
  }, []);

  const disciplineText = useMemo(() => {
    if (selectedCategories.length === 1) return tDisc(selectedCategories[0]);
    if (selectedCategories.length > 1) return `${selectedCategories.length} ${tx('Disciplinas', 'Disciplines')}`;
    if (quickChip === 'favoritos') return `⚡ ${tx('Favoritos', 'Favorites')}`;
    if (quickChip === 'mas_buscados') return `🔥 ${tx('Tendencia', 'Trending')}`;
    if (quickChip === 'wellness') return `✨ ${tx('Wellness & Spa', 'Wellness & Spa')}`;
    return quickChip === 'fitness' ? tx('Fitness & Deportes', 'Fitness & Sports') : tx('Todas', 'All');
  }, [quickChip, selectedCategories, tx, tDisc, language]);

  const sortLabelText = useMemo(() => {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      relevancia: tx('Relevancia', 'Relevance'), distancia: tx('Más Cercanos', 'Nearest'),
      rating_desc: tx('Mejor Valorados ★', 'Top Rated ★'), rating_asc: tx('Menor Valoración', 'Lowest Rated'),
      disponibilidad: tx('Mayor Cupo', 'Most Open Spots'), precio_asc: tx('Menor Precio KC', 'Lowest KC Price'),
      precio_desc: tx('Mayor Precio KC', 'Highest KC Price'), az: tx('Alfabético (A-Z)', 'Alphabetical (A-Z)'),
      za: tx('Alfabético (Z-A)', 'Alphabetical (Z-A)'),
    };
    return map[sortBy] || tx('Relevancia', 'Relevance');
  }, [sortBy, tx, language]);

  const visibleSectionsByDiscipline = useMemo(() => {
    if (isAdminView) return sectionsByDiscipline;
    return sectionsByDiscipline
      .map((sec: any) => {
        const rawItems = sec.data || sec.items || [];
        const activeOnly = rawItems.filter((club: any) => isClubStrictlyActiveUI(club));
        return activeOnly.length === rawItems.length
          ? sec
          : { ...sec, data: activeOnly, items: activeOnly };
      })
      .filter((sec: any) => (sec.data || sec.items || []).length > 0);
  }, [sectionsByDiscipline, isAdminView]);

  const visibleMapMarkers = useMemo(() => {
    if (isAdminView) return mapMarkers;
    return mapMarkers.filter((m: any) => {
      const mType = String(m?.markerType || '').toLowerCase();
      const isEv = mType === 'event' || mType === 'evento';
      const parentClub = m?.rawAffiliate ?? (!isEv ? m : null);
      return parentClub ? isClubStrictlyActiveUI(parentClub) : true;
    });
  }, [mapMarkers, isAdminView]);

  const isNearMeZone = useMemo(() => isNearMeExploreZone(selectedZone), [selectedZone]);

  const activeMapInitialRegion = useMemo(() => {
    if (nativeFocusRegion) {
      return {
        latitude: nativeFocusRegion.latitude,
        longitude: nativeFocusRegion.longitude,
        latitudeDelta: nativeFocusRegion.latitudeDelta,
        longitudeDelta: nativeFocusRegion.longitudeDelta,
      };
    }
    return resolveExploreZoneCamera(selectedZone, userCoords);
  }, [nativeFocusRegion, selectedZone, userCoords]);

  const previewCardData = useMemo(() => {
    const data = buildMapPreviewCardData(mapPinPreview, { tDisc, fmtDist, fmtDate, tx, language });
    if (!data) return null;
    const rawPin = mapPinPreview?.pin;
    const rawClub = rawPin?.rawAffiliate || (!mapPinPreview?.isEvent ? rawPin : null);
    const rawEv = rawPin?.rawEvent || (mapPinPreview?.isEvent ? rawPin : null);
    const isHidden = rawClub ? !isClubStrictlyActiveUI(rawClub) : false;
    if (!isAdminView && isHidden) return null;
    const rawDiscipline = (mapPinPreview?.isEvent ? rawEv?.categoria : rawClub?.disciplina) || rawPin?.disciplina || 'Fuerza';
    const fallbackUri = resolveCategoryImage(rawDiscipline, rawClub?.categoria || rawClub?.nombre || rawPin?.title);
    return {
      ...data,
      title: data.isEvent ? td(data.title) : data.title,
      bannerUri: data.bannerUri || rawEv?.portada_url || rawEv?.imagen_url || rawClub?.portada_url || fallbackUri,
      fallbackUri,
      rawDiscipline,
      isSpec: Boolean(rawClub?.es_especialista),
      isHidden,
      borderColor: data.borderColor,
      marginBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom + 82, 86),
      elevation: 0,
    };
  }, [mapPinPreview, isAdminView, tDisc, fmtDist, fmtDate, tx, td, language, insets.bottom]);

  if (isLoading && !hasLoadedInitial && afiliadosRaw.length === 0) return <ScreenSkeletonScanner variant="cards" topInset={insets.top} />;

  return (
    <View style={[globalStyles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={themeColors.statusBarStyle} backgroundColor={themeColors.background} />
      <ExplorePinCacheWarmer visibleMapMarkers={visibleMapMarkers} hiddenStyle={styles.hiddenExpoWarmer} />

      <ExploreHeaderControls
        insetsTop={insets.top}
        selectedZone={selectedZone}
        onOpenZonePicker={() => setZonePickerOpen(true)}
        viewMode={viewMode}
        onToggleViewMode={() => {
          triggerHaptic('Selection');
          resetMapLayers();
          setMapPinPreview(null);
          setNativeFocusRegion(null);
          const nextMode = viewMode === 'lista' ? 'mapa' : 'lista';
          if (nextMode === 'mapa') {
            const cam = resolveExploreZoneCamera(selectedZone, userCoords);
            setWebMapFocus({ lat: cam.latitude, lng: cam.longitude, zoom: cam.webZoom, ts: Date.now() });
          }
          setViewMode(nextMode);
        }}
        searchQuery={searchQuery}
        setSearchQuery={setSearchQuery}
        disciplineText={disciplineText}
        sortLabelText={sortLabelText}
        onOpenCatPicker={() => setCatPickerOpen(true)}
        onOpenSortPicker={() => setSortPickerOpen(true)}
      />

      {viewMode === 'mapa' ? (
        <ExploreMapViewSection
          mapRef={mapRef}
          mapMarkers={visibleMapMarkers}
          userCoords={userCoords}
          webMapFocus={webMapFocus}
          nativeFocusRegion={nativeFocusRegion}
          initialRegionOverride={activeMapInitialRegion}
          previewCardData={previewCardData}
          mapLayers={mapLayers}
          toggleMapLayer={toggleMapLayer}
          handleSelectPin={handleSelectPin}
          handlePressMyUserPin={handlePressMyUserPin}
          handleOpenPreviewFullModal={handleOpenPreviewFullModal}
          setMapPinPreview={setMapPinPreview}
          centerOnUserGps={centerOnUserGps}
          isAdminView={isAdminView}
          isNearMeEmpty={isNearMeZone && visibleSectionsByDiscipline.length === 0}
          onResetToAllCaboZones={handleViewAllCaboZones}
        />
      ) : (
        <ExploreVenuesListSection
          mainScrollRef={mainScrollRef}
          refreshing={refreshing}
          onPullToRefresh={handlePullToRefresh}
          sectionsByDiscipline={visibleSectionsByDiscipline}
          isNearMeZone={isNearMeZone}
          onViewAllCaboZones={handleViewAllCaboZones}
          selectedCategories={selectedCategories}
          sortBy={sortBy}
          quickChip={quickChip}
          favoriteIds={favoriteIds}
          isAdminView={isAdminView}
          hasRealUserCoords={hasRealUserCoords}
          isAcquiringRealGps={isAcquiringRealGps}
          onToggleFavorite={toggleFavorite}
          onFocusClubOnMap={handleFocusClubOnMap}
          onSelectClub={setSelectedClub}
          onSelectClase={openClaseModal}
        />
      )}

      <ExploreOverlaysStack
        insetsBottom={insets.bottom}
        catPickerOpen={catPickerOpen} setCatPickerOpen={setCatPickerOpen}
        sortPickerOpen={sortPickerOpen} setSortPickerOpen={setSortPickerOpen}
        zonePickerOpen={zonePickerOpen} setZonePickerOpen={setZonePickerOpen}
        selectedCategories={selectedCategories} setSelectedCategories={setSelectedCategories}
        quickChip={quickChip} setQuickChip={setQuickChip}
        favoriteIds={favoriteIds}
        sortBy={sortBy} setSortBy={setSortBy}
        selectedZone={selectedZone}
        setSelectedZone={(z: string) => {
          setNativeFocusRegion(null);
          setSelectedZone(z);
        }}
        categoryCountsMap={categoryCountsMap} viewMode={viewMode}
        setMapPinPreview={setMapPinPreview} setWebMapFocus={setWebMapFocus}
        mapRef={mapRef} centerOnUserGps={centerOnUserGps}
        selectedClub={selectedClub} setSelectedClub={setSelectedClub}
        onToggleClubFavorite={() => selectedClub && toggleFavorite(selectedClub)}
        onSelectClaseFromClub={(cl: any) => {
          const parent = selectedClub;
          setSelectedClub(null);
          scheduleUi(() => openClaseModal(cl, parent), 190);
        }}
        onRefreshRadar={() => loadExploreData(true, true)}
        selectedEvent={selectedEvent} setSelectedEvent={setSelectedEvent}
        onReserveEvent={handleReserveEvent}
        showMyRpgModal={showMyRpgModal} setShowMyRpgModal={setShowMyRpgModal}
        onGoToTrain={() => {
          setShowMyRpgModal(false);
          scheduleUi(() => router.push('/(tabs)/eventos'), 190);
        }}
        nivel={profile?.nivel || 'Acceso Base'}
        bonusXp={Number(profile?.xp_total) || 0}
        myUserStats={myUserStats} mySeasonInfo={mySeasonInfo} myPrestigeInfo={myPrestigeInfo}
        isMembershipOpen={isMembershipOpen} setIsMembershipOpen={setIsMembershipOpen}
        alertConfig={alertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

    FILE: src/components/AffiliateCard.styles.ts

import { Platform, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { COLORS, RADIUS } from '../styles/globalStyles';

const webGlassBlur: any =
  Platform.OS === 'web'
    ? { backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)', WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(10px)' }
    : {};

export const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  cardContainer: {
    backgroundColor: '#090C12',
    borderRadius: RADIUS.xxl,
    borderWidth: 1.2,
    borderColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)',
    borderTopWidth: 1.5,
    borderTopColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16)',
    borderBottomWidth: 4,
    borderBottomColor: '#03060B',
    overflow: 'hidden',
    shadowColor: '#000000',
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 6 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.32,
    shadowRadius: 10,
    elevation: 8,
  },
  mediaWrapper: {

    FILE: src/components/AffiliateCard.tsx

    // === src/components/AffiliateCard.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Dimensions, Platform, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { cleanLocationDisplay, resolveCategoryImage } from '../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { evaluateVenueOpenStatusMazatlan } from '../services/kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';
import { COLORS } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { styles as baseStyles } from './AffiliateCard.styles';
import { IKineticStoryOwner, KineticStoriesModal, SkeletonPulse, SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';
import { shouldShowLastSpotsBadge } from './eventos/eventDetailsPureHelpers';
import { getAvatarUrl, getCoverUrl } from '../utils/supabaseImage';

const { width: SCREEN_WIDTH } = Dimensions.get('window');
const DEFAULT_CARD_WIDTH = Math.round(SCREEN_WIDTH * 0.84);
const KINETIC_LOGO = require('../../assets/logo.png');
const ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } : {};
const ON_MEDIA_WHITE_TEXT_SET: any =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticOnMedia: 'true', kineticInk: 'light' } } : {};
const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;
const AFFILIATE_MEDIA_LOCK_KEYS = ['mediaWrapper', 'prospectDimOverlay', 'disciplinePill', 'disciplinePillText', 'activeStatusPill', 'activeStatusText', 'prospectPill', 'prospectPillText', 'circleActionBtn', 'circleActionBtnFavBlackCyan', 'storyMiniDot'];

type AffiliateExpPreview = { id?: string | number; duracion_minutos?: string | number; nombre_servicio?: string | null; titulo?: string | null; [k: string]: unknown };

const toSafeKcPrice = (val: unknown, fallback: number): number => {
  const n = Number(val);
  return val !== null && val !== undefined && val !== '' && Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.max(0, n) : fallback;
};

export const AffiliateCard = React.memo(
  ({
    affiliate,
    cardWidth = DEFAULT_CARD_WIDTH,
    contextualDiscipline,
    isFavorite = false,
    isAdminView = false,
    hasRealUserCoords = true,
    isAcquiringRealGps = false,
    onToggleFavorite,
    onFocusMap,
    onSelectClub,
    onSelectClase,
  }: any) => {
    const { tx, td, tDisc, fmtDist, language, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(
      () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, AFFILIATE_MEDIA_LOCK_KEYS),
      [resolvedTheme]
    );
    const [isStoryModalOpen, setIsStoryModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [optimisticFav, setOptimisticFav] = useState<boolean>(() => Boolean(isFavorite));

    useEffect(() => {
      setOptimisticFav(Boolean(isFavorite));
    }, [isFavorite]);

    const openStatus = evaluateVenueOpenStatusMazatlan(affiliate?.horarios, undefined, language);

    const nombreClub = affiliate?.nombre || tx('Sede Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Venue');
    const rawPrimaryDisc = String(nombreClub).trim().toLowerCase().includes('kinetic especialista')
      ? 'Fisioterapia'
      : affiliate?.disciplina || affiliate?.categoria || 'Fuerza';

    const displayDisciplina = useMemo(() => {
      const ctx = String(contextualDiscipline || '').trim();
      if (!ctx || ctx.toLowerCase() === 'todas') return rawPrimaryDisc;
      const pool: string[] = [
        rawPrimaryDisc,
        ...(Array.isArray(affiliate?.allDisciplines) ? affiliate.allDisciplines : []),
        ...(Array.isArray(affiliate?.disciplinas_secundarias) ? affiliate.disciplinas_secundarias : []),
      ].filter(Boolean);
      const matched = pool.find((d) => String(d).trim().toLowerCase() === ctx.toLowerCase());
      return matched || rawPrimaryDisc;
    }, [contextualDiscipline, rawPrimaryDisc, affiliate?.allDisciplines, affiliate?.disciplinas_secundarias]);

    const disciplina = displayDisciplina;
    const isSpec = Boolean(affiliate?.es_especialista);
    const rawEst = String(affiliate?.estado ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const esActivo =
      affiliate?.esClubActivo !== false &&
      affiliate?.esActivo !== false &&
      affiliate?.activo !== false &&
      (!rawEst || rawEst === 'activo') &&
      Boolean(rawEst === 'activo' || affiliate?.esClubActivo === true || affiliate?.esActivo === true);
    const experiencias = Array.isArray(affiliate?.experiencias) ? affiliate.experiencias : [];

    const categoryFallbackUri = useMemo(
      () => resolveCategoryImage(affiliate?.disciplina || disciplina, nombreClub),
      [affiliate?.disciplina, disciplina, nombreClub]
    );

    const cleanValidUri = (val: any): string => {
      const s = typeof val === 'string' ? val.trim() : '';
      return s.length >= 8 && s !== 'null' && s !== 'undefined' && /^(https?:\/\/|data:image\/|file:\/\/|blob:)/i.test(s) ? s : '';
    };

    const coverImageUri = useMemo(() => {
      const rawCover = cleanValidUri(affiliate?.portada_url || affiliate?.imagen || affiliate?.coverResolved);
      if (rawCover) return cleanValidUri(getCoverUrl(rawCover, 720, 405)) || rawCover || categoryFallbackUri;
      return categoryFallbackUri;
    }, [affiliate?.portada_url, affiliate?.imagen, affiliate?.coverResolved, categoryFallbackUri]);

    const isOfficialKineticBrand = useMemo(() => {
      const lower = String(nombreClub).trim().toLowerCase();
      return lower === 'kinetic oficial' || lower === 'kinetic sports club';
    }, [nombreClub]);

    const rawAvatarInput = cleanValidUri(affiliate?.avatar_url || affiliate?.logo_url);
    const avatarCandidates = useMemo(() => {
      const optAv = rawAvatarInput ? cleanValidUri(getAvatarUrl(rawAvatarInput, 160)) : '';
      const rawCov = cleanValidUri(affiliate?.portada_url || affiliate?.imagen || affiliate?.coverResolved);
      return Array.from(new Set([optAv, rawAvatarInput, coverImageUri, rawCov, categoryFallbackUri].filter(Boolean)));
    }, [rawAvatarInput, coverImageUri, affiliate?.portada_url, affiliate?.imagen, affiliate?.coverResolved, categoryFallbackUri]);

    const [avatarCandidateIdx, setAvatarCandidateIdx] = useState(0);
    const [isLogoLoaded, setIsLogoLoaded] = useState(false);
    useEffect(() => {
      setAvatarCandidateIdx(0);
    }, [affiliate?.id, rawAvatarInput, coverImageUri]);

    const logoSquareUri = useMemo(() => {
      if (isOfficialKineticBrand && !rawAvatarInput) return null;
      return avatarCandidates[Math.min(avatarCandidateIdx, Math.max(0, avatarCandidates.length - 1))] || coverImageUri || categoryFallbackUri;
    }, [isOfficialKineticBrand, rawAvatarInput, avatarCandidates, avatarCandidateIdx, coverImageUri, categoryFallbackUri]);

    useEffect(() => {
      setIsLogoLoaded(!logoSquareUri);
    }, [logoSquareUri]);

    const { totalCuposDisponibles, totalCuposOcupados, totalCuposMaximos } = useMemo(() => {
      return experiencias.reduce(
        (acc: { totalCuposDisponibles: number; totalCuposOcupados: number; totalCuposMaximos: number }, e: any) => {
          const maxC = Math.max(1, Number(e.cupo_maximo) || 10);
          const occC = Math.max(0, Number(e.cupo_actual) || 0);
          acc.totalCuposMaximos += maxC;
          acc.totalCuposOcupados += occC;
          acc.totalCuposDisponibles += Math.max(0, maxC - occC);
          return acc;
        },
        { totalCuposDisponibles: 0, totalCuposOcupados: 0, totalCuposMaximos: 0 }
      );
    }, [experiencias]);

    const minEscrowKc = useMemo(() => {
      if (experiencias.length === 0) return 60;
      const minPrice = Math.min(...experiencias.map((e: AffiliateExpPreview) => toSafeKcPrice(e?.precio_kc, 100)));
      return Math.round(minPrice * 0.4);
    }, [experiencias]);

    const accentColor = isSpec ? COLORS.purple : COLORS.cyan;
    const cleanUbicacion = cleanLocationDisplay(
      affiliate?.ubicacion,
      tx('Ubicación Verificada', 'Verified Location')
    );

    const storyOwnerPayload = useMemo<IKineticStoryOwner>(
      () => ({
        id: String(affiliate?.id || nombreClub),
        name: nombreClub,
        subtitle: `${isSpec ? tx('Especialista', 'Specialist') : tx('Club', 'Club')} • ${tDisc(disciplina)}`,
        avatarUrl: logoSquareUri,
        isOfficialKinetic: isOfficialKineticBrand,
        isSpecialist: isSpec,
        discipline: disciplina,
        canUpload: false,
      }),
      [affiliate?.id, nombreClub, isSpec, disciplina, logoSquareUri, isOfficialKineticBrand, tx, tDisc]
    );

    // Doble candado visual [V5-02]: jamás renderizar clubes inactivos/ocultos si !isAdminView
    if (!isAdminView && !esActivo) return null;

    const cardBorderColor = esActivo
      ? isSpec
        ? themeColors.isLight ? '#C4B5FD' : 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.45)'
        : themeColors.isLight ? '#7DD3FC' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.38)'
      : themeColors.isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.24)';

    return (
      <View
        style={[
          styles.cardContainer,
          NO_SEL,
          { width: cardWidth, borderColor: cardBorderColor },
          !esActivo && { opacity: 0.62 },
          themeColors.isLight && {
            backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
            borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
            borderBottomColor: !esActivo ? '#94A3B8' : isSpec ? '#7C3AED' : '#0284C7',
            shadowColor: '#0F172A',
            shadowOpacity: 0.1,
          },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={styles.mediaWrapper}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
            activeOpacity={0.92}
            onPress={() => onSelectClub?.(affiliate)}
          >
            <SmartImage
              source={{ uri: coverImageUri }}
              fallbackUri={categoryFallbackUri}
              style={styles.coverImage}
              contentFit="cover"
              fallbackCategory={affiliate?.disciplina || disciplina}
            />
            {!esActivo && <View style={styles.prospectDimOverlay} />}
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <View style={styles.topBadgesRow} pointerEvents="box-none">
            <View style={[styles.leftBadgesRow, { flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 5 }]} pointerEvents="none">
              <View
                {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
                style={[styles.disciplinePill, { elevation: 0, maxWidth: '58%', borderColor: accentColor, borderBottomColor: isSpec ? '#5A189A' : '#006B78' }]}
              >
                <View style={[styles.dotIndicator, { backgroundColor: accentColor }]} />
                <Text {...ON_MEDIA_WHITE_TEXT_SET} style={[styles.disciplinePillText, { color: '#FFFFFF', flexShrink: 1 }]} numberOfLines={1} ellipsizeMode="tail">
                  {tDisc(disciplina).toUpperCase()}
                </Text>
              </View>

              {esActivo && experiencias.length === 0 ? (
                <View {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET} style={[styles.activeStatusPill, { elevation: 0, flexShrink: 0, maxWidth: '60%', borderColor: openStatus.isOpen ? '#10B981' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.55)' }]}>
                  <Text {...ON_MEDIA_WHITE_TEXT_SET} style={[styles.activeStatusText, { color: openStatus.isOpen ? '#00FF66' : '#FFFFFF' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {openStatus.isOpen ? openStatus.badgeText : tx('🕒 PRONTO', '🕒 SOON')}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              ) : esActivo && totalCuposDisponibles === 0 ? (
                <View {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET} style={[styles.prospectPill, { elevation: 0, borderColor: '#F59E0B', paddingHorizontal: 10 }]}>
                  <Feather name="alert-circle" size={10} color="#FBBF24" />
                  <Text {...ON_MEDIA_WHITE_TEXT_SET} style={[styles.prospectPillText, { color: '#FBBF24', fontSize: 9.5, letterSpacing: 0.5 }]} numberOfLines={1} ellipsizeMode="tail">
                    {tx('CUPO LLENO', 'SOLD OUT')}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              ) : esActivo && totalCuposDisponibles > 0 ? (
                <View {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET} style={[styles.activeStatusPill, { elevation: 0 }]}>
                  <Ionicons name="flash" size={10} color={COLORS.cyan} />
                  <Text {...ON_MEDIA_WHITE_TEXT_SET} style={[styles.activeStatusText, { color: '#FFFFFF' }]} numberOfLines={1} ellipsizeMode="tail">
                    {shouldShowLastSpotsBadge(totalCuposOcupados, totalCuposMaximos)
                      ? tx(`ÚLTIMOS ${totalCuposDisponibles} LUGARES`, `LAST ${totalCuposDisponibles} SPOTS`)
                      : tx(`${totalCuposDisponibles} LUGARES DISP.`, `${totalCuposDisponibles} SPOTS OPEN`)}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              ) : !esActivo ? (
                <View {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET} style={[styles.prospectPill, { elevation: 0, borderColor: COLORS.yellow, paddingHorizontal: 10 }]}>
                  <Feather name="eye-off" size={11} color={COLORS.yellow} />
                  <Text {...ON_MEDIA_WHITE_TEXT_SET} style={[styles.prospectPillText, { color: COLORS.yellow, fontSize: 9.5, letterSpacing: 0.5 }]} numberOfLines={1} ellipsizeMode="tail">
                    {tx('OCULTO', 'HIDDEN')}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              ) : null}
            </View>

            <View style={styles.rightActionsRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
                style={[
                  styles.circleActionBtn,
                  { elevation: 0 },
                  optimisticFav && styles.circleActionBtnFavBlackCyan,
                ]}
                activeOpacity={0.78}
                hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}
                onPress={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('Light');
                  setOptimisticFav((prev) => !prev);
                  onToggleFavorite?.(affiliate);
                }}
              >
                <Ionicons
                  name={optimisticFav ? 'flash' : 'flash-outline'}
                  size={17}
                  color={optimisticFav ? COLORS.cyan : '#FFFFFF'}
                />
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
                style={[styles.circleActionBtn, { elevation: 0 }]}
                activeOpacity={0.78}
                hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}
                onPress={() => onFocusMap?.(affiliate)}
              >
                <Feather name="map-pin" size={15} color={COLORS.cyan} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={[styles.darkInfoBody, themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF' }]}>
          <View style={styles.clubIdentityRow}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.logoSquareBox,
                NO_SEL,
                { borderColor: accentColor, borderTopColor: isSpec ? '#E0AAFF' : '#B8FFFF', borderBottomColor: isSpec ? '#5A189A' : '#006B78' },
                themeColors.isLight && {
                  backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderBottomColor: isSpec ? '#7C3AED' : '#00B8D4',
                  shadowColor: isSpec ? '#7C3AED' : '#00B8D4',
                  shadowOpacity: 0.22,
                },
              ]}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={() => {
                triggerHaptic('Selection');
                setIsStoryModalOpen(true);
              }}
            >
              {logoSquareUri ? (
                <>
                  {!isLogoLoaded && <SkeletonPulse style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} />}
                  <Image
                    source={{ uri: logoSquareUri }}
                    recyclingKey={`${affiliate?.id || nombreClub}_av_${avatarCandidateIdx}`}
                    style={styles.logoSquareImg}
                    contentFit="cover"
                    cachePolicy="memory-disk"
                    transition={0}
                    onLoad={() => setIsLogoLoaded(true)}
                    onLoadEnd={() => setIsLogoLoaded(true)}
                    onError={() => {
                      setIsLogoLoaded(true);
                      setAvatarCandidateIdx((prev) =>
                        prev + 1 < avatarCandidates.length ? prev + 1 : prev
                      );
                    }}
                  />
                </>
              ) : (
                <Image source={KINETIC_LOGO} style={styles.logoSquareImg} contentFit="cover" />
              )}
              <View style={[styles.storyMiniDot, { backgroundColor: accentColor }]} />
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.clubTextBlock} activeOpacity={0.88} onPress={() => onSelectClub?.(affiliate)}>
              <Text style={[styles.clubTitleText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={2}>
                {nombreClub}
              </Text>
              <View style={styles.metaRow}>
                <Text style={[styles.ratingStarText, { color: themeColors.amberText }]}>
                  ★ {Number(affiliate?.rating || 5.0).toFixed(1)}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[styles.distanceGreenText, { color: themeColors.green }]}>
                  • {!hasRealUserCoords && isAcquiringRealGps
                    ? tx('Ubicando GPS...', 'Locating GPS...')
                    : fmtDist(affiliate?.distanciaKm ?? 1.2)}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[styles.locationMutedText, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                  • {cleanUbicacion}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {experiencias.length > 0 && (
            <View style={[styles.experiencesSection, themeColors.isLight && { borderTopColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}>
              <View style={styles.expHeaderRow}>
                <Text style={[styles.expCountLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                  {tx('EVENTOS ACTIVOS', 'ACTIVE EVENTS')} ({experiencias.length})
                </Text>
                <Text style={[styles.expEscrowHint, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                  {tx(`Aparta desde ${minEscrowKc} KC (40%)`, `Book from ${minEscrowKc} KC (40%)`)}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <ScrollView
                horizontal
                removeClippedSubviews={false}
                showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
                nestedScrollEnabled={true}
                contentContainerStyle={styles.expHorizontalList}
              >
                {experiencias.map((exp: AffiliateExpPreview, idx: number) => {
                  const precioTotal = toSafeKcPrice(exp?.precio_kc, 100);
                  const escrow = Math.round(precioTotal * 0.4);
                  const cupoDisp = Math.max(0, (Number(exp.cupo_maximo) || 10) - (Number(exp.cupo_actual) || 0));
                  const horaStr = String(exp.hora_inicio || exp.hora || '08:00').substring(0, 5);
                  const durStr = exp.duracion_minutos || 60;

                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      key={exp.id || idx}
                      style={[
                        styles.miniExpCard, NO_SEL,
                        themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7', shadowColor: '#0284C7', shadowOpacity: 0.12 },
                      ]}
                      activeOpacity={0.85}
                      onPress={() => onSelectClase?.({ ...exp, negocio_nombre: nombreClub, ubicacion: exp.ubicacion || affiliate?.ubicacion, avatar_club: logoSquareUri })}
                    >
                      <View style={styles.miniExpTopRow}>
                        <View style={[styles.miniExpTimePill, { elevation: 0 }, themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE' }]}>
                          <Text style={[styles.miniExpTimeText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{horaStr} • {durStr}m</Text>
                        </View>
                        <View style={[styles.miniExpPricePill, { elevation: 0 }, themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F3E8FF', borderColor: '#C4B5FD' }]}>
                          <Text style={[styles.miniExpPriceText, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>{precioTotal} KC</Text>
                        </View>
                      </View>

                      <Text style={[styles.miniExpTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {td(exp.nombre_servicio || exp.titulo) || tx('Evento Kinetic', 'Kinetic Event')}
                      </Text>

                      <View style={styles.miniExpBottomRow}>
                        <Text style={[styles.miniExpEscrowText, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>40%: {escrow} KC</Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.miniExpCuposText, { color: cupoDisp === 0 ? '#F59E0B' : themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                          {cupoDisp === 0 ? tx('Cupo lleno', 'Full') : `${cupoDisp} ${cupoDisp === 1 ? tx('lugar', 'spot') : tx('lugares', 'spots')}`}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                })}
              </ScrollView>
            </View>
          )}

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              styles.exploreSedeBtn,
              NO_SEL,
              !themeColors.isLight && { elevation: 0 },
              experiencias.length > 0 && { marginTop: 12 },
              isSpec && styles.exploreSpecialistBtn,
              themeColors.isLight && {
                backgroundColor: isSpec ? '#F5F3FF' : '#ECFEFF',
                borderColor: isSpec ? '#C4B5FD' : '#7DD3FC',
                borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderBottomColor: isSpec ? '#7C3AED' : '#0284C7',
                elevation: 4,
              },
            ]}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={() => onSelectClub?.(affiliate)}
          >
            <Feather name={isSpec ? 'user-check' : 'compass'} size={14} color={isSpec ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[styles.exploreSedeBtnText, { color: isSpec ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {isSpec
                ? tx('VER PERFIL DE ESPECIALISTA ›', 'VIEW SPECIALIST PROFILE ›')
                : tx('EXPLORAR SEDE E INSTALACIONES ›', 'EXPLORE VENUE & FACILITIES ›')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <KineticStoriesModal
          visible={isStoryModalOpen}
          owner={storyOwnerPayload}
          onClose={() => setIsStoryModalOpen(false)}
          onPrimaryAction={() => onSelectClub?.(affiliate)}
          primaryActionLabel={tx('ABRIR FICHA DE SEDE ›', 'OPEN VENUE PROFILE ›')}
        />
      </View>
    );
  },
  (prev: any, next: any) => {
    const pAf = prev.affiliate || {};
    const nAf = next.affiliate || {};
    const pExp = Array.isArray(pAf.experiencias) ? pAf.experiencias : [];
    const nExp = Array.isArray(nAf.experiencias) ? nAf.experiencias : [];
    const sumOcc = (arr: any[]) =>
      arr.reduce((s: number, e: any) => s + (Number(e?.cupo_actual ?? e?.reservadosCount) || 0), 0);
    return (
      pAf.id === nAf.id &&
      pAf.nombre === nAf.nombre &&
      pAf.portada_url === nAf.portada_url &&
      pAf.avatar_url === nAf.avatar_url &&
      pAf.imagen === nAf.imagen &&
      pAf.estado === nAf.estado &&
      pAf.esClubActivo === nAf.esClubActivo &&
      Boolean(pAf.esActivo) === Boolean(nAf.esActivo) &&
      Boolean(prev.isAdminView) === Boolean(next.isAdminView) &&
      Boolean(prev.isFavorite) === Boolean(next.isFavorite) &&
      Boolean(prev.hasRealUserCoords) === Boolean(next.hasRealUserCoords) &&
      Boolean(prev.isAcquiringRealGps) === Boolean(next.isAcquiringRealGps) &&
      Number(pAf.rating || 0) === Number(nAf.rating || 0) &&
      prev.cardWidth === next.cardWidth &&
      prev.contextualDiscipline === next.contextualDiscipline &&
      pAf.distanciaKm === nAf.distanciaKm &&
      pExp.length === nExp.length &&
      sumOcc(pExp) === sumOcc(nExp)
    );
  }
);

export default AffiliateCard;

import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, Pressable, ScrollView, Share, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View, useWindowDimensions } from 'react-native';
