const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;
const CD_LOCK_KEYS = [
  'heroBox', 'heroOverlay', 'heroNavCircle3D', 'circleTopBtn', 'circleTopBtnFavBlackCyan',
  'logoAvatarBox', 'dotItem', 'dotItemActive', 'photoCountBadge', 'photoCountText',
  'jumpToClassesPillBtn', 'jumpToClassesPillText', 'bottomPrimaryCyanBtn', 'bottomPrimaryCyanText',
];

export const ClubDetailsModal = ({
  visible, affiliate, isFavorite = false, onToggleFavorite, onClose, onSelectClase,
  onRefreshRadar, openInEditMode = false, isCreateMode = false, onCreatedAffiliate, onOpenCreateEvent,
  inlineOverlay = false, isAdmin: isAdminProp = false, portalOnWeb = true,
}: any) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { width: winW } = useWindowDimensions();
  const { user, session, profile } = useAuth();
  const { tx, td, tDisc, fmtDist, themeColors, language, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
  const cd = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseCd, resolvedTheme, CD_LOCK_KEYS), [resolvedTheme]);
  const scrollViewRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const igInputRef = useRef<TextInput | null>(null);
  const waInputRef = useRef<TextInput | null>(null);
  const focusTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const eventsSectionYRef = useRef<number>(420);
  const lastValidDataRef = useRef<any>(null);
  if (affiliate) lastValidDataRef.current = affiliate;
  const activeClub = affiliate || lastValidDataRef.current;

  useEffect(() => () => { if (focusTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(focusTimerRef.current); }, []);

  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<any>({ visible: false });
  const showTacticalAlert = useCallback(
    (title: string, message: string, type = 'alert', onConfirm: (() => void) | null = null) => {
      setAlertConfig({ visible: true, title, message, type, onConfirm });
    },
    []
  );

  const {
    isEditMode, setIsEditMode, isSaving, isUploadingImage, editData, setEditData, selectedAmenityIds,
    toggleAmenity, actualizarFotoCarruselOSede, guardarCambiosTexto, guardarCalificacionAuditoria,
  } = useClubDetailsModal(activeClub, user, onRefreshRadar, showTacticalAlert);

  const [heroCarouselIndex, setHeroCarouselIndex] = useState(0);
  const [isBioExpanded, setIsBioExpanded] = useState(false);
  const [isRatingModalOpen, setIsRatingModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isAmenitiesModalOpen, setIsAmenitiesModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isActivateB2BOpen, setIsActivateB2BOpen] = useState(false);
  const [storyState, setStoryState] = useState<{ open: boolean; uploadMode: boolean }>({ open: false, uploadMode: false });
  const [hasActiveStory, setHasActiveStory] = useState(false);
  const [scrollY, setScrollY] = useState(0);
  const [isCreating, setIsCreating] = useState(false);
  const createBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const [lightboxState, setLightboxState] = useState<{ visible: boolean; index: number; customImages?: string[] | null; isProfile?: boolean }>({ visible: false, index: 0, customImages: null, isProfile: false });

  const prevOpenClubKeyRef = useRef<string>('');
  const prevOpenClubNameRef = useRef<string>('');
  const frozenCreateModeRef = useRef<boolean>(Boolean(isCreateMode));
  if (visible) frozenCreateModeRef.current = Boolean(isCreateMode);
  const effectiveCreateMode = visible ? Boolean(isCreateMode) : frozenCreateModeRef.current;

  useEffect(() => {
    const clubKey = visible ? String(activeClub?.id || activeClub?.nombre || 'open') : '';
    const normName = visible ? String(activeClub?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() : '';
    const isSameOpenClub = Boolean(prevOpenClubKeyRef.current) && (prevOpenClubKeyRef.current === clubKey || (Boolean(normName) && prevOpenClubNameRef.current === normName));
    if (visible && !isSameOpenClub) {
      prevOpenClubKeyRef.current = clubKey;
      prevOpenClubNameRef.current = normName;
      setScrollY(0);
      scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false });
      createBusyRef.current = false;
      setIsCreating(false);
      setHeroCarouselIndex(0);
      setIsBioExpanded(false);
      if (openInEditMode || effectiveCreateMode) {
        setIsEditMode(true);
        if (effectiveCreateMode) {
          setEditData({ nombre: '', es_especialista: false, disciplina: 'Fuerza', ubicacion: '', horarios: 'Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 21:00', whatsapp: '', instagram: '', activo: true, amenidades: [] });
        }
      } else { setIsEditMode(false); }
    } else if (visible && isSameOpenClub) {
      prevOpenClubKeyRef.current = clubKey;
      if (normName) prevOpenClubNameRef.current = normName;
      if (openInEditMode || effectiveCreateMode) setIsEditMode(true);
    }
  }, [visible, activeClub?.id, activeClub?.nombre, openInEditMode, effectiveCreateMode, setIsEditMode, setEditData]);

  const handleModalHidden = useCallback(() => {
    prevOpenClubKeyRef.current = '';
    prevOpenClubNameRef.current = '';
    setIsEditMode(false);
  }, [setIsEditMode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (inlineOverlay && !visible) {
      handleModalHidden();
    }
  }, [inlineOverlay, visible, handleModalHidden]);

  const hasValidJwt = isRealSupabaseJwt(session?.access_token);
  const perms = useMemo(
    () => resolveClubOwnerPermissions(hasValidJwt, user, profile, activeClub),
    [hasValidJwt, user, profile, activeClub]
  );
  const isAdmin = Boolean(isAdminProp || perms.isAdmin);
  const isOwnerOfThisClub = perms.isOwnerOfThisClub;

  const currentClubKey = String(activeClub?.id || activeClub?.nombre || '');
  const hasSyncedEditData = Boolean(editData && editData.__clubKey === currentClubKey);
  const effectiveRecord = hasSyncedEditData || isEditMode || effectiveCreateMode ? editData : activeClub;

  const nombreSede = effectiveRecord?.nombre || activeClub?.nombre || tx('Sede Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Venue');
  const disciplina = effectiveRecord?.disciplina || activeClub?.disciplina || 'Fuerza';
  const isSpec = Boolean(activeClub?.es_especialista);
  const esActivo = Boolean(activeClub?.esActivo);
  const experiencias = Array.isArray(activeClub?.experiencias) ? activeClub.experiencias : [];
  const currentRatingValue = Number(effectiveRecord?.rating ?? activeClub?.rating ?? activeClub?.calificacion ?? 5.0);
  const canonicalClubCoords = useMemo(() => {
    const ubEdited =
      Boolean(editData?.ubicacion) &&
      String(editData?.ubicacion || '').trim() !== String(activeClub?.ubicacion || '').trim();
    const latUnchanged = editData?.latitud == null || Number(editData.latitud) === Number(activeClub?.latitud);
    const lonUnchanged = editData?.longitud == null || Number(editData.longitud) === Number(activeClub?.longitud);
    const mergedVenue = {
      ...(activeClub || {}),
      ...(effectiveRecord || {}),
      ...(ubEdited && latUnchanged && lonUnchanged
        ? { latitud: null, longitud: null, latitude: null, longitude: null }
        : {}),
    };
    return resolveCanonicalVenueCoordinates(mergedVenue, activeClub);
  }, [activeClub, effectiveRecord, editData?.ubicacion, editData?.latitud, editData?.longitud]);
  const canonicalDistanciaKm = useMemo(
    () => computeCanonicalVenueDistanceKm(canonicalClubCoords.latitud, canonicalClubCoords.longitud),
    [canonicalClubCoords.latitud, canonicalClubCoords.longitud]
  );
  const venueOpenStatus = useMemo(
    () => evaluateVenueOpenStatusMazatlan(editData?.horarios || activeClub?.horarios, undefined, language),
    [editData?.horarios, activeClub?.horarios, language]
  );

  const { clubFallbackUri, allCarouselImages, logoSquareUri } = useMemo(
    () => resolveClubCarouselMedia(disciplina, nombreSede, activeClub, effectiveRecord),
    [disciplina, nombreSede, activeClub, effectiveRecord]
  );
  const resolvedIgAndSched = useMemo(() => extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule({ ...activeClub, ...effectiveRecord }), [activeClub, effectiveRecord]);

  const handleOpenGoogleMaps = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    openGoogleMapsVenue({ ...activeClub, ...editData, nombre: nombreSede, latitud: canonicalClubCoords.latitud, longitud: canonicalClubCoords.longitud });
  }, [activeClub, editData, nombreSede, canonicalClubCoords.latitud, canonicalClubCoords.longitud]);

  const scrollToB2BInput = useCallback((targetRef: React.RefObject<TextInput | null>, extraY = 80) => {
    const heroH = Math.min(360, Math.round((Math.max(280, winW) * 9) / 16));
    scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: heroH + extraY, animated: true });
    if (focusTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(focusTimerRef.current);
    focusTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { targetRef.current?.focus(); }, 150);
  }, [winW]);

  const handleOpenInstagram = useCallback(() => {
    if (isEditMode) { triggerHaptic('Selection'); scrollToB2BInput(igInputRef, 75); return; }
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    const isOwnerManaged = Boolean(activeClub?.propietario_id || editData?.propietario_id);
    const rawWa = editData?.whatsapp || activeClub?.whatsapp;
    openAffiliateInstagramSafe({
      handle: editData?.instagram || resolvedIgAndSched.instagram || activeClub?.instagram,
      isVerified: Boolean(editData?.instagram_verificado || activeClub?.instagram_verificado || isOwnerManaged || activeClub?.instagram || resolvedIgAndSched.instagram),
      whatsapp: isValidB2BWhatsapp(rawWa) ? rawWa : null,
      clubName: nombreSede,
      city: cleanLocationDisplay(editData?.ubicacion || activeClub?.ubicacion, '').split(',')[0],
      language,
      onMissingHandle: (msg) =>
        showTacticalAlert(tx('INSTAGRAM NO VINCULADO', 'INSTAGRAM NOT LINKED'), msg, 'info'),
    });
  }, [isEditMode, scrollToB2BInput, resolvedIgAndSched.instagram, editData?.instagram, editData?.instagram_verificado, editData?.whatsapp, editData?.ubicacion, editData?.propietario_id, activeClub?.instagram, activeClub?.instagram_verificado, activeClub?.propietario_id, activeClub?.whatsapp, activeClub?.ubicacion, nombreSede, language, showTacticalAlert, tx]);

  const handleWhatsAppPress = useCallback(() => {
    if (isEditMode) { triggerHaptic('Selection'); scrollToB2BInput(waInputRef, 145); return; }
    const rawWa = editData?.whatsapp || activeClub?.whatsapp;
    if (!isValidB2BWhatsapp(rawWa)) {
      if (isAdmin) { setIsEditMode(true); scrollToB2BInput(waInputRef, 145); }
      else showTacticalAlert(tx('WHATSAPP COMERCIAL NO REGISTRADO', 'COMMERCIAL WHATSAPP NOT REGISTERED'), tx('Esta sede aún no publica su número directo de WhatsApp.', 'This venue has not published its direct WhatsApp number yet.'), 'info');
      return;
    }
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    openAffiliateWhatsAppSafe(String(rawWa).replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10), nombreSede, language);
  }, [isEditMode, scrollToB2BInput, editData?.whatsapp, activeClub?.whatsapp, isAdmin, setIsEditMode, showTacticalAlert, tx, nombreSede, language]);

  const handleOpenCreateEventFromClub = useCallback(() => {
    if (onOpenCreateEvent) {
      onOpenCreateEvent({ negocio_nombre: nombreSede, categoria: disciplina, ubicacion: editData?.ubicacion || activeClub?.ubicacion, avatar_club: logoSquareUri });
      return;
    }
    showTacticalAlert(tx('CREAR EVENTO EN PANEL', 'CREATE EVENT IN PANEL'), tx('Publica clases o eventos para esta sede desde la pestaña Eventos.', 'Publish classes or events for this venue from the Events tab.'), 'info');
  }, [onOpenCreateEvent, nombreSede, disciplina, editData?.ubicacion, activeClub?.ubicacion, logoSquareUri, showTacticalAlert, tx]);

  const handleScrollToActiveEvents = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: Math.max(280, eventsSectionYRef.current - 20), animated: true });
  }, []);

  const handleShareAffiliate = useCallback(async () => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    const p = buildClubSharePayload({ nombreSede, disciplina, ubicacion: editData?.ubicacion || activeClub?.ubicacion, rating: currentRatingValue, language, discLoc: tDisc(disciplina) });
    try {
      if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && typeof navigator.share === 'function') { await navigator.share({ title: p.title, text: p.webText, url: p.url }); return; }
      await Share.share({ title: p.title, message: p.message, url: p.url });
    } catch {}
  }, [editData?.ubicacion, activeClub?.ubicacion, nombreSede, disciplina, currentRatingValue, language, tDisc]);

  const activeAmenitiesList = useMemo(() => resolveLocalizedClubAmenities(selectedAmenityIds, language), [selectedAmenityIds, language]);
  const canUploadStory = useMemo(() => Boolean(!isAdmin && isOwnerOfThisClub), [isAdmin, isOwnerOfThisClub]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible || !activeClub) { setHasActiveStory(false); return; }
    let mounted = true;
    const syncActiveStoryFlag = () => {
      hasActiveStoriesForOwner(String(activeClub?.id || ''), nombreSede).then((has) => { if (mounted) setHasActiveStory(Boolean(has)); }).catch(() => { if (mounted) setHasActiveStory(false); });
    };
    syncActiveStoryFlag();
    const unsub = subscribeKineticStoriesUpdated(syncActiveStoryFlag);
    return () => { mounted = false; unsub(); };
  }, [visible, activeClub, nombreSede]);

  const storyOwnerPayload = useMemo<IKineticStoryOwner>(
    () => ({ id: String(activeClub?.id || nombreSede), name: nombreSede, subtitle: `${isSpec ? tx('Especialista', 'Specialist') : tx('Club', 'Club')} • ${tDisc(disciplina)}`, avatarUrl: logoSquareUri, isOfficialKinetic: false, isSpecialist: isSpec, discipline: disciplina, canUpload: canUploadStory, allowUpload: canUploadStory }),
    [activeClub?.id, nombreSede, isSpec, disciplina, logoSquareUri, canUploadStory, tx, tDisc]
  );

  const rawUbicacionClub = editData?.ubicacion || activeClub?.ubicacion;
  const resolvedCleanCity = formatClubZoneDisplay(
    rawUbicacionClub || cleanLocationDisplay(rawUbicacionClub, ''),
    canonicalClubCoords.latitud,
    canonicalClubCoords.longitud,
    language === 'en'
  );
  const handleDismiss = useCallback(() => { if (!isUploadingImage && !isSaving && !isCreating) onClose?.(); }, [isUploadingImage, isSaving, isCreating, onClose]);

  const handleSaveVenueChanges = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isCreateMode) {
      if (createBusyRef.current || isSaving) return;
      if (!editData?.nombre?.trim()) { showTacticalAlert(tx('NOMBRE REQUERIDO', 'NAME REQUIRED'), tx('Escribe el nombre comercial de la sede.', 'Please enter the commercial name.'), 'alert'); return; }
      createBusyRef.current = true;
      setIsCreating(true);
      try {
        const created = await crearNuevoAfiliadoDesdeModal(editData, selectedAmenityIds);
        triggerHaptic('Success'); onCreatedAffiliate?.(created); onClose?.();
      } catch (err: any) {
        triggerHaptic('Error'); showTacticalAlert(tx('ERROR AL CREAR', 'CREATION ERROR'), err?.message || tx('No se pudo registrar la sede.', 'Could not register venue.'), 'error');
      } finally {
        createBusyRef.current = false;
        setIsCreating(false);
      }
      return;
    }
    await guardarCambiosTexto(isAdmin);
  }, [isAdmin, isCreateMode, isSaving, editData, selectedAmenityIds, showTacticalAlert, onCreatedAffiliate, onClose, guardarCambiosTexto, tx]);

  const sheetBody = (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, width: '100%', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }} pointerEvents="box-none">
      <View style={[globalStyles.bottomSheet, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background, flex: 1, paddingHorizontal: 0, paddingTop: 0, paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 12 : 22, overflow: 'hidden' }]}>
        {!lightboxState.visible && (
          <View
            style={{
              backgroundColor: themeColors.background,
              overflow: 'hidden',
              zIndex: 12,
              borderBottomWidth: scrollY > 4 ? 1 : 0,
              borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.16)',
            }}
          >
            <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={handleDismiss} style={{ paddingTop: 8, marginBottom: 8 }} />
          </View>
        )}
        <ScrollView
          ref={scrollViewRef}
          style={{ flex: 1 }}
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={true}
          nestedScrollEnabled={true}
          bounces={true}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          scrollEventThrottle={16}
          onScroll={(e) => setScrollY(e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y)}
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 36 }}
        >
          <ClubDetailsHeroHeader
            allCarouselImages={allCarouselImages} heroCarouselIndex={heroCarouselIndex} setHeroCarouselIndex={setHeroCarouselIndex}
            onOpenLightbox={(idx) => (isEditMode ? actualizarFotoCarruselOSede(idx, allCarouselImages) : setLightboxState({ visible: true, index: idx, customImages: null, isProfile: false }))}
            disciplina={disciplina} fallbackImageUri={clubFallbackUri} onClose={handleDismiss} onShare={handleShareAffiliate}
            isFavorite={isFavorite} onToggleFavorite={onToggleFavorite} isOwnerOfThisClub={isOwnerOfThisClub} isEditMode={isEditMode}
            onToggleEditMode={() => setIsEditMode((p) => !p)} logoSquareUri={logoSquareUri} hasActiveStory={hasActiveStory} canUploadStory={canUploadStory}
            onPressUploadStory={canUploadStory ? () => setStoryState({ open: true, uploadMode: true }) : undefined}
            onPressAvatar={() => {
              if (isEditMode) actualizarFotoCarruselOSede('avatar');
              else if (hasActiveStory) setStoryState({ open: true, uploadMode: false });
              else setLightboxState({ visible: true, index: 0, customImages: [logoSquareUri || clubFallbackUri], isProfile: true });
            }}
          />

          <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 18, paddingTop: 14 }}>
            {effectiveCreateMode ? (
              <View style={cd.statusBarRow}>
                <View style={[cd.statusTagPill, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#7DD3FC', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' }]}>
                  <Text style={[cd.statusTagText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                    {tx('NUEVA SEDE B2B', 'NEW B2B VENUE')}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <View style={cd.statusBarRow}>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                  <View style={[cd.statusTagPill, !esActivo && cd.statusTagProspect, isLight && (esActivo ? { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#7DD3FC', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' } : { backgroundColor: '#FEF3C7', borderColor: '#F59E0B', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#FFAB00' })]}>
                    <Text style={[cd.statusTagText, { color: !esActivo ? (isLight ? '#B45309' : themeColors.amberText) : themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                      {esActivo ? (isSpec ? tx('ESPECIALISTA', 'SPECIALIST') : tx('CLUB', 'CLUB')) : tx('PROSPECTO', 'PROSPECT')}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  {esActivo && venueOpenStatus.isOpen && (
                    <View style={[cd.statusTagPill, { borderColor: '#10B981', backgroundColor: isLight ? '#DCFCE7' : 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.14)', elevation: 0 }]}>
                      <Text style={[cd.statusTagText, { color: isLight ? '#047857' : '#00FF66' }]}>{venueOpenStatus.badgeText}</Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                  <View style={[cd.distanceTagPill, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#7DD3FC', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' }]}>
                    <Feather name="navigation" size={10} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                    <Text style={[cd.distanceTagText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{fmtDist(canonicalDistanciaKm, true)}</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                <TouchableOpacity style={[cd.ratingYellowBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FEF3C7', borderColor: '#F59E0B', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#FFAB00' }]} activeOpacity={0.8} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setIsRatingModalOpen(true); }}>
                  <Feather name="star" size={12} color={themeColors.amberText} />
                  <Text style={[cd.ratingYellowBtnText, { color: themeColors.amberText }]}>{currentRatingValue.toFixed(1)}</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            )}

            <ClubDetailsTitleSocialRow
              nombreSede={nombreSede} hasValidWhatsapp={isValidB2BWhatsapp(editData?.whatsapp || activeClub?.whatsapp)}
              isEditMode={isEditMode} experienciasCount={effectiveCreateMode ? 0 : experiencias.length} onPressInstagram={handleOpenInstagram}
              onPressWhatsapp={handleWhatsAppPress} onPressActiveClasses={handleScrollToActiveEvents}
            />

            <ClubDetailsB2BInfoCard
              isEditMode={isEditMode} disciplina={disciplina} horarios={editData?.horarios || activeClub?.horarios}
              distanciaKm={effectiveCreateMode ? null : canonicalDistanciaKm} resolvedCleanCity={resolvedCleanCity} nombreSede={nombreSede}
              editData={editData} setEditData={setEditData} handleOpenGoogleMaps={handleOpenGoogleMaps} igInputRef={igInputRef} waInputRef={waInputRef}
            />

            <Text style={[cd.sectionBlockTitle, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('SOBRE NOSOTROS', 'ABOUT US')}</Text>
            {isEditMode ? (
              <TextInput
                style={[cd.bioInput, { fontSize: 16 }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', color: '#0F172A', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' }]}
                multiline value={editData?.descripcion} placeholder={tx('Describe la sede e instalaciones...', 'Describe the venue and facilities...')}
                placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted} onChangeText={(t) => setEditData((p: any) => ({ ...p, descripcion: t }))}
              />
            ) : (
              <View style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
                <Text style={[cd.bioReadText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]} numberOfLines={isBioExpanded ? undefined : 4}>
                  {td(effectiveRecord?.descripcion || activeClub?.descripcion || tx(`Ubicado en ${resolvedCleanCity}, ${nombreSede} es un espacio verificado dentro de la red Kinetic dedicado al entrenamiento deportivo y bienestar de alto nivel.`, `Located in ${resolvedCleanCity}, ${nombreSede} is a verified venue in the Kinetic network dedicated to high-performance sports training and wellness.`))}
                </Text>
                <TouchableOpacity style={{ paddingVertical: 4 }} onPress={() => setIsBioExpanded((p) => !p)}>
                  <Text style={[cd.moreInfoLink, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{isBioExpanded ? tx('Menos info ↑', 'Less info ↑') : tx('Más info ↓', 'More info ↓')}</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            )}

            <ClubDetailsEventsAndEditSection
              activeAmenitiesList={activeAmenitiesList} isEditMode={isEditMode} setIsAmenitiesModalOpen={setIsAmenitiesModalOpen}
              experiencias={experiencias} nombreSede={nombreSede} editDataUbicacion={effectiveRecord?.ubicacion} activeClubUbicacion={activeClub?.ubicacion}
              logoSquareUri={logoSquareUri} onSelectClase={onSelectClase} isOwnerOfThisClub={isOwnerOfThisClub}
              canonicalCoords={canonicalClubCoords} canonicalDistanciaKm={canonicalDistanciaKm} isCreateMode={effectiveCreateMode}
              onOpenCreateEvent={handleOpenCreateEventFromClub} onEventsSectionLayout={(y) => { eventsSectionYRef.current = y; }}
            />
          </View>
        </ScrollView>

        <View style={[cd.bottomFixedBar, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }, isLight && { borderTopColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[cd.bottomBackBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }, NO_SEL]} onPress={handleDismiss}>
            <Feather name="arrow-left" size={15} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
            <Text style={[cd.bottomBackText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('ATRÁS', 'BACK')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          {isEditMode ? (
            <TouchableOpacity style={[cd.bottomPrimaryCyanBtn, (isSaving || isCreating) && { opacity: 0.55 }, NO_SEL]} onPress={handleSaveVenueChanges} disabled={isSaving || isCreating}>
              <Feather name="save" size={15} color="#050505" />
              <Text style={cd.bottomPrimaryCyanText}>{tx('GUARDAR CAMBIOS', 'SAVE CHANGES')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ) : experiencias.length > 0 && !isAdmin ? (
            <TouchableOpacity style={[cd.bottomPrimaryCyanBtn, NO_SEL]} onPress={handleScrollToActiveEvents}>
              <Feather name="calendar" size={15} color="#050505" />
              <Text style={cd.bottomPrimaryCyanText}>{tx(`VER CLASES (${experiencias.length})`, `VIEW SESSIONS (${experiencias.length})`)}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ) : isAdmin ? (
            <TouchableOpacity style={[cd.bottomPrimaryCyanBtn, esActivo && { backgroundColor: COLORS.green, borderTopColor: '#B3FFD1', borderBottomColor: '#007A33' }, NO_SEL]} onPress={() => (esActivo ? setIsEditMode(true) : setIsActivateB2BOpen(true))}>
              <Feather name={esActivo ? 'edit-2' : 'power'} size={15} color="#050505" />
              <Text style={cd.bottomPrimaryCyanText}>{esActivo ? tx('EDITAR SEDE B2B', 'EDIT B2B VENUE') : tx('ACTIVAR OPERADOR B2B', 'ACTIVATE B2B OPERATOR')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ) : null}
        </View>
      </View>

      <ClubDetailsOverlayOrchestrator
        isRatingModalOpen={isRatingModalOpen} setIsRatingModalOpen={setIsRatingModalOpen} currentRatingValue={currentRatingValue}
        initialScores={effectiveRecord?.audit_scores || activeClub?.audit_scores || null} initialChecklists={effectiveRecord?.audit_checklists || activeClub?.audit_checklists || null}
        clubId={activeClub?.id} clubName={nombreSede} canEditRating={isEditMode || isAdmin}
        onSaveRating={(newR, newScores, checks) => guardarCalificacionAuditoria(newR, newScores, checks, isAdmin)}
        isAmenitiesModalOpen={isAmenitiesModalOpen} setIsAmenitiesModalOpen={setIsAmenitiesModalOpen} selectedAmenityIds={selectedAmenityIds}
        toggleAmenity={toggleAmenity} isActivateB2BOpen={isActivateB2BOpen} setIsActivateB2BOpen={setIsActivateB2BOpen}
        lightboxState={lightboxState} setLightboxState={setLightboxState} allCarouselImages={allCarouselImages}
        clubFallbackUri={clubFallbackUri} disciplina={disciplina} storyState={storyState} setStoryState={setStoryState}
        storyOwnerPayload={storyOwnerPayload} canUploadStory={canUploadStory} alertConfig={alertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig}
      />
    </View>
  );

  if (inlineOverlay) {
    if (!visible) return null;
    const inlineWrap = (
      <View
        {...(Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ dataSet: { kineticSheetOpen: 'true' } } as any) : {})}
        style={{
          position: (Platform.OS === 'web' && portalOnWeb ? 'fixed' : 'absolute') as any,
          top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0,
          ...(Platform.OS === 'web' && portalOnWeb ? ({ width: '100vw', height: '100dvh', zIndex: 9920 } as any) : { zIndex: 9950 }),
          backgroundColor: isLight ? 'rgba(5, 5, 5, 0.55)' : 'rgba(5, 5, 5, 0.92)',
          justifyContent: 'flex-end',
          elevation: 0,
        }}
      >
        <Pressable style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 }} onPress={handleDismiss} />
        {sheetBody}
      </View>
    );
    return Platform.OS === 'web' && portalOnWeb ? renderInWebPortal(inlineWrap) : inlineWrap;
  }

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(visible)}
      onBackButtonPress={() => {
        if (alertConfig?.visible) setAlertConfig({ ...alertConfig, visible: false });
        else if (lightboxState.visible) setLightboxState({ visible: false, index: 0, customImages: null, isProfile: false });
        else if (storyState.open) setStoryState({ open: false, uploadMode: false });
        else if (isRatingModalOpen) setIsRatingModalOpen(false);
        else if (isAmenitiesModalOpen) setIsAmenitiesModalOpen(false);
        else if (isActivateB2BOpen) setIsActivateB2BOpen(false);
        else handleDismiss();
      }}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }} backdropColor="#050505" backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.55 : 0.92}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={120} animationInTiming={200} animationOutTiming={180}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false} useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'} useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      onModalHide={handleModalHidden}
      onBackdropPress={handleDismiss} avoidKeyboard={true}
    >
      {sheetBody}
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

export default ClubDetailsModal;

    // === src/components/ClubDetailsSubModals.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Alert, Keyboard, KeyboardAvoidingView, PanResponder, Platform, ScrollView,
  Share, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View,
} from 'react-native';
import { KINETIC_AMENITIES } from '../constants/exploreData';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { IRealClubReview, fetchRealClubReviewsFromSupabase, localizeSingleAmenity } from '../services/clubSheetPersistenceHelper';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { generateSecureB2BTempPassword } from '../services/kineticFintechSecurityGuards';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { sub as baseSub } from './ClubDetailsSubModals.styles';
import { ClubTechnicalAudit5StarBody, buildDefaultAuditChecklists } from './explorar/ClubTechnicalAudit5StarBody';
import { ClubGalleryLightboxModal } from './explorar/ClubGalleryLightboxModal';
import { KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';

export { ClubGalleryLightboxModal };

const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

const SUB_MODAL_LOCK_KEYS = ['overlayBackdrop', 'primaryCyanBtn', 'primaryCyanBtnText'];

export const deriveScoresFromRating = (targetRating: number, savedScores?: number[] | null): number[] => {
  if (Array.isArray(savedScores) && savedScores.length === 5) {
    return savedScores.map((n) => Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(Number(n) || 5))));
  }
  if (Array.isArray(savedScores) && savedScores.length === 4) {
    const clean4 = savedScores.map((n) => Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(Number(n) || 5))));
    const avg4 = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(clean4.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / 4)));
    return [...clean4, avg4];
  }
  const safeRating = Math.max(1.0, Math.min(5.0, Number(targetRating) || 5.0));
  const targetSum5 = Math.max(5, Math.min(25, Math.round(safeRating * 5)));
  const baseFloor = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.floor(targetSum5 / 5)));
  const remainder = targetSum5 - baseFloor * 5;
  return [0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map((idx) => (idx < remainder ? Math.min(5, baseFloor + 1) : baseFloor));
};

export const ClubRatingAuditModal = React.memo(
  ({
    visible,
    onClose,
    rating = 5.0,
    initialScores,
    initialChecklists,
    clubId,
    clubName,
    canEdit = false,
    onSaveRating,
  }: {
    visible: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    rating?: number;
    initialScores?: number[] | null;
    initialChecklists?: Record<string, boolean> | null;
    clubId?: string | null;
    clubName?: string | null;
    canEdit?: boolean;
    onSaveRating?: (newRating: number, newScores: number[], checklists?: Record<string, boolean>) => void;
  }) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { tx, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const sub = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSub, resolvedTheme, SUB_MODAL_LOCK_KEYS), [resolvedTheme]);
    const baseScore = Number(rating || 5.0);
    const [scores, setScores] = useState<number[]>(() => deriveScoresFromRating(baseScore, initialScores));
    const [checkedSubpoints, setCheckedSubpoints] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>(() =>
      initialChecklists && typeof initialChecklists === 'object'
        ? initialChecklists
        : buildDefaultAuditChecklists(deriveScoresFromRating(baseScore, initialScores))
    );
    const [realReviews, setRealReviews] = useState<IRealClubReview[]>([]);
    const [loadingReviews, setLoadingReviews] = useState(false);
    const [scrollY, setScrollY] = useState(0);
    const scrollYRef = useRef(0);
    const swipeClosePan = useMemo(
      () =>
        PanResponder.create({
          onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: (_, g) =>
            scrollYRef.current <= 0 && g.dy > 8 && Math.abs(g.dy) > Math.abs(g.dx) * 1.4,
          onPanResponderRelease: (_, g) => {
            if (scrollYRef.current <= 0 && g.dy > 52) onClose();
          },
        }),
      [onClose]
    );

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!visible) return;
      setScrollY(0);
      scrollYRef.current = 0;
      const nextScores = deriveScoresFromRating(baseScore, initialScores);
      setScores(nextScores);
      setCheckedSubpoints(
        initialChecklists && typeof initialChecklists === 'object'
          ? initialChecklists
          : buildDefaultAuditChecklists(nextScores)
      );
      let active = true;
      setLoadingReviews(true);
      fetchRealClubReviewsFromSupabase(clubId, clubName)
        .then((rows) => {
          if (active) setRealReviews(rows);
        })
        .catch(() => {
          if (active) setRealReviews([]);
        })
        .finally(() => {
          if (active) setLoadingReviews(false);
        });
      return () => {
        active = false;
      };
    }, [visible, baseScore, initialScores, initialChecklists, clubId, clubName]);

    const computedAvg = useMemo(() => {
      const sum = scores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
      return Number((sum / Math.max(1, scores.length)).toFixed(1));
    }, [scores]);

    if (!visible) return null;

    return (
      <View style={sub.overlayBackdrop}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} activeOpacity={1} onPress={onClose} />
        <View
          style={[
            globalStyles.bottomSheet,
            sub.sheetContainer,
            { paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 18) + 8, overflow: 'hidden' },
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
          ]}
        >
          <View
            {...swipeClosePan.panHandlers}
            style={{
              backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0A0D14',
              zIndex: 20,
              paddingBottom: 6,
              borderBottomWidth: scrollY > 4 ? 1 : 0,
              borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.16)',
            }}
          >
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', position: 'relative', paddingHorizontal: 44 }}>
              <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} style={{ paddingTop: 4, marginBottom: 0 }} />
              <TouchableOpacity
                hitSlop={{ top: 14, bottom: 14, left: 14, right: 14 }}
                onPress={onClose}
                style={{
                  position: 'absolute', right: 0, width: 32, height: 32, borderRadius: 16,
                  alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', borderWidth: 1,
                  backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F1F5F9' : '#111827',
                  borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)',
                  elevation: 0,
                }}
              >
                <Feather name="x" size={16} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
          <ScrollView
            style={{ flex: 1 }}
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            scrollEventThrottle={16}
            onScroll={(e) => {
              const y = e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y;
              scrollYRef.current = y;
              setScrollY(y);
            }}
            contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 14, paddingTop: 6 }}
          >
            <ClubTechnicalAudit5StarBody
              canEdit={canEdit}
              scores={scores}
              setScores={setScores}
              checkedSubpoints={checkedSubpoints}
              setCheckedSubpoints={setCheckedSubpoints}
              realReviews={realReviews}
              loadingReviews={loadingReviews}
              computedAvg={computedAvg}
              sub={sub}
              themeColors={themeColors}
              isLight={isLight}
              tx={tx}
              triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
            />
          </ScrollView>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[sub.primaryCyanBtn, NO_SEL]}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Success');
              if (canEdit) onSaveRating?.(computedAvg, scores, checkedSubpoints);
              onClose();
            }}
          >
            <Text style={sub.primaryCyanBtnText}>
              {canEdit ? tx('CONFIRMAR AUDITORÍA', 'CONFIRM AUDIT') : tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }
);

export const ClubAmenitiesSelectorModal = React.memo(
  ({
    visible,
    selectedIds = [],
    onToggleAmenity,
    onClose,
  }: {
    visible: boolean;
    selectedIds: string[];
    onToggleAmenity: (id: string) => void;
    onClose: () => void;
  }) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { tx, language, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const sub = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSub, resolvedTheme, SUB_MODAL_LOCK_KEYS), [resolvedTheme]);
    if (!visible) return null;

    return (
      <View style={sub.overlayBackdrop}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} activeOpacity={1} onPress={onClose} />
        <View
          style={[
            globalStyles.bottomSheet,
            sub.sheetContainer,
            { paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 18) + 8 },
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
          ]}
        >
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} style={{ paddingTop: 4, marginBottom: 8 }} />
          <Text style={[sub.modalTitleCenter, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
            {tx('Configurar Amenidades Verificadas', 'Configure Verified Amenities')}
          </Text>
          <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 14 }}>
            {KINETIC_AMENITIES.map((rawAm) => {
              const am = localizeSingleAmenity(rawAm, language);
              const isSelected = selectedIds.includes(am.id);
              const isVip = am.tier === 'vip';
              const accent = isVip ? COLORS.purple : COLORS.cyan;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={am.id}
                  style={[
                    sub.amenityRow,
                    NO_SEL,
                    isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
                    isSelected && {
                      borderColor: accent,
                      borderBottomColor: isVip ? '#6D28D9' : '#0284C7',
                      backgroundColor: isLight
                        ? isVip ? '#F3E8FF' : '#E0F2FE'
                        : isVip ? '#150D24' : '#071921',
                    },
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('Selection');
                    onToggleAmenity(am.id);
                  }}
                >
                  <Feather name={am.icon as any} size={18} color={isVip ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText} />
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingHorizontal: 12 }}>
                    <Text style={[sub.amenityTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{am.title}</Text>
                    <Text style={[sub.amenitySub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{am.desc}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      sub.chkBox,
                      isLight && { borderColor: '#94A3B8' },
                      isSelected && { backgroundColor: accent, borderColor: accent },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {isSelected && <Feather name="check" size={12} color="#050505" />}
                  </View>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </ScrollView>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[sub.primaryCyanBtn, NO_SEL]} onPress={onClose}>
            <Text style={sub.primaryCyanBtnText}>
              {tx(`CONFIRMAR AMENIDADES (${selectedIds.length})`, `CONFIRM AMENITIES (${selectedIds.length})`)}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }
);

export const ActivateB2BOperatorModal = React.memo(
  ({
    visible,
    affiliateId,
    clubName,
    disciplina,
    esEspecialista = false,
    onProvisioned,
    onClose,
  }: {
    visible: boolean;
    affiliateId?: string | null;
    clubName: string;
    disciplina?: string;
    esEspecialista?: boolean;
    onProvisioned?: () => void;
    onClose: () => void;
    isAdmin?: boolean;
  }) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { tx, language, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const sub = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSub, resolvedTheme, SUB_MODAL_LOCK_KEYS), [resolvedTheme]);
    const slug = String(clubName || 'club').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 12) || 'club';
    const [corpEmail, setCorpEmail] = useState(`recepcion@${slug}.com`);
    const [tempPass, setTempPass] = useState('');
    const [isProvisioning, setIsProvisioning] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      if (visible) {
        setIsProvisioning(false);
        setCorpEmail(`recepcion@${slug}.com`);
        try {
          setTempPass(generateSecureB2BTempPassword());
        } catch {
          setTempPass('');
        }
      }
    }, [visible, slug]);

    if (!visible) return null;

    const handleClose = () => { if (isProvisioning) return; Keyboard.dismiss(); onClose(); };

    const handleShareCredentials = async () => {
      if (isProvisioning) return;
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      const cleanEmail = corpEmail.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(cleanEmail)) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        Alert.alert(tx('Correo Inválido', 'Invalid Email'), tx('Ingresa un correo electrónico válido para el operador B2B.', 'Enter a valid email address for the B2B operator.'));
        return;
      }
      let passToShare = tempPass.trim();
      if (!passToShare) {
        try {
          passToShare = generateSecureB2BTempPassword();
        } catch {
          triggerHaptic('Error');
          Alert.alert(tx('Entropía Segura Requerida', 'Secure Entropy Required'), tx('Ingresa manualmente una contraseña segura de al menos 14 caracteres.', 'Enter a strong password (14+ chars) manually.'));
          return;
        }
      }
      if (passToShare.length < 14) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        Alert.alert(tx('Contraseña Demasiado Corta', 'Password Too Short'), tx('La contraseña temporal B2B debe tener al menos 14 caracteres.', 'The temporary B2B password must be at least 14 characters long.'));
        return;
      }

      setIsProvisioning(true);
      try {
        const isSpecRole = Boolean(esEspecialista || String(disciplina || '').toLowerCase().includes('fisio') || String(disciplina || '').toLowerCase().includes('plunge') || String(disciplina || '').toLowerCase().includes('masaje'));
        const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-provision-b2b-operator', {
          body: {
            affiliateId: affiliateId && /^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(affiliateId) ? affiliateId : undefined,
            clubName,
            disciplina,
            email: cleanEmail,
            password: passToShare,
            esEspecialista: isSpecRole,
          },
        });
        const provisionPayload = data && typeof data === 'object' ? (data as { ok?: boolean; error?: string }) : null;
        if (error || !provisionPayload?.ok) {
          throw new Error(provisionPayload?.error || error?.message || 'PROVISION_FAILED');
        }
        onProvisioned?.();
        triggerHaptic('Success');
        const msg =
          language === 'en'
            ? `⚡ Kinetic B2B Front-Desk Credentials — ${clubName}\nEmail: ${cleanEmail}\nTemporary Password: ${passToShare}\n40/60 Protected Deposit Protocol active.\n🌐 Official Portal: https://kineticapp.com.mx`
            : `⚡ Credenciales Operativas Kinetic B2B — ${clubName}\nCorreo: ${cleanEmail}\nContraseña Temporal: ${passToShare}\nProtocolo de Custodia 40/60 activo.\n🌐 Portal Oficial: https://kineticapp.com.mx`;
        Share.share({ message: msg }).catch(() => {});
        Keyboard.dismiss();
        onClose();
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : '';
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        Alert.alert(tx('Error al Provisionar Operador B2B', 'B2B Provisioning Failed'), message || tx('No se pudo activar la cuenta B2B en Supabase Auth.', 'Could not activate B2B account in Supabase Auth.'));
      } finally {
        setIsProvisioning(false);
      }
    };

    const lightInput = isLight
      ? { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', color: '#0F172A', borderColor: '#38BDF8', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4' }
      : undefined;

    return (
      <View style={sub.overlayBackdrop}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} activeOpacity={1} onPress={handleClose} />
        <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}>
          <View
            style={[
              globalStyles.bottomSheet,
              sub.sheetContainer,
              { paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 18) + 8 },
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
            ]}
          >
            <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={handleClose} style={{ paddingTop: 4, marginBottom: 8 }} />
            <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
              <Text style={[sub.modalTitleCenter, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                {tx('Activar Operador B2B', 'Activate B2B Operator')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[sub.b2bDesc, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {tx(`Provisiona la cuenta real en Auth y comparte acceso para ${clubName}.`, `Provision live Auth account and share access for ${clubName}.`)}
              </Text>
              <TextInput
                style={[sub.inputBox, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, elevation: 0 }, lightInput]}
                value={corpEmail}
                onChangeText={setCorpEmail}
                autoCapitalize="none"
                placeholder={tx('Correo corporativo B2B', 'B2B corporate email')}
                placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              />
              <TextInput
                style={[sub.inputBox, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, elevation: 0 }, lightInput]}
                value={tempPass}
                onChangeText={setTempPass}
                autoCapitalize="none"
                placeholder={tx('Contraseña temporal (mín. 14 caracteres)', 'Temporary password (min 14 chars)')}
                placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              />
            </ScrollView>
            <TouchableOpacity style={[sub.primaryCyanBtn, isProvisioning && { opacity: 0.6 }, NO_SEL]} disabled={isProvisioning} onPress={handleShareCredentials}>
              <Text style={sub.primaryCyanBtnText}>{isProvisioning ? tx('ACTIVANDO EN AUTH...', 'PROVISIONING AUTH...') : tx('ACTIVAR Y COMPARTIR ACCESO B2B', 'PROVISION & SHARE B2B ACCESS')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/explorar/ClubDetailsB2BInfoCard.tsx

    // === src/components/explorar/ClubDetailsB2BInfoCard.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { localizeClubSchedule } from '../../services/clubSheetPersistenceHelper';
import { evaluateVenueOpenStatusMazatlan } from '../../services/kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';
import { stripAffiliateIgMeta } from '../../utils/exploreEnrichmentHelpers';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { cd as baseCd } from '../ClubDetailsModal.styles';
import {
  KineticDisciplineSelectorField,
  KineticGoogleMapsLinkField,
  KineticOperatingHoursDualField,
} from '../shared/KineticB2BSelectorsCard';

const NO_SEL: any =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

export interface ClubDetailsB2BInfoCardProps {
  isEditMode: boolean;
  disciplina: string;
  horarios?: string | null;
  distanciaKm?: number | string | null;
  resolvedCleanCity: string;
  nombreSede: string;
  editData: any;
  setEditData: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<any>>;
  handleOpenGoogleMaps: () => void;
  igInputRef?: React.RefObject<TextInput | null>;
  waInputRef?: React.RefObject<TextInput | null>;
}

export const ClubDetailsB2BInfoCard = memo(
  ({
    isEditMode,
    disciplina,
    horarios,
    distanciaKm,
    resolvedCleanCity,
    nombreSede,
    editData,
    setEditData,
    handleOpenGoogleMaps,
    igInputRef,
    waInputRef,
  }: ClubDetailsB2BInfoCardProps) => {
    const { tx, tDisc, fmtDist, language, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const [isNameFocused, setIsNameFocused] = useState(false);
    const cd = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseCd, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);

    const cardLightOverride = isLight
      ? {
          backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderColor: '#38BDF8',
          borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4',
          shadowColor: '#00B8D4',
          shadowOpacity: 0.16,
        }
      : undefined;

    if (isEditMode) {
      const inputBoxStyle: any = {
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: 8,
        borderRadius: 12,
        borderWidth: 1.2,
        paddingHorizontal: 12,
        height: 46,
        marginBottom: 10,
        backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : '#05070B',
        borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.32)',
        elevation: 0,
      };
      return (
        <View style={[cd.infoBoxCard, cardLightOverride]}>
          <Text style={[cd.infoLabelMicro, { color: themeColors.textMuted, marginBottom: 5 }]}>
            {tx('TIPO DE SEDE / MODELO B2B', 'VENUE TYPE / B2B MODEL')}
          </Text>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 }}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              style={{
                flex: 1, paddingVertical: 9, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1.2,
                backgroundColor: !editData?.es_especialista ? (isLight ? '#ECFEFF' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)') : (isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : '#0A0F1A'),
                borderColor: !editData?.es_especialista ? themeColors.cyanText : (isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)'),
                elevation: 0,
              }}
              onPress={() => setEditData((p: any) => ({ ...p, es_especialista: false }))}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: !editData?.es_especialista ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted, letterSpacing: 0.6 }}>
                {tx('CLUB / ESTUDIO', 'CLUB / STUDIO')}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              style={{
                flex: 1, paddingVertical: 9, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1.2,
                backgroundColor: editData?.es_especialista ? (isLight ? '#F5F3FF' : 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.16)') : (isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : '#0A0F1A'),
                borderColor: editData?.es_especialista ? (isLight ? '#7C3AED' : '#C77DFF') : (isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)'),
                elevation: 0,
              }}
              onPress={() => setEditData((p: any) => ({ ...p, es_especialista: true }))}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: editData?.es_especialista ? (isLight ? '#7C3AED' : '#C77DFF') : themeColors.textMuted, letterSpacing: 0.6 }}>
                {tx('ESPECIALISTA', 'SPECIALIST')}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <Text style={[cd.infoLabelMicro, { color: themeColors.textMuted, marginBottom: 5 }]}>
            {tx('NOMBRE COMERCIAL DE LA SEDE', 'COMMERCIAL VENUE NAME')}
          </Text>
          <View style={inputBoxStyle}>
            <Feather name="award" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <TextInput
              style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '700', color: themeColors.textPrimary, textAlign: 'left' }}
              value={String(editData?.nombre ?? '')}
              selection={!isNameFocused ? { start: 0, end: 0 } : undefined}
              onFocus={() => setIsNameFocused(true)}
              onBlur={() => setIsNameFocused(false)}
              placeholder={tx('Nombre de la sede', 'Venue name')}
              placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              onChangeText={(raw) => setEditData((p: any) => ({ ...p, nombre: raw }))}
            />
          </View>

          <Text style={[cd.infoLabelMicro, { color: themeColors.textMuted, marginBottom: 5 }]}>
            {tx('INSTAGRAM OFICIAL', 'OFFICIAL INSTAGRAM')}
          </Text>
          <View style={inputBoxStyle}>
            <Feather name="instagram" size={15} color="#E11D48" />
            <TextInput
              ref={igInputRef}
              style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '700', color: themeColors.textPrimary }}
              value={String(editData?.instagram || '')}
              placeholder={tx('@cluboficial', '@officialclub')}
              placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              autoCapitalize="none"
              autoCorrect={false}
              onChangeText={(raw) => {
                const clean = raw.replace(/\s+/g, '').replace(/^@+/, '');
                setEditData((p: any) => ({ ...p, instagram: clean ? `@${clean}` : '' }));
              }}
            />
          </View>

          <Text style={[cd.infoLabelMicro, { color: themeColors.textMuted, marginBottom: 5 }]}>
            {tx('WHATSAPP COMERCIAL (10 DÍGITOS)', 'COMMERCIAL WHATSAPP (10 DIGITS)')}
          </Text>
          <View style={inputBoxStyle}>
            <Feather name="phone" size={15} color={themeColors.green} />
            <TextInput
              ref={waInputRef}
              style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '700', color: themeColors.textPrimary }}
              value={String(editData?.whatsapp === '0000000000' ? '' : (editData?.whatsapp || ''))}
              placeholder="6241234567"
              placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              keyboardType="phone-pad"
              maxLength={10}
              onChangeText={(raw) => {
                const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 10);
                setEditData((p: any) => ({ ...p, whatsapp: digits }));
              }}
            />
          </View>

          <KineticDisciplineSelectorField
            selectedDiscipline={editData?.disciplina || disciplina}
            onSelectDiscipline={(d) => setEditData((p: any) => ({ ...p, disciplina: d }))}
          />
          <KineticGoogleMapsLinkField
            venueName={`${editData?.nombre || nombreSede}, Los Cabos, B.C.S.`}
            value={editData?.ubicacion || ''}
            onChangeValue={(v) => setEditData((p: any) => ({ ...p, ubicacion: v }))}
          />
          <KineticOperatingHoursDualField
            horariosValue={editData?.horarios}
            onChangeHorarios={(h) => setEditData((p: any) => ({ ...p, horarios: h }))}
          />
        </View>
      );
    }

    const cleanHorarios =
      stripAffiliateIgMeta(horarios) ||
      tx('Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 21:00', 'Monday to Saturday • 06:00 to 21:00');
    const translatedHours = localizeClubSchedule(cleanHorarios, language);
    const openStatus = evaluateVenueOpenStatusMazatlan(horarios, undefined, language);
    const hasDist = typeof distanciaKm === 'number' ? Number.isFinite(distanciaKm) : Boolean(distanciaKm);
    const distFormatted = hasDist ? fmtDist(Number(distanciaKm), true) : null;

    return (
      <View style={[cd.infoBoxCard, cardLightOverride, NO_SEL]}>
        <View style={cd.infoLineRow}>
          <Feather name="tag" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <View style={{ marginLeft: 12, flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={[cd.infoLabelMicro, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('DISCIPLINA', 'DISCIPLINE')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[cd.infoValBold, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tDisc(disciplina)}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={[cd.divider, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />

        <View style={cd.infoLineRow}>
          <Feather name="clock" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <View style={{ marginLeft: 12, flex: 1 }}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
              <Text style={[cd.infoLabelMicro, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {tx('HORARIOS DE ATENCIÓN', 'OPERATING HOURS')}
              </Text>
              {openStatus.isOpen && (
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', color: isLight ? '#047857' : '#00FF66' }}>
                  {openStatus.badgeText}
                </Text>
              )}
            </View>
            <Text style={[cd.infoValBold, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {translatedHours}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={[cd.divider, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[
            cd.maps3DBox,
            isLight && {
              backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF',
              borderColor: '#06B6D4',
              borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4',
              shadowColor: '#00B8D4',
              shadowOpacity: 0.22,
            },
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={handleOpenGoogleMaps}
        >
          <Feather name="map-pin" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <View style={{ marginLeft: 12, flex: 1, flexShrink: 1 }}>
            <Text style={[cd.infoLabelMicro, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {distFormatted
                ? tx(`Ubicación • ${distFormatted} (Abrir en Google Maps ↗)`, `Location • ${distFormatted} (Open in Google Maps ↗)`)
                : tx('Ubicación de la sede (Abrir en Google Maps ↗)', 'Venue location (Open in Google Maps ↗)')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[cd.mapsLinkText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {resolvedCleanCity}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Feather name="external-link" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/explorar/ClubDetailsEventsAndEditSection.tsx

    // === src/components/explorar/ClubDetailsEventsAndEditSection.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { formatFechaCortaEs } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { localizeSingleAmenity } from '../../services/clubSheetPersistenceHelper';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

export const ClubDetailsEventsAndEditSection = memo(
  ({
    activeAmenitiesList,
    isEditMode,
    setIsAmenitiesModalOpen,
    experiencias,
    nombreSede,
    editDataUbicacion,
    activeClubUbicacion,
    logoSquareUri,
    onSelectClase,
    isOwnerOfThisClub,
    setIsCreateExpModalOpen,
    onOpenCreateEvent,
    onEventsSectionLayout,
    canonicalCoords,
    canonicalDistanciaKm,
    isCreateMode = false,
  }: {
    activeAmenitiesList: any[];
    isEditMode: boolean;
    setIsAmenitiesModalOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
    experiencias: any[];
    nombreSede: string;
    editDataUbicacion?: string;
    activeClubUbicacion?: string;
    logoSquareUri?: string | null;
    onSelectClase?: (cl: any) => void;
    isOwnerOfThisClub: boolean;
    setIsCreateExpModalOpen?: (v: boolean) => void;
    onOpenCreateEvent?: () => void;
    onEventsSectionLayout?: (y: number) => void;
    canonicalCoords?: { latitud: number; longitud: number };
    canonicalDistanciaKm?: number;
    isCreateMode?: boolean;
  }) => {
    const { tx, td, fmtDate, language, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);

    return (
      <>
        <View style={styles.sectionHeaderRow}>
          <Text style={[styles.sectionBlockTitle, { color: themeColors.textMuted, flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }]}>
            {tx('AMENIDADES E INSTALACIONES VERIFICADAS', 'VERIFIED AMENITIES & FACILITIES')}
          </Text>
          <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', flexShrink: 0, marginRight: 2 }}>
            {tx('DESLIZA →', 'SWIPE →')}
          </Text>
        </View>

        {isEditMode && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              styles.configAmenitiesBtn,
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
            ]}
            onPress={() => setIsAmenitiesModalOpen(true)}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.configAmenitiesText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {tx(
                `Configurar Amenidades (${activeAmenitiesList.length} activas)`,
                `Configure Amenities (${activeAmenitiesList.length} active)`
              )}
            </Text>
            <Feather name="sliders" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}

        <ScrollView
          horizontal
          removeClippedSubviews={false}
          nestedScrollEnabled={true}
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 10, paddingBottom: 14 }}
        >
          {activeAmenitiesList.map((rawAm) => {
            const am = localizeSingleAmenity(rawAm, language);
            const isVip = am.tier === 'vip';
            const accent = isVip ? COLORS.purple : COLORS.cyan;
            return (
              <View
                key={am.id}
                style={[
                  styles.amenityCardBox,
                  NO_SEL,
                  { borderColor: `${accent}55` },
                  isLight && {
                    backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderColor: isVip ? '#C4B5FD' : '#CBD5E1',
                    borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderBottomColor: isVip ? '#7C3AED' : '#0284C7',
                    shadowOpacity: 0.1,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.amenityIconCircle,
                    { backgroundColor: `${accent}18`, borderColor: accent },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Feather
                    name={am.icon as any}
                    size={15}
                    color={isVip ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText}
                  />
                </View>
                <Text
                  style={[styles.amenityCardTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}
                  numberOfLines={2}
                >
                  {am.title}
                </Text>
                <Text
                  style={[styles.amenityCardSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}
                  numberOfLines={2}
                >
                  {am.desc}
                </Text>
              </View>
            );
          })}
        </ScrollView>

        {!isCreateMode && (
          <View
            onLayout={(e) => {
              onEventsSectionLayout?.(e.nativeEvent.layout.y);
            }}
            style={{ marginTop: 6, marginBottom: 10 }}
          >
            <View style={styles.sectionHeaderRow}>
              <Text style={[styles.sectionBlockTitle, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {tx('EVENTOS ACTIVOS', 'ACTIVE EVENTS')} ({experiencias.length})
              </Text>
              {experiencias.length > 0 && (
                <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                  {tx('TOCA PARA RESERVAR ›', 'TAP TO BOOK ›')}
                </Text>
              )}
            </View>

            {experiencias.length === 0 ? (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.noEventsBox,
                  isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1' },
                ]}
              >
                <Feather name="calendar" size={18} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                <Text style={[styles.noEventsText, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                  {tx(
                    'Esta sede está actualizando sus horarios de esta semana. Explora la pestaña EVENTOS o vuelve pronto.',
                    'This venue is updating its weekly schedule. Explore the EVENTS tab or check back soon.'
                  )}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ) : (
              experiencias.map((evItem, idx: number) => {
                const precioTotal = Number(evItem.precio_kc ?? evItem.precio ?? 180);
                const escrow40 = Math.round(precioTotal * 0.4);
                const cuposDisp = Math.max(
                  0,
                  (Number(evItem.cupo_maximo) || 12) - (Number(evItem.cupo_actual) || 0)
                );
                const horaStr = String(
                  evItem.hora_inicio || evItem.hora || '08:00'
                ).substring(0, 5);
                const fechaCorta =
                  language === 'en' && evItem.fecha
                    ? fmtDate(evItem.fecha, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })
                    : formatFechaCortaEs(evItem.fecha);

                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={String(evItem.id || idx)}
                    style={[
                      styles.activeEventRowCard,
                      NO_SEL,
                      isLight && {
                        backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                        borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
                        borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                        borderBottomColor: '#0284C7',
                        shadowOpacity: 0.1,
                      },
                    ]}
                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                    onPress={() => {
                      triggerHaptic('Light');
                      const isExt = evItem?.es_sede_externa === true;
                      onSelectClase?.({
                        ...evItem,
                        negocio_nombre: nombreSede,
                        ubicacion: evItem.ubicacion || editDataUbicacion || activeClubUbicacion,
                        avatar_club: logoSquareUri,
                        ...(!isExt && canonicalCoords ? { latitud: canonicalCoords.latitud, longitud: canonicalCoords.longitud } : {}),
                        ...(!isExt && Number.isFinite(canonicalDistanciaKm) ? { distanciaKm: canonicalDistanciaKm } : {}),
                      });
                    }}
                  >
                    <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
                      <Text
                        style={[styles.activeEventTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}
                        numberOfLines={1}
                      >
                        {td(evItem.nombre_servicio || evItem.titulo || tx('Evento Kinetic', 'Kinetic Event'))}
                      </Text>
                      <Text
                        style={[styles.activeEventMeta, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}
                        numberOfLines={1}
                      >
                        {fechaCorta} • {horaStr} {tx('hrs', 'hrs')} • {cuposDisp}{' '}
                        {tx('lugares disp.', 'spots open')}
                      </Text>
                    </View>

                    <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                      <View
                        style={[
                          styles.activeEventPricePill,
                          isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Text style={[styles.activeEventPriceTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                          {precioTotal} KC
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                      <Text
                        style={[
                          styles.activeEventEscrowSub,
                          { color: isLight ? '#475569' : COLORS.textLight },
                        ]}
                      >
                        40%: {escrow40} KC
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })
            )}
          </View>
        )}

        {!isCreateMode && isOwnerOfThisClub && (Boolean(onOpenCreateEvent) || Boolean(setIsCreateExpModalOpen)) && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              styles.injectExpDashedBtn,
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
            ]}
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              if (onOpenCreateEvent) onOpenCreateEvent();
              else setIsCreateExpModalOpen?.(true);
            }}
          >
            <Feather name="plus-circle" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[styles.injectExpDashedText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {experiencias.length === 0
                ? tx('INYECTAR PRIMER EVENTO', 'PUBLISH FIRST EVENT')
                : tx('CREAR NUEVO EVENTO', 'CREATE NEW EVENT')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </>
    );
  }
);
