    // === src/components/explorar/ClubDetailsHeroHeader.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, useWindowDimensions } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { cd } from '../ClubDetailsModal.styles';
import { KineticLiquidGlassSurface } from '../shared/KineticLiquidGlassSurface';
import { SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { getAvatarUrl, getCoverUrl } from '../../utils/supabaseImage';

const KINETIC_LOGO = require('../../../assets/logo.png');
const ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET: any = { dataSet: { kineticOnMedia: 'true' } };

interface ClubDetailsHeroHeaderProps {
  allCarouselImages: string[];
  heroCarouselIndex: number;
  setHeroCarouselIndex: (idx: number) => void;
  onOpenLightbox: (idx: number) => void;
  disciplina: string;
  fallbackImageUri?: string;
  onClose?: () => void;
  onShare: () => void;
  isFavorite: boolean;
  onToggleFavorite: () => void;
  isOwnerOfThisClub: boolean;
  isEditMode?: boolean;
  onToggleEditMode: () => void;
  logoSquareUri?: string | null;
  onPressAvatar: () => void;
  hasActiveStory?: boolean;
  canUploadStory?: boolean;
  onPressUploadStory?: () => void;
}

export const ClubDetailsHeroHeader = memo(
  ({
    allCarouselImages,
    heroCarouselIndex,
    setHeroCarouselIndex,
    onOpenLightbox,
    disciplina,
    fallbackImageUri,
    onShare,
    onClose,
    isFavorite,
    onToggleFavorite,
    isOwnerOfThisClub,
    isEditMode = false,
    onToggleEditMode,
    logoSquareUri,
    onPressAvatar,
    hasActiveStory = false,
    canUploadStory = false,
    onPressUploadStory,
  }: ClubDetailsHeroHeaderProps) => {
    const { tx, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const { width: winW } = useWindowDimensions();
    const slideW = Math.max(280, winW);
    const heroH = Math.min(360, Math.round((slideW * 9) / 16));
    const navTop = Math.max(52, Math.min(heroH - 106, Math.round(heroH * 0.43 - 17)));
    const flatListRef = useRef<FlatList>(null);
    const totalPhotos = allCarouselImages.length;

    const goToSlide = useCallback(
      (targetIdx: number) => {
        if (totalPhotos <= 1) return;
        const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(totalPhotos - 1, targetIdx));
        triggerHaptic('Selection');
        setHeroCarouselIndex(clamped);
        flatListRef.current?.scrollToOffset({ offset: clamped * slideW, animated: true });
      },
      [totalPhotos, slideW, setHeroCarouselIndex]
    );

    const handleScrollSync = useCallback(
      (offsetX: number) => {
        if (!slideW || totalPhotos <= 0) return;
        const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(totalPhotos - 1, Math.round(offsetX / slideW)));
        if (idx !== heroCarouselIndex) setHeroCarouselIndex(idx);
      },
      [slideW, totalPhotos, heroCarouselIndex, setHeroCarouselIndex]
    );

    return (
      <View style={[cd.heroBox, { height: heroH, minHeight: heroH }]}>
        <FlatList
          ref={flatListRef}
          data={allCarouselImages}
          horizontal
          pagingEnabled
          removeClippedSubviews={false}
          nestedScrollEnabled={true}
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          scrollEventThrottle={16}
          keyExtractor={(_, idx) => `hero_slot_${idx}`}
          onScroll={(e) => handleScrollSync(e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.x)}
          onMomentumScrollEnd={(e) => handleScrollSync(e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.x)}
          renderItem={({ item, index }) => (
            <TouchableOpacity
              activeOpacity={0.95}
              style={{
                width: slideW,
                height: heroH,
                minHeight: heroH,
                flexShrink: 0,
              }}
              onPress={() => onOpenLightbox(index)}
            >
              <SmartImage
                source={{ uri: item?.startsWith('data:image/') ? item : getCoverUrl(item, 800, 450) }}
                fallbackUri={fallbackImageUri}
                style={{ width: '100%', height: heroH }}
                contentFit="cover"
                fallbackCategory={disciplina}
                transition={0}
              />
              <View style={cd.heroOverlay} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        />

        {isEditMode && (
          <KineticLiquidGlassSurface
            isLight={false}
            borderRadius={999}
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              top: 56,
              left: 12,
              backgroundColor: 'rgba(6, 10, 18, 0.62)',
              borderColor: COLORS.cyan,
              zIndex: 26,
              elevation: 0,
            }}
          >
            <TouchableOpacity
              {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={() => onOpenLightbox(heroCarouselIndex)}
              style={{
                flexDirection: 'row',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: 6,
                paddingHorizontal: 11,
                paddingVertical: 6,
              }}
            >
              <Feather name="camera" size={13} color={COLORS.cyan} />
              <Text {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET} style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                {tx(
                  `FOTO ${heroCarouselIndex + 1}/5 (16:9)`,
                  `PHOTO ${heroCarouselIndex + 1}/5 (16:9)`
                )}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </KineticLiquidGlassSurface>
        )}

        {totalPhotos > 1 && (
          <>
            <KineticLiquidGlassSurface
              isLight={false}
              borderRadius={999}
              style={[
                cd.heroNavCircle3D,
                { left: 10, top: navTop, backgroundColor: 'rgba(6, 10, 18, 0.56)', elevation: 0 },
              ]}
            >
              <TouchableOpacity
                {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
                accessibilityLabel={tx('Foto anterior', 'Previous photo')}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                onPress={() => goToSlide((heroCarouselIndex - 1 + totalPhotos) % totalPhotos)}
                style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
              >
                <Feather name="chevron-left" size={16} color="#FFFFFF" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </KineticLiquidGlassSurface>
            <KineticLiquidGlassSurface
              isLight={false}
              borderRadius={999}
              style={[
                cd.heroNavCircle3D,
                { right: 10, top: navTop, backgroundColor: 'rgba(6, 10, 18, 0.56)', elevation: 0 },
              ]}
            >
              <TouchableOpacity
                {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
                accessibilityLabel={tx('Siguiente foto', 'Next photo')}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                onPress={() => goToSlide((heroCarouselIndex + 1) % totalPhotos)}
                style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
              >
                <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color="#FFFFFF" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </KineticLiquidGlassSurface>
          </>
        )}

        <View style={cd.heroTopBar} pointerEvents="box-none">
          <View style={{ width: 1 }} />

          <View style={cd.topRightActionsRow}>
            <KineticLiquidGlassSurface
              isLight={false}
              borderRadius={999}
              style={[cd.circleTopBtn, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(6, 10, 18, 0.56)', elevation: 0 }]}
            >
              <TouchableOpacity
                {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
                style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                onPress={onShare}
              >
                <Feather name="share-2" size={15} color="#FFFFFF" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </KineticLiquidGlassSurface>

            <KineticLiquidGlassSurface
              isLight={false}
              active={isFavorite}
              borderRadius={999}
              style={[
                cd.circleTopBtn,
                { backgroundColor: 'rgba(6, 10, 18, 0.56)', elevation: 0 },
                isFavorite && cd.circleTopBtnFavBlackCyan,
              ]}
            >
              <TouchableOpacity
                {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
                style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                onPress={onToggleFavorite}
              >
                <Ionicons
                  name={isFavorite ? 'flash' : 'flash-outline'}
                  size={17}
                  color={isFavorite ? COLORS.cyan : '#FFFFFF'}
                />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </KineticLiquidGlassSurface>

            {isOwnerOfThisClub && (
              <KineticLiquidGlassSurface
                isLight={false}
                active={isEditMode}
                borderRadius={999}
                style={[cd.circleTopBtn, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(6, 10, 18, 0.56)', borderColor: COLORS.cyan, elevation: 0 }]}
              >
                <TouchableOpacity
                  {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
                  style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('Medium');
                    onToggleEditMode();
                  }}
                >
                  <Feather name="edit-2" size={14} color={COLORS.cyan} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </KineticLiquidGlassSurface>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={cd.heroBottomRow} pointerEvents="box-none">
          <View style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                cd.logoAvatarBox,
                hasActiveStory && !isEditMode && {
                  borderWidth: 2.4,
                  borderColor: '#00FF66',
                  borderTopColor: '#B3FFD1',
                  borderBottomColor: '#00A844',
                },
              ]}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={onPressAvatar}
            >
              {logoSquareUri ? (
                <SmartImage
                  source={{ uri: logoSquareUri.startsWith('data:image/') ? logoSquareUri : getAvatarUrl(logoSquareUri, 160) }}
                  fallbackUri={fallbackImageUri}
                  style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
                  contentFit="cover"
                  fallbackCategory={disciplina}
                  transition={0}
                />
              ) : (
                <Image
                  source={KINETIC_LOGO}
                  style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
                  contentFit="cover"
                />
              )}
              {isEditMode && (
                <View
                  style={{
                    position: 'absolute',
                    left: 0,
                    right: 0,
                    bottom: 0,
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(5, 8, 15, 0.82)',
                    paddingVertical: 2,
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    flexDirection: 'row',
                    gap: 3,
                    elevation: 0,
                  }}
                >
                  <Feather name="camera" size={10} color={COLORS.cyan} />
                  <Text {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET} style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                    1:1
                  </Text>
                </View>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {canUploadStory && !isEditMode && onPressUploadStory && (
              <TouchableOpacity
                {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                hitSlop={{ top: 6, bottom: 6, left: 6, right: 6 }}
                onPress={onPressUploadStory}
                style={{
                  position: 'absolute',
                  right: -4,
                  bottom: -4,
                  width: 22,
                  height: 22,
                  borderRadius: 11,
                  backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan,
                  borderWidth: 1.5,
                  borderColor: '#05070C',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  zIndex: 10,
                }}
              >
                <Feather name="plus" size={12} color="#050505" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>

          <KineticLiquidGlassSurface
            isLight={false}
            borderRadius={999}
            style={[cd.photoCountBadge, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(6, 10, 18, 0.58)', elevation: 0 }]}
          >
            <TouchableOpacity
              {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET}
              style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}
              onPress={() => onOpenLightbox(heroCarouselIndex)}
            >
              <Feather name="image" size={11} color="#FFFFFF" />
              <Text {...ON_MEDIA_DATA_SET} style={cd.photoCountText}>
                {heroCarouselIndex + 1}/{allCarouselImages.length}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </KineticLiquidGlassSurface>
        </View>

        <View style={cd.dotsIndicatorRow} pointerEvents="box-none">
          {allCarouselImages.slice(0, 5).map((_, idx) => (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={idx}
              hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 4, right: 4 }}
              onPress={() => goToSlide(idx)}
              style={[
                cd.dotItem,
                idx === heroCarouselIndex && cd.dotItemActive,
              ]}
            />
          ))}
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/explorar/ClubDetailsOverlayOrchestrator.tsx

    // === src/components/explorar/ClubDetailsOverlayOrchestrator.tsx === //
import React, { memo } from 'react';
import { TacticalAlertModal } from '../BovedaModals';
import {
  ActivateB2BOperatorModal,
  ClubAmenitiesSelectorModal,
  ClubGalleryLightboxModal,
  ClubRatingAuditModal,
} from '../ClubDetailsSubModals';
import { IKineticStoryOwner, KineticStoriesModal, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache } from '../../services/adminDashboardFetcher';
import { exploreService } from '../../services/exploreService';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';

interface ClubDetailsOverlayOrchestratorProps {
  isRatingModalOpen: boolean;
  setIsRatingModalOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  currentRatingValue: number;
  initialScores: number[] | null;
  initialChecklists: any;
  clubId?: string | number | null;
  clubName: string;
  canEditRating: boolean;
  onSaveRating: (newRating: number, newScores: number[], checklists?: any) => Promise<void> | void;
  isAmenitiesModalOpen: boolean;
  setIsAmenitiesModalOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  selectedAmenityIds: string[];
  toggleAmenity: (id: string) => void;
  isActivateB2BOpen: boolean;
  setIsActivateB2BOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  lightboxState: { visible: boolean; index: number; customImages?: string[] | null; isProfile?: boolean };
  setLightboxState: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<{ visible: boolean; index: number; customImages?: string[] | null; isProfile?: boolean }>>;
  allCarouselImages: string[];
  clubFallbackUri: string;
  disciplina: string;
  storyState: { open: boolean; uploadMode: boolean };
  setStoryState: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<{ open: boolean; uploadMode: boolean }>>;
  storyOwnerPayload: IKineticStoryOwner;
  canUploadStory: boolean;
  alertConfig: any;
  setAlertConfig: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<any>>;
}

export const ClubDetailsOverlayOrchestrator = memo(
  ({
    isRatingModalOpen,
    setIsRatingModalOpen,
    currentRatingValue,
    initialScores,
    initialChecklists,
    clubId,
    clubName,
    canEditRating,
    onSaveRating,
    isAmenitiesModalOpen,
    setIsAmenitiesModalOpen,
    selectedAmenityIds,
    toggleAmenity,
    isActivateB2BOpen,
    setIsActivateB2BOpen,
    lightboxState,
    setLightboxState,
    allCarouselImages,
    clubFallbackUri,
    disciplina,
    storyState,
    setStoryState,
    storyOwnerPayload,
    canUploadStory,
    alertConfig,
    setAlertConfig,
  }: ClubDetailsOverlayOrchestratorProps) => (
    <>
      <ClubRatingAuditModal
        visible={isRatingModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsRatingModalOpen(false)}
        rating={currentRatingValue}
        initialScores={initialScores}
        initialChecklists={initialChecklists}
        clubId={clubId != null ? String(clubId) : undefined}
        clubName={clubName}
        canEdit={canEditRating}
        onSaveRating={onSaveRating}
      />
      <ClubAmenitiesSelectorModal
        visible={isAmenitiesModalOpen}
        selectedIds={selectedAmenityIds}
        onToggleAmenity={toggleAmenity}
        onClose={() => setIsAmenitiesModalOpen(false)}
      />
      <ActivateB2BOperatorModal
        visible={isActivateB2BOpen}
        affiliateId={clubId != null ? String(clubId) : undefined}
        clubName={clubName}
        disciplina={disciplina}
        onProvisioned={() => {
          exploreService.clearRadarCache();
          invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
          emitKineticRadarUpdated();
        }}
        onClose={() => setIsActivateB2BOpen(false)}
      />
      <ClubGalleryLightboxModal
        inlineOverlay={true}
        visible={lightboxState.visible}
        images={lightboxState.customImages || allCarouselImages}
        initialIndex={lightboxState.index}
        fallbackUri={clubFallbackUri}
        fallbackCategory={disciplina}
        isProfilePhoto={Boolean(lightboxState.isProfile)}
        onClose={() => setLightboxState({ visible: false, index: 0, customImages: null, isProfile: false })}
      />
      <KineticStoriesModal
        inlineOverlay={true}
        visible={storyState.open}
        owner={storyOwnerPayload}
        canUpload={canUploadStory}
        startInUploadMode={storyState.uploadMode && canUploadStory}
        onClose={() => setStoryState({ open: false, uploadMode: false })}
      />
      <TacticalAlertModal inlineOverlay={true} alertConfig={alertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />
    </>
  )
);

    // === src/components/explorar/ClubDetailsTitleSocialRow.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo } from 'react';
import { Platform, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Circle, Defs, Path, RadialGradient, Rect, Stop } from 'react-native-svg';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { cd as baseCd } from '../ClubDetailsModal.styles';

const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

const OfficialInstagramSvg = memo(() => (
  <Svg width={22} height={22} viewBox="0 0 24 24">
    <Defs>
      <RadialGradient id="igGrad" cx="30%" cy="107%" r="125%">
        <Stop offset="0%" stopColor="#FEDA75" />
        <Stop offset="25%" stopColor="#FA7E1E" />
        <Stop offset="50%" stopColor="#D62976" />
        <Stop offset="78%" stopColor="#962FBF" />
        <Stop offset="100%" stopColor="#4F5BD5" />
      </RadialGradient>
    </Defs>
    <Rect x={1.5} y={1.5} width={21} height={21} rx={6} fill="url(#igGrad)" />
    <Rect x={5.5} y={5.5} width={13} height={13} rx={3.6} stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth={1.7} fill="none" />
    <Circle cx={12} cy={12} r={3.1} stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth={1.7} fill="none" />
    <Circle cx={16.1} cy={7.9} r={0.95} fill="#FFFFFF" />
  </Svg>
));

const OfficialWhatsAppSvg = memo(({ active }: { active: boolean }) => (

    // === src/components/explorar/ClubGalleryLightboxModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Dimensions, FlatList, Modal, Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, useWindowDimensions,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { renderInWebPortal } from '../../utils/kineticWebPortal';
import { sub as baseSub } from '../ClubDetailsSubModals.styles';
import { KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;
const LIGHTBOX_LOCK_KEYS = [
  'overlayBackdrop', 'lightboxContainer', 'lightboxTopHeader', 'lightboxNavCircle3D',
  'lightboxFooter', 'lightboxCounterText', 'lightboxCloseBtn', 'lightboxCloseText',
];
const { width: SCREEN_W, height: SCREEN_H } = Dimensions.get('window');

export const ClubGalleryLightboxModal = React.memo(
  ({
    visible,
    images = [],
    initialIndex = 0,
    fallbackUri,
    fallbackCategory = 'Fuerza',
    isProfilePhoto = false,
    onClose,
    inlineOverlay = false,
  }: {
    visible: boolean;
    images: string[];
    initialIndex: number;
    fallbackUri?: string;
    fallbackCategory?: string;
    isProfilePhoto?: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    inlineOverlay?: boolean;
  }) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { width: winW, height: winH } = useWindowDimensions();
    const slideW = Math.max(280, winW || SCREEN_W);
    const slideH = Math.max(400, winH || SCREEN_H);
    const { tx, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const sub = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSub, resolvedTheme, LIGHTBOX_LOCK_KEYS), [resolvedTheme]);
    const flatListRef = useRef<FlatList>(null);
    const scrollTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    const safeImages = useMemo(
      () => (images && images.length > 0 ? images : fallbackUri ? [fallbackUri] : ['']),
      [images, fallbackUri]
    );
    const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(initialIndex);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (visible) {
        const startIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(initialIndex, Math.max(0, safeImages.length - 1)));
        setCurrentIndex(startIdx);
        if (scrollTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(scrollTimerRef.current);
        scrollTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          flatListRef.current?.scrollToOffset({ offset: startIdx * slideW, animated: false });
        }, 40);
      }
      return () => {
        if (scrollTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(scrollTimerRef.current);
      };
    }, [visible, initialIndex, safeImages.length, slideW]);

    if (!visible) return null;

    const stepLightbox = (targetIdx: number) => {
      if (safeImages.length <= 1) return;
      const next = (targetIdx + safeImages.length) % safeImages.length;
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      setCurrentIndex(next);
      flatListRef.current?.scrollToOffset({ offset: next * slideW, animated: true });
    };

    const syncIdx = (offsetX: number) => {
      if (!slideW || safeImages.length <= 0) return;
      const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(safeImages.length - 1, Math.round(offsetX / slideW)));
      if (idx !== currentIndex) setCurrentIndex(idx);
    };

    const lightboxBody = (
      <View
        style={[
          sub.lightboxContainer,
          Platform.OS === 'web'
            ? ({ position: 'fixed', top: 0, left: 0, width: '100vw', height: '100dvh', zIndex: 99999 } as any)
            : null,
        ]}
        {...({ dataSet: { kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } as any)}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            sub.lightboxTopHeader,
            { top: Math.max(insets.top, 14) + 6, flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={{ width: 38 }} />
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} />
          <TouchableOpacity
            accessibilityLabel={tx('Cerrar', 'Close')}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={onClose}
            style={{
              width: 38, height: 38, borderRadius: 19, backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.88)',
              borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: COLORS.cyan, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', elevation: 0,
            }}
          >
            <Feather name="x" size={18} color="#FFFFFF" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
        <FlatList
          ref={flatListRef}
          data={safeImages}
          horizontal
          pagingEnabled
          removeClippedSubviews={false}
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          scrollEventThrottle={16}
          keyExtractor={(_, i) => `lightbox_slot_${i}`}
          onScroll={(e) => syncIdx(e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.x)}
          onMomentumScrollEnd={(e) => syncIdx(e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.x)}
          renderItem={({ item }) => (
            <View style={{ width: slideW, height: slideH, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <SmartImage
                source={{ uri: item || fallbackUri || '' }}
                fallbackUri={fallbackUri}
                fallbackCategory={fallbackCategory}
                style={{ width: slideW, height: slideH * 0.72 }}
                contentFit="contain"
                transition={0}
              />
            </View>
          )}
        />
        {safeImages.length > 1 && (
          <>
            <TouchableOpacity
              accessibilityLabel={tx('Fotografía anterior', 'Previous photo')}
              activeOpacity={0.82}
              onPress={() => stepLightbox(currentIndex - 1)}
              style={[sub.lightboxNavCircle3D, NO_SEL, { left: 14, top: slideH * 0.46 }]}
            >
              <Feather name="chevron-left" size={20} color="#FFFFFF" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity
              accessibilityLabel={tx('Siguiente fotografía', 'Next photo')}
              activeOpacity={0.82}
              onPress={() => stepLightbox(currentIndex + 1)}
              style={[sub.lightboxNavCircle3D, NO_SEL, { right: 14, top: slideH * 0.46 }]}
            >
              <Feather name="chevron-right" size={20} color="#FFFFFF" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </>
        )}
        <View style={[sub.lightboxFooter, { bottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 20) + 12 }]}>
          <Text style={sub.lightboxCounterText}>
            {isProfilePhoto
              ? tx('FOTO DE PERFIL OFICIAL', 'OFFICIAL PROFILE PHOTO')
              : tx(
                  `FOTOGRAFÍA ${currentIndex + 1} DE ${Math.max(1, safeImages.length)}`,
                  `PHOTO ${currentIndex + 1} OF ${Math.max(1, safeImages.length)}`
                )}
          </Text>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[sub.lightboxCloseBtn, NO_SEL]} onPress={onClose}>
            <Feather name="x" size={15} color="#FFFFFF" />
            <Text style={sub.lightboxCloseText}>
              {isProfilePhoto ? tx('CERRAR FOTO', 'CLOSE PHOTO') : tx('CERRAR GALERÍA', 'CLOSE GALLERY')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    );

    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      return renderInWebPortal(lightboxBody);
    }

    if (inlineOverlay) {
      return (
        <View style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0, zIndex: 9999, elevation: 0 }}>
          {lightboxBody}
        </View>
      );
    }

    return (
      <Modal visible={visible} transparent={true} animationType="fade" statusBarTranslucent onRequestClose={onClose}>
        {lightboxBody}
      </Modal>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/explorar/ClubTechnicalAudit5StarBody.tsx

import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import type { IRealClubReview } from '../../services/clubSheetPersistenceHelper';

export interface IAuditRubro {

  label: string;

  icon: any;

}

export const buildDefaultAuditChecklists = (scores: number[]): Record<string, boolean> => {
  const map: Record<string, boolean> = {};
  for (let r = 0; r < 5; r++) {
    const sc = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(scores[r] ?? 5)));
    const checkedCount = Math.max(0, sc - 1);
    for (let s = 0; s < 4; s++) {
      map[`${r}-${s}`] = s < checkedCount;
    }
  }
  return map;
};

export interface ClubTechnicalAudit5StarBodyProps {
  canEdit: boolean;
  scores: number[];
  setScores: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<number[]>>;
  checkedSubpoints?: Record<string, boolean>;
  setCheckedSubpoints?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<string, boolean>>>;
  realReviews?: IRealClubReview[];
  loadingReviews?: boolean;
  computedAvg: number;
  sub: any;
  themeColors: any;
  isLight: boolean;
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
  triggerHaptic: (type?: any) => void;
}

export const ClubTechnicalAudit5StarBody = memo((props: ClubTechnicalAudit5StarBodyProps) => {
  const {
    canEdit,
    scores,
    setScores,
    checkedSubpoints: externalChecks,
    setCheckedSubpoints: setExternalChecks,
    realReviews = [],
    loadingReviews = false,
    computedAvg,
    sub,
    themeColors,
    isLight,
    tx,
    triggerHaptic,
  } = props;
  const [expandedRubro, setExpandedRubro] = useState<number | null>(0);
  const [localChecks, setLocalChecks] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>(() =>
    buildDefaultAuditChecklists(scores)
  );
  const checkedSubpoints = externalChecks || localChecks;
  const updateChecks = setExternalChecks || setLocalChecks;

  const toggleSubpoint = (rIdx: number, sIdx: number) => {
    if (!canEdit) return;
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    const key = `${rIdx}-${sIdx}`;
    const nextMap = { ...checkedSubpoints, [key]: !checkedSubpoints[key] };
    updateChecks(nextMap);
    const countChecked = [0, 1, 2, 3].reduce((acc, i) => acc + (nextMap[`${rIdx}-${i}`] ? 1 : 0), 0);
    const derivedScore = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, 1 + countChecked));
    setScores((prev) => prev.map((v, i) => (i === rIdx ? derivedScore : v)));
  };

  const setRubroStarsDirect = (rIdx: number, star: number) => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setScores((prev) => prev.map((v, i) => (i === rIdx ? star : v)));
    const targetChecks = Math.max(0, star - 1);
    const nextMap = { ...checkedSubpoints };
    for (let s = 0; s < 4; s++) nextMap[`${rIdx}-${s}`] = s < targetChecks;
    updateChecks(nextMap);
  };

  const realAvg =
    realReviews.length > 0
      ? realReviews.reduce((acc, r) => acc + Number(r.stars || 0), 0) / realReviews.length
      : null;

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
      <Text style={[sub.modalTitleCenter, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
        {canEdit ? tx('Auditoría Técnica 5★ Kinetic', '5★ Kinetic Technical Audit') : tx('Auditoría Técnica Oficial 5★', 'Official 5★ Technical Audit')}
      </Text>
      <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginVertical: 8 }}>
        <Feather name={canEdit ? 'shield' : 'star'} size={24} color={canEdit ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.amberText} />
        <Text style={[sub.ratingBigNumber, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{computedAvg.toFixed(1)}</Text>
        <Text style={[sub.ratingSubLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('CALIFICACIÓN PONDERADA OFICIAL 5★', 'OFFICIAL 5★ WEIGHTED RATING')}</Text>
      </View>

      <View style={[sub.criteriaBox, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' }]}>
        {AUDIT_RUBROS_5STAR.map((rubro, rIdx) => {
          const isExp = expandedRubro === rIdx;
          const currentScore = scores[rIdx] || 5;
          return (
            <View key={rubro.label} style={{ borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#E2E8F0' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)' }}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                style={[sub.criteriaRow, { borderBottomWidth: 0 }]}
                onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); setExpandedRubro(isExp ? null : rIdx); }}
              >
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, flex: 1 }}>
                  <Feather name={rubro.icon} size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  <Text style={[sub.criteriaLabel, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx(rubro.label, rubro.labelEn)}</Text>
                </View>
                {canEdit ? (
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 5, alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((star) => (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={star}
                        hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 3, right: 3 }}
                        onPress={() => setRubroStarsDirect(rIdx, star)}
                      >
                        <Feather name="star" size={15} color={star <= currentScore ? themeColors.cyanText : isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)'} />
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    ))}
                    <Feather name={isExp ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'} size={14} color={themeColors.textMuted} style={{ marginLeft: 4 }} />
                  </View>
                ) : (
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                    <Text style={[sub.criteriaScoreText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{currentScore.toFixed(1)} ★</Text>
                    <Feather name={isExp ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'} size={14} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
                  </View>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>

              {isExp && (
                <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingBottom: 10, gap: 6, backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F1F5F9' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.04)', borderRadius: 10, marginBottom: 8, elevation: 0 }}>
                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.cyanText, letterSpacing: 0.5 }}>
                    {tx('CHECKLIST DE 4 PUNTOS TÉCNICOS:', '4-POINT TECHNICAL CHECKLIST:')}
                  </Text>
                  {rubro.subpoints.map((subp, sIdx) => {
                    const isChecked = Boolean(checkedSubpoints[`${rIdx}-${sIdx}`]);
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={subp.es}
                        activeOpacity={0.8}
                        disabled={!canEdit}
                        style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, paddingVertical: 3 }}
                        onPress={() => toggleSubpoint(rIdx, sIdx)}
                      >
                        <Feather name={isChecked ? 'check-square' : 'square'} size={14} color={isChecked ? (isLight ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan) : themeColors.textMuted} />
                        <Text style={{ fontSize: 11.5, color: isChecked ? themeColors.textPrimary : themeColors.textMuted, fontWeight: isChecked ? '700' : '500', flex: 1 }}>
                          {tx(subp.es, subp.en)}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>
              )}
            </View>
          );
        })}

        <View style={[sub.criteriaRow, { borderBottomWidth: 0, paddingTop: 10 }]}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, flex: 1 }}>
            <Feather name="users" size={15} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
            <Text style={[sub.criteriaLabel, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tx('Reseñas Verificadas (Supabase)', 'Verified Reviews (Supabase)')}
            </Text>
          </View>
          {loadingReviews ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={themeColors.purpleText} />
          ) : realAvg !== null ? (
            <Text style={[sub.criteriaScoreText, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
              {realAvg.toFixed(1)} ★ ({realReviews.length})
            </Text>
          ) : (
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.textMuted }}>
              {tx('Sin reseñas verificadas aún', 'No verified reviews yet')}
            </Text>
          )}
        </View>
      </View>

      {realReviews.length > 0 ? (
        <View
          style={{
            marginBottom: 12,
            padding: 12,
            borderRadius: 12,
            borderWidth: 1,
            borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.35)',
            backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : '#090C14',
            gap: 8,
            elevation: 0,
          }}
        >
          <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.purpleText, letterSpacing: 0.6 }}>
            {tx(
              `OPINIONES DE ATLETAS VERIFICADOS (${realReviews.length})`,
              `VERIFIED ATHLETE REVIEWS (${realReviews.length})`
            )}
          </Text>
          {realReviews.slice(0, 8).map((rev) => (
            <View
              key={rev.id}
              style={{
                paddingVertical: 6,
                borderTopWidth: 1,
                borderTopColor: isLight ? '#E2E8F0' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)',
              }}
            >
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.textPrimary }}>
                  {rev.author}
                </Text>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.amberText }}>
                  {'★'.repeat(Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(rev.stars))))}
                </Text>
              </View>
              {Boolean(rev.serviceName) && (
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '700', color: themeColors.cyanText, marginTop: 1 }}>
                  {rev.serviceName}
                </Text>
              )}
              {Boolean(rev.comment) && (
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 11.5, color: themeColors.textSecondary, marginTop: 3, lineHeight: 16 }}>
                  {rev.comment}
                </Text>
              )}
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
      ) : (
        <View
          style={{
            marginBottom: 12,
            padding: 12,
            borderRadius: 12,
            borderWidth: 1,
            borderColor: isLight ? '#E2E8F0' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)',
            backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : '#080B12',
            flexDirection: 'row',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: 10,
            elevation: 0,
          }}
        >
          <Feather name="check-circle" size={16} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.textPrimary }}>
              {tx('Sin reseñas de atletas aún', 'No athlete reviews yet')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 10.5, color: themeColors.textMuted, marginTop: 2, lineHeight: 14 }}>
              {tx(
                'Sin reseñas de atletas aún — Las calificaciones se habilitan únicamente tras validar Pase QR en recepción.',
                'No athlete reviews yet — Ratings unlock exclusively after front-desk QR check-in.'
              )}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      )}

      <View
        style={{
          marginTop: 2,
          padding: 12,
          borderRadius: 12,
          borderWidth: 1,
          borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.22)',
          backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#080C14',
          gap: 5,
          elevation: 0,
        }}
      >
        <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.cyanText, letterSpacing: 0.6, marginBottom: 2 }}>
          {tx(
            'GUÍA DEL DIRECTOR GENERAL (CRITERIOS OFICIALES 1★ A 5★)',
            'CEO CLASSIFICATION GUIDE (OFFICIAL 1★ TO 5★ CRITERIA)'
          )}
        </Text>
        {CEO_STAR_GUIDE.map((row) => (
          <View key={row.stars} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: 6 }}>
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.amberText, minWidth: 22 }}>
              {row.stars}
            </Text>
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 10.5, color: themeColors.textSecondary, lineHeight: 14.5, flex: 1 }}>
              {tx(row.es, row.en)}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ))}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
});

export default ClubTechnicalAudit5StarBody;

    FILE: src/components/explorar/ExploreFilterModals.tsx

    // === src/components/explorar/ExploreFilterModals.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Platform, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { SORTED_CATEGORIES, isWellnessCategory } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { ExploreSortOption, QuickExploreChip } from '../../hooks/useExplorar';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { ExploreZoneAndSortModals } from './ExploreZoneAndSortModals';

const resolveMedallionIcon = (cat: string, isWell: boolean): any => {
  const s = String(cat || '').toLowerCase();
  if (isWell) {
    return s.includes('fisio') || s.includes('masaje') || s.includes('rehab') || s.includes('quiro')
      ? 'heart'
      : 'sun';
  }
  return s.includes('cross') || s.includes('hiit') || s.includes('funcional') || s.includes('box')
    ? 'zap'
    : 'activity';
};

interface ExploreFilterModalsProps {
  insetsBottom: number;
  catPickerOpen: boolean;
  setCatPickerOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  sortPickerOpen: boolean;
  setSortPickerOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  zonePickerOpen: boolean;
  setZonePickerOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  selectedCategories: string[];
  setSelectedCategories: (v: string[]) => void;
  quickChip: QuickExploreChip;
  setQuickChip: (v: QuickExploreChip) => void;
  favoriteCount: number;
  sortBy: ExploreSortOption;
  setSortBy: (v: ExploreSortOption) => void;
  selectedZone: string;
  setSelectedZone: (v: string) => void;
  categoryCountsMap: Record<string, number>;
  viewMode: 'lista' | 'mapa';
  setMapPinPreview: (v: any) => void;
  setWebMapFocus: (v: any) => void;
  mapRef: any;
  centerOnUserGps: () => void;
}

export const ExploreFilterModals = memo((props: ExploreFilterModalsProps) => {
  const {
    insetsBottom,
    catPickerOpen,
    setCatPickerOpen,
    sortPickerOpen,
    setSortPickerOpen,
    zonePickerOpen,
    setZonePickerOpen,
    selectedCategories,
    setSelectedCategories,
    quickChip,
    setQuickChip,
    favoriteCount,
    sortBy,
    setSortBy,
    selectedZone,
    setSelectedZone,
    categoryCountsMap,
    viewMode,
    setMapPinPreview,
    setWebMapFocus,
    mapRef,
    centerOnUserGps,
  } = props;
  const { tx, tDisc, language, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
  const styles = useMemo(
    () =>
      adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, [
        'medallionCyanDark',
        'medallionCyanLight',
        'medallionPurpleDark',
        'medallionPurpleLight',
        'quickRow3DDark',
        'quickRow3DLight',
        'quickRowCyanActiveDark',
        'quickRowCyanActiveLight',
        'quickRowGreenActiveDark',
        'quickRowGreenActiveLight',
        'quickRowPurpleActiveDark',
        'quickRowPurpleActiveLight',
      ]),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );

  const quickFilterRows = useMemo(
    () => [
      { key: 'todas' as QuickExploreChip, label: selectedCategories.length > 0 ? tx('Ninguno • Ver Todas', 'None • View All') : tx('Todas', 'All'), tone: 'cyan' as const },
      { key: 'favoritos' as QuickExploreChip, label: `⭐ ${tx('Favoritos', 'Favorites')} (${favoriteCount})`, tone: 'cyan' as const },
      { key: 'fitness' as QuickExploreChip, label: `⚡ ${tx('Fitness & Deportes', 'Fitness & Sports')}`, tone: 'cyan' as const },
      { key: 'wellness' as QuickExploreChip, label: `🟣 ${tx('Wellness & Spa', 'Wellness & Spa')}`, tone: 'purple' as const },
      { key: 'mas_buscados' as QuickExploreChip, label: `🔥 ${tx('Tendencia', 'Trending')}`, tone: 'green' as const },
    ],
    [tx, favoriteCount, language, selectedCategories.length]
  );
  const [divTab, setDivTab] = useState<'all' | 'fitness' | 'wellness'>('all');
  const [catSort, setCatSort] = useState<'active_first' | 'az' | 'za'>('active_first');
  const [catSearch, setCatSearch] = useState('');

  const catFilterChips = useMemo(
    () => [
      { key: 'all', label: tx('Todas', 'All'), count: SORTED_CATEGORIES.length },
      { key: 'fitness', label: tx('⚡ Fitness & Deporte', '⚡ Fitness & Sports') },
      { key: 'wellness', label: tx('🟣 Wellness & Spa', '🟣 Wellness & Spa') },
    ],
    [tx, language]
  );

  const catSortOptions = useMemo(
    () => [
      { key: 'active_first', label: tx('Activas Primero', 'Active First') },
      { key: 'az', label: 'A-Z' },
      { key: 'za', label: 'Z-A' },
    ],
    [tx, language]
  );

  const prioritizedCategories = useMemo(() => {
    const q = catSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
    const locale = language === 'en' ? 'en' : 'es';
    const filtered = SORTED_CATEGORIES.filter((cat) => {
      const isWell = isWellnessCategory(cat);
      if ((divTab === 'fitness' && isWell) || (divTab === 'wellness' && !isWell)) return false;
      if (q && !cat.toLowerCase().includes(q) && !tDisc(cat).toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
      return true;
    });
    if (catSort === 'az') {
      return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => tDisc(a).localeCompare(tDisc(b), locale));
    }
    if (catSort === 'za') {
      return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => tDisc(b).localeCompare(tDisc(a), locale));
    }
    const withActive: string[] = [];
    const others: string[] = [];
    filtered.forEach((cat) => {
      if ((categoryCountsMap?.[cat] || 0) > 0) withActive.push(cat);
      else others.push(cat);
    });
    withActive.sort(
      (a, b) => (categoryCountsMap?.[b] || 0) - (categoryCountsMap?.[a] || 0) || tDisc(a).localeCompare(tDisc(b), locale)
    );
    others.sort((a, b) => tDisc(a).localeCompare(tDisc(b), locale));
    return [...withActive, ...others];
  }, [categoryCountsMap, divTab, catSort, catSearch, tDisc, language]);

  const sheetBgStyle = isLight ? { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderTopColor: '#CBD5E1' } : undefined;
  const itemBorderStyle = isLight ? { borderBottomColor: '#E2E8F0' } : undefined;

  return (
    <>
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={catPickerOpen}
        onBackdropPress={() => setCatPickerOpen(false)}
        onBackButtonPress={() => setCatPickerOpen(false)}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.55 : 0.88}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        animationInTiming={200}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      >
        <View style={[globalStyles.bottomSheet, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, { paddingBottom: insetsBottom + 16 }, sheetBgStyle]}>
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={() => setCatPickerOpen(false)} />
          <Text style={[styles.pickerTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
            {tx(
              `Catálogo Oficial (${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length} Disciplinas & Terapias)`,
              `Official Catalog (${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length} Disciplines & Therapies)`
            )}
          </Text>

          <KineticListFilterSortBar
            searchQuery={catSearch}
            onChangeSearch={setCatSearch}
            searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar deporte, masaje, fisio, spa, yoga...', 'Search sport, massage, physio, spa, yoga...')}
            filterChips={catFilterChips}
            activeFilter={divTab}
            onSelectFilter={(key) => setDivTab(key as 'all' | 'fitness' | 'wellness')}
            sortOptions={catSortOptions}
            activeSort={catSort}
            onSelectSort={(key) => setCatSort(key as 'active_first' | 'az' | 'za')}
          />

          <FlatList
            data={prioritizedCategories}
            keyExtractor={(item) => item}
            initialNumToRender={18}
            maxToRenderPerBatch={24}
            windowSize={7}
            removeClippedSubviews={false}
            keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            style={{ flex: 1 }}
            ListHeaderComponent={
              <>
                <View style={{ gap: 7, marginBottom: 10 }}>
                  {quickFilterRows.map((qf) => {
                    const isActive = selectedCategories.length === 0 && quickChip === qf.key;
                    const ink =
                      qf.tone === 'purple'
                        ? isLight ? '#6D28D9' : '#E0AAFF'
                        : qf.tone === 'green'
                          ? themeColors.green
                          : isLight ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan;
                    const activeStyle =
                      qf.tone === 'purple'
                        ? isLight ? styles.quickRowPurpleActiveLight : styles.quickRowPurpleActiveDark
                        : qf.tone === 'green'
                          ? isLight ? styles.quickRowGreenActiveLight : styles.quickRowGreenActiveDark
                          : isLight ? styles.quickRowCyanActiveLight : styles.quickRowCyanActiveDark;
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={qf.key}
                        activeOpacity={0.85}
                        style={[
                          styles.quickRow3DBase,
                          isLight ? styles.quickRow3DLight : styles.quickRow3DDark,
                          isActive && activeStyle,
                          Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null,
                        ]}
                        onPress={() => {
                          triggerHaptic('Selection');
                          setSelectedCategories([]);
                          setQuickChip(qf.key);
                          setCatPickerOpen(false);
                        }}
                      >
                        <Text style={[styles.quickRow3DText, { color: isActive ? ink : themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                          {qf.label}
                        </Text>
                        <Feather name={isActive ? 'check-circle' : 'chevron-right'} size={15} color={isActive ? ink : themeColors.textMuted} />
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>
                <Text style={[styles.catListMicroHeader, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                  {tx(`DISCIPLINAS Y TERAPIAS (${prioritizedCategories.length})`, `DISCIPLINES & THERAPIES (${prioritizedCategories.length})`)}
                </Text>
              </>
            }
            renderItem={({ item: cat }) => {
              const active = selectedCategories.includes(cat);
              const count = categoryCountsMap?.[cat] || 0;
              const isWell = isWellnessCategory(cat);
              const accent = isWell ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText;
              const medallionIcon = resolveMedallionIcon(cat, isWell);
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.pickerItem, itemBorderStyle]}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('Selection');
                    setQuickChip('todas');
                    setSelectedCategories([cat]);
                    setCatPickerOpen(false);
                  }}
                >
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, flex: 1 }}>
                    <View
                      style={[
                        styles.medallionBase,
                        isWell
                          ? isLight ? styles.medallionPurpleLight : styles.medallionPurpleDark
                          : isLight ? styles.medallionCyanLight : styles.medallionCyanDark,
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Feather
                        name={medallionIcon}
                        size={13}
                        color={isWell ? (isLight ? '#7C3AED' : '#E0AAFF') : isLight ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan}
                      />
                    </View>
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.pickerItemText,
                        { color: themeColors.textPrimary, flexShrink: 1 },
                        active && { color: accent, fontWeight: '900' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {tDisc(cat)}
                    </Text>
                    {count > 0 && (
                      <View
                        style={[
                          styles.activeCatCountPill,
                          isLight && { backgroundColor: isWell ? '#F3E8FF' : '#E0F2FE', borderColor: isWell ? '#C4B5FD' : '#7DD3FC' },
                          !isLight && isWell && { backgroundColor: 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.16)', borderColor: 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.5)' },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Text style={[styles.activeCatCountText, { color: accent }]}>
                          ⚡ {count} {count === 1 ? tx('sede', 'venue') : tx('sedes', 'venues')}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    )}
                  </View>
                  {active && <Feather name="check" size={16} color={accent} />}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            }}
          />
        </View>
      </ReactNativeModal>

      <ExploreZoneAndSortModals
        insetsBottom={insetsBottom}
        sortPickerOpen={sortPickerOpen}
        setSortPickerOpen={setSortPickerOpen}
        zonePickerOpen={zonePickerOpen}
        setZonePickerOpen={setZonePickerOpen}
        sortBy={sortBy}
        setSortBy={setSortBy}
        selectedZone={selectedZone}
        setSelectedZone={setSelectedZone}
        setMapPinPreview={setMapPinPreview}
        setWebMapFocus={setWebMapFocus}
        mapRef={mapRef}
        centerOnUserGps={centerOnUserGps}
      />
    </>
  );
});

    // === src/components/explorar/ExploreHeaderControls.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Platform, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { styles as baseStyles } from '../../styles/tabs/index.styles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticPhysicsPressable } from '../../utils/kineticPhysicsEngine';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const NO_SEL: any =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

interface ExploreHeaderControlsProps {
  insetsTop: number;
  selectedZone: string;
  onOpenZonePicker: () => void;
  viewMode: 'lista' | 'mapa';
  onToggleViewMode: () => void;
  searchQuery: string;
  setSearchQuery: (q: string) => void;
  disciplineText: string;
  sortLabelText: string;
  onOpenCatPicker: () => void;
  onOpenSortPicker: () => void;
}

export const ExploreHeaderControls: React.FC<ExploreHeaderControlsProps> = ({
  insetsTop,
  selectedZone,
  onOpenZonePicker,
  viewMode,
  onToggleViewMode,
  searchQuery,
  setSearchQuery,
  disciplineText,
  sortLabelText,
  onOpenCatPicker,
  onOpenSortPicker,
}) => {
  const { themeColors, t, tx, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
  const styles = useMemo(
    () =>
      adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, [
        'gpsZonePill',
        'gpsZonePillLight',
        'filterBtnDark',
        'filterBtnLight',
        'sortBtnDark',
        'sortBtnLight',
      ]),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );

  const cyanInk = isLight ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan;
  const purpleInk = isLight ? '#6D28D9' : '#D8B4FE';

  const zoneDisplayLabel = useMemo(() => {
    const zNorm = String(selectedZone || '')
      .toUpperCase()
      .trim();
    if (
      !zNorm ||
      zNorm.includes('CERCA') ||
      zNorm.includes('10 KM') ||
      zNorm.includes('5 KM') ||
      zNorm.includes('NEAR') ||
      zNorm === 'MI GPS ACTUAL'
    ) {
      return tx('Cerca de mí (radio 10 km)', 'Near me (10 km radius)');
    }
    if (
      zNorm === 'TODAS' ||
      zNorm === 'ALL' ||
      zNorm.includes('TODAS LAS ZONAS') ||
      zNorm.includes('ALL LOS CABOS')
    ) {
      return tx('Todo Los Cabos', 'All Los Cabos');
    }
    if (zNorm.includes('CORREDOR') || zNorm.includes('CORRIDOR')) {
      return tx('Corredor Turístico', 'Tourist Corridor');
    }
    if (zNorm.includes('SAN JOSÉ') || zNorm.includes('SAN JOSE')) {
      return 'San José del Cabo';
    }
    if (zNorm.includes('CABO SAN LUCAS')) {
      return 'Cabo San Lucas';
    }
    return selectedZone;
  }, [selectedZone, tx]);

  return (
    <>
      <View
        style={[
          styles.topHeader,
          { paddingTop: insetsTop + 8, backgroundColor: themeColors.background },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="tactileChip"
            style={[
              styles.gpsZonePill,
              isLight ? styles.gpsZonePillLight : { elevation: 0 },
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              onOpenZonePicker();
            }}
          >
            <Feather
              name="navigation"
              size={11}
              color={isLight ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan}
            />
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.gpsZoneText,
                { color: isLight ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan },
              ]}
              numberOfLines={1}
            >
              {zoneDisplayLabel}
            </Text>
            <Feather
              name="chevron-down"
              size={12}
              color={isLight ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan}
            />
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>
          <Text style={[styles.screenTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
            {t('explore.title')}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <KineticPhysicsPressable
          preset="tactileChip"
          style={[
            styles.viewModeBtn,
            isLight
              ? {
                  backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF',
                  borderColor: '#06B6D4',
                  borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4',
                  shadowColor: '#00B8D4',
                  shadowOpacity: 0.24,
                  elevation: 4,
                }
              : { elevation: 0 },
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
          onPress={onToggleViewMode}
        >
          <Feather
            name={viewMode === 'lista' ? 'map' : 'list'}
            size={14}
            color={cyanInk}
          />
          <Text style={[styles.viewModeBtnText, { color: cyanInk }]}>
            {viewMode === 'lista' ? t('explore.map') : t('explore.list')}
          </Text>
        </KineticPhysicsPressable>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.controlsBlock}>
        <View
          style={[
            styles.searchBar,
            isLight && {
              backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderColor: '#38BDF8',
              borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4',
              shadowColor: '#00B8D4',
              shadowOpacity: 0.16,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Feather name="search" size={16} color={cyanInk} />
          <TextInput
            style={[styles.searchInput, { fontSize: 16, color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}
            placeholder={t('explore.search_placeholder')}
            placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
            value={searchQuery}
            onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
            multiline={false}
            numberOfLines={1}
            maxFontSizeMultiplier={1.1}
          />
          {searchQuery.length > 0 && (
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearchQuery('')}>
              <Feather name="x" size={16} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.dropdownsRow}>
          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="tactileChip"
            style={[
              styles.halfBtn3D,
              isLight ? styles.filterBtnLight : styles.filterBtnDark,
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              onOpenCatPicker();
            }}
          >
            <Feather name="filter" size={12} color={cyanInk} />
            <Text
              style={[styles.halfBtnText, { color: cyanInk }]}
              numberOfLines={1}
            >
              {tx('Filtrar', 'Filter')}: {disciplineText} ▼
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="tactileChip"
            style={[
              styles.halfBtn3D,
              isLight ? styles.sortBtnLight : styles.sortBtnDark,
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              onOpenSortPicker();
            }}
          >
            <Feather name="sliders" size={12} color={purpleInk} />
            <Text
              style={[styles.halfBtnText, { color: purpleInk }]}
              numberOfLines={1}
            >
              {tx('Ordenar', 'Sort')}: {sortLabelText} ▼
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>
        </View>
      </View>
    </>
  );
};

    FILE: src/components/explorar/ExploreMapPreviewOverlay.tsx

    // === src/components/explorar/ExploreMapPreviewOverlay.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo } from 'react';
import { Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

interface ExploreMapPreviewOverlayProps {
  styles: any;
  safePreview: any;
  bottomSafeOffset: number;
  isAdminView: boolean;
  isNearMeEmpty: boolean;
  hasVisibleMarkers: boolean;
  onResetToAllCaboZones?: () => void;
  onOpenPreviewFullModal: () => void;
  onDismissPreview: () => void;
}

export const ExploreMapPreviewOverlay = memo(
  ({
    styles,
    safePreview,
    bottomSafeOffset,
    isAdminView,
    isNearMeEmpty,
    hasVisibleMarkers,
    onResetToAllCaboZones,
    onOpenPreviewFullModal,
    onDismissPreview,
  }: ExploreMapPreviewOverlayProps) => {
    const { themeColors, tx, tDisc } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const elevatedBottom = Math.max(Number(bottomSafeOffset) || 0, 86);
    const translatedDiscipline = safePreview
      ? tDisc(safePreview.rawDiscipline || safePreview.discipline).toUpperCase()
      : '';

    return (
      <>
        {!safePreview && (isNearMeEmpty || !hasVisibleMarkers) && isNearMeEmpty && onResetToAllCaboZones && (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.mapEmptyZoneBanner,
              { bottom: elevatedBottom },
              isLight && {
                backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
                borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderBottomColor: '#0284C7',
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.mapEmptyZoneTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tx(
                'Sin sedes activas a 10 km a la redonda de tu GPS actual.',
                'No active venues within a 10 km radius of your current GPS.'
              )}
            </Text>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.mapEmptyZoneBtn, NO_SEL]}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={onResetToAllCaboZones}
            >
              <Text style={styles.mapEmptyZoneBtnText}>
                {tx('VER TODAS LAS ZONAS DE LOS CABOS', 'VIEW ALL LOS CABOS ZONES')}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}

        {safePreview && (
          <View
            style={[styles.mapPreviewCardWrapper, { bottom: elevatedBottom }]}
            pointerEvents="box-none"
          >
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.mapPreviewCard,
                NO_SEL,
                { borderColor: safePreview.isHidden ? '#94A3B8' : safePreview.borderColor },
                safePreview.isHidden && { opacity: 0.62 },
                isLight && {
                  backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderBottomColor: safePreview.isHidden ? '#94A3B8' : safePreview.borderColor,
                },
              ]}
              activeOpacity={0.9}
              onPress={onOpenPreviewFullModal}
            >
              <View style={styles.mapPreviewImageContainer}>
                <SmartImage
                  source={{ uri: safePreview.bannerUri }}
                  fallbackUri={safePreview.fallbackUri}
                  fallbackCategory={safePreview.rawDiscipline || 'Fuerza'}
                  style={styles.mapPreviewImage}
                  contentFit="cover"
                  priority="high"
                />
                <View style={styles.mapPreviewImageOverlay} />
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.mapPreviewDiscPill,
                    { backgroundColor: safePreview.badgeColor },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.mapPreviewDiscText,
                      { color: safePreview.badgeTextColor },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {translatedDiscipline}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                {isAdminView && safePreview.isHidden && (
                  <View style={styles.mapHiddenBadgePill}>
                    <Feather name="eye-off" size={10} color={COLORS.yellow} />
                    <Text style={styles.mapHiddenBadgeText}>
                      {tx('OCULTO', 'HIDDEN')}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                )}
                <View style={styles.mapPreviewTopRightRow}>
                  <View
                    {...({
                      dataSet: isLight
                        ? { kineticInk: 'dark' }
                        : { kineticOnMedia: 'true', kineticInk: 'light' },
                    } as any)}
                    style={[
                      styles.mapPreviewTimePill,
                      isLight && {
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95)',
                        borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
                        borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8',
                        elevation: 0,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      {...({
                        dataSet: isLight
                          ? { kineticInk: 'dark' }
                          : { kineticOnMedia: 'true', kineticInk: 'light' },
                      } as any)}
                      style={[
                        styles.mapPreviewTimeText,
                        { color: isLight ? '#0F172A' : '#FFFFFF' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {safePreview.topRightMeta}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              </View>
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.mapPreviewInfoBody,
                  isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF' },
                ]}
              >
                <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.mapPreviewTitle,
                      { color: themeColors.textPrimary },
                    ]}
                    numberOfLines={1}
                  >
                    {safePreview.title}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.mapPreviewSub,
                      { color: themeColors.textSecondary, lineHeight: 16 },
                    ]}
                    numberOfLines={2}
                  >
                    {safePreview.subLine}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.mapPreviewPriceText,
                      {
                        color: isLight
                          ? safePreview.isEvent
                            ? themeColors.greenText
                            : safePreview.isSpec
                              ? themeColors.purpleText
                              : themeColors.cyanText
                          : safePreview.borderColor,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {safePreview.rightMainValue}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.mapPreviewEscrowText,
                      { color: themeColors.textMuted },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {safePreview.rightSubValue}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity
              {...({
                dataSet: isLight
                  ? { kineticInk: 'dark' }
                  : { kineticOnMedia: 'true', kineticInk: 'light' },
              } as any)}
              style={[
                styles.mapPreviewCloseBtn,
                NO_SEL,
                isLight && {
                  backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.96)',
                  borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
                  borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8',
                },
              ]}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
              hitSlop={{ top: 12, bottom: 12, left: 12, right: 12 }}
              onPress={() => {
                triggerHaptic('Light');
                onDismissPreview();
              }}
            >
              <Feather name="x" size={14} color={isLight ? '#0F172A' : '#FFFFFF'} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}
      </>
    );
  }
);

   borderBottomWidth: 4, elevation: 6,  },

  mapEmptyZoneBanner: {

    FILE: src/components/explorar/ExploreMapViews.tsx

    // === src/components/explorar/ExploreMapViews.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
// Carga condicional segura de react-native-maps solo en Android/iOS nativo (evita crash codegenNativeComponent en Web)
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports
const NativeMaps = Platform.OS !== 'web' ? require('react-native-maps') : null;
const MapView: any = NativeMaps ? NativeMaps.default : View;
const Marker: any = NativeMaps ? NativeMaps.Marker : View;
const PROVIDER_GOOGLE: any = NativeMaps ? NativeMaps.PROVIDER_GOOGLE : undefined;

import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { computeUserMarkerSafeCoordinate } from '../../services/exploreZoneHelpers';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { IMapLayerVisibility } from '../../types';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticPhysicsPressable } from '../../utils/kineticPhysicsEngine';
import { SkeletonPulse } from '../SharedUI';
import { ExploreMapPreviewOverlay } from './ExploreMapPreviewOverlay';
import { styles as baseStyles } from './ExploreMapViews.styles';
import { RadarMapPin, isClubStrictlyActiveUI } from './ExploreRadarMapPin';
import { KineticWebInteractiveMap } from './KineticWebInteractiveMap';

export { KineticWebInteractiveMap, RadarMapPin };

const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

export const SAFE_LIGHT_MAP_STYLE = [
  { featureType: 'water', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#7ce0ea' }] }, { featureType: 'water', elementType: 'labels.text.fill', stylers: [{ color: '#006b82' }] },
  { featureType: 'landscape', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#f4f8fa' }] }, { featureType: 'landscape.natural', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#e9f5ef' }] },
  { featureType: 'poi.park', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#d4f0e2' }] }, { featureType: 'poi.park', elementType: 'labels.text.fill', stylers: [{ color: '#1e6b52' }] },
  { featureType: 'poi.business', stylers: [{ visibility: 'off' }] }, { featureType: 'poi.attraction', elementType: 'labels.icon', stylers: [{ visibility: 'off' }] },
  { featureType: 'road', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#ffffff' }] }, { featureType: 'road', elementType: 'geometry.stroke', stylers: [{ color: '#cbd5e1' }] },
  { featureType: 'road', elementType: 'labels.text.fill', stylers: [{ color: '#334155' }] }, { featureType: 'road.highway', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#e0f2fe' }] },
  { featureType: 'road.highway', elementType: 'geometry.stroke', stylers: [{ color: '#7dd3fc' }] }, { featureType: 'administrative.locality', elementType: 'labels.text.fill', stylers: [{ color: '#0f172a' }] },
  { featureType: 'administrative', elementType: 'labels.text.stroke', stylers: [{ color: '#ffffff' }, { weight: 3 }] },
];

export const SAFE_DARK_MAP_STYLE = [
  { elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#0f1c20' }] }, { elementType: 'labels.text.fill', stylers: [{ color: '#9cc2ce' }] },
  { elementType: 'labels.text.stroke', stylers: [{ color: '#050b0e' }, { weight: 3 }] }, { featureType: 'landscape.natural', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#122423' }] },
  { featureType: 'poi.park', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#132c28' }] }, { featureType: 'poi.business', stylers: [{ visibility: 'off' }] },
  { featureType: 'poi.attraction', elementType: 'labels.icon', stylers: [{ visibility: 'off' }] }, { featureType: 'poi', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#132227' }] },
  { featureType: 'poi', elementType: 'labels.text.fill', stylers: [{ color: '#769aa6' }] }, { featureType: 'road', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#1d313a' }] },
  { featureType: 'road', elementType: 'geometry.stroke', stylers: [{ color: '#0a1419' }] }, { featureType: 'road', elementType: 'labels.text.fill', stylers: [{ color: '#bdd7e2' }] },
  { featureType: 'road.highway', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#284654' }] }, { featureType: 'road.highway', elementType: 'geometry.stroke', stylers: [{ color: '#0d1d24' }] },
  { featureType: 'administrative.locality', elementType: 'labels.text.fill', stylers: [{ color: '#e6f4f8' }] }, { featureType: 'water', elementType: 'geometry', stylers: [{ color: '#062b36' }] },
  { featureType: 'water', elementType: 'labels.text.fill', stylers: [{ color: '#4ea8be' }] },
