export const ExploreMapViewSection = memo(
  ({
    mapRef,
    mapMarkers,
    userCoords,
    webMapFocus,
    nativeFocusRegion,
    initialRegionOverride,
    previewCardData,
    mapLayers,
    toggleMapLayer,
    handleSelectPin,
    handlePressMyUserPin,
    handleOpenPreviewFullModal,
    setMapPinPreview,
    centerOnUserGps,
    isAdminView = false,
    isNearMeEmpty = false,
    onResetToAllCaboZones,
  }: {
    mapRef: any;
    mapMarkers: any[];
    userCoords: { latitude: number; longitude: number };
    webMapFocus: { lat: number; lng: number; zoom?: number; ts: number } | null;
    nativeFocusRegion?: {
      latitude: number;
      longitude: number;
      latitudeDelta: number;
      longitudeDelta: number;
      ts: number;
    } | null;
    initialRegionOverride?: {
      latitude: number;
      longitude: number;
      latitudeDelta: number;
      longitudeDelta: number;
    };
    previewCardData: any;
    mapLayers: IMapLayerVisibility;
    toggleMapLayer: (layerKey: keyof IMapLayerVisibility) => void;
    handleSelectPin: (m: any, isEvent: boolean) => void;
    handlePressMyUserPin: () => void;
    handleOpenPreviewFullModal: () => void;
    setMapPinPreview: (val: any) => void;
    centerOnUserGps: () => void;
    isAdminView?: boolean;
    isNearMeEmpty?: boolean;
    onResetToAllCaboZones?: () => void;
  }) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const bottomSafeOffset = Math.max(insets.bottom + 82, 86);

    const styles = useMemo(
      () =>
        adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, [
          'mapPreviewTimePill',
          'mapPreviewTimeText',
          'mapPreviewCloseBtn',
          'mapPreviewDiscPill',
          'mapPreviewDiscText',
          'mapHiddenBadgePill',
          'mapHiddenBadgeText',
          'customMapPin',
          'userGpsLabelPill',
          'userGpsLabelText',
          'mapEmptyZoneBtn',
          'mapEmptyZoneBtnText',
        ]),
      [resolvedTheme]
    );

    const visibleMarkers = useMemo(() => {
      if (isAdminView) return mapMarkers || [];
      return (mapMarkers || []).filter((m: any) => {
        const mType = String(m?.markerType || '').toLowerCase();
        const isEv = mType === 'event' || mType === 'evento';
        const parentClub = m?.rawAffiliate ?? (!isEv ? m : null);
        return parentClub ? isClubStrictlyActiveUI(parentClub) : true;
      });
    }, [mapMarkers, isAdminView]);

    const safePreview =
      previewCardData && (!previewCardData.isHidden || isAdminView)
        ? previewCardData
        : null;

    const effectiveInitialRegion = initialRegionOverride || {
      latitude: Number(userCoords.latitude || 22.8905),
      longitude: Number(userCoords.longitude || -109.9167),
      latitudeDelta: 0.08,
      longitudeDelta: 0.08,
    };

    const [mapRegion, setMapRegion] = useState(effectiveInitialRegion);
    const [isNativeMapReady, setIsNativeMapReady] = useState<boolean>(Platform.OS === 'web');
    const readyFadeTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    useEffect(() => {
      if (Platform.OS === 'web') { setIsNativeMapReady(true); return; }
      setIsNativeMapReady(false);
      const t = setTimeout(() => setIsNativeMapReady(true), 1800);
      return () => {
        clearTimeout(t);
        if (readyFadeTimerRef.current) { clearTimeout(readyFadeTimerRef.current); readyFadeTimerRef.current = null; }
      };
    }, [isLight]);
    const markNativeMapReady = useCallback(() => {
      if (Platform.OS === 'web') return;
      if (readyFadeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(readyFadeTimerRef.current);
      readyFadeTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setIsNativeMapReady(true), 120);
    }, []);
    useEffect(() => {
      if (nativeFocusRegion) {
        setMapRegion({
          latitude: nativeFocusRegion.latitude,
          longitude: nativeFocusRegion.longitude,
          latitudeDelta: nativeFocusRegion.latitudeDelta,
          longitudeDelta: nativeFocusRegion.longitudeDelta,
        });
      }
    }, [nativeFocusRegion]);

    const { nativePinScale, nativeRenderItems } = useMemo(() => {
      const reg = mapRegion || effectiveInitialRegion;
      const latDelta = Math.max(0.005, Number(reg.latitudeDelta) || 0.08);
      const lngDelta = Math.max(0.005, Number(reg.longitudeDelta) || 0.08);
      const scale = Math.max(0.76, Math.min(1.12, 1.06 - Math.log2(latDelta / 0.025) * 0.1));
      const latHalf = (latDelta / 2) * 1.4;
      const lngHalf = (lngDelta / 2) * 1.4;
      const inBounds = visibleMarkers.filter((m: any) => {
        const mLat = Number(m?.latitude);
        const mLng = Number(m?.longitude);
        return (
          Number.isFinite(mLat) &&
          Number.isFinite(mLng) &&
          Math.abs(mLat) > 0.1 &&
          Math.abs(mLng) > 0.1 &&
          Math.abs(mLat - reg.latitude) <= latHalf &&
          Math.abs(mLng - reg.longitude) <= lngHalf
        );
      });
      if (latDelta < 0.055 || inBounds.length <= 1) {
        return { nativePinScale: scale, nativeRenderItems: inBounds.map((m: any) => ({ kind: 'pin' as const, pin: m })) };
      }
      const cellLat = latDelta * 0.18;
      const cellLng = lngDelta * 0.18;
      const buckets = new Map<string, any[]>();
      inBounds.forEach((m: any) => {
        const key = `${Math.round(Number(m.latitude) / cellLat)}_${Math.round(Number(m.longitude) / cellLng)}`;
        const list = buckets.get(key);
        if (list) list.push(m);
        else buckets.set(key, [m]);
      });
      const items: Array<{ kind: 'pin'; pin: any } | { kind: 'cluster'; id: string; latitude: number; longitude: number; count: number }> = [];
      buckets.forEach((group, key) => {
        if (group.length < 2) {
          items.push({ kind: 'pin', pin: group[0] });
          return;
        }
        items.push({
          kind: 'cluster',
          id: `cluster_${key}`,
          latitude: group.reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.latitude), 0) / group.length,
          longitude: group.reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.longitude), 0) / group.length,
          count: group.length,
        });
      });
      return { nativePinScale: scale, nativeRenderItems: items };
    }, [visibleMarkers, mapRegion, effectiveInitialRegion]);

    const safeUserCoord = useMemo(
      () =>
        computeUserMarkerSafeCoordinate(
          userCoords,
          nativeRenderItems.map((entry: any) => (entry.kind === 'cluster' ? entry : entry.pin)),
          mapRegion
        ),
      [userCoords, nativeRenderItems, mapRegion]
    );

    const lightInactivePill = isLight
      ? {
          backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
          borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8',
          elevation: 3,
        }
      : undefined;

    return (
      <View
        style={[
          styles.mapViewportContainer,
          isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' },
        ]}
      >
        {Platform.OS === 'web' ? (
          <KineticWebInteractiveMap
            markers={visibleMarkers}
            userCoords={userCoords}
            focusTarget={webMapFocus}
            isAdminView={isAdminView}
            onSelectPin={handleSelectPin}
            onPressUserPin={handlePressMyUserPin}
            onDismissPreview={() => setMapPinPreview(null)}
          />
        ) : (
          <MapView
            key={'knt-native-map-' + (isLight ? 'light' : 'dark')}
            ref={mapRef}
            provider={PROVIDER_GOOGLE}
            style={styles.mapCanvasFull}
            userInterfaceStyle={isLight ? 'light' : 'dark'}
            customMapStyle={isLight ? SAFE_LIGHT_MAP_STYLE : SAFE_DARK_MAP_STYLE}
            initialRegion={effectiveInitialRegion}
            mapPadding={{
              top: 64,
              right: 16,
              bottom: Platform.OS === 'android' ? Math.max(insets.bottom + 96, 112) : bottomSafeOffset,
              left: 16,
            }}
            showsUserLocation={false}
            showsMyLocationButton={false}
            showsCompass={false}
            toolbarEnabled={false}
            moveOnMarkerPress={false}
            onRegionChangeComplete={(reg: any) => {
              if (reg && typeof reg.latitude === 'number') setMapRegion(reg);
            }}
            onMapLoaded={markNativeMapReady}
            onMapReady={() => {
              markNativeMapReady();
              if (nativeFocusRegion) {
                mapRef.current?.animateToRegion?.(
                  {
                    latitude: nativeFocusRegion.latitude,
                    longitude: nativeFocusRegion.longitude,
                    latitudeDelta: nativeFocusRegion.latitudeDelta,
                    longitudeDelta: nativeFocusRegion.longitudeDelta,
                  },
                  220
                );
              }
            }}
            onPress={() => setMapPinPreview(null)}
          >
            <Marker
              identifier="kinetic_user_gps_pin"
              coordinate={{
                latitude: safeUserCoord.latitude,
                longitude: safeUserCoord.longitude,
              }}
              anchor={{ x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }}
              zIndex={380}
              tappable={false}
            >
              <View style={styles.userGpsContainer} collapsable={false} pointerEvents="none">
                <View style={styles.userGpsOuterHalo} collapsable={false}>
                  <View style={styles.userGpsCoreCircle} collapsable={false}>
                    <Ionicons name="flash" size={14} color="#050505" />
                  </View>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.userGpsLabelPill} collapsable={false}>
                  <Text style={styles.userGpsLabelText}>{tx('TÚ', 'YOU')}</Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            </Marker>
            {nativeRenderItems.map((entry: any, idx: number) =>
              entry.kind === 'cluster' ? (
                <Marker
                  key={entry.id}
                  identifier={entry.id}
                  coordinate={{ latitude: entry.latitude, longitude: entry.longitude }}
                  anchor={{ x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }}
                  zIndex={620}
                  onPress={(e: any) => {
                    e?.stopPropagation?.();
                    mapRef.current?.animateToRegion?.(
                      {
                        latitude: entry.latitude,
                        longitude: entry.longitude,
                        latitudeDelta: Math.max(0.012, (mapRegion?.latitudeDelta || 0.08) * 0.42),
                        longitudeDelta: Math.max(0.012, (mapRegion?.longitudeDelta || 0.08) * 0.42),
                      },
                      380
                    );
                  }}
                >
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.nativeClusterBadge,
                      isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' },
                    ]}
                    collapsable={false}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.nativeClusterBadgeText, isLight && { color: '#0284C7' }]}>
                      +{entry.count}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </Marker>
              ) : (
                <RadarMapPin
                  key={String(entry.pin.id)}
                  pin={entry.pin}
                  index={idx}
                  isAdminView={isAdminView}
                  pinScale={nativePinScale}
                  onSelectPin={handleSelectPin}
                />
              )
            )}
          </MapView>
        )}

        {Platform.OS !== 'web' && !isNativeMapReady && (
          <View
            pointerEvents="none"
            style={{
              position: 'absolute', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0, zIndex: 40, elevation: 0,
              backgroundColor: isLight ? '#EEF2F7' : '#070B12', paddingTop: 72, paddingHorizontal: 14, gap: 10,
            }}
          >
            <SkeletonPulse isLightMode={isLight} style={{ width: '46%', height: 14, borderRadius: 7 }} />
            <SkeletonPulse isLightMode={isLight} style={{ flex: 1, borderRadius: 20, marginBottom: bottomSafeOffset + 12 }} />
          </View>
        )}

        <View style={styles.topMapLayersBar} pointerEvents="box-none">
          <ScrollView
            horizontal
            showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
            style={{ flex: 1 }}
            contentContainerStyle={[styles.mapLayersRowPillGroup, { paddingRight: 6 }]}
          >
            <KineticPhysicsPressable
              preset="tactileChip"
              style={[
                styles.mapLayerPill, NO_SEL, lightInactivePill,
                mapLayers.showClubs && (isLight ? { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7', elevation: 4 } : styles.mapLayerPillCyan),
              ]}
              onPress={() => toggleMapLayer('showClubs')}
            >
              <Feather name={mapLayers.showClubs ? 'eye' : 'eye-off'} size={11} color={mapLayers.showClubs ? (isLight ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan) : themeColors.textMuted} />
              <Text style={[styles.mapLayerText, { color: themeColors.textMuted }, mapLayers.showClubs && { color: isLight ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan }]}>
                {tx('+ CLUBES', '+ CLUBS')}
              </Text>
            </KineticPhysicsPressable>

            <KineticPhysicsPressable
              preset="tactileChip"
              style={[
                styles.mapLayerPill, NO_SEL, lightInactivePill,
                mapLayers.showSpecialists && (isLight ? { backgroundColor: '#F3E8FF', borderColor: '#9333EA', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED', elevation: 4 } : styles.mapLayerPillPurple),
              ]}
              onPress={() => toggleMapLayer('showSpecialists')}
            >
              <Feather name={mapLayers.showSpecialists ? 'eye' : 'eye-off'} size={11} color={mapLayers.showSpecialists ? (isLight ? '#6D28D9' : COLORS.purple) : themeColors.textMuted} />
              <Text style={[styles.mapLayerText, { color: themeColors.textMuted }, mapLayers.showSpecialists && { color: isLight ? '#6D28D9' : '#D8B4FE' }]}>
                {tx('+ ESPECIALISTAS', '+ SPECIALISTS')}
              </Text>
            </KineticPhysicsPressable>

            <KineticPhysicsPressable
              preset="tactileChip"
              style={[
                styles.mapLayerPill, NO_SEL, lightInactivePill,
                mapLayers.showEvents && (isLight ? { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#10B981', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#059669', elevation: 4 } : styles.mapLayerPillGreen),
              ]}
              onPress={() => toggleMapLayer('showEvents')}
            >
              <Feather name={mapLayers.showEvents ? 'eye' : 'eye-off'} size={11} color={mapLayers.showEvents ? themeColors.green : themeColors.textMuted} />
              <Text style={[styles.mapLayerText, { color: themeColors.textMuted }, mapLayers.showEvents && { color: themeColors.green }]}>
                {tx('+ EVENTOS', '+ EVENTS')}
              </Text>
            </KineticPhysicsPressable>
          </ScrollView>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="tactileChip"
            style={[styles.centerGpsFloatingBtn, NO_SEL, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' }]}
            onPress={centerOnUserGps}
          >
            <Feather name="navigation" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>
        </View>

        <ExploreMapPreviewOverlay
          styles={styles}
          safePreview={safePreview}
          bottomSafeOffset={bottomSafeOffset}
          isAdminView={isAdminView}
          isNearMeEmpty={isNearMeEmpty}
          hasVisibleMarkers={visibleMarkers.length > 0}
          onResetToAllCaboZones={onResetToAllCaboZones}
          onOpenPreviewFullModal={handleOpenPreviewFullModal}
          onDismissPreview={() => setMapPinPreview(null)}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/explorar/ExploreOverlaysStack.tsx

    // === src/components/explorar/ExploreOverlaysStack.tsx === //
import { Image as ExpoImage } from 'expo-image';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, View } from 'react-native';
import { resolveCategoryImage } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { ExploreSortOption, QuickExploreChip, WARMED_PIN_URLS } from '../../hooks/useExplorar';
import { MembershipModal, TacticalAlertModal } from '../BovedaModals';
import { ClubDetailsModal } from '../ClubDetailsModal';
import { EventDetailsModal } from '../EventDetailsModal';
import { RpgModal } from '../KinetiClubModals';
import { ExploreFilterModals } from './ExploreFilterModals';

export const ExplorePinCacheWarmer = memo(
  ({
    visibleMapMarkers,
    hiddenStyle,
  }: {
    visibleMapMarkers: any[];
    hiddenStyle: any;
  }) => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') return null;
    return (
      <View style={hiddenStyle} pointerEvents="none">
        {visibleMapMarkers.map((m: any) => {
          const parentClub = m?.rawAffiliate;
          const fallbackUri = resolveCategoryImage(
            m?.disciplina || parentClub?.disciplina || 'Fuerza',
            parentClub?.categoria || m?.title
          );
          const uri =
            m?.avatar_url && String(m.avatar_url).trim().length > 5
              ? String(m.avatar_url).trim()
              : fallbackUri;
          return (
            <ExpoImage
              key={`warm_${m.id}`}
              source={{ uri }}
              style={{ width: 34, height: 34 }}
              contentFit="cover"
              cachePolicy="memory-disk"
              priority="high"
              onLoad={() => WARMED_PIN_URLS.add(uri)}
            />
          );
        })}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

interface ExploreOverlaysStackProps {
  insetsBottom: number;
  catPickerOpen: boolean;
  setCatPickerOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  sortPickerOpen: boolean;
  setSortPickerOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  zonePickerOpen: boolean;
  setZonePickerOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  selectedCategories: string[];
  setSelectedCategories: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string[]>>;
  quickChip: QuickExploreChip;
  setQuickChip: (v: QuickExploreChip) => void;
  favoriteIds: string[];
  sortBy: ExploreSortOption;
  setSortBy: (v: ExploreSortOption) => void;
  selectedZone: string;
  setSelectedZone: (v: string) => void;
  categoryCountsMap: Record<string, number>;
  viewMode: 'lista' | 'mapa';
  setMapPinPreview: (v: any) => void;
  setWebMapFocus: (v: any) => void;
  mapRef: any;
  centerOnUserGps: () => void;
  selectedClub: any | null;
  setSelectedClub: (v: any | null) => void;
  onToggleClubFavorite: () => void;
  onSelectClaseFromClub: (cl: any) => void;
  onRefreshRadar: () => void;
  selectedEvent: any | null;
  setSelectedEvent: (v: any | null) => void;
  onReserveEvent: (ev: any) => Promise<boolean>;
  showMyRpgModal: boolean;
  setShowMyRpgModal: (v: boolean) => void;
  onGoToTrain: () => void;
  nivel: string;
  bonusXp: number;
  myUserStats: any;
  mySeasonInfo: any;
  myPrestigeInfo: any;
  isMembershipOpen: boolean;
  setIsMembershipOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  alertConfig: any;
  setAlertConfig: (v: any) => void;
  triggerHaptic: (t?: any) => void;
}

export const ExploreOverlaysStack = memo((props: ExploreOverlaysStackProps) => {
  const {
    insetsBottom,
    catPickerOpen,
    setCatPickerOpen,
    sortPickerOpen,
    setSortPickerOpen,
    zonePickerOpen,
    setZonePickerOpen,
    selectedCategories,
    setSelectedCategories,
    quickChip,
    setQuickChip,
    favoriteIds,
    sortBy,
    setSortBy,
    selectedZone,
    setSelectedZone,
    categoryCountsMap,
    viewMode,
    setMapPinPreview,
    setWebMapFocus,
    mapRef,
    centerOnUserGps,
    selectedClub,
    setSelectedClub,
    onToggleClubFavorite,
    onSelectClaseFromClub,
    onRefreshRadar,
    selectedEvent,
    setSelectedEvent,
    onReserveEvent,
    showMyRpgModal,
    setShowMyRpgModal,
    onGoToTrain,
    nivel,
    bonusXp,
    myUserStats,
    mySeasonInfo,
    myPrestigeInfo,
    isMembershipOpen,
    setIsMembershipOpen,
    alertConfig,
    setAlertConfig,
    triggerHaptic,
  } = props;

  const [createEventDraft, setCreateEventDraft] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const openCreateTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => () => { if (openCreateTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(openCreateTimerRef.current); }, []);
  const handleOpenCreateEventFromClub = useCallback((draft: any) => {
    setSelectedClub(null);
    if (openCreateTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(openCreateTimerRef.current);
    openCreateTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { setCreateEventDraft(draft); }, 190);
  }, [setSelectedClub]);

  const isClubFavorite = selectedClub
    ? favoriteIds.includes(String(selectedClub.id)) ||
      favoriteIds.includes(String(selectedClub.nombre))
    : false;

  return (
    <>
      <ExploreFilterModals
        insetsBottom={insetsBottom}
        catPickerOpen={catPickerOpen}
        setCatPickerOpen={setCatPickerOpen}
        sortPickerOpen={sortPickerOpen}
        setSortPickerOpen={setSortPickerOpen}
        zonePickerOpen={zonePickerOpen}
        setZonePickerOpen={setZonePickerOpen}
        selectedCategories={selectedCategories}
        setSelectedCategories={setSelectedCategories}
        quickChip={quickChip}
        setQuickChip={setQuickChip}
        favoriteCount={favoriteIds.length}
        sortBy={sortBy}
        setSortBy={setSortBy}
        selectedZone={selectedZone}
        setSelectedZone={setSelectedZone}
        categoryCountsMap={categoryCountsMap}
        viewMode={viewMode}
        setMapPinPreview={setMapPinPreview}
        setWebMapFocus={setWebMapFocus}
        mapRef={mapRef}
        centerOnUserGps={centerOnUserGps}
      />

      <ClubDetailsModal
        visible={Boolean(selectedClub)}
        affiliate={selectedClub}
        isFavorite={isClubFavorite}
        onToggleFavorite={onToggleClubFavorite}
        onClose={() => setSelectedClub(null)}
        onSelectClase={onSelectClaseFromClub}
        onOpenCreateEvent={handleOpenCreateEventFromClub}
        onRefreshRadar={onRefreshRadar}
      />

      <EventDetailsModal
        visible={Boolean(selectedEvent)}
        event={selectedEvent}
        onClose={() => setSelectedEvent(null)}
        onNavigateVault={() => { setSelectedEvent(null); setSelectedClub(null); }}
        onReserve={onReserveEvent}
        onRefreshEvents={onRefreshRadar}
      />

      <EventDetailsModal
        visible={Boolean(createEventDraft)}
        event={createEventDraft}
        isCreateMode={true}
        defaultClubName={createEventDraft?.negocio_nombre}
        onClose={() => setCreateEventDraft(null)}
        onRefreshEvents={onRefreshRadar}
      />

      <RpgModal
        showRpgModal={showMyRpgModal}
        onClose={() => setShowMyRpgModal(false)}
        onGoToTrain={onGoToTrain}
        nivel={nivel}
        bonusXp={bonusXp}
        userStats={myUserStats}
        seasonInfo={mySeasonInfo}
        prestigeInfo={myPrestigeInfo}
      />

      <MembershipModal isVisible={isMembershipOpen} onClose={() => setIsMembershipOpen(false)} />
      <TacticalAlertModal alertConfig={alertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />
    </>
  );
});

    // === src/components/explorar/ExploreRadarMapPin.tsx === //
import { Image as ExpoImage } from 'expo-image';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { resolveCategoryImage } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { WARMED_PIN_URLS } from '../../hooks/useExplorar';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { styles as baseStyles } from './ExploreMapViews.styles';
import { KineticOfficialPinVectorSvg, resolveOfficialPinSvgKind } from './kineticOfficialRadarSvgs';

const KINETIC_OFFICIAL_PIN_LOGO = require('../../../assets/logo.png');

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports
const NativeMaps = Platform.OS !== 'web' ? require('react-native-maps') : null;
const Marker: any = NativeMaps ? NativeMaps.Marker : View;

export const isClubStrictlyActiveUI = (club: any): boolean => {
  if (!club) return false;
  if (
    club.esClubActivo === false ||
    club.esActivo === false ||
    club.activo === false
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  const est = String(club.estado ?? '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
  if (est && est !== 'activo') return false;
  return Boolean(
    est === 'activo' || club.esClubActivo === true || club.esActivo === true
  );
};

export const RadarMapPin = memo(
  ({
    pin,
    index,
    isAdminView = false,
    pinScale = 1,
    onSelectPin,
  }: {
    pin: any;
    index: number;
    isAdminView?: boolean;
    pinScale?: number;
    onSelectPin: (item: any, isEvent: boolean) => void;
  }) => {
    const { tx, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const styles = baseStyles;
    const pinId = String(pin?.id || index);
    const mType = String(pin?.markerType || '').toLowerCase();
    const isEvent = mType === 'event' || mType === 'evento';
    const isSpec = mType === 'specialist' || mType === 'especialista';
    const parentClub = pin?.rawAffiliate ?? (!isEvent ? pin : null);
    const isHiddenClub = parentClub ? !isClubStrictlyActiveUI(parentClub) : false;

    const fallbackUri = resolveCategoryImage(
      pin?.disciplina || parentClub?.disciplina || 'Fuerza',
      parentClub?.categoria || pin?.title
    );
    const rawCandidate =
      pin?.avatar_url && String(pin.avatar_url).trim().length > 5
        ? String(pin.avatar_url).trim()
        : '';
    const [imgError, setImgError] = useState(false);
    const effectiveUri = !imgError && rawCandidate ? rawCandidate : fallbackUri;

    const [iconReady, setIconReady] = useState(() =>
      WARMED_PIN_URLS.has(effectiveUri)
    );
    const [tracks, setTracks] = useState(true);
    const hasTriggeredRemountRef = useRef(false);

    useEffect(() => {
      setImgError(false);
      hasTriggeredRemountRef.current = false;
      setTracks(true);
    }, [rawCandidate]);

    useEffect(() => {
      const t = setTimeout(() => setTracks(false), 900);
      return () => clearTimeout(t);
    }, [iconReady, imgError]);

    if (
      pin?.latitude === null ||
      pin?.latitude === undefined ||
      String(pin.latitude).trim() === '' ||
      pin?.longitude === null ||
      pin?.longitude === undefined ||
      String(pin.longitude).trim() === ''
    ) {
      return null;
    }

    const lat = Number(pin?.latitude);
    const lng = Number(pin?.longitude);
    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lng) || Math.abs(lat) > 90 || Math.abs(lng) > 180 || (lat === 0 && lng === 0)) {
      return null;
    }
    // Doble candado visual [V5-02]: Explorar muestra exclusivamente sedes activas
    if (isHiddenClub) return null;

    const ringColor = isLight
      ? (isHiddenClub ? '#64748B' : isEvent ? '#059669' : isSpec ? '#7C3AED' : '#0284C7')
      : isHiddenClub
        ? '#94A3B8'
        : pin?.pinColor || (isEvent ? '#00FF66' : isSpec ? '#9D4EDD' : '#00F0FF');
    const titleLower = String(pin?.title || '')
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();
    const hostLower = String(
      pin?.negocio_nombre || pin?.rawEvent?.negocio_nombre || pin?.rawAffiliate?.nombre || ''
    )
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();
    const isOfficial =
      Boolean(pin?.isOfficialKinetic) ||
      titleLower === 'kinetic oficial' ||
      titleLower === 'kinetic sports club' ||
      titleLower === 'kinetic especialista' ||
      titleLower === 'terminal global kinetic' ||
      hostLower === 'kinetic oficial' ||
      hostLower === 'kinetic sports club' ||
      hostLower === 'kinetic especialista' ||
      hostLower === 'terminal global kinetic';
    const showOfficialAssetLogo = Boolean(pin?.useOfficialLogo) || isOfficial;
    const showFallbackSvg = !showOfficialAssetLogo && (!rawCandidate || imgError);
    const svgKind = resolveOfficialPinSvgKind(
      pin?.disciplina || parentClub?.disciplina,
      pin?.title,
      Boolean(pin?.useOfficialLogo) || isOfficial,
      isSpec
    );

    const handleExpoImageLoad = () => {
      WARMED_PIN_URLS.add(effectiveUri);
      if (!hasTriggeredRemountRef.current) {
        hasTriggeredRemountRef.current = true;
        setTracks(true);
        setIconReady(true);
      }
    };

    const handleExpoImageError = () => {
      if (!imgError) {
        setImgError(true);
        hasTriggeredRemountRef.current = false;
        setTracks(true);
      }
    };

    return (
      <Marker
        identifier={pinId}
        coordinate={{
          latitude: lat,
          longitude: lng,
        }}
        anchor={{ x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }}
        tracksViewChanges={tracks}
        zIndex={isHiddenClub ? 4 : isEvent ? 30 : isSpec ? 20 : 10}
        onPress={(e: any) => {
          e?.stopPropagation?.();
          triggerHaptic('Light');
          onSelectPin(pin, isEvent);
        }}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            styles.markerCanvasSafeBox,
            isHiddenClub && { opacity: 0.62 },
            pinScale !== 1 && { transform: [{ scale: pinScale }] },
          ]}
          collapsable={false}
          pointerEvents="none"
        >
          <View
            style={[
              styles.outerHaloRing,
              {
                elevation: 0,
                borderColor: `${ringColor}66`,
                backgroundColor: `${ringColor}24`,
              },
            ]}
            collapsable={false}
          >
            <View
              style={[styles.customMapPin, { borderColor: ringColor }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF' }]}
              collapsable={false}
            >
              <View style={styles.avatarMapPin} collapsable={false}>
                {showOfficialAssetLogo ? (
                  <ExpoImage
                    source={KINETIC_OFFICIAL_PIN_LOGO}
                    style={styles.avatarImage}
                    contentFit="cover"
                    transition={0}
                  />
                ) : showFallbackSvg ? (
                  <KineticOfficialPinVectorSvg kind={svgKind} color={ringColor} size={20} />
                ) : (
                  <ExpoImage
                    source={{ uri: effectiveUri }}
                    recyclingKey={`${pinId}_${imgError ? 'fb' : 'raw'}`}
                    style={styles.avatarImage}
                    contentFit="cover"
                    cachePolicy="memory-disk"
                    priority="high"
                    transition={0}
                    onLoad={handleExpoImageLoad}
                    onError={handleExpoImageError}
                  />
                )}
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
          {isAdminView && isHiddenClub && (
            <View style={styles.pinHiddenMicroPill} collapsable={false}>
              <Text style={styles.pinHiddenMicroText}>
                {tx('OCULTO', 'HIDDEN')}
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      </Marker>
    );
  }
);

import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  Animated,
  Dimensions,
  Easing,
  FlatList,
  Platform,
  RefreshControl,
  ScrollView,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { styles as baseStyles } from '../../styles/tabs/index.styles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { AffiliateCard } from '../AffiliateCard';

const { width } = Dimensions.get('window');
const CARD_WIDTH = Math.round(width * 0.84);
const CARD_GAP = 16;

const ExploreClockwiseBoltBanner = memo(({ visible, isLight }: { visible: boolean; isLight: boolean }) => {
  const { tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const spinAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) {
      spinAnim.setValue(0);
      return;
    }
    const loop = Animated.loop(
      Animated.timing(spinAnim, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 900,
        easing: Easing.linear,
        useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web',
      })
    );
    loop.start();
    return () => loop.stop();
  }, [visible, spinAnim]);

  if (!visible) return null;

  // Sentido horario positivo estricto (manecillas del reloj: 0deg -> 360deg)
  const clockwiseSpin = spinAnim.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1],
    outputRange: ['0deg', '360deg'],
  });

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        baseStyles.refreshBoltBanner,
        isLight && {
          backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderColor: '#06B6D4',
          borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderBottomColor: '#0284C7',
        },
      ]}
    >
      <Animated.View style={{ transform: [{ rotate: clockwiseSpin }] }}>
        <Ionicons name="flash" size={17} color="#00F0FF" />
      </Animated.View>
      <Text
        style={[
          baseStyles.refreshBoltText,
          isLight && { color: '#006B82' },
        ]}
      >
        {tx('SINCRONIZANDO RADAR GPS...', 'SYNCING GPS RADAR...')}
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
});

export interface ExploreVenuesListSectionProps {
  mainScrollRef: React.RefObject<ScrollView | null>;
  refreshing: boolean;
  onPullToRefresh: () => void;
  sectionsByDiscipline: any[];
  isNearMeZone: boolean;
  onViewAllCaboZones: () => void;
  selectedCategories: string[];
  sortBy: string;
  quickChip: string;
  favoriteIds: string[];
  isAdminView: boolean;
  hasRealUserCoords: boolean;
  isAcquiringRealGps: boolean;
  onToggleFavorite: (club: any) => void;
  onFocusClubOnMap: (club: any) => void;
  onSelectClub: (club: any) => void;
  onSelectClase: (cl: any, parentClub: any) => void;
}

export const ExploreVenuesListSection = memo((props: ExploreVenuesListSectionProps) => {
  const {
    mainScrollRef,
    refreshing,
    onPullToRefresh,
    sectionsByDiscipline,
    isNearMeZone,
    onViewAllCaboZones,
    selectedCategories,
    sortBy,
    quickChip,
    favoriteIds,
    isAdminView,
    hasRealUserCoords,
    isAcquiringRealGps,
    onToggleFavorite,
    onFocusClubOnMap,
    onSelectClub,
    onSelectClase,
  } = props;

  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { themeColors, tx, tDisc, language, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
  const styles = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, ['resetZoneBtn', 'resetZoneBtnText']),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );

  const favKeySignature = useMemo(() => favoriteIds.join(','), [favoriteIds]);

  return (
    <ScrollView
      ref={mainScrollRef as any}
      style={{ flex: 1 }}
      contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom + 116, 132) }}
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
      refreshControl={
        <RefreshControl
          refreshing={refreshing}
          onRefresh={onPullToRefresh}
          tintColor="#00F0FF"
          colors={['#00F0FF']}
          progressBackgroundColor={isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#090C12'}
        />
      }
    >
      <ExploreClockwiseBoltBanner visible={refreshing} isLight={isLight} />

      {sectionsByDiscipline.length === 0 ? (
        <View
          style={[
            styles.emptyStateBox,
            isLight && {
              backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
              borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderBottomColor: '#0284C7',
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Feather name="compass" size={28} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text style={[styles.emptyStateTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
            {isNearMeZone
              ? tx(
                  'Sin sedes activas a 10 km a la redonda',
                  'No active venues within a 10 km radius'
                )
              : tx(
                  'Sin afiliados activos en este filtro',
                  'No active venues in this filter'
                )}
          </Text>
          <Text style={[styles.emptyStateSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
            {isNearMeZone
              ? tx(
                  'Tu ubicación GPS actual está fuera del radio de 10 km o no hay sedes cercanas. Explora toda la red oficial en Los Cabos con un toque.',
                  'Your current GPS is outside the 10 km radius or no nearby venues were found. Explore the full Los Cabos network with one tap.'
                )
              : tx(
                  'Amplía el radar a todas las zonas de Los Cabos o explora otra disciplina activa.',
                  'Expand the radar to all Los Cabos zones or explore another active discipline.'
                )}
          </Text>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.resetZoneBtn}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={onViewAllCaboZones}
          >
            <Text style={styles.resetZoneBtnText}>
              {tx('VER TODAS LAS ZONAS DE LOS CABOS', 'VIEW ALL LOS CABOS ZONES')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      ) : (
        sectionsByDiscipline.map((sec: any) => {
          const secTitle = sec.discipline || sec.title;
          const secData = sec.data || sec.items || [];
          return (
            <View key={secTitle} style={styles.sectionBlock}>
              <View style={styles.sectionHeaderRow}>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
                  <Text
                    style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, flexShrink: 1 }]}
                    numberOfLines={1}
                  >
                    {tDisc(secTitle)}
                  </Text>
                  {sec.isTopSearched && (
                    <View
                      style={[
                        styles.trendingMiniBadge,
                        { elevation: 0 },
                        isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#10B981', borderBottomColor: '#059669' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Feather name="trending-up" size={10} color={themeColors.green} />
                      <Text style={[styles.trendingMiniText, { color: themeColors.green }]}>
                        {tx('MÁS BUSCADA', 'TRENDING')}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>
                <Text style={[styles.sectionCountText, { color: themeColors.cyanText, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }]}>
                  {secData.length} {secData.length === 1 ? tx('AFILIADO', 'VENUE') : tx('AFILIADOS', 'VENUES')}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <FlatList
                data={secData}
                extraData={`${sortBy}_${quickChip}_${selectedCategories.join(',')}_${favKeySignature}_${isAdminView}_${language}_${resolvedTheme}_${hasRealUserCoords ? 1 : 0}_${isAcquiringRealGps ? 1 : 0}_${secData.map((x: any) => `${x.id}:${Number(x.rating || 0).toFixed(1)}`).join('|')}`}
                horizontal
                keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
                initialNumToRender={6}
                maxToRenderPerBatch={8}
                windowSize={5}
                removeClippedSubviews={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
                showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
                snapToInterval={CARD_WIDTH + CARD_GAP}
                decelerationRate="fast"
                contentContainerStyle={styles.carouselListContent}
                keyExtractor={(item) => `${secTitle}_${item.id}`}
                renderItem={({ item }) => {
                  const isFavNow =
                    favoriteIds.includes(String(item.id)) ||
                    favoriteIds.includes(String(item.nombre)) ||
                    Boolean(item.isFavorite);
                  return (
                    <View style={{ width: CARD_WIDTH, marginRight: CARD_GAP, alignSelf: 'flex-start' }}>
                      <AffiliateCard
                        affiliate={item}
                        cardWidth={CARD_WIDTH}
                        contextualDiscipline={selectedCategories.length === 1 ? selectedCategories[0] : secTitle}
                        isFavorite={isFavNow}
                        isAdminView={isAdminView}
                        hasRealUserCoords={hasRealUserCoords}
                        isAcquiringRealGps={isAcquiringRealGps}
                        onToggleFavorite={() => onToggleFavorite(item)}
                        onFocusMap={() => onFocusClubOnMap(item)}
                        onSelectClub={() => onSelectClub(item)}
                        onSelectClase={(cl: any) => onSelectClase(cl, item)}
                      />
                    </View>
                  );
                }}
              />
            </View>
          );
        })
      )}
    </ScrollView>
  );
});

import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { ExploreSortOption } from '../../hooks/useExplorar';
import { padZoneCameraRegion, resolveZoneCameraDurationMs } from '../../services/exploreZoneHelpers';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';

export interface IZoneCameraRegion {
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  latitudeDelta: number;
  longitudeDelta: number;
  webZoom: number;
}

export const EXPLORE_ZONE_CAMERA_TARGETS: Record<string, IZoneCameraRegion> = {
  'CABO SAN LUCAS': {
    latitude: 22.8905,
    longitude: -109.9167,
    latitudeDelta: 0.045,
    longitudeDelta: 0.045,
    webZoom: 14,
  },
  'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO': {
    latitude: 22.9750,
    longitude: -109.7800,
    latitudeDelta: 0.095,
    longitudeDelta: 0.095,
    webZoom: 12,
  },
  'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO': {
    latitude: 23.0545,
    longitude: -109.6970,
    latitudeDelta: 0.045,
    longitudeDelta: 0.045,
    webZoom: 14,
  },
  TODAS: {
    latitude: 22.9750,
    longitude: -109.8000,
    latitudeDelta: 0.22,
    longitudeDelta: 0.22,
    webZoom: 11,
  },
  todas: {
    latitude: 22.9750,
    longitude: -109.8000,
    latitudeDelta: 0.22,
    longitudeDelta: 0.22,
    webZoom: 11,
  },
};

export const isNearMeExploreZone = (zone?: string | null): boolean => {
  const z = String(zone || '')
    .toUpperCase()
    .trim();
  return (
    Boolean(z) &&
    (z.includes('CERCA') ||
      z.includes('10 KM') ||
      z.includes('5 KM') ||
      z.includes('NEAR') ||
      z === 'MI GPS ACTUAL')
  );
};

export const isAllCaboExploreZone = (zone?: string | null): boolean => {
  const z = String(zone || '')
    .toUpperCase()
    .trim();
  return (
    !z ||
    z === 'TODAS' ||
    z === 'ALL' ||
    z === 'NINGUNO' ||
    z === 'NONE' ||
    z.includes('TODAS LAS ZONAS') ||
    z.includes('ALL LOS CABOS')
  );
};

export const resolveExploreZoneCamera = (
  zone?: string | null,
  userCoords?: { latitude: number; longitude: number }
): IZoneCameraRegion => {
  if (isAllCaboExploreZone(zone)) return padZoneCameraRegion(EXPLORE_ZONE_CAMERA_TARGETS.TODAS, 1.18);
  const z = String(zone || '')
    .toUpperCase()
    .trim();
  if (z.includes('CORREDOR') || z.includes('CORRIDOR')) {
    return padZoneCameraRegion(EXPLORE_ZONE_CAMERA_TARGETS['CORREDOR TURÍSTICO'], 1.18);
  }
  if (z.includes('SAN JOSÉ') || z.includes('SAN JOSE')) {
    return padZoneCameraRegion(EXPLORE_ZONE_CAMERA_TARGETS['SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO'], 1.18);
  }
  if (z.includes('CABO SAN LUCAS')) {
    return padZoneCameraRegion(EXPLORE_ZONE_CAMERA_TARGETS['CABO SAN LUCAS'], 1.18);
  }
  return padZoneCameraRegion(
    {
      latitude: Number(userCoords?.latitude || 22.8905),
      longitude: Number(userCoords?.longitude || -109.9167),
      latitudeDelta: 0.045,
      longitudeDelta: 0.045,
      webZoom: 14,
    },
    1.18
  );
};

interface ExploreZoneAndSortModalsProps {
  insetsBottom: number;
  sortPickerOpen: boolean;
  setSortPickerOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  zonePickerOpen: boolean;
  setZonePickerOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  sortBy: ExploreSortOption;
  setSortBy: (v: ExploreSortOption) => void;
  selectedZone: string;
  setSelectedZone: (v: string) => void;
  setMapPinPreview: (v: any) => void;
  setWebMapFocus: (v: any) => void;
  mapRef: any;
  centerOnUserGps: () => void;
}

export const ExploreZoneAndSortModals = memo((props: ExploreZoneAndSortModalsProps) => {
  const {
    insetsBottom,
    sortPickerOpen,
    setSortPickerOpen,
    zonePickerOpen,
    setZonePickerOpen,
    sortBy,
    setSortBy,
    selectedZone,
    setSelectedZone,
    setMapPinPreview,
    setWebMapFocus,
    mapRef,
    centerOnUserGps,
  } = props;

  const { tx, language, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
  const sheetBgStyle = isLight ? { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderTopColor: '#CBD5E1' } : undefined;
  const itemBorderStyle = isLight ? { borderBottomColor: '#E2E8F0' } : undefined;

  const sortOptions = useMemo(
    () => [
      { key: 'relevancia' as ExploreSortOption, label: tx('Relevancia', 'Relevance') },
      { key: 'distancia' as ExploreSortOption, label: tx('Más Cercanos (Distancia GPS en km)', 'Nearest (GPS Distance in km)') },
      { key: 'rating_desc' as ExploreSortOption, label: tx('Valoración (Mayor a Menor ★)', 'Rating (Highest to Lowest ★)') },
      { key: 'rating_asc' as ExploreSortOption, label: tx('Valoración (Menor a Mayor ★)', 'Rating (Lowest to Highest ★)') },
      { key: 'disponibilidad' as ExploreSortOption, label: tx('Mayor Disponibilidad de Clases / Citas', 'Most Available Classes / Sessions') },
      { key: 'precio_asc' as ExploreSortOption, label: tx('Precio (Menor a Mayor KC)', 'Price (Lowest to Highest KC)') },
      { key: 'precio_desc' as ExploreSortOption, label: tx('Precio (Mayor a Menor KC)', 'Price (Highest to Lowest KC)') },
      { key: 'az' as ExploreSortOption, label: tx('Alfabético (A-Z)', 'Alphabetical (A-Z)') },
      { key: 'za' as ExploreSortOption, label: tx('Alfabético (Z-A)', 'Alphabetical (Z-A)') },
    ],
    [tx, language]
  );

  const zoneOptions = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        id: 'all_cabos',
        zoneKey: 'todas',
        label: tx('🌎 Todas las zonas de Los Cabos', '🌎 All Los Cabos Zones'),
        isSelected: isAllCaboExploreZone(selectedZone),
      },
      {
        id: 'near_me',
        zoneKey: 'Cerca de mí (10 km a la redonda)',
        label: tx('📍 Cerca de mí (radio 10 km)', '📍 Near me (10 km radius)'),
        isSelected: isNearMeExploreZone(selectedZone),
      },
      {
        id: 'csl',
        zoneKey: 'CABO SAN LUCAS',
        label: 'Cabo San Lucas',
        isSelected: String(selectedZone || '').toUpperCase().trim() === 'CABO SAN LUCAS',
      },
      {
        id: 'corredor',
        zoneKey: 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO',
        label: tx('Corredor Turístico', 'Tourist Corridor'),
        isSelected: String(selectedZone || '').toUpperCase().trim().includes('CORREDOR'),
      },
      {
        id: 'sjc',
        zoneKey: 'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO',
        label: 'San José del Cabo',
        isSelected:
          String(selectedZone || '').toUpperCase().trim().includes('SAN JOSÉ') ||
          String(selectedZone || '').toUpperCase().trim().includes('SAN JOSE'),
      },
    ],
    [selectedZone, tx, language]
  );

  const handleSelectZoneOption = (zoneKey: string, id: string) => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setSelectedZone(zoneKey);
    setZonePickerOpen(false);
    setMapPinPreview(null);

    if (id === 'near_me') {
      centerOnUserGps();
      return;
    }

    const rawCam = isAllCaboExploreZone(zoneKey)
      ? EXPLORE_ZONE_CAMERA_TARGETS.TODAS
      : EXPLORE_ZONE_CAMERA_TARGETS[zoneKey];
    if (!rawCam) return;
    const cam = padZoneCameraRegion(rawCam, 1.18);
    const prevCam = resolveExploreZoneCamera(selectedZone);
    const animMs = resolveZoneCameraDurationMs(prevCam, cam);

    setWebMapFocus({
      lat: cam.latitude,
      lng: cam.longitude,
      zoom: cam.webZoom,
      ts: Date.now(),
    });

    if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
      mapRef.current?.animateToRegion?.(
        {
          latitude: cam.latitude,
          longitude: cam.longitude,
          latitudeDelta: cam.latitudeDelta,
          longitudeDelta: cam.longitudeDelta,
        },
        animMs
      );
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={sortPickerOpen}
        onBackdropPress={() => setSortPickerOpen(false)}
        onBackButtonPress={() => setSortPickerOpen(false)}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.55 : 0.88}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
        backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        animationInTiming={200}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      >
        <View style={[globalStyles.bottomSheet, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, { paddingBottom: insetsBottom + 16 }, sheetBgStyle]}>
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={() => setSortPickerOpen(false)} />
          <Text style={[localStyles.pickerTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
            {tx('Ordenar Directorio', 'Sort Directory')}
          </Text>
          <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
            {sortOptions.map((opt) => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={opt.key}
                style={[localStyles.pickerItem, itemBorderStyle]}
                onPress={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('Selection');
                  setSortBy(opt.key);
                  setSortPickerOpen(false);
                }}
              >
                <Text
                  style={[
                    localStyles.pickerItemText,
                    { color: themeColors.textPrimary },
                    sortBy === opt.key && { color: themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900' },
                  ]}
                >
                  {opt.label}
                </Text>
                {sortBy === opt.key && <Feather name="check" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </ReactNativeModal>

      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={zonePickerOpen}
        onBackdropPress={() => setZonePickerOpen(false)}
        onBackButtonPress={() => setZonePickerOpen(false)}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.55 : 0.88}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
        backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        animationInTiming={200}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      >
        <View style={[globalStyles.bottomSheet, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, { paddingBottom: insetsBottom + 16 }, sheetBgStyle]}>
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={() => setZonePickerOpen(false)} />
          <Text style={[localStyles.pickerTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
            {tx('Zona del Radar GPS', 'GPS Radar Zone')}
          </Text>
          {zoneOptions.map((z) => (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={z.id}
              style={[localStyles.pickerItem, itemBorderStyle]}
              onPress={() => handleSelectZoneOption(z.zoneKey, z.id)}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  localStyles.pickerItemText,
                  { color: themeColors.textPrimary },
                  z.isSelected && { color: themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900' },
                ]}
              >
                {z.label}
              </Text>
              {z.isSelected && <Feather name="check" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />}
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ))}
        </View>
      </ReactNativeModal>
    </>
  );
});

const localStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  pickerTitle: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '900', textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 10 },
  pickerItem: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingVertical: 12,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderBottomColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)',
  },
  pickerItemText: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '600', flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 },
});

import React, { memo } from 'react';
import Svg, { Circle, Line, Path, Polygon, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';

export type OfficialPinSvgKind =
  | 'kinetic'
  | 'strength'
  | 'pilates'
  | 'yoga'
  | 'combat'
  | 'racket'
  | 'cycling'
  | 'aquatic'
  | 'physio';

const cleanColorHex = (c?: string): string => {
  const s = String(c || '').trim();
  return /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}$/.test(s) ? s : '#00F0FF';
};

export const resolveOfficialPinSvgKind = (
  disciplina?: string,
  title?: string,
  isOfficial = false,
  isSpecialist = false
): OfficialPinSvgKind => {
  if (isOfficial) return 'kinetic';
  const txt = `${disciplina || ''} ${title || ''}`.toLowerCase();
  if (txt.includes('pilates') || txt.includes('reformer') || txt.includes('barre')) return 'pilates';
  if (txt.includes('yoga') || txt.includes('medit') || txt.includes('breath') || txt.includes('mindful')) return 'yoga';
  if (txt.includes('box') || txt.includes('mma') || txt.includes('muay') || txt.includes('jiu') || txt.includes('kick') || txt.includes('combat')) return 'combat';
  if (txt.includes('padel') || txt.includes('pádel') || txt.includes('tenis') || txt.includes('tennis') || txt.includes('pickleball')) return 'racket';
  if (txt.includes('cycling') || txt.includes('spinning') || txt.includes('bici') || txt.includes('bike') || txt.includes('triat')) return 'cycling';
  if (txt.includes('surf') || txt.includes('swim') || txt.includes('nataci') || txt.includes('aqua') || txt.includes('paddle') || txt.includes('sup')) return 'aquatic';
  if (
    isSpecialist ||
    txt.includes('fisio') ||
    txt.includes('physio') ||
    txt.includes('quiro') ||
    txt.includes('chiro') ||
    txt.includes('rehab') ||
    txt.includes('nutri') ||
    txt.includes('masaje') ||
    txt.includes('spa') ||
    txt.includes('wellness')
  ) {
    return 'physio';
  }
  return 'strength';
};

    );
  }
);

export const buildOfficialPinSvgMarkup = (
  kind: OfficialPinSvgKind,
  colorHex = '#00F0FF',
  sizePx = 19
): string => {
  const c = cleanColorHex(colorHex);
  const sz = Math.max(12, Math.min(36, Math.round(Number(sizePx) || 19)));
  const bodyByKind: Record<OfficialPinSvgKind, string> = {
    kinetic: `<polygon points="13,2 4,14 11.5,14 10.5,22 20,10 12.5,10" fill="${c}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.2" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`,
    strength: `<line x1="6" y1="12" x2="18" y2="12" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round"/><rect x="3" y="8" width="3" height="8" rx="1.2" fill="${c}"/><rect x="18" y="8" width="3" height="8" rx="1.2" fill="${c}"/><rect x="6.5" y="9.5" width="2" height="5" rx="0.8" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.4"/><rect x="15.5" y="9.5" width="2" height="5" rx="0.8" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.4"/>`,
    pilates: `<rect x="3" y="9" width="18" height="6" rx="3" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.8"/><circle cx="8" cy="12" r="2" fill="${c}"/><circle cx="16" cy="12" r="2" fill="${c}"/><line x1="10" y1="12" x2="14" y2="12" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.6"/>`,
    yoga: `<path d="M12 4C14.8 7.6 15.6 11.4 12 16C8.4 11.4 9.2 7.6 12 4Z" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.8" fill="${c}28" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="M4.5 11.5C7.6 10.6 10.2 12.4 11.6 16C8.2 16.4 5.8 14.8 4.5 11.5Z" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.6"/><path d="M19.5 11.5C16.4 10.6 13.8 12.4 12.4 16C15.8 16.4 18.2 14.8 19.5 11.5Z" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.6"/><line x1="6" y1="19" x2="18" y2="19" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/>`,
    combat: `<polygon points="12,3 19.5,6.8 18.2,14.5 12,21 5.8,14.5 4.5,6.8" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.8" fill="${c}22"/><path d="M9.2 10.5L14.8 14.5M14.8 10.5L9.2 14.5" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round"/>`,
    racket: `<circle cx="10.5" cy="9.5" r="5.5" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.9" fill="${c}20"/><line x1="14.5" y1="13.5" x2="20" y2="19" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round"/><circle cx="18.5" cy="6.5" r="2" fill="${c}"/>`,
    cycling: `<circle cx="6.5" cy="15.5" r="3.5" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.8"/><circle cx="17.5" cy="15.5" r="3.5" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.8"/><path d="M6.5 15.5L10.5 9.5H15L17.5 15.5M10.5 9.5L12.5 15.5" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`,
    aquatic: `<path d="M3 10C5.2 8 7.8 8 10 10C12.2 12 14.8 12 17 10C18.5 8.6 19.8 8.4 21 9.2" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M3 15.5C5.2 13.5 7.8 13.5 10 15.5C12.2 17.5 14.8 17.5 17 15.5C18.5 14.1 19.8 13.9 21 14.7" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/>`,
    physio: `<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8.5" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.8" fill="${c}1E"/><line x1="12" y1="7.5" x2="12" y2="16.5" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round"/><line x1="7.5" y1="12" x2="16.5" y2="12" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round"/>`,
  };
  return `<svg width="${sz}" height="${sz}" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none">${bodyByKind[kind] || bodyByKind.kinetic}</svg>`;
};
