import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Platform, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { isClubStrictlyActiveUI } from './ExploreRadarMapPin';
import { resolveOfficialPinSvgKind } from './kineticOfficialRadarSvgs';
import {
  buildKineticFallbackRasterStyle,
  buildKineticMapLibreStyle,
  buildPinMarkerHtml,
  buildUserMarkerHtml,
  ensureLeafletEngine,
  ensureMapLibreEngine,
  safeHex,
  safeImgUrl,
} from '../../utils/kineticWebMapEngine';

export const KineticWebInteractiveMap = memo(
  ({
    markers,
    userCoords,
    focusTarget,
    isAdminView = false,
    onSelectPin,
    onPressUserPin,
    onDismissPreview,
  }: {
    markers: any[];
    userCoords: { latitude: number; longitude: number };
    focusTarget: { lat: number; lng: number; zoom?: number; offset?: [number, number]; ts: number } | null;
    isAdminView?: boolean;
    onSelectPin: (item: any, isEvent: boolean) => void;
    onPressUserPin: () => void;
    onDismissPreview: () => void;
  }) => {
    const { language, tx, isLightMode, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const userLabel = language === 'en' ? 'YOU' : 'TÚ';
    const hiddenLabel = language === 'en' ? 'HIDDEN' : 'OCULTO';

    const hostRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
    const engineRef = useRef<'maplibre' | 'leaflet' | null>(null);
    const mapInstanceRef = useRef<any>(null);
    const tileLayerRef = useRef<any>(null);
    const pinsLayerRef = useRef<any>(null);
    const mlMarkersRef = useRef<any[]>([]);
    const userMarkerRef = useRef<any>(null);
    const lastStyleLightRef = useRef<boolean | null>(null);
    const usingFallbackTilesRef = useRef(false);
    const styleFallbackAppliedRef = useRef(false);
    const callbacksRef = useRef({ onSelectPin, onPressUserPin, onDismissPreview });
    callbacksRef.current = { onSelectPin, onPressUserPin, onDismissPreview };

    const [mapMountId, setMapMountId] = useState<number>(0);

    const preparedPins = useMemo(() => {
      return (markers || [])
        .map((m, idx) => {
          const mType = String(m?.markerType || '').toLowerCase();
          const isEv = mType === 'event' || mType === 'evento';
          const isSpec = mType === 'specialist' || mType === 'especialista';
          const parentClub = m?.rawAffiliate ?? (!isEv ? m : null);
          const isHidden = parentClub ? !isClubStrictlyActiveUI(parentClub) : false;
          if (isHidden) return null;

          if (m?.latitude === null || m?.latitude === undefined || m?.latitude === '' || m?.longitude === null || m?.longitude === undefined || m?.longitude === '') return null;
          const lat = Number(m.latitude);
          const lng = Number(m.longitude);
          if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lng) || Math.abs(lat) > 90 || Math.abs(lng) > 180 || (lat === 0 && lng === 0)) return null;

          const titleLower = String(m?.title || '').trim().toLowerCase();
          const hostLower = String(
            m?.negocio_nombre || m?.rawEvent?.negocio_nombre || m?.rawAffiliate?.nombre || ''
          )
            .trim()
            .toLowerCase();
          const isOfficial =
            Boolean(m?.isOfficialKinetic) ||
            titleLower === 'kinetic oficial' ||
            titleLower === 'kinetic sports club' ||
            titleLower === 'kinetic especialista' ||
            titleLower === 'terminal global kinetic' ||
            hostLower === 'kinetic oficial' ||
            hostLower === 'kinetic sports club' ||
            hostLower === 'kinetic especialista' ||
            hostLower === 'terminal global kinetic';
          const rawUrl = String(m?.avatar_url || '').trim();
          const useOfficialAssetLogo = Boolean(m?.useOfficialLogo) || isOfficial;
          const defaultColor = isHidden
            ? isLightMode ? '#64748B' : '#94A3B8'
            : isLightMode
              ? isEv ? '#059669' : isSpec ? '#7C3AED' : '#0284C7'
              : m?.pinColor || (isEv ? '#00FF66' : isSpec ? '#9D4EDD' : '#00F0FF');

          return {
            id: String(m?.id || `pin_${idx}`),
            raw: m,
            isEv,
            lat,
            lng,
            color: safeHex(defaultColor),
            avatar: useOfficialAssetLogo ? '' : safeImgUrl(rawUrl),
            isOfficial: useOfficialAssetLogo,
            isHidden,
            svgKind: resolveOfficialPinSvgKind(
              m?.disciplina || parentClub?.disciplina,
              m?.title,
              isOfficial,
              isSpec
            ),
          };
        })
        .filter(Boolean) as any[];
    }, [markers, isAdminView, isLightMode]);

    // 1. Montaje primario MapLibre GL 60fps + Respaldo automático Leaflet
    useEffect(() => {
      if (Platform.OS !== 'web') return;
      let cancelled = false;
      let rafId = 0;
      let resizeObs: ResizeObserver | null = null;
      const timers: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[] = [];

      const initLat = Number(focusTarget?.lat || userCoords.latitude || 22.8905);
      const initLng = Number(focusTarget?.lng || userCoords.longitude || -109.9167);
      const initZoom = Number(focusTarget?.zoom || 12.6);

      const bootLeafletFallback = () =>
        ensureLeafletEngine().then((L) => {
          if (cancelled || !hostRef.current) return;
          engineRef.current = 'leaflet';
          const map = L.map(hostRef.current, {
            center: [initLat, initLng],
            zoom: Math.round(initZoom),
            zoomControl: false,
            attributionControl: false,
            tap: false,
          });
          map.on('click', () => callbacksRef.current.onDismissPreview());
          pinsLayerRef.current = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
          mapInstanceRef.current = map;
          setMapMountId((n) => n + 1);
          const forceMeasure = () => {
            try { map.invalidateSize({ pan: false }); } catch {}
          };
          rafId = requestAnimationFrame(forceMeasure);
          timers.push(setTimeout(forceMeasure, 120), setTimeout(forceMeasure, 420));
          if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined' && hostRef.current) {
            resizeObs = new ResizeObserver(forceMeasure);
            resizeObs.observe(hostRef.current);
          }
        });

      ensureMapLibreEngine()
        .then((ml) => {
          if (cancelled || !hostRef.current) return;
          engineRef.current = 'maplibre';
          styleFallbackAppliedRef.current = false;
          lastStyleLightRef.current = Boolean(isLightMode);
          const map = new ml.Map({
            container: hostRef.current,
            style: buildKineticMapLibreStyle(Boolean(isLightMode)),
            center: [initLng, initLat],
            zoom: initZoom,
            minZoom: 8.5,
            maxZoom: 19.5,
            attributionControl: false,
            dragRotate: false,
            pitchWithRotate: false,
          });
          let styleErrCount = 0;
          map.on('error', () => {
            styleErrCount += 1;
            if (styleErrCount >= 2 && !styleFallbackAppliedRef.current && mapInstanceRef.current === map) {
              styleFallbackAppliedRef.current = true;
              if (hostRef.current) {
                hostRef.current.classList.toggle('knt-osm-dark', !lastStyleLightRef.current);
              }
              try {
                map.setStyle(buildKineticFallbackRasterStyle());
              } catch {}
            }
          });
          map.on('click', () => callbacksRef.current.onDismissPreview());
          mapInstanceRef.current = map;
          setMapMountId((n) => n + 1);
          const forceResize = () => {
            try { map.resize(); } catch {}
          };
          rafId = requestAnimationFrame(forceResize);
          timers.push(setTimeout(forceResize, 120), setTimeout(forceResize, 420));
          if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined' && hostRef.current) {
            resizeObs = new ResizeObserver(forceResize);
            resizeObs.observe(hostRef.current);
          }
        })
        .catch(() => bootLeafletFallback().catch(() => {}));

      return () => {
        cancelled = true;
        if (rafId) cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
        timers.forEach(clearTimeout);
        resizeObs?.disconnect();
        mlMarkersRef.current.forEach((m) => { try { m.remove(); } catch {} });
        mlMarkersRef.current = [];
        try { userMarkerRef.current?.remove?.(); } catch {}
        userMarkerRef.current = null;
        if (mapInstanceRef.current) {
          try { mapInstanceRef.current.remove(); } catch {}
          mapInstanceRef.current = null;
          tileLayerRef.current = null;
          pinsLayerRef.current = null;
          engineRef.current = null;
        }
      };
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, []);

    // 2. Sincronización de tema Claro / Oscuro e idioma en tiles
    useEffect(() => {
      if (Platform.OS !== 'web' || mapMountId === 0 || !mapInstanceRef.current) return;
      const map = mapInstanceRef.current;
      const hostEl = hostRef.current;
      if (hostEl) {
        hostEl.classList.toggle('knt-light-pins', Boolean(isLightMode));
        hostEl.classList.toggle('knt-google-dark', !isLightMode && engineRef.current === 'leaflet');
        hostEl.classList.toggle('knt-osm-dark', !isLightMode && engineRef.current === 'maplibre' && styleFallbackAppliedRef.current);
      }

      if (engineRef.current === 'maplibre') {
        if (lastStyleLightRef.current !== Boolean(isLightMode)) {
          lastStyleLightRef.current = Boolean(isLightMode);
          try {
            map.setStyle(
              styleFallbackAppliedRef.current
                ? buildKineticFallbackRasterStyle()
                : buildKineticMapLibreStyle(Boolean(isLightMode))
            );
          } catch {}
        }
        return;
      }

      const L = (window as any).L;
      if (!L) return;
      if (tileLayerRef.current) {
        try { map.removeLayer(tileLayerRef.current); } catch {}
        tileLayerRef.current = null;
      }
      usingFallbackTilesRef.current = false;
      const osmTilesUrl = 'https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png';
      let errCount = 0;
      const tileOpts = { maxZoom: 20, maxNativeZoom: 19, minZoom: 9, keepBuffer: 6, updateWhenIdle: false, updateWhenZooming: true };
      const primaryLayer = L.tileLayer(osmTilesUrl, tileOpts);
      primaryLayer.on('tileerror', () => {
        errCount += 1;
        if (errCount >= 4 && !usingFallbackTilesRef.current && mapInstanceRef.current === map) {
          usingFallbackTilesRef.current = true;
          try { map.removeLayer(primaryLayer); } catch {}
          tileLayerRef.current = L.tileLayer('https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', tileOpts).addTo(map);
        }
      });
      primaryLayer.addTo(map);
      tileLayerRef.current = primaryLayer;
    }, [mapMountId, isLightMode, language]);

    // 3. Renderizado reactivo de pines (MapLibre GL DOM markers o Leaflet fallback) + clustering
    useEffect(() => {
      if (Platform.OS !== 'web' || mapMountId === 0 || !mapInstanceRef.current) return;
      const map = mapInstanceRef.current;
      const uLat = Number(userCoords.latitude || 22.8905);
      const uLng = Number(userCoords.longitude || -109.9167);
      const userHtml = buildUserMarkerHtml(userLabel);

      if (engineRef.current === 'maplibre') {
        const ml = (window as any).maplibregl;
        if (!ml) return;
        try { userMarkerRef.current?.remove?.(); } catch {}
        const uEl = document.createElement('div');
        uEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        uEl.innerHTML = userHtml;
        userMarkerRef.current = new ml.Marker({ element: uEl, anchor: 'center' })
          .setLngLat([uLng, uLat])
          .addTo(map);

        const clearMlPins = () => {
          mlMarkersRef.current.forEach((m) => { try { m.remove(); } catch {} });
          mlMarkersRef.current = [];
        };

        const renderMlPins = () => {
          if (!mapInstanceRef.current) return;
          clearMlPins();
          const zoom = typeof map.getZoom === 'function' ? Number(map.getZoom()) : 12.6;
          const pinScale = Math.max(0.78, Math.min(1.14, 0.78 + (zoom - 10) * 0.06)).toFixed(2);
          const bounds = typeof map.getBounds === 'function' ? map.getBounds() : null;
          const inView = bounds
            ? preparedPins.filter((p) => {
                const sw = bounds.getSouthWest();
                const ne = bounds.getNorthEast();
                const latPad = (ne.lat - sw.lat) * 0.35;
                const lngPad = (ne.lng - sw.lng) * 0.35;
                return p.lat >= sw.lat - latPad && p.lat <= ne.lat + latPad && p.lng >= sw.lng - lngPad && p.lng <= ne.lng + lngPad;
              })
            : preparedPins;

          const addSingle = (p: any) => {
            const el = document.createElement('div');
            el.style.zIndex = p.isHidden ? '4' : p.isEv ? '30' : '15';
            el.innerHTML = buildPinMarkerHtml(p, pinScale, hiddenLabel);
            el.onclick = (ev: MouseEvent) => {
              ev.stopPropagation();
              triggerHaptic('Light');
              callbacksRef.current.onSelectPin(p.raw, p.isEv);
            };
            const mk = new ml.Marker({ element: el, anchor: 'center' }).setLngLat([p.lng, p.lat]).addTo(map);
            mlMarkersRef.current.push(mk);
          };

          if (zoom <= 12.5 && inView.length > 1) {
            const cellSize = 0.018 * Math.pow(2, Math.max(0, 12.5 - zoom));
            const buckets = new Map<string, any[]>();
            inView.forEach((p) => {
              const key = `${Math.round(p.lat / cellSize)}_${Math.round(p.lng / cellSize)}`;
              const arr = buckets.get(key);
              if (arr) arr.push(p);
              else buckets.set(key, [p]);
            });
            buckets.forEach((group) => {
              if (group.length < 2) { addSingle(group[0]); return; }
              const cLat = group.reduce((s, x) => s + x.lat, 0) / group.length;
              const cLng = group.reduce((s, x) => s + x.lng, 0) / group.length;
              const cEl = document.createElement('div');
              cEl.innerHTML = `<div class="knt-cluster-pin" style="transform:scale(${pinScale});">+${group.length}</div>`;
              cEl.onclick = (ev: MouseEvent) => {
                ev.stopPropagation();
                triggerHaptic('Light');
                map.flyTo({ center: [cLng, cLat], zoom: Math.min(18.5, zoom + 2.2), speed: 1.4 });
              };
              mlMarkersRef.current.push(new ml.Marker({ element: cEl, anchor: 'center' }).setLngLat([cLng, cLat]).addTo(map));
            });
            return;
          }
          inView.forEach(addSingle);
        };

        renderMlPins();
        map.on('moveend', renderMlPins);
        map.on('zoomend', renderMlPins);
        return () => {
          clearMlPins();
          try { map.off('moveend', renderMlPins); map.off('zoomend', renderMlPins); } catch {}
        };
      }

      const L = (window as any).L;
      const pinsLayer = pinsLayerRef.current;
      if (!L || !pinsLayer) return;
      if (userMarkerRef.current) {
        try { map.removeLayer(userMarkerRef.current); } catch {}
        userMarkerRef.current = null;
      }
      const uMarker = L.marker([uLat, uLng], {
        icon: L.divIcon({ html: userHtml, className: '', iconSize: [42, 52], iconAnchor: [21, 26] }),
        zIndexOffset: 900,
        interactive: false,
        bubblingMouseEvents: false,
      }).addTo(map);
      userMarkerRef.current = uMarker;

      const renderLeafletPins = () => {
        if (!pinsLayerRef.current || !mapInstanceRef.current) return;
        pinsLayer.clearLayers();
        const zoom = typeof map.getZoom === 'function' ? Number(map.getZoom()) : 13;
        const pinScale = Math.max(0.78, Math.min(1.14, 0.78 + (zoom - 10) * 0.06)).toFixed(2);
        const padded = typeof map.getBounds === 'function' ? map.getBounds()?.pad?.(0.35) : null;
        const inView = padded ? preparedPins.filter((p) => padded.contains([p.lat, p.lng])) : preparedPins;
        inView.forEach((p) => {
          const marker = L.marker([p.lat, p.lng], {
            icon: L.divIcon({ html: buildPinMarkerHtml(p, pinScale, hiddenLabel), className: '', iconSize: [52, p.isHidden ? 58 : 44], iconAnchor: [26, 22] }),
            zIndexOffset: p.isHidden ? 40 : p.isEv ? 300 : 150,
            interactive: true,
            bubblingMouseEvents: false,
          }).addTo(pinsLayer);
          marker.on('click', (ev: any) => {
            L.DomEvent.stopPropagation(ev);
            triggerHaptic('Light');
            callbacksRef.current.onSelectPin(p.raw, p.isEv);
          });
        });
      };

      renderLeafletPins();
      map.on('moveend', renderLeafletPins);
      map.on('zoomend', renderLeafletPins);
      return () => {
        try { map.off('moveend', renderLeafletPins); map.off('zoomend', renderLeafletPins); } catch {}
      };
    }, [mapMountId, preparedPins, userCoords.latitude, userCoords.longitude, userLabel, hiddenLabel]);

    // 4. Vuelo suave de cámara
    useEffect(() => {
      if (Platform.OS !== 'web' || mapMountId === 0 || !mapInstanceRef.current || !focusTarget) return;
      const lat = Number(focusTarget.lat);
      const lng = Number(focusTarget.lng);
      if (Number.isNaN(lat) || Number.isNaN(lng)) return;
      const timers: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[] = [];
      try {
        if (engineRef.current === 'maplibre') {
          mapInstanceRef.current.flyTo({
            center: [lng, lat],
            zoom: focusTarget.zoom || 16.2,
            offset: focusTarget.offset || [0, 0],
            speed: 1.45,
            essential: true,
          });
        } else {
          const L = (window as any).L;
          const targetZoom = focusTarget.zoom || 17;
          let targetLatLng: [number, number] = [lat, lng];
          if (L && Array.isArray(focusTarget.offset) && (focusTarget.offset[0] || focusTarget.offset[1])) {
            const pt = mapInstanceRef.current.project([lat, lng], targetZoom);
            const shifted = pt.subtract(L.point(focusTarget.offset[0] || 0, focusTarget.offset[1] || 0));
            const ll = mapInstanceRef.current.unproject(shifted, targetZoom);
            if (ll && Number.isFinite(ll.lat) && Number.isFinite(ll.lng)) {
              targetLatLng = [ll.lat, ll.lng];
            }
          }
          mapInstanceRef.current.stop?.();
          mapInstanceRef.current.invalidateSize({ pan: false });
          mapInstanceRef.current.setView(targetLatLng, targetZoom, { animate: true, duration: 0.35 });
          timers.push(setTimeout(() => { try { mapInstanceRef.current?.invalidateSize({ pan: false }); } catch {} }, 180));
        }
      } catch {}
      return () => { timers.forEach(clearTimeout); };
    }, [mapMountId, focusTarget]);

    if (Platform.OS !== 'web') return null;

    return (
      <View style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, { zIndex: 1, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#E2E8F0' : '#080C12' }]}>
        {React.createElement('div' as any, {
          ref: hostRef,
          className: 'knt-web-maplibre-host knt-web-leaflet-host',
          'data-kinetic-on-media': 'true',
          style: {
            position: 'absolute',
            top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0,
            width: '100%', height: '100%',
            backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#E2E8F0' : '#080C12',
            touchAction: 'none', overscrollBehavior: 'contain', zIndex: 1,
          },
        })}
        {mapMountId === 0 && (
          <View pointerEvents="none" style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, { alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 8, zIndex: 2 }]}>
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 1 }}>
              {tx('INICIALIZANDO MAPLIBRE 60FPS...', 'BOOTING MAPLIBRE 60FPS...')}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/FastMarker.tsx

    // === src/components/FastMarker.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Platform,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports
const NativeMaps = Platform.OS !== 'web' ? require('react-native-maps') : null;
const Marker: any = NativeMaps ? NativeMaps.Marker : View;
import { resolveCategoryImage } from '../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';

const KINETIC_LOGO = require('../../assets/logo.png');

interface FastMarkerProps {
  m: any;
  onSelectClase?: (item: any) => void;
  onSelectClub?: (club: any) => void;
  isFocused?: boolean;
  layerHash?: string;
}

export const resolveMarkerTricolor = (m: any): string => {
  const markerType = String(m?.markerType || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (markerType === 'evento' || markerType === 'event') {
    return '#00FF66';
  }
  if (markerType === 'especialista' || markerType === 'specialist') {
    return '#9D4EDD';
  }
  if (markerType === 'club') {
    return '#00F0FF';
  }
  if (m?.pinColor && typeof m.pinColor === 'string') {
    return m.pinColor;
  }
  const cat = String(m?.categoria || m?.rol || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (cat.includes('especialista')) {
    return '#9D4EDD';
  }
  return '#00F0FF';
};

export const FastMarker = memo(
  ({
    m,
    onSelectClase,
    onSelectClub,
    isFocused = false,
    layerHash = '',
  }: FastMarkerProps) => {
    const { resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const [tracksViewChanges, setTracksViewChanges] = useState(true);
    const [imgError, setImgError] = useState(false);
    const freezeTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

    const latestPropsRef = useRef({ m, onSelectClase, onSelectClub });
    latestPropsRef.current = { m, onSelectClase, onSelectClub };

    const scheduleFreeze = useCallback((delayMs = 450) => {
      if (freezeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(freezeTimerRef.current);
      freezeTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        setTracksViewChanges(false);
      }, delayMs);
    }, []);

    const pinColor = resolveMarkerTricolor(m);

    useEffect(() => {
      setImgError(false);
      setTracksViewChanges(true);
      scheduleFreeze(600);
      return () => {
        if (freezeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(freezeTimerRef.current);
      };
    }, [
      isFocused,
      layerHash,
      m?.id,
      m?.markerType,
      m?.avatar_url,
      m?.portada_url,
      pinColor,
      m?.estado,
      scheduleFreeze,
    ]);

    const handleImageSettled = useCallback(() => {
      scheduleFreeze(220);
    }, [scheduleFreeze]);

    const handleImageError = useCallback(() => {
      setImgError(true);
      scheduleFreeze(220);
    }, [scheduleFreeze]);

    const lat = Number(
      m?.latitude ?? m?.coords?.latitude ?? m?.latitud ?? 22.8905
    );
    const lng = Number(
      m?.longitude ?? m?.coords?.longitude ?? m?.longitud ?? -109.9167
    );

    if (
      Number.isNaN(lat) ||
      Number.isNaN(lng) ||
      lat < -90 ||
      lat > 90 ||
      lng < -180 ||
      lng > 180 ||
      (lat === 0 && lng === 0)
    ) {
      return null;
    }

    const markerTypeLower = String(m?.markerType || '')
      .toLowerCase()
      .trim();
    const isEvent =
      markerTypeLower === 'evento' || markerTypeLower === 'event';
    const isEspecialista = pinColor === '#9D4EDD';

    const titleStr = String(
      m?.title || m?.nombre_servicio || m?.nombre || ''
    )
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();
    const isOfficialKinetic =
      titleStr === 'kinetic oficial' || titleStr === 'kinetic sports club';

    const fallbackUri = resolveCategoryImage(
      m?.categoria_real || m?.categoria || m?.disciplina,
      m?.title || m?.nombre_servicio || m?.nombre
    );

    const candidateUri =
      m?.avatar_url && String(m.avatar_url).trim() !== ''
        ? String(m.avatar_url).trim()
        : m?.logo_url && String(m.logo_url).trim() !== ''
        ? String(m.logo_url).trim()
        : m?.portada_url && String(m.portada_url).trim() !== ''
        ? String(m.portada_url).trim()
        : fallbackUri;

    const finalAvatarUri =
      !imgError && candidateUri ? candidateUri : fallbackUri;

    const handleMarkerPress = useCallback((e?: any) => {
      if (e?.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation();
      triggerHaptic('Light');

      const current = latestPropsRef.current;
      const itemData = current.m;
      if (!itemData) return;

      const typeLower = String(itemData?.markerType || '')
        .toLowerCase()
        .trim();
      const itemIsEvent = typeLower === 'evento' || typeLower === 'event';

      if (itemIsEvent && current.onSelectClase) {
        current.onSelectClase(itemData.rawEvent || itemData);
      } else if (current.onSelectClub) {
        current.onSelectClub(itemData.rawAffiliate || itemData);
      } else if (current.onSelectClase) {
        current.onSelectClase(itemData.rawEvent || itemData);
      }
    }, []);

    const markerId = `${String(m?.markerType || 'pin')}-${String(
      m?.id || `${lat}-${lng}`
    )}`;
    const markerZIndex = isFocused
      ? 99
      : isEvent
      ? 30
      : isEspecialista
      ? 20
      : 10;

    return (
      <Marker
        identifier={markerId}
        coordinate={{ latitude: lat, longitude: lng }}
        anchor={{ x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }}
        tracksViewChanges={tracksViewChanges}
        stopPropagation={true}
        onPress={handleMarkerPress}
        zIndex={markerZIndex}
      >
        <View
          style={styles.markerCanvasSafeBox}
          collapsable={false}
          pointerEvents="none"
        >
          <View
            style={[
              styles.outerHaloRing,
              {
                borderColor: `${pinColor}55`,
                backgroundColor: `${pinColor}20`,
              },
            ]}
            collapsable={false}
          >
            <View
              style={[
                styles.customMapPin,
                {
                  borderColor: pinColor,
                },
              ]}
              collapsable={false}
            >
              <View style={styles.avatarMapPin} collapsable={false}>
                {isOfficialKinetic && !m?.avatar_url ? (
                  <Image
                    source={KINETIC_LOGO}
                    style={styles.avatarImage}
                    contentFit="cover"
                    onLoad={handleImageSettled}
                  />
                ) : finalAvatarUri ? (
                  <Image
                    source={{ uri: finalAvatarUri }}
                    recyclingKey={markerId}
                    style={styles.avatarImage}
                    contentFit="cover"
                    cachePolicy="memory-disk"
                    onLoad={handleImageSettled}
                    onError={handleImageError}
                  />
                ) : (
                  <Feather
                    name={
                      isEvent ? 'zap' : isEspecialista ? 'user' : 'shield'
                    }
                    size={16}
                    color={pinColor}
                  />
                )}
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Marker>
    );
  },
  (prev, next) =>
    prev.m?.id === next.m?.id &&
    prev.isFocused === next.isFocused &&
    prev.layerHash === next.layerHash &&
    prev.m?.markerType === next.m?.markerType &&
    prev.m?.categoria === next.m?.categoria &&
    prev.m?.pinColor === next.m?.pinColor &&
    prev.m?.estado === next.m?.estado &&
    prev.m?.avatar_url === next.m?.avatar_url &&
    (prev.m?.latitude ?? prev.m?.coords?.latitude) ===
      (next.m?.latitude ?? next.m?.coords?.latitude) &&
    (prev.m?.longitude ?? next.m?.coords?.longitude) ===
      (next.m?.longitude ?? next.m?.coords?.longitude)
);

export default FastMarker;

const baseStyles = StyleSheet.create({

    // === src/constants/affiliatesCatalog.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { CABO_CLUBS_AND_BOUTIQUE } from './catalog/caboClubsAndBoutique';
import { CABO_SPORTS_AND_SPECIALISTS } from './catalog/caboSportsAndSpecialists';

export const AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY = '@kinetic_affiliates_runtime_state_v1';

export interface IKineticAffiliateCatalogItem {
  id: string;
  nombre: string;
  disciplina: string;
  disciplinas_secundarias?: string[];
  categoria?: string;
  es_especialista: boolean;
  ubicacion: string;
  direccion?: string;
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  latitud?: number;
  longitud?: number;
  rating: number;
  estado: 'activo' | 'inactivo' | 'pendiente' | string;
  verificado: boolean;
  esClubActivo: boolean;
  horarios?: string;
  instagram?: string;
  whatsapp?: string;
  portada_url?: string;
  avatar_url?: string;
  descripcion?: string;
  amenidades?: string[];
  galeria?: string[];
  audit_scores?: number[];
  rfc?: string;
  clabe?: string;
  regimen_fiscal?: string;
  razon_social?: string;
  codigo_postal_fiscal?: string;
  email_facturacion?: string;
  google_maps_url?: string;
  cambios_pendientes_b2b?: any;
  [key: string]: any;
}

const normalizeKey = (val?: string | null): string =>
  String(val || '')
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');

const ADDRESS_ACRONYMS = new Set(['B.C.S.', 'B.C.S', 'BCS', 'SJC', 'CSL', 'C.P.', 'C.P', 'CP', 'KM', 'MXN', 'USA', 'VIP', 'I', 'II', 'III', 'IV']);
const ADDRESS_LOWER_WORDS = new Set(['de', 'del', 'la', 'las', 'los', 'y', 'en', 'el', 'al', 'a', 'con']);

export const formatAddressSmartTitleCase = (raw?: string | null): string => {
  const input = String(raw || '').trim();
  if (!input) return '';
  return input
    .split(/(\s+)/)
    .map((tok, idx) => {
      if (!tok || /^\s+$/.test(tok)) return tok;
      const lettersOnly = tok.replace(/[^A-Za-zÁÉÍÓÚÜÑáéíóúüñ]/g, '');
      const upperNorm = tok.replace(/[,.;:]+$/g, '').toUpperCase();
      if (ADDRESS_ACRONYMS.has(upperNorm) || ADDRESS_ACRONYMS.has(tok.toUpperCase())) {
        return upperNorm === 'KM' ? 'Km' : tok.toUpperCase();
      }
      if (lettersOnly.length >= 2 && lettersOnly === lettersOnly.toUpperCase()) {
        const lowerWord = tok.toLowerCase();
        if (idx > 0 && ADDRESS_LOWER_WORDS.has(lowerWord)) return lowerWord;
        return lowerWord.replace(/^[a-záéíóúüñ]/, (ch) => ch.toUpperCase());
      }
      return tok;
    })
    .join('');
};

export const canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram = (
  rawIg?: string | null,
  clubName?: string | null
): string => {
  const core = String(rawIg || '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?instagram\.com\//i, '')
    .replace(/[/?#].*$/, '')
    .replace(/^@+/, '')
    .trim();
  const lowCore = core.toLowerCase();
  const normName = normalizeKey(clubName);
  const isInCorFit = normName.includes('incorfit');
  if (lowCore === 'julibbb3' || (isInCorFit && (!lowCore || lowCore === 'incorfitcabo' || lowCore === 'julibbb3'))) {
    return '@incorfitbarremx';
  }
  return core ? `@${core}` : '';
};

export const resolveCanonicalAffiliateDiscipline = (
  clubName?: string | null,
  rawDiscipline?: string | null,
  isSpecialist?: boolean
): string => {
  const normName = normalizeKey(clubName);
  if (normName.includes('kinetic especialista') || normName === 'kinetic-especialista') {
    return 'Fisioterapia';
  }
  const clean = String(rawDiscipline || '').trim();
  if (clean && !['club', 'especialista'].includes(clean.toLowerCase())) {
    return clean;
  }
  return isSpecialist ? 'Fisioterapia' : 'Fuerza';
};

const BANNED_CATALOG_NAMES = new Set([
  'kinetic oficial',
  'sede kinetic',
  'sede oficial kinetic',
  'terminal global kinetic',
]);

const isBannedCatalogEntry = (item?: { nombre?: string | null; id?: string | null } | null): boolean => {
  if (!item) return true;
  const n = normalizeKey(item.nombre);
  const id = normalizeKey(item.id);
  if (!n && !id) return true;
  if (n && (BANNED_CATALOG_NAMES.has(n) || n.includes('atleta 5975') || n.includes('atleta kinetic'))) {
    return true;
  }
  if (id === 'kinetic-oficial' || id === 'sede-kinetic' || id === 'terminal-global-kinetic') {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
};

const BASE_STATIC_CATALOG: IKineticAffiliateCatalogItem[] = [
  ...CABO_CLUBS_AND_BOUTIQUE,
  ...CABO_SPORTS_AND_SPECIALISTS,
].filter((item) => !isBannedCatalogEntry(item));

export const KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES: IKineticAffiliateCatalogItem[] = BASE_STATIC_CATALOG.map(
  (item) => ({ ...item })
);

export const upsertRuntimeAffiliateState = (
  partialAffiliate: Partial<IKineticAffiliateCatalogItem> & { nombre?: string; id?: string }
): IKineticAffiliateCatalogItem => {
  if (isBannedCatalogEntry(partialAffiliate)) {
    return (KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES[0] || ({} as IKineticAffiliateCatalogItem));
  }
  const targetNameKey = normalizeKey(partialAffiliate.nombre);
  const targetIdKey = normalizeKey(partialAffiliate.id);

  const existingIdx = KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES.findIndex((item) => {
    const itemNameKey = normalizeKey(item.nombre);
    const itemIdKey = normalizeKey(item.id);
    return (
      (targetIdKey && itemIdKey === targetIdKey) ||
      (targetNameKey && itemNameKey === targetNameKey)
    );
  });

  const isActiveNow =
    partialAffiliate.estado === 'activo' ||
    (partialAffiliate.estado === undefined &&
      (partialAffiliate.esClubActivo === true || partialAffiliate.verificado === true));

  let updatedRecord: IKineticAffiliateCatalogItem;

  if (existingIdx >= 0) {
    const current = KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES[existingIdx];
    const isKineticSpec =
      normalizeKey(current.nombre).includes('kinetic especialista') ||
      normalizeKey(current.id) === 'kinetic-especialista';
    const nextEstado =
      partialAffiliate.estado ||
      (typeof partialAffiliate.esClubActivo === 'boolean'
        ? partialAffiliate.esClubActivo
          ? 'activo'
          : 'inactivo'
        : current.estado);

    updatedRecord = {
      ...current,
      ...partialAffiliate,
      disciplina: resolveCanonicalAffiliateDiscipline(
        partialAffiliate.nombre || current.nombre,
        partialAffiliate.disciplina || current.disciplina,
        isKineticSpec || Boolean(partialAffiliate.es_especialista ?? current.es_especialista)
      ),
      disciplinas_secundarias: isKineticSpec
        ? ['Fisioterapia', 'Masaje Deportivo', 'Cold Plunge', 'Crioterapia']
        : partialAffiliate.disciplinas_secundarias || current.disciplinas_secundarias,
      es_especialista: isKineticSpec
        ? true
        : typeof partialAffiliate.es_especialista === 'boolean'
          ? partialAffiliate.es_especialista
          : current.es_especialista,
      categoria: isKineticSpec ? 'Especialista' : partialAffiliate.categoria || current.categoria,
      instagram: canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram(
        partialAffiliate.instagram ?? current.instagram,
        partialAffiliate.nombre || current.nombre
      ),
      estado: nextEstado,
      verificado:
        typeof partialAffiliate.verificado === 'boolean'
          ? partialAffiliate.verificado
          : nextEstado === 'activo',
      esClubActivo:
        typeof partialAffiliate.esClubActivo === 'boolean'
          ? partialAffiliate.esClubActivo
          : nextEstado === 'activo',
    };
    KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES[existingIdx] = updatedRecord;
  } else {
    const cleanName = String(partialAffiliate.nombre || 'Nuevo Afiliado Kinetic').trim();
    const isNewSpec = Boolean(partialAffiliate.es_especialista) || normalizeKey(cleanName).includes('kinetic especialista');
    const slugId =
      partialAffiliate.id ||
      cleanName
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
        .replace(/^-|-$/g, '') ||
      `af_${Date.now()}`;

    const canonicalDisc = resolveCanonicalAffiliateDiscipline(cleanName, partialAffiliate.disciplina, isNewSpec);
    updatedRecord = {
      ...partialAffiliate,
      id: slugId,
      nombre: cleanName,
      disciplina: canonicalDisc,
      disciplinas_secundarias: partialAffiliate.disciplinas_secundarias || [canonicalDisc],
      categoria: isNewSpec ? 'Especialista' : 'Club',
      es_especialista: isNewSpec,
      ubicacion: partialAffiliate.ubicacion || 'Ubicación Verificada',
      direccion: partialAffiliate.direccion || partialAffiliate.ubicacion || 'Ubicación Verificada',
      latitude: Number(partialAffiliate.latitude ?? partialAffiliate.latitud) || 22.8935,
      longitude: Number(partialAffiliate.longitude ?? partialAffiliate.longitud) || -109.9142,
      rating: Number(partialAffiliate.rating) || 5.0,
      estado: isActiveNow ? 'activo' : partialAffiliate.estado || 'inactivo',
      verificado: isActiveNow,
      esClubActivo: isActiveNow,
      horarios: partialAffiliate.horarios || 'Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 22:00',
      instagram: canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram(partialAffiliate.instagram, cleanName),
      whatsapp: partialAffiliate.whatsapp || '',
    };
    KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES.unshift(updatedRecord);
  }

  AsyncStorage.getItem(AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY)
    .then((raw) => {
      const map: Record<string, any> = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
      const key = normalizeKey(updatedRecord.nombre);
      map[key] = updatedRecord;
      return AsyncStorage.setItem(AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(map));
    })
    .catch(() => {});

  return updatedRecord;
};

export const reconcileCatalogWithSupabaseDb = (dbRows: any[]): void => {
  if (!Array.isArray(dbRows) || dbRows.length === 0) return;
  const dbByName = new Map<string, any>();
  const dbById = new Map<string, any>();
  dbRows.forEach((row) => {
    if (!row || isBannedCatalogEntry(row)) return;
    const nKey = normalizeKey(row.nombre);
    const idKey = normalizeKey(row.id);
    if (nKey) dbByName.set(nKey === 'palestra club' ? 'palesttra club' : nKey, row);
    if (idKey) dbById.set(idKey, row);
  });

  KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES.length = 0;
  BASE_STATIC_CATALOG.forEach((baseItem) => {
    const nKey = normalizeKey(baseItem.nombre);
    const idKey = normalizeKey(baseItem.id);
    const dbMatch = (idKey && dbById.get(idKey)) || (nKey && dbByName.get(nKey));
    if (dbMatch) {
      const dbEst = normalizeKey(dbMatch.estado) || 'inactivo';
      const isAct = dbEst === 'activo';
      KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES.push({
        ...baseItem,
        disciplina: resolveCanonicalAffiliateDiscipline(baseItem.nombre, baseItem.disciplina, baseItem.es_especialista),
        instagram: canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram(dbMatch.instagram || baseItem.instagram, baseItem.nombre),
        estado: dbEst,
        verificado: isAct,
        esClubActivo: isAct,
      });
    } else {
      KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES.push({
        ...baseItem,
        instagram: canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram(baseItem.instagram, baseItem.nombre),
      });
    }
  });
};

AsyncStorage.getItem(AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY)
  .then((raw) => {
    if (!raw) return;
    const savedMap: Record<string, Partial<IKineticAffiliateCatalogItem>> = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!savedMap || typeof savedMap !== 'object') return;
    let mutated = false;
    Object.keys(savedMap).forEach((k) => {
      const item = savedMap[k];
      if (!item || isBannedCatalogEntry(item) || isBannedCatalogEntry({ nombre: k })) {
        delete savedMap[k];
        mutated = true;
      }
    });
    if (mutated) {
      const keys = Object.keys(savedMap);
      if (keys.length === 0) {
        void AsyncStorage.removeItem(AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY);
      } else {
        void AsyncStorage.setItem(AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(savedMap));
      }
    }
  })
  .catch(() => {});

    FILE: src/constants/categoriesCatalog.ts

    import { IKineticCategoryMeta, KineticCategoryDivision } from './catalog/catalogTypes';
import { CATEGORIES_CHUNK_1 } from './catalog/categoriesChunk1';
import { CATEGORIES_CHUNK_2 } from './catalog/categoriesChunk2';
import { CATEGORIES_CHUNK_3 } from './catalog/categoriesChunk3';
import { CATEGORIES_CHUNK_4 } from './catalog/categoriesChunk4';
import { CATEGORIES_CHUNK_5 } from './catalog/categoriesChunk5';
import { CATEGORIES_CHUNK_6 } from './catalog/categoriesChunk6';
export type { IKineticCategoryMeta, KineticCategoryDivision };

export const KINETIC_CATEGORIES_CATALOG: IKineticCategoryMeta[] = [
  ...CATEGORIES_CHUNK_1,
  ...CATEGORIES_CHUNK_2,
  ...CATEGORIES_CHUNK_3,
  ...CATEGORIES_CHUNK_4,
  ...CATEGORIES_CHUNK_5,
  ...CATEGORIES_CHUNK_6,
];

const BASE_CATEGORY_IMAGES: Record<string, string> = Object.fromEntries(
  KINETIC_CATEGORIES_CATALOG.map((c) => [c.name, c.imageUrl]),
);

export const EXTENDED_CATEGORY_IMAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  ...BASE_CATEGORY_IMAGES,
  "default": "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1534438327276-14e5300c3a48?q=80&w=1000&auto=format&fit=crop&sig=0",
  "Pilates Mat (Piso)": "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1518611012118-696072aa579a?auto=format&fit=crop&w=1000&q=80",
  "Power Pilates & Sculpt": "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1599901860904-17e6ed7083a0?auto=format&fit=crop&w=1000&q=80",
  "Cadillac & Tower Pilates": "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1518611012118-696072aa579a?auto=format&fit=crop&w=1000&q=80",
};

export const FITNESS_CATEGORIES_LIST: string[] = KINETIC_CATEGORIES_CATALOG
  .filter((c) => c.division === 'fitness')
  .map((c) => c.name)
  .sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));

export const WELLNESS_CATEGORIES_LIST: string[] = KINETIC_CATEGORIES_CATALOG
  .filter((c) => c.division === 'wellness')
  .map((c) => c.name)
  .sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));

export const WELLNESS_CATEGORY_NAMES_SET = new Set<string>(
  KINETIC_CATEGORIES_CATALOG.filter((c) => c.division === 'wellness').map((c) => c.name.toLowerCase()),
);

const WELLNESS_KEYWORDS = [
  'spa', 'masaje', 'fisio', 'quiropr', 'osteop', 'sound', 'cuenco', 'gong', 'meditaci',
  'mindfulness', 'breath', 'wim hof', 'yoga', 'stretching', 'animal flow', 'tai chi',
  'qi gong', 'nutrici', 'inbody', 'suplement', 'sauna', 'plunge', 'crio', 'contraste',
  'temazcal', 'hammam', 'hidrotermal', 'reiki', 'cacao', 'flotaci', 'haloterapia',
  'sonoterapia', 'facial', 'maderoterapia', 'aromaterapia', 'exfoliaci', 'envoltura',
  'reflexolog', 'acupuntura', 'puncion', 'punción', 'ventosa', 'cupping', 'kinesiotap',
  'percusi', 'theragun', 'normatec', 'compresi', 'presoterapia', 'miofascial', 'pisada',
  'postural', 'hidroterapia', 'vo2', 'sueroterapia', 'nad+', 'luz roja', 'hiperbarica',
  'hiperbárica', 'rehabilitaci', 'readaptaci', 'podolog', 'psicolog', 'sueño',
  'hormonal', 'detox', 'quiromasaje', 'camilla', 'wellness',
];

export const isWellnessCategory = (categoryName?: string | null): boolean => {
  const clean = String(categoryName || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!clean) return false;
  if (WELLNESS_CATEGORY_NAMES_SET.has(clean)) return true;
  return WELLNESS_KEYWORDS.some((kw) => clean.includes(kw));
};

export const getCategoryAccentColor = (categoryName?: string | null): '#00F0FF' | '#9D4EDD' =>
  isWellnessCategory(categoryName) ? '#9D4EDD' : '#00F0FF';

export const formatFechaCortaEs = (rawDate?: string): string => {
  const lang = getActiveKineticLanguage();
  if (!rawDate) return lang === 'en' ? 'Today' : 'Hoy';
  return formatKineticDate(rawDate, lang, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
};

export const cleanLocationDisplay = (
  rawLoc?: string | null,
  fallback = 'Ubicación Verificada'
): string => {
  const str = String(rawLoc || '').trim();
  if (!str) return fallback;
  const withoutUrls = str
    .replace(/https?:\/\/\S+/gi, '')
    .replace(/(?:^|[\s•|–—-]+)$/g, '')
    .replace(/^[\s•|–—-]+|[\s•|–—-]+$/g, '')
    .trim();
  if (
    !withoutUrls ||
    withoutUrls.startsWith('&query=') ||
    withoutUrls.startsWith('?query=') ||
    withoutUrls.includes('google.com/maps') ||
    withoutUrls.includes('maps.app.goo.gl') ||
    withoutUrls.includes('goo.gl/maps')
  ) {
    return fallback;
  }
  return formatAddressSmartTitleCase(withoutUrls);
};

const U = (id: string, sig: number) =>
  `https://images.unsplash.com/photo-${id}?q=80&w=1000&auto=format&fit=crop&sig=${sig}`;

  new Set(KINETIC_CATEGORIES_CATALOG.map((c) => c.name))
).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'es'));

export const resolveCategoryImage = (categoria?: string, titulo?: string): string => {
  const rawCat = String(categoria || '').trim();
  const combined = `${rawCat} ${String(titulo || '')}`
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase();

  if (combined.includes('padel')) return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Pádel'];
  if (rawCat && CATEGORY_IMAGES[rawCat]) return CATEGORY_IMAGES[rawCat];

  if (rawCat) {
    const normCat = rawCat.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase();
    const matchedKey = Object.keys(CATEGORY_IMAGES).find(
      (k) => k.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase() === normCat
    );
    if (matchedKey && CATEGORY_IMAGES[matchedKey]) return CATEGORY_IMAGES[matchedKey];
  }

  for (const key of SORTED_CATEGORIES) {
    const normKey = key.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase();
    if (combined.includes(normKey) && CATEGORY_IMAGES[key]) return CATEGORY_IMAGES[key];
  }

  if (combined.includes('fisio') || combined.includes('rehab') || combined.includes('especialista')) {
    return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Fisioterapia'];
  }
  if (combined.includes('masaje')) return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Masaje Deportivo'];
  if (combined.includes('badminton')) return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Bádminton'];
  if (combined.includes('basquet')) return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Básquetbol'];
  if (combined.includes('beisbol')) return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Béisbol'];
  if (combined.includes('futbol')) return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Fútbol'];
  if (combined.includes('natacion') || combined.includes('nado')) return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Natación'];
  if (combined.includes('spin') || combined.includes('cycling')) return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Indoor Cycling'];
  if (combined.includes('gym') || combined.includes('gimnasio')) return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Gimnasio'];

  return CATEGORY_IMAGES['Fuerza'] || CATEGORY_IMAGES['default'];
};

export const resolveDisciplineGalleryFallbacks = (
  disciplina?: string,
  categoriaOrTitle?: string
): string[] => {
  const norm = `${disciplina || ''} ${categoriaOrTitle || ''}`
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase();
  if (/(padel|pickleball|tenis|tennis|squash|racquet|frontenis)/.test(norm)) {
    return [
      'https://images.pexels.com/photos/32897040/pexels-photo-32897040.jpeg?auto=compress&cs=tinysrgb&w=1000',
      'https://images.pexels.com/photos/35646550/pexels-photo-35646550.jpeg?auto=compress&cs=tinysrgb&w=1000',
      'https://images.pexels.com/photos/32897038/pexels-photo-32897038.jpeg?auto=compress&cs=tinysrgb&w=1000',
      'https://images.pexels.com/photos/17299534/pexels-photo-17299534.jpeg?auto=compress&cs=tinysrgb&w=1000',
    ];
  }
  if (/(pilates|barre|yoga|meditacion|stretch|wellness|spa|rehab|fisio|masaje|cold plunge|crio|sauna)/.test(norm)) {
    return [
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Pilates Reformer'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Yoga'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Fisioterapia'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Cold Plunge'],
    ];
  }
  if (/(boxeo|boxing|mma|muay|jiu|kickbox|artes marciales|karate|judo|taekwondo)/.test(norm)) {
    return [
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Boxeo'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Artes Marciales'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['CrossFit'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Entrenamiento Funcional'],
    ];
  }
  if (/(surf|natacion|sup|kayak|acuatic|aqua|kitesurf|wakeboard)/.test(norm)) {
    return [
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Surf'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Natación'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Kayak'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Yoga'],
    ];
  }
  if (/(cycling|ciclismo|spin)/.test(norm)) {
    return [
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Indoor Cycling'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Ciclismo'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['HIIT'],
      CATEGORY_IMAGES['Stretching'],
    ];
  }
  const primary = resolveCategoryImage(disciplina, categoriaOrTitle);
  return [
    primary,
    CATEGORY_IMAGES['CrossFit'],
    CATEGORY_IMAGES['Entrenamiento Funcional'],
    CATEGORY_IMAGES['HIIT'],
  ];
};
