export const DISCIPLINE_ES_MAP: Record<string, string> = (() => {
  const rev: Record<string, string> = { "Indoor Cycling": "Ciclismo Indoor", "INDOOR CYCLING": "CICLISMO INDOOR", "indoor cycling": "Ciclismo Indoor", "Ciclismo Indoor": "Ciclismo Indoor" };
  for (const [esKey, enVal] of Object.entries(DISCIPLINE_EN_MAP)) {
    if (esKey !== "Indoor Cycling" && enVal) {
      if (!rev[enVal]) rev[enVal] = esKey;
      if (!rev[enVal.toLowerCase()]) rev[enVal.toLowerCase()] = esKey;
    }
  }
  return rev;
})();

export const formatDisciplineBilingual = (raw: unknown, language = 'es', fallbackTDisc?: (d: string) => string): string => {
  const clean = String(raw || '').trim();
  if (!clean) return language === 'en' ? 'Strength' : 'Fuerza';
  const low = clean.toLowerCase();
  if (low === 'indoor cycling' || low === 'ciclismo indoor') return language === 'en' ? 'Indoor Cycling' : 'Ciclismo Indoor';
  if (language === 'en') return DISCIPLINE_EN_MAP[clean] || (fallbackTDisc ? fallbackTDisc(clean) : clean);
  return DISCIPLINE_ES_MAP[clean] || DISCIPLINE_ES_MAP[low] || (fallbackTDisc ? fallbackTDisc(clean) : clean);
};

    FILE: src/hooks/explorar/useExploreBookingAndRpg.ts

    // === src/hooks/explorar/useExploreBookingAndRpg.ts === //
import { useCallback, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { getLevelNumericWeight } from '../../constants/memberships';
import { resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing } from '../../services/kineticFlashSaleHelper';
import { executeOptimisticEscrowBooking } from '../../services/kineticOptimisticStore';
import { computeSplitEscrow1NMath } from '../../services/kineticSplitPreBookingStore';
import {
  computeAthleteRpgStats,
  computePrestigeInfo,
  computeSeasonInfo,
  isStrictB2BPartnerAccount,
} from '../../services/kineticlubHelpers';

interface UseExploreBookingAndRpgParams {
  user: any;
  profile: any;
  isAdminView: boolean;
  activateResilientSession?: any;
  refreshProfile: () => Promise<any>;
  loadExploreData: (isSilent?: boolean, force?: boolean) => Promise<void>;
  setSelectedEvent: (v: any | null) => void;
  setAlertConfig: (v: any) => void;
  setIsMembershipOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  scheduleUi: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => void;
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
}

export function useExploreBookingAndRpg({
  user,
  profile,
  isAdminView,
  activateResilientSession,
  refreshProfile,
  loadExploreData,
  setSelectedEvent,
  setAlertConfig,
  setIsMembershipOpen,
  scheduleUi,
  tx,
}: UseExploreBookingAndRpgParams) {
  const rachaActual = Math.max(1, Number(profile?.racha_dias) || 1);
  const userLevelRank = getLevelNumericWeight(profile?.nivel || 'Acceso Base');
  const claimedList: string[] = useMemo(
    () => (Array.isArray(profile?.retos_reclamados) ? profile.retos_reclamados : []),
    [profile?.retos_reclamados]
  );
  const myUserStats = useMemo(
    () => computeAthleteRpgStats([], rachaActual, userLevelRank, claimedList.length),
    [rachaActual, userLevelRank, claimedList.length]
  );
  const myPrestigeInfo = useMemo(
    () => computePrestigeInfo(0, rachaActual, Number(profile?.xp_total) || 0, 0, claimedList, false),
    [rachaActual, profile?.xp_total, claimedList]
  );
  const mySeasonInfo = useMemo(
    () => computeSeasonInfo(myPrestigeInfo.totalXp),
    [myPrestigeInfo.totalXp]
  );

  const isReservingRef = useRef(false);
  const handleReserveEvent = useCallback(
    async (ev: any): Promise<boolean> => {
      if (!user?.id || !ev || isReservingRef.current) return false;
      if (isStrictB2BPartnerAccount(user, profile)) {
        setSelectedEvent(null);
        scheduleUi(() => setAlertConfig({
          visible: true,
          title: tx('SOLO CUENTAS DE ATLETA', 'ATHLETE ACCOUNTS ONLY'),
          message: tx(
            'Tu cuenta es operativa de socio B2B y no puede generar reservas ni gastar Kineticoins como atleta.',
            'Your account is a B2B partner operator account and cannot book or spend Kineticoins as an athlete.'
          ),
          type: 'error',
        }), 120);
        return false;
      }
      const fp = resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing({
        event: ev,
        vipLevel: profile?.nivel,
        kcMembresia: profile?.kc_membresia,
        isAdmin: isAdminView,
      });
      const total = isAdminView ? 0 : fp.discountedTotalKc;
      const splitCount = Math.min(4, Math.max(1, Number(ev?.split_count || ev?.splitCount) || 1));
      const esc = isAdminView
        ? 0
        : computeSplitEscrow1NMath(total, splitCount, fp.escrow40, isAdminView).effectiveEscrowKc;
      const evWithPrice = isAdminView ? ev : { ...ev, precio_kc: total, split_count: splitCount };
      const kcMem = Math.max(0, Number(profile?.kc_membresia) || 0);
      const kcTop = Math.max(0, Number(profile?.kc_topup) || 0);
      if (!isAdminView && kcMem + kcTop < esc) {
        if (typeof ev._onInsufficientKc === 'function') {
          ev._onInsufficientKc(Math.max(1, esc - (kcMem + kcTop)), kcMem + kcTop, esc);
          return false;
        }
        setSelectedEvent(null);
        scheduleUi(() => setAlertConfig({
          visible: true,
          title: tx('SALDO INSUFICIENTE EN BÓVEDA', 'INSUFFICIENT VAULT BALANCE'),
          message: tx(
            `Necesitas ${esc} KC (40% Apartado) para apartar tu lugar. ¿Deseas recargar o subir de nivel ahora?`,
            `You need ${esc} KC (40% Deposit) to reserve your spot. Would you like to top up or upgrade now?`
          ),
          type: 'confirm',
          onConfirm: () => {
            setAlertConfig({ visible: false });
            scheduleUi(() => setIsMembershipOpen(true), 180);
          },
        }), 180);
        return false;
      }
      isReservingRef.current = true;
      try {
        const ok = await executeOptimisticEscrowBooking({
          user,
          profile,
          ev: evWithPrice,
          isAdmin: isAdminView,
          activateResilientSession,
          refreshProfile,
          onInstantBooked: () => { setSelectedEvent(null); },
          onSilentRevalidate: () => loadExploreData(true, true),
          onRollbackError: (msg) => {
            const safeMsg = msg || tx('No se pudo completar la reserva.', 'Could not complete reservation.');
            const errCb = (evWithPrice as { _onBookingError?: (m: string) => void } | null)?._onBookingError;
            if (typeof errCb === 'function') {
              errCb(safeMsg);
              return;
            }
            setSelectedEvent(null);
            scheduleUi(() => setAlertConfig({
              visible: true,
              title: tx('RESERVA NO PROCESADA', 'BOOKING FAILED'),
              message: safeMsg,
              type: 'error',
            }), 160);
          },
          fallbackTitle: tx('RESERVA NO PROCESADA', 'BOOKING FAILED'),
        });
        return ok;
      } finally {
        isReservingRef.current = false;
      }
    },
    [
      user,
      profile,
      isAdminView,
      activateResilientSession,
      refreshProfile,
      loadExploreData,
      setSelectedEvent,
      setAlertConfig,
      setIsMembershipOpen,
      scheduleUi,
      tx,
    ]
  );

  return {
    myUserStats,
    myPrestigeInfo,
    mySeasonInfo,
    handleReserveEvent,
  };
}

    FILE: src/hooks/explorar/useExploreFavoritesAndSort.ts

    // === src/hooks/explorar/useExploreFavoritesAndSort.ts === //
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import { exploreService } from '../../services/exploreService';

export type QuickExploreChip = 'todas' | 'fitness' | 'wellness' | 'favoritos' | 'mas_buscados';

export type ExploreSortOption =
  | 'relevancia' | 'distancia' | 'rating_desc' | 'rating_asc' | 'az'
  | 'za' | 'recientes' | 'disponibilidad' | 'precio_asc' | 'precio_desc';

const EXPLORE_FAVS_TTL_MS = 120_000;
let exploreFavsCache: { uid: string; ids: string[]; ts: number } | null = null;

export function useExploreFavorites(
  userId: string | undefined,
  isMountedRef: React.MutableRefObject<boolean>
) {
  const [favoriteIds, setFavoriteIds] = useState<string[]>(() =>
    userId && exploreFavsCache?.uid === userId ? exploreFavsCache.ids || [] : []
  );
  const favoriteIdsRef = useRef<string[]>(favoriteIds);
  favoriteIdsRef.current = favoriteIds;
  const favMutationSeqRef = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    const reqSeq = favMutationSeqRef.current;
    const applyFavs = (nextIds: string[]) => {
      favoriteIdsRef.current = nextIds;
      setFavoriteIds((prev) =>
        prev.length === nextIds.length && prev.every((v, idx) => v === nextIds[idx]) ? prev : nextIds
      );
    };
    if (!userId) {
      applyFavs([]);
      return;
    }
    const favHit = exploreFavsCache;
    if (favHit && favHit.uid === userId && Date.now() - favHit.ts < EXPLORE_FAVS_TTL_MS) {
      applyFavs(favHit.ids);
      return;
    }
    exploreService
      .fetchUserFavorites(userId)
      .then((ids) => {
        if (favMutationSeqRef.current !== reqSeq) return;
        const clean = ids || [];
        exploreFavsCache = { uid: userId, ids: clean, ts: Date.now() };
        if (active && isMountedRef.current) applyFavs(clean);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [userId, isMountedRef]);

  const toggleFavorite = useCallback(
    (clubOrId: any) => {
      const idKey =
        typeof clubOrId === 'string' ? clubOrId.trim() : String(clubOrId?.id || '').trim();
      const nameKey =
        typeof clubOrId === 'object' && clubOrId !== null ? String(clubOrId?.nombre || '').trim() : '';
      const primaryKey = idKey || nameKey;
      if (!primaryKey) return;

      favMutationSeqRef.current += 1;
      triggerHaptic('Medium');
      const prev = favoriteIdsRef.current || [];
      const idLow = idKey.toLowerCase();
      const nameLow = nameKey.toLowerCase();
      const isCurrentlyFav = prev.some((existing) => {
        const ex = String(existing || '').trim();
        const exLow = ex.toLowerCase();
        return (
          (idKey && (ex === idKey || exLow === idLow)) ||
          (nameKey && (ex === nameKey || exLow === nameLow))
        );
      });

      const next = isCurrentlyFav
        ? prev.filter((existing) => {
            const ex = String(existing || '').trim();
            const exLow = ex.toLowerCase();
            if (idKey && (ex === idKey || exLow === idLow)) return false;
            if (nameKey && (ex === nameKey || exLow === nameLow)) return false;
            return true;
          })
        : [...prev, primaryKey];

      favoriteIdsRef.current = next;
      setFavoriteIds(next);

      const persistUid = userId ? String(userId) : 'guest';
      if (userId) {
        exploreFavsCache = { uid: persistUid, ids: next, ts: Date.now() };
      }
      void Promise.resolve().then(() =>
        exploreService.saveUserFavorites(persistUid, next).catch(() => {})
      );
    },
    [userId]
  );

  return { favoriteIds, toggleFavorite };
}

export function useExploreCompareAffiliates(sortBy: ExploreSortOption, quickChip: QuickExploreChip) {
  return useCallback(
    (a: any, b: any) => {
      if (Boolean(a.esClubActivo) !== Boolean(b.esClubActivo)) return a.esClubActivo ? -1 : 1;
      if (sortBy === 'distancia') {
        return (a.distanciaKm || 0) - (b.distanciaKm || 0) || String(a.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b.nombre || ''), 'es');
      }
      if (sortBy === 'rating_desc') return (b.rating || 0) - (a.rating || 0) || (a.distanciaKm || 0) - (b.distanciaKm || 0);
      if (sortBy === 'rating_asc') return (a.rating || 0) - (b.rating || 0) || (a.distanciaKm || 0) - (b.distanciaKm || 0);
      if (sortBy === 'disponibilidad') {
        const sumOpenSpots = (af: any) =>
          (Array.isArray(af?.experiencias) ? af.experiencias : []).reduce(
            (acc: number, e: any) => acc + Math.max(0, (Number(e?.cupo_maximo) || 10) - (Number(e?.cupo_actual) || 0)),
            0
          );
        return sumOpenSpots(b) - sumOpenSpots(a) || (b.experiencias?.length || 0) - (a.experiencias?.length || 0) || (b.rating || 0) - (a.rating || 0);
      }
      if (sortBy === 'precio_asc') return (a.minPrice || 150) - (b.minPrice || 150) || String(a.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b.nombre || ''), 'es');
      if (sortBy === 'precio_desc') return (b.minPrice || 150) - (a.minPrice || 150) || String(a.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b.nombre || ''), 'es');
      if (sortBy === 'az') return String(a.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b.nombre || ''), 'es');
      if (sortBy === 'za') return String(b.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(a.nombre || ''), 'es');
      if (quickChip === 'mas_buscados' && (b.trendingScore || 0) !== (a.trendingScore || 0)) {
        return (b.trendingScore || 0) - (a.trendingScore || 0);
      }
      return (b.rating || 0) - (a.rating || 0) || (a.distanciaKm || 0) - (b.distanciaKm || 0);
    },
    [sortBy, quickChip]
  );
}

    FILE: src/hooks/useClubDetailsModal.ts

    // === src/hooks/useClubDetailsModal.ts === //
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../components/SharedUI';
import { KINETIC_AMENITIES } from '../constants/exploreData';
import { isValidB2BWhatsapp } from '../services/adminService';
import {
  normalizeClubAmenityIds,
  persistClubAuditAndChecklists,
  persistClubSheetAsAdmin,
  submitClubChangesForAdminReview,
} from '../services/clubSheetPersistenceHelper';
import { canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey } from '../services/exploreRadarFilters';
import { exploreService } from '../services/exploreService';
import {
  DIAS_SEMANA,
  formatHorarioCompacto,
  readClubExtendedCache,
} from '../utils/clubLocalOverridesHelper';
import {
  extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule,
  readSyncLocalClubOverride,
  setSyncClubMediaMemory,
  stripAffiliateIgMeta,
} from '../utils/exploreEnrichmentHelpers';
import { compressWebImageUri } from '../utils/externalLinks';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';

export { DIAS_SEMANA, formatHorarioCompacto };

const bi = (es: string, en: string) =>
  getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en' ? en : es;

const sanitizeB2BWa = (raw?: any): string => {
  const digits = String(raw || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
  const ten = digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('52') ? digits.slice(2) : digits.slice(0, 10);
  return isValidB2BWhatsapp(ten) ? ten : '';
};

const padPositionalGallery = (raw?: unknown): string[] => {
  const arr = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : [];
  return [0, 1, 2, 3].map((idx) => {
    const val = String(arr[idx] || '').trim();
    return val.length > 5 && !val.startsWith('data:image/nonexistent') ? val : '';
  });
};

const mergePositionalGallery = (primary?: unknown, fallback?: unknown): string[] => {
  const a = padPositionalGallery(primary);
  const b = padPositionalGallery(fallback);
  return [0, 1, 2, 3].map((idx) => a[idx] || b[idx] || '');
};

const buildBaseEditState = (affiliate: any) => {
  const igAndSched = extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule(affiliate);
  const nameKey = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(affiliate?.nombre);
  const syncOv = readSyncLocalClubOverride(affiliate?.id, nameKey);
  const hasFreshSync = Boolean(
    syncOv?.updatedAt && Date.now() - Number(syncOv.updatedAt) < 12 * 3600 * 1000
  );
  const baseGal = mergePositionalGallery(
    hasFreshSync ? syncOv?.galeria : affiliate?.galeria,
    hasFreshSync ? affiliate?.galeria : syncOv?.galeria
  );
  return {
    ...affiliate,
    __clubKey: String(affiliate?.id || affiliate?.nombre || ''),
    nombre: affiliate?.nombre || '',
    disciplina: affiliate?.disciplina || affiliate?.categoria || 'Fuerza',
    ubicacion: affiliate?.ubicacion || bi('Ubicación Verificada', 'Verified Location'),
    horarios: formatHorarioCompacto(stripAffiliateIgMeta(igAndSched.horarios || affiliate?.horarios)),
    instagram: igAndSched.instagram || affiliate?.instagram || '',
    whatsapp: sanitizeB2BWa(affiliate?.whatsapp),
    descripcion: affiliate?.descripcion || '',
    portada_url:
      (hasFreshSync && syncOv?.portada_url) || affiliate?.portada_url || syncOv?.portada_url || null,
    avatar_url:
      (hasFreshSync && syncOv?.avatar_url) || affiliate?.avatar_url || syncOv?.avatar_url || null,
    galeria: baseGal,
    rating: Number(affiliate?.rating ?? affiliate?.calificacion ?? 5.0),
    audit_scores: Array.isArray(affiliate?.audit_scores) ? affiliate.audit_scores : null,
    audit_checklists: affiliate?.audit_checklists || null,
  };
};

export const useClubDetailsModal = (
  affiliate: any,
  userOrVisible?: any,
  onRefreshData?: () => void,
  externalShowAlert?: (
    title: string,
    message: string,
    type?: string,
    onConfirm?: (() => void) | null
  ) => void
) => {
  const [isEditMode, setIsEditMode] = useState(false);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [isUploadingImage, setIsUploadingImage] = useState(false);
  const [descExpanded, setDescExpanded] = useState(false);
  const isEditModeRef = useRef(false);
  isEditModeRef.current = isEditMode;
  const isPickingImageRef = useRef(false);
  const pickCooldownTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (pickCooldownTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(pickCooldownTimerRef.current);
  }, []);

  const defaultAmenityIds = useMemo(
    () => KINETIC_AMENITIES.slice(0, 6).map((a) => a.id),
    []
  );

  const [editData, setEditData] = useState<any>(() => buildBaseEditState(affiliate));
  const [selectedAmenityIds, setSelectedAmenityIds] = useState<string[]>(() => {
    const init = extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule(affiliate);
    return normalizeClubAmenityIds(init.amenidades || affiliate?.amenidades);
  });

  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState({
    visible: false,
    title: '',
    message: '',
    type: 'alert',
    onConfirm: null as any,
    confirmText: bi('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT'),
    cancelText: bi('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
  });

  const showTacticalAlert = useCallback(
    (title: string, message: string, type = 'alert', onConfirm: any = null) => {
      if (externalShowAlert) {
        externalShowAlert(title, message, type, onConfirm);
        return;
      }
      setAlertConfig({
        visible: true,
        title,
        message,
        type,
        confirmText: type === 'confirm' ? bi('CONFIRMAR', 'CONFIRM') : bi('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT'),
        cancelText: bi('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
        onConfirm,
      });
    },
    [externalShowAlert]
  );

  const lastAffiliateIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const lastAffiliateNameRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    if (!affiliate) return;
    const clubId = String(affiliate.id || affiliate.nombre || 'club');
    const nameKey = String(affiliate.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const isSameClub =
      lastAffiliateIdRef.current === clubId ||
      (Boolean(nameKey) && lastAffiliateNameRef.current === nameKey);

    if (isSameClub && (isEditModeRef.current || isPickingImageRef.current)) {
      lastAffiliateIdRef.current = clubId;
      if (nameKey) lastAffiliateNameRef.current = nameKey;
      return;
    }

    if (!isSameClub) {
      lastAffiliateIdRef.current = clubId;
      lastAffiliateNameRef.current = nameKey;
      setIsEditMode(false);
      setDescExpanded(false);
    }

    const baseState = buildBaseEditState(affiliate);
    const igAndSched = extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule(affiliate);
    setEditData(baseState);
    setSelectedAmenityIds(normalizeClubAmenityIds(igAndSched.amenidades || affiliate?.amenidades));

    readClubExtendedCache(clubId, nameKey)
      .then(({ parsed, savedRatingEntry }) => {
        if (!isMounted || isEditModeRef.current || isPickingImageRef.current) return;
        const mergedRating =
          typeof savedRatingEntry?.rating === 'number'
            ? savedRatingEntry.rating
            : typeof parsed?.rating === 'number'
              ? parsed.rating
              : baseState.rating;
        const mergedScores = Array.isArray(savedRatingEntry?.audit_scores)
          ? savedRatingEntry.audit_scores
          : Array.isArray(parsed?.audit_scores)
            ? parsed.audit_scores
            : baseState.audit_scores;

        affiliate.rating = mergedRating;
        affiliate.calificacion = mergedRating;
        if (mergedScores) affiliate.audit_scores = mergedScores;

        const parsedClean = extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule(parsed);
        const canonName = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(affiliate?.nombre);
        const syncOv = readSyncLocalClubOverride(clubId, canonName || nameKey);
        const hasFreshSync = Boolean(
          syncOv?.updatedAt && Date.now() - Number(syncOv.updatedAt) < 12 * 3600 * 1000
        );
        setEditData((prev: any) => ({
          ...prev,
          ...parsed,
          portada_url:
            (hasFreshSync && syncOv?.portada_url) ||
            prev.portada_url ||
            parsed?.portada_url ||
            null,
          avatar_url:
            (hasFreshSync && syncOv?.avatar_url) ||
            prev.avatar_url ||
            parsed?.avatar_url ||
            null,
          galeria: mergePositionalGallery(
            hasFreshSync ? syncOv?.galeria : prev.galeria,
            mergePositionalGallery(parsed?.galeria, prev.galeria)
          ),
          instagram: parsedClean.instagram || prev.instagram,
          whatsapp: sanitizeB2BWa(parsed?.whatsapp ?? prev.whatsapp),
          rating: mergedRating,
          audit_scores: mergedScores,
          audit_checklists: parsed?.audit_checklists || prev.audit_checklists || null,
          horarios: formatHorarioCompacto(stripAffiliateIgMeta(parsedClean.horarios || prev.horarios)),
        }));
        if (parsedClean.amenidades && parsedClean.amenidades.length > 0) {
          setSelectedAmenityIds(normalizeClubAmenityIds(parsedClean.amenidades));
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});

    const hasAnyGallery = Array.isArray(baseState.galeria) && baseState.galeria.some(Boolean);
    if (affiliate.id && !hasAnyGallery) {
      exploreService
        .fetchClubGalleryOnDemand(String(affiliate.id))
        .then((extra) => {
          if (!isMounted || !extra || isEditModeRef.current) return;
          if (extra.galeria.some(Boolean)) {
            const merged = mergePositionalGallery(baseState.galeria, extra.galeria);
            affiliate.galeria = merged;
            setSyncClubMediaMemory([clubId, nameKey], {
              portada_url: baseState.portada_url,
              avatar_url: baseState.avatar_url,
              galeria: merged,
            });
            setEditData((prev: any) => ({
              ...prev,
              galeria: mergePositionalGallery(prev.galeria, extra.galeria),
            }));
          }
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [affiliate, defaultAmenityIds]);

  const guardarCalificacionAuditoria = useCallback(
    async (
      newRating: number,
      newScores?: number[],
      checklists?: Record<string, boolean>,
      isAdminUser = true
    ) => {
      if (!affiliate) return;
      const cleanRating = Number(Number(newRating || 5.0).toFixed(1));
      const cleanScores =
        Array.isArray(newScores) && newScores.length >= 4 ? newScores : null;
      const clubId = String(affiliate.id || affiliate.nombre || 'club');
      const clubName = String(editData?.nombre || affiliate.nombre || '').trim();

      setEditData((prev: any) => ({
        ...prev,
        rating: cleanRating,
        calificacion: cleanRating,
        audit_scores: cleanScores || prev.audit_scores,
        audit_checklists: checklists || prev.audit_checklists,
      }));
      affiliate.rating = cleanRating;
      affiliate.calificacion = cleanRating;
      if (cleanScores) affiliate.audit_scores = cleanScores;
      if (checklists) affiliate.audit_checklists = checklists;

      await persistClubAuditAndChecklists(
        affiliate,
        clubId,
        clubName,
        cleanRating,
        cleanScores,
        checklists || null,
        isAdminUser
      );
      onRefreshData?.();
    },
    [affiliate, editData?.nombre, onRefreshData]
  );

  const toggleAmenity = useCallback((amenityId: string) => {
    setSelectedAmenityIds((prev) =>
      prev.includes(amenityId)
        ? prev.filter((id) => id !== amenityId)
        : [...prev, amenityId]
    );
  }, []);

  const guardarCambiosTexto = async (isAdminUser = true) => {
    if (!affiliate || isSaving) return;
    setIsSaving(true);
    try {
      const rawIg = String(editData.instagram || '').replace(/\s+/g, '').replace(/^@+/, '');
      const cleanIg = rawIg ? `@${rawIg}` : '';
      const cleanWa = sanitizeB2BWa(editData.whatsapp);
      const updatedFields = {
        nombre: String(editData.nombre || affiliate.nombre || '').trim(),
        disciplina: editData.disciplina || affiliate.disciplina || 'Fuerza',
        ubicacion:
          String(editData.ubicacion || affiliate.ubicacion || '').trim() ||
          bi('Ubicación Verificada', 'Verified Location'),
        horarios: formatHorarioCompacto(stripAffiliateIgMeta(editData.horarios)),
        instagram: cleanIg,
        whatsapp: cleanWa,
        instagram_verificado: Boolean(cleanIg) || Boolean(affiliate.instagram_verificado),
        descripcion: String(editData.descripcion || '').trim(),
        portada_url: editData.portada_url || affiliate.portada_url || null,
        avatar_url: editData.avatar_url || affiliate.avatar_url || null,
        galeria: Array.isArray(editData.galeria) ? editData.galeria.slice(0, 4) : [],
        amenidades: selectedAmenityIds,
        rating: Number(editData.rating ?? affiliate.rating ?? 5.0),
        audit_scores: editData.audit_scores || affiliate.audit_scores || null,
        audit_checklists: editData.audit_checklists || affiliate.audit_checklists || null,
      };

      if (!isAdminUser) {
        await submitClubChangesForAdminReview(affiliate, updatedFields, userOrVisible);
        affiliate.cambios_pendientes_b2b = updatedFields;
        setEditData(buildBaseEditState(affiliate));
        setSelectedAmenityIds(normalizeClubAmenityIds(affiliate?.amenidades));
        setIsEditMode(false);
        triggerHaptic('Success');
        showTacticalAlert(
          bi('ENVIADO A REVISIÓN DEL ADMINISTRADOR', 'SENT FOR ADMIN REVIEW'),
          bi(
            'Tu solicitud de edición fue registrada en la bandeja de Revisión (Moderación de Contenido B2B). Se aplicará en cuanto el Administrador la autorice.',
            'Your profile update request was submitted to the Admin Review queue (B2B Content Moderation). It will go live once approved.'
          ),
          'success'
        );
        return;
      }

      const persisted = await persistClubSheetAsAdmin(affiliate, updatedFields);
      Object.assign(affiliate, persisted);
      setEditData((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, ...persisted }));
      setIsEditMode(false);
      onRefreshData?.();
      triggerHaptic('Success');
      showTacticalAlert(
        bi('SEDE ACTUALIZADA', 'VENUE UPDATED'),
        bi(
          'Los cambios en la ficha del afiliado se guardaron directamente en Supabase.',
          'Changes to the venue profile were saved directly to Supabase.'
        ),
        'success'
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      triggerHaptic('Error');
      showTacticalAlert(
        bi('FRICCIÓN AL GUARDAR', 'SAVE ERROR'),
        err?.message || bi('No se pudieron guardar los cambios.', 'Could not save changes.'),
        'error'
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const actualizarFotoCarruselOSede = async (
    target: 'avatar' | number,
    _currentCarouselSlides: string[] = []
  ) => {
    if (!affiliate || isUploadingImage || isPickingImageRef.current) return;
    isPickingImageRef.current = true;
    setIsUploadingImage(true);
    try {
      const isAvatar = target === 'avatar';
      const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
        mediaTypes: ['images'],
        allowsEditing: true,
        aspect: isAvatar ? [1, 1] : [16, 9],
        quality: 0.55,
        base64: true,
      });
      if (result.canceled || !result.assets?.[0]) return;
      const asset = result.assets[0];
      const rawUri = asset.base64
        ? `data:image/jpeg;base64,${asset.base64}`
        : asset.uri;
      const finalUri = await compressWebImageUri(rawUri, isAvatar ? 600 : 1200, 0.72);
      if (!finalUri) return;

      const clubKeys = [
        affiliate?.id,
        affiliate?.nombre,
        canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(affiliate?.nombre),
      ];
      if (isAvatar) {
        setEditData((prev: any) => {
          setSyncClubMediaMemory(clubKeys, {
            portada_url: prev.portada_url,
            avatar_url: finalUri,
            galeria: padPositionalGallery(prev.galeria),
          });
          return { ...prev, avatar_url: finalUri };
        });
      } else {
        const slotIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(4, Number(target) || 0));
        setEditData((prev: any) => {
          const nextPortada = slotIdx === 0 ? finalUri : prev.portada_url || null;
          const nextGaleria = padPositionalGallery(prev.galeria);
          if (slotIdx >= 1 && slotIdx <= 4) {
            nextGaleria[slotIdx - 1] = finalUri;
          }
          setSyncClubMediaMemory(clubKeys, {
            portada_url: nextPortada,
            avatar_url: prev.avatar_url,
            galeria: nextGaleria,
          });
          return {
            ...prev,
            portada_url: nextPortada,
            galeria: nextGaleria,
          };
        });
      }
      triggerHaptic('Success');
    } catch (err: any) {
      triggerHaptic('Error');
      showTacticalAlert(
        bi('FRICCIÓN DE SUBIDA', 'UPLOAD ERROR'),
        err?.message || bi('No se pudo cargar la imagen.', 'Could not upload image.'),
        'error'
      );
    } finally {
      if (pickCooldownTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(pickCooldownTimerRef.current);
      pickCooldownTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        isPickingImageRef.current = false;
        setIsUploadingImage(false);
      }, 280);
    }
  };

  return {
    isEditMode,
    setIsEditMode,
    isSaving,
    isUploadingImage,
    descExpanded,
    setDescExpanded,
    editData,
    setEditData,
    selectedAmenityIds,
    toggleAmenity,
    alertConfig,
    setAlertConfig,
    showTacticalAlert,
    guardarCambiosTexto,
    guardarCalificacionAuditoria,
    actualizarFotoCarruselOSede,
  };
};

export default useClubDetailsModal;

    FILE: src/hooks/useExplorar.ts

    // === src/hooks/useExplorar.ts === //
import { Image as ExpoImage } from 'expo-image';
import * as Location from 'expo-location';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../components/SharedUI';
import {
  CABO_BASE_COORDS,
  isWellnessCategory,
  resolveCategoryImage,
} from '../constants/exploreData';
import { isSupabaseQuotaExceeded } from '../lib/supabase';
import {
  buildSectionsByDiscipline,
  filterValidRadarServices,
  isBannedAffiliateRecord,
  isCoordsInsideLosCabos,
  isExploreAdminUser,
  normalizeRadarText,
  resolveZoneFilterMode,
} from '../services/exploreRadarFilters';
import {
  KINETIC_RADAR_RADIUS_KM,
  hydrateServicesGpsWithClubCoords,
} from '../services/exploreZoneHelpers';
import { setCanonicalUserGpsOrigin } from '../services/kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';
import { exploreService } from '../services/exploreService';
import {
  filterOutServiceId,
  reconcileActiveLiveServicesSnapshot,
  registerGhostPurgeHook,
} from '../services/kineticGhostEventsPurgeHelper';
import { hydrateExploreSWRFromStorage } from '../services/kineticOptimisticStore';
import { IMapLayerVisibility } from '../types';
import { buildEnrichedAffiliates, buildExploreMapMarkers } from '../utils/exploreEnrichmentHelpers';
import {
  buildCompleteExploreMapMarkers,
  deduplicateAffiliatesList,
  deduplicateSectionsForSearch,
  DEFAULT_EXPLORE_ZONE_ID,
  expandShortMapsUrlsInBackground,
  isAllCaboZone,
  matchesCleanExploreSearch,
} from '../utils/exploreMapAndSearchHelpers';
import { createDebouncedEventRunner, subscribeKineticRadarUpdated } from '../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import {
  ExploreSortOption,
  QuickExploreChip,
  useExploreCompareAffiliates,
  useExploreFavorites,
} from './explorar/useExploreFavoritesAndSort';

export type { ExploreSortOption, QuickExploreChip };

export const WARMED_PIN_URLS = new Set<string>();
const EXPLORE_EGRESS_TTL_MS = 120_000;
let exploreLastFetchedAt = 0;
let exploreMemoryCache: any[] | null = null;
let exploreServiciosMemoryCache: any[] | null = null;
let exploreRealGpsCache: { latitude: number; longitude: number } | null = null;
export const getExploreServiciosMemoryCache = (): any[] | null => exploreServiciosMemoryCache;
export const getExploreMemoryCache = (): any[] | null => exploreMemoryCache;

export function useExplorar(user?: any, profile?: any, session?: any) {
  const hasLoadedInitialRef = useRef<boolean>(Boolean(exploreMemoryCache?.length));
  const inFlightPromiseRef = useRef<Promise<void> | null>(null);
  const lastValidDataRef = useRef<any[] | null>(exploreMemoryCache);
  const [hasLoadedInitial, setHasLoadedInitial] = useState<boolean>(Boolean(exploreMemoryCache?.length));
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<boolean>(!exploreMemoryCache?.length);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [afiliadosRaw, setAfiliadosRaw] = useState<any[]>(() => exploreMemoryCache || []);
  const [serviciosRaw, setServiciosRaw] = useState<any[]>(() => exploreServiciosMemoryCache || []);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedCategories, setSelectedCategories] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [quickChip, setQuickChip] = useState<QuickExploreChip>('todas');
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<ExploreSortOption>('relevancia');
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'lista' | 'mapa'>('lista');
  const [selectedZone, setSelectedZone] = useState<string>(DEFAULT_EXPLORE_ZONE_ID);
  const [expandedUrlTick, setExpandedUrlTick] = useState(0);
  const [mapLayers, setMapLayers] = useState<IMapLayerVisibility>({
    showClubs: true,
    showSpecialists: true,
    showEvents: true,
  });
  const [hasRealUserCoords, setHasRealUserCoords] = useState<boolean>(() => Boolean(exploreRealGpsCache));
  const [isAcquiringRealGps, setIsAcquiringRealGps] = useState<boolean>(() => !exploreRealGpsCache);
  const [userCoords, setUserCoords] = useState(() =>
    exploreRealGpsCache || { latitude: CABO_BASE_COORDS.latitude, longitude: CABO_BASE_COORDS.longitude }
  );
  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const { favoriteIds, toggleFavorite } = useExploreFavorites(user?.id, isMountedRef);

  const isAdminView = useMemo(
    () => isExploreAdminUser(user, profile, session?.access_token ?? null),
    [
      session?.access_token,
      user?.email,
      user?.user_metadata?.rol,
      user?.user_metadata?.tipo_cuenta,
      user?.app_metadata?.rol,
      profile?.email,
      profile?.rol,
      profile?.tipo_cuenta,
    ]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => { isMountedRef.current = false; };
  }, []);

  const resetMapLayers = useCallback(() => {
    setMapLayers((prev) =>
      prev.showClubs && prev.showSpecialists && prev.showEvents
        ? prev
        : { showClubs: true, showSpecialists: true, showEvents: true }
    );
  }, []);

  const toggleMapLayer = useCallback((layerKey: keyof IMapLayerVisibility) => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setMapLayers((prev) => ({ ...prev, [layerKey]: !prev[layerKey] }));
  }, []);

  const syncUserGpsLocation = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!exploreRealGpsCache && isMountedRef.current) setIsAcquiringRealGps(true);
    try {
      const { status } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
      if (status !== 'granted') {
        exploreRealGpsCache = null;
        if (isMountedRef.current) setHasRealUserCoords(false);
        return;
      }
      const loc = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({ accuracy: Location.Accuracy.Balanced });
      if (loc?.coords) {
        const { latitude, longitude } = loc.coords;
        if (isCoordsInsideLosCabos(latitude, longitude)) {
          exploreRealGpsCache = { latitude, longitude };
          setCanonicalUserGpsOrigin({ latitude, longitude }, true);
          if (isMountedRef.current) {
            setUserCoords({ latitude, longitude });
            setHasRealUserCoords(true);
          }
          return;
        }
      }
      if (isMountedRef.current && !exploreRealGpsCache) setHasRealUserCoords(false);
    } catch {
      if (isMountedRef.current && !exploreRealGpsCache) setHasRealUserCoords(false);
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setIsAcquiringRealGps(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (exploreRealGpsCache) { setIsAcquiringRealGps(false); return; }
    const fallbackTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setIsAcquiringRealGps(false);
    }, 4500);
    return () => clearTimeout(fallbackTimer);
  }, []);

  const loadExploreData = useCallback(
    async (isSilent = false, force = false) => {
      if (inFlightPromiseRef.current) {
        if (!force) return inFlightPromiseRef.current;
        await inFlightPromiseRef.current.catch(() => {});
      }
      const hasCached = Boolean(exploreMemoryCache?.length || lastValidDataRef.current?.length);
      if (!force && hasCached && Date.now() - exploreLastFetchedAt < EXPLORE_EGRESS_TTL_MS) {
        hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setHasLoadedInitial((p) => (p ? p : true));
          setIsLoading((p) => (p ? false : p));
          setRefreshing((p) => (p ? false : p));
        }
        return;
      }
      if (!isSilent && !hasLoadedInitialRef.current && !hasCached && isMountedRef.current) setIsLoading(true);
      const runTask = (async () => {
        try {
          if (force) exploreService.clearRadarCache();
          const data = await exploreService.fetchRadarData(isAdminView, force);
          const nextAfiliados = (data.afiliados || []).filter((a: any) => Boolean(a) && !isBannedAffiliateRecord(a));
          const nextServicios = hydrateServicesGpsWithClubCoords(
            filterValidRadarServices(reconcileActiveLiveServicesSnapshot((data.servicios || []).filter(Boolean))),
            nextAfiliados
          );
          exploreMemoryCache = nextAfiliados;
          exploreServiciosMemoryCache = nextServicios;
          lastValidDataRef.current = nextAfiliados;
          exploreLastFetchedAt = Date.now();
          if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
          setAfiliadosRaw(nextAfiliados);
          setServiciosRaw(nextServicios);
        } catch {
          // Silencioso
        } finally {
          inFlightPromiseRef.current = null;
          hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
          if (isMountedRef.current) {
            setHasLoadedInitial((p) => (p ? p : true));
            setIsLoading((p) => (p ? false : p));
            setRefreshing((p) => (p ? false : p));
          }
        }
      })();
      inFlightPromiseRef.current = runTask;
      return runTask;
    },
    [isAdminView]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const hasWarmMem = Boolean(exploreMemoryCache?.length);
    if (!hasWarmMem) {
      hydrateExploreSWRFromStorage().then((snap) => {
        if (!snap || exploreMemoryCache?.length) return;
        const safeAf = (snap.afiliados || []).filter((a: any) => Boolean(a) && !isBannedAffiliateRecord(a));
        const safeSv = hydrateServicesGpsWithClubCoords(
          filterValidRadarServices((snap.servicios || []).filter(Boolean)),
          safeAf
        );
        if (safeAf.length === 0) return;
        exploreMemoryCache = safeAf;
        exploreServiciosMemoryCache = safeSv;
        lastValidDataRef.current = safeAf;
        hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setAfiliadosRaw(safeAf);
          setServiciosRaw(safeSv);
          setHasLoadedInitial(true);
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }).catch(() => {});
    }
    loadExploreData(hasWarmMem, false);
    const debouncedMutate = createDebouncedEventRunner(() => {
      exploreLastFetchedAt = 0;
      loadExploreData(true, true);
    }, 150);
    const unsubRadar = subscribeKineticRadarUpdated(debouncedMutate.trigger);
    const unsubGhost = registerGhostPurgeHook('useExplorar', (cleanId) => {
      if (exploreServiciosMemoryCache) {
        exploreServiciosMemoryCache = filterOutServiceId(exploreServiciosMemoryCache, cleanId);
      }
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setServiciosRaw((prev) => filterOutServiceId(prev, cleanId));
      }
    });
    const hasWin = typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.addEventListener === 'function';
    if (hasWin) window.addEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedMutate.trigger);
    return () => {
      debouncedMutate.cancel();
      unsubRadar();
      unsubGhost();
      if (hasWin) window.removeEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedMutate.trigger);
    };
  }, [loadExploreData]);

  useEffect(() => {
    expandShortMapsUrlsInBackground(serviciosRaw, () => {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setExpandedUrlTick((t) => t + 1);
    });
  }, [serviciosRaw]);

  const afiliadosEnriquecidos = useMemo(
    () => buildEnrichedAffiliates(afiliadosRaw, serviciosRaw, userCoords, favoriteIds),
    [afiliadosRaw, serviciosRaw, userCoords, favoriteIds]
  );

  const categoryCountsMap = useMemo(() => {
    const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
    afiliadosEnriquecidos.forEach((af: any) => {
      const isClubActive = af.estado === 'activo' && af.esClubActivo === true && Boolean(af.esActivo);
      if (!isClubActive) return;
      const list: string[] = Array.isArray(af.allDisciplines)
        ? af.allDisciplines
        : [af.disciplina].filter(Boolean);
      const seenForClub = new Set<string>();
      list.forEach((d) => {
        const clean = String(d || '').trim();
        if (clean && !seenForClub.has(clean)) {
          seenForClub.add(clean);
          counts[clean] = (counts[clean] || 0) + 1;
        }
      });
    });
    return counts;
  }, [afiliadosEnriquecidos, isAdminView]);

  const compareAffiliates = useExploreCompareAffiliates(sortBy, quickChip);

  const filteredAffiliates = useMemo(() => {
    const q = normalizeRadarText(searchQuery);
    const allZones = isAllCaboZone(selectedZone);
    const zoneMode = allZones ? { mode: 'ALL' as const } : resolveZoneFilterMode(selectedZone);

    const list = afiliadosEnriquecidos.filter((af: any) => {
      const isClubActive = af.estado === 'activo' && af.esClubActivo === true && Boolean(af.esActivo);
      if (!isClubActive) return false;

      if (!allZones) {
        if (zoneMode.mode === 'NEAR_10KM') {
          if (hasRealUserCoords && typeof af.distanciaKm === 'number' && af.distanciaKm > KINETIC_RADAR_RADIUS_KM) return false;
        } else if (zoneMode.mode === 'ZONE' && af.zoneKey !== zoneMode.zoneKey) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      const clubDiscs: string[] = Array.isArray(af.allDisciplines)
        ? af.allDisciplines
        : [af.disciplina].filter(Boolean);
      if (q && !matchesCleanExploreSearch(af, q)) return false;
      if (quickChip === 'fitness' && (Boolean(af.es_especialista) || isWellnessCategory(af.disciplina))) {
        return false;
      }
      if (
        quickChip === 'wellness' &&
        !Boolean(af.es_especialista) &&
        !clubDiscs.some((cd) => isWellnessCategory(cd))
      ) {
        return false;
      }
      if (quickChip === 'favoritos' && !af.isFavorite) return false;
      if (quickChip === 'mas_buscados' && !af.isTrending) return false;
      if (selectedCategories.length > 0) {
        const matchesAny = selectedCategories.some((sel) =>
          clubDiscs.some((cd) => cd.toLowerCase() === sel.toLowerCase())
        );
        if (!matchesAny) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
    const sorted = [...list].sort(compareAffiliates);
    return q ? deduplicateAffiliatesList(sorted) : sorted;
  }, [
    afiliadosEnriquecidos,
    isAdminView,
    selectedZone,
    hasRealUserCoords,
    searchQuery,
    quickChip,
    selectedCategories,
    compareAffiliates,
  ]);

  const sectionsByDiscipline = useMemo(() => {
    const rawSections = buildSectionsByDiscipline(
      filteredAffiliates,
      selectedCategories,
      quickChip,
      sortBy,
      compareAffiliates
    );
    return selectedCategories.length === 0 || searchQuery.trim().length > 0
      ? deduplicateSectionsForSearch(rawSections)
      : rawSections;
  }, [filteredAffiliates, selectedCategories, quickChip, sortBy, compareAffiliates, searchQuery]);

  const mapMarkers = useMemo(() => {
    void expandedUrlTick;
    const baseMarkers = buildExploreMapMarkers(filteredAffiliates, mapLayers, isAdminView);
    return buildCompleteExploreMapMarkers({
      baseMarkers, serviciosRaw, afiliadosEnriquecidos, mapLayers, selectedZone,
      hasRealUserCoords, userCoords, searchQuery, selectedCategories, quickChip, isAdminView,
    });
  }, [
    filteredAffiliates, mapLayers, isAdminView, serviciosRaw, afiliadosEnriquecidos,
    selectedZone, hasRealUserCoords, userCoords, searchQuery, selectedCategories, quickChip, expandedUrlTick,
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isSupabaseQuotaExceeded()) return;
    const topCoverUrls = filteredAffiliates.slice(0, 5).map((af: any) =>
      af?.portada_url || af?.fallbackImage || resolveCategoryImage(af?.disciplina || 'Fuerza', af?.categoria)
    );
    const pinAvatarUrls = mapMarkers.map((m) => m.avatar_url).slice(0, 8);
    const urlsToWarm = Array.from(new Set([...topCoverUrls, ...pinAvatarUrls])).filter(
      (u): u is string => Boolean(u && !WARMED_PIN_URLS.has(u))
    );
    if (urlsToWarm.length > 0) {
      ExpoImage.prefetch(urlsToWarm, 'memory-disk')
        .then(() => { urlsToWarm.forEach((u) => WARMED_PIN_URLS.add(u)); })
        .catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [filteredAffiliates, mapMarkers]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    exploreMemoryCache = null;
    exploreServiciosMemoryCache = null;
    exploreLastFetchedAt = 0;
    exploreService.clearRadarCache();
    try {
      await Promise.all([loadExploreData(true, true), syncUserGpsLocation()]);
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, [loadExploreData, syncUserGpsLocation]);

  return {
    isLoading, hasLoadedInitial, refreshing, isAdminView, afiliadosRaw, afiliadosEnriquecidos, filteredAffiliates,
    sectionsByDiscipline, categoryCountsMap, mapMarkers, favoriteIds, toggleFavorite,
    searchQuery, setSearchQuery, selectedCategories, setSelectedCategories, quickChip, setQuickChip,
    sortBy, setSortBy, viewMode, setViewMode, selectedZone, setSelectedZone,
    mapLayers, toggleMapLayer, resetMapLayers, userCoords, hasRealUserCoords, isAcquiringRealGps, syncUserGpsLocation, onRefresh, loadExploreData,
  };
}

export default useExplorar;

import { TRENDING_CATEGORIES, isWellnessCategory, resolveCategoryImage } from '../constants/exploreData';
import { isRealSupabaseJwt } from './kineticSessionPersistence';

const KINETIC_OFFICIAL_LOGO = require('../../assets/logo.png');
const KINETIC_OFFICIAL_DEFAULT_LOCATION = 'Los Cabos, B.C.S.';

const BANNED_EXACT_NAMES = new Set<string>([
  'kinetic oficial',
  'sede kinetic',
  'sede oficial kinetic',
  'terminal global kinetic',
]);

const BANNED_SUBSTRINGS = ['atleta 5975', 'atleta kinetic'];

const ADMIN_EMAILS = new Set<string>([
  'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
  'administrador@kinetic.com',
  'kinetic@kinetic.com',
  'admin@kinetic.com',
]);

const ADMIN_ROLES = new Set<string>(['admin', 'fundador', 'ceo']);

const INACTIVE_ESTADOS = new Set<string>([
  'inactivo',
  'prospecto',
  'oculto',
  'pendiente',
  'suspendido',
  'cancelado',
  'rechazado',
  'eliminado',
  'bloqueado',
]);

export const normalizeRadarText = (val?: unknown): string =>
  String(val ?? '')
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');

export const canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey = (rawName?: unknown): string => {
  const norm = normalizeRadarText(rawName).replace(/&/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
  if (norm === 'palestra club' || norm.includes('palesttra')) return 'palesttra club';
  if (/baja\s+fitness.*medano/.test(norm)) return 'baja fitness medano';
  if (norm.includes('incorfit')) return 'incorfit barre & reformer studio';
  if (norm.includes('cabo yoga')) return 'cabo yoga & fitness center';
  if (norm.includes('el tule fitness')) return 'el tule fitness & crossfit';
  if (norm.includes('elemental strength')) return 'elemental strength studio';
  if (norm.includes('truefit cabo')) return 'truefit cabo studio';
  if (norm.includes('f45 training san jose')) return 'f45 training san jose del cabo';
  if (norm.includes('fitbox')) return 'fitbox cabo boxing studio';
  if (norm.includes('fortress fitness')) return 'fortress fitness gym';
  if (norm.includes('seal cross')) return 'seal crosstraining';
  if (norm.includes('seis24')) return 'seis24 strength & conditioning';
  if (norm.includes('tropical fitness')) return 'tropical fitness center';
  if (norm.includes('cagers')) return 'cagers gym mma & muay thai';
  return norm;
};

export const isKineticOfficialServiceClub = (rawName?: unknown): boolean =>
  normalizeRadarText(rawName) === 'kinetic oficial';

export const isBannedAffiliateName = (rawName?: unknown): boolean => {
  const norm = normalizeRadarText(rawName);
  if (!norm) return true;
  if (BANNED_EXACT_NAMES.has(norm)) return true;
  return BANNED_SUBSTRINGS.some((sub) => norm.includes(sub));
};

export const isBannedAffiliateRecord = (raw: any): boolean => {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') return true;
  if (isBannedAffiliateName(raw.nombre)) return true;
  const idNorm = normalizeRadarText(raw.id);
  if (idNorm === 'kinetic-oficial' || idNorm === 'sede-kinetic' || idNorm === 'terminal-global-kinetic') {
    return true;
  }
  const rawEst = normalizeRadarText(raw.estado);
  if (rawEst === 'eliminado' || raw.eliminado === true) return true;
  return false;
};

export const sanitizeImageUrl = (val: unknown): string => {
  if (typeof val !== 'string') return '';
  const trimmed = val.trim();
  if (!trimmed || trimmed.length < 8) return '';
  const low = trimmed.toLowerCase();
  if (
    low === 'null' ||
    low === 'undefined' ||
    low === 'none' ||
    low === 'false' ||
    low === 'nan' ||
    low.includes('[object')
  ) {
    return '';
  }
  if (
    /^https?:\/\//i.test(trimmed) ||
    trimmed.startsWith('data:image/') ||
    trimmed.startsWith('blob:') ||
    trimmed.startsWith('file:') ||
    (trimmed.startsWith('/') && !trimmed.startsWith('//'))
  ) {
    return trimmed;
  }
  return '';
};

export const resolveStrictAffiliateStatus = (
  rawEstadoInput: unknown,
  fallbackActive?: unknown
): {
  estado: string;
  esClubActivo: boolean;
  esActivo: boolean;
  activo: boolean;
} => {
  const rawEstado = normalizeRadarText(rawEstadoInput);
  if (rawEstado === 'activo') {
    return { estado: 'activo', esClubActivo: true, esActivo: true, activo: true };
  }
  if (INACTIVE_ESTADOS.has(rawEstado)) {
    return { estado: rawEstado, esClubActivo: false, esActivo: false, activo: false };
  }
  if (!rawEstado && fallbackActive === true) {
    return { estado: 'activo', esClubActivo: true, esActivo: true, activo: true };
  }
  return {
    estado: rawEstado || 'inactivo',
    esClubActivo: false,
    esActivo: false,
    activo: false,
  };
};

export const isExploreAdminUser = (user?: any, profile?: any, accessToken?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (accessToken !== undefined && !isRealSupabaseJwt(accessToken)) return false;
  const emailCandidates = [user?.email, profile?.email]
    .map((e) => String(e || '').trim().toLowerCase())
    .filter(Boolean);
  if (emailCandidates.some((em) => ADMIN_EMAILS.has(em))) return true;

  const roleCandidates = [
    profile?.rol,
    profile?.tipo_cuenta,
    user?.app_metadata?.rol,
  ]
    .map((r) => normalizeRadarText(r))
    .filter(Boolean);

  return roleCandidates.some((r) => ADMIN_ROLES.has(r));
};

export const computeExactHaversineKm = (
  lat1: number,
  lon1: number,
  lat2?: number | null,
  lon2?: number | null
): number => {
  if (
    typeof lat1 !== 'number' ||
    typeof lon1 !== 'number' ||
    typeof lat2 !== 'number' ||
    typeof lon2 !== 'number' ||
    Number.isNaN(lat1) ||
    Number.isNaN(lon1) ||
    Number.isNaN(lat2) ||
    Number.isNaN(lon2)
  ) {
    return 0;
  }
  const R = 6371;
  const dLat = ((lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dLon = ((lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const a =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) +
    Math.cos((lat1 * Math.PI) / 180) *
      Math.cos((lat2 * Math.PI) / 180) *
      Math.sin(dLon / 2) *
      Math.sin(dLon / 2);
  const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - a)));
  return Math.max(0, Math.round(R * c * 100) / 100);
};

export const isCoordsInsideLosCabos = (lat?: number | null, lon?: number | null): boolean =>
  typeof lat === 'number' &&
  typeof lon === 'number' &&
  !Number.isNaN(lat) &&
  !Number.isNaN(lon) &&
  lat >= 22.75 &&
  lat <= 23.32 &&
  lon >= -110.22 &&
  lon <= -109.42;

} from './exploreZoneHelpers';

const formatLocalIsoDate = (d: Date): string =>
  `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

const parseServiceRecurrence = (serv: any): 'Única Vez' | 'Diario' | 'Semanal' | 'Mensual' => {
  const direct = normalizeRadarText(serv?.frecuencia);
  if (direct === 'diario' || direct === 'daily' || direct === 'diaria') return 'Diario';
  if (direct === 'semanal' || direct === 'weekly') return 'Semanal';
  if (direct === 'mensual' || direct === 'monthly') return 'Mensual';

  const desc = String(serv?.descripcion || '');
  const match = desc.match(/\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
  if (match?.[1]) {
    const parsed = normalizeRadarText(match[1]);
    if (parsed.includes('diario') || parsed.includes('daily')) return 'Diario';
    if (parsed.includes('semanal') || parsed.includes('weekly')) return 'Semanal';
    if (parsed.includes('mensual') || parsed.includes('monthly')) return 'Mensual';
  }
  return 'Única Vez';
};

const parseServiceLocalHourMinute = (
  serv: any
): { hh: number; mm: number; hasExplicitTime: boolean } => {
  const raw = String(serv?.hora_inicio || serv?.hora || '').trim();
  if (!raw) return { hh: 23, mm: 59, hasExplicitTime: false };
  const m = raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?\s*(a\.?m\.?|p\.?m\.?)?/i);
  if (!m) return { hh: 23, mm: 59, hasExplicitTime: false };
  let hh = parseInt(m[1], 10);
  const mm = Math.max(0, Math.min(59, parseInt(m[2], 10) || 0));
  const ampm = (m[3] || '').toLowerCase();
  if (ampm.startsWith('p') && hh < 12) hh += 12;
  if (ampm.startsWith('a') && hh === 12) hh = 0;
  hh = Math.max(0, Math.min(23, Number.isNaN(hh) ? 8 : hh));
  return { hh, mm, hasExplicitTime: true };
};

export const filterValidRadarServices = (rawServices: any[], nowRef?: Date): any[] => {
  if (!Array.isArray(rawServices) || rawServices.length === 0) return [];
  const now = nowRef || new Date();
  const nowMs = now.getTime();
  const todayIso = formatLocalIsoDate(now);
  const valid: Array<{ item: any; sortTs: number }> = [];

  for (const serv of rawServices) {
    if (!serv || typeof serv !== 'object') continue;
    if (serv.activo === false) continue;
    const estado = normalizeRadarText(serv.estado);
    if (estado !== 'aprobado' && estado !== 'activo') continue;
    if (serv.eliminado === true || serv.atributos?.eliminado === true || serv.atributos?.cancelado === true) {
      continue;
    }

    const rawNegocio = String(serv.negocio_nombre || '').trim();
    const isOfficialKinetic = isKineticOfficialServiceClub(rawNegocio);
    const negocioNombre = isOfficialKinetic ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : rawNegocio;
    if (!negocioNombre || (!isOfficialKinetic && isBannedAffiliateName(negocioNombre))) continue;

    const titulo = String(serv.nombre_servicio || serv.titulo || '').trim();
    const normTitulo = normalizeRadarText(titulo);
    if (!normTitulo || normTitulo.includes('borrado') || normTitulo.includes('eliminado')) continue;

    const endDateRaw = String(serv.fecha_fin || serv.atributos?.fecha_fin || '').trim().slice(0, 10);
    if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(endDateRaw) && endDateRaw < todayIso) continue;

    const freq = parseServiceRecurrence(serv);
    const dateStr = String(serv.fecha || '').trim().slice(0, 10);
    const hasValidDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateStr);
    const { hh, mm, hasExplicitTime } = parseServiceLocalHourMinute(serv);

    let nextDateStr = dateStr;
    let nextTs = 0;

    if (freq === 'Única Vez') {
      if (!hasValidDate) continue;
      const [y, mo, d] = dateStr.split('-').map(Number);
      const localDt = new Date(y, mo - 1, d, hh, mm, hasExplicitTime ? 0 : 59);
      const ts = localDt.getTime();
      if (Number.isNaN(ts) || ts <= nowMs) continue;
      nextTs = ts;
    } else {
      const baseStr = hasValidDate ? dateStr : todayIso;
      const [bY, bM, bD] = baseStr.split('-').map(Number);
      const candidate = new Date(bY, bM - 1, bD, hh, mm, hasExplicitTime ? 0 : 59);
      if (Number.isNaN(candidate.getTime())) continue;

      if (candidate.getTime() > nowMs) {
        nextDateStr = baseStr;
        nextTs = candidate.getTime();
      } else if (freq === 'Diario') {
        const nextD = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), hh, mm, 0);
        if (nextD.getTime() <= nowMs) nextD.setDate(nextD.getDate() + 1);
        nextDateStr = formatLocalIsoDate(nextD);
        nextTs = nextD.getTime();
      } else if (freq === 'Semanal') {
        const nextW = new Date(candidate.getTime());
        while (nextW.getTime() <= nowMs) nextW.setDate(nextW.getDate() + 7);
        nextDateStr = formatLocalIsoDate(nextW);
        nextTs = nextW.getTime();
      } else {
        const nextM = new Date(candidate.getTime());
        while (nextM.getTime() <= nowMs) nextM.setMonth(nextM.getMonth() + 1);
        nextDateStr = formatLocalIsoDate(nextM);
        nextTs = nextM.getTime();
      }
      if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(endDateRaw) && nextDateStr > endDateRaw) continue;
    }

    const fallbackEvImg = resolveCategoryImage(serv.categoria || 'Fuerza', titulo);
    const resolvedImg =
      sanitizeImageUrl(serv.imagen_evento_url) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(serv.imagen_url) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(serv.portada_url) ||
      fallbackEvImg;

    const resolvedUbicacion =
      String(serv.ubicacion || '').trim() ||
      (isOfficialKinetic ? KINETIC_OFFICIAL_DEFAULT_LOCATION : serv.ubicacion);

    valid.push({
      sortTs: nextTs,
      item: {
        ...serv,
        negocio_nombre: negocioNombre,
        ...(isOfficialKinetic
          ? {
              esKineticOficial: true,
              esClubActivo: true,
              clubEstado: 'activo',
              officialLogoAsset: KINETIC_OFFICIAL_LOGO,
              ubicacion: resolvedUbicacion,
              lugar: String(serv.lugar || '').trim() || resolvedUbicacion,
            }
          : {}),
        fecha: nextDateStr,
        fecha_proyectada: nextDateStr,
        fechaInicioOriginal: hasValidDate ? dateStr : nextDateStr,
        imagen_url: sanitizeImageUrl(serv.imagen_url) || resolvedImg,
        portada_url: sanitizeImageUrl(serv.portada_url) || resolvedImg,
        imagen_evento_url: resolvedImg,
        fallbackImage: fallbackEvImg,
      },
    });
  }

  valid.sort((a, b) => a.sortTs - b.sortTs);
  return valid.map((v) => v.item);
};

export const buildSectionsByDiscipline = (
  filteredAffiliates: any[],
  selectedCategories: string[],
  quickChip: string,
  sortBy: string,
  compareAffiliates: (a: any, b: any) => number
): any[] => {
  const map = new Map<string, any[]>();
  const seenBySec = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  const pushToSection = (secName: string, af: any) => {
    const cleanSec = String(secName || '').trim();
    if (!cleanSec) return;
    if (!seenBySec.has(cleanSec)) seenBySec.set(cleanSec, new Set());
    const seen = seenBySec.get(cleanSec)!;
    const afKey = String(af.id || af.nombre);
    if (seen.has(afKey)) return;
    seen.add(afKey);
    map.set(cleanSec, [...(map.get(cleanSec) || []), af]);
  };

  (filteredAffiliates || []).forEach((af: any) => {
    const clubDiscs: string[] =
      Array.isArray(af.allDisciplines) && af.allDisciplines.length > 0
        ? af.allDisciplines
        : [af.disciplina].filter(Boolean);
    if (selectedCategories.length > 0) {
      selectedCategories.forEach((sel) => {
        if (clubDiscs.some((cd) => cd.toLowerCase() === sel.toLowerCase())) pushToSection(sel, af);
      });
    } else {
      clubDiscs.forEach((disc) => {
        if (quickChip === 'fitness' && isWellnessCategory(disc)) return;
        if (quickChip === 'wellness' && !isWellnessCategory(disc) && !af.es_especialista) return;
        pushToSection(disc, af);
      });
    }
  });

  const sections = Array.from(map.entries()).map(([title, items]) => {
    const sortedData = [...items].sort(compareAffiliates);
    const totalExp = sortedData.reduce((acc, it) => acc + (it.experiencias?.length || 0), 0);
    const maxScore = sortedData.reduce((acc, it) => Math.max(acc, Number(it.trendingScore) || 0), 0);
    return {
      title,
      discipline: title,
      isTrending: TRENDING_CATEGORIES.includes(title) || totalExp > 0,
      isTopSearched: TRENDING_CATEGORIES.includes(title) || totalExp > 0,
      data: sortedData,
      items: sortedData,
      topItem: sortedData[0],
      totalExp,
      maxScore,
    };
  });

  return sections.sort((sA, sB) => {
    if (sortBy === 'az') return sA.title.localeCompare(sB.title, 'es');
    if (sortBy === 'za') return sB.title.localeCompare(sA.title, 'es');
    if (sortBy === 'disponibilidad' || (sortBy === 'relevancia' && quickChip === 'mas_buscados')) {
      if (sB.totalExp !== sA.totalExp) return sB.totalExp - sA.totalExp;
      if (sB.maxScore !== sA.maxScore) return sB.maxScore - sA.maxScore;
    }
    if (['distancia', 'rating_desc', 'rating_asc', 'precio_asc', 'precio_desc'].includes(sortBy)) {
      return compareAffiliates(sA.topItem, sB.topItem);
    }
    return sA.title.localeCompare(sB.title, 'es');
  });
};
