const normalizeAffiliateRecord = (raw: any, fallbackIdx = 0, fromSupabaseDb = false): any => {
  const nombre = String(raw?.nombre || '').trim();
  const parsed = extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule(raw);
  const rawDisc = String(raw?.disciplina || '').trim();
  const isSpec = Boolean(
    raw?.es_especialista || String(raw?.categoria || '').toLowerCase().includes('especialista')
  );
  const disciplina =
    rawDisc && !['club', 'especialista'].includes(rawDisc.toLowerCase())
      ? rawDisc
      : isSpec
        ? 'Fisioterapia'
        : 'Fuerza';
  const categoria = raw?.categoria || (isSpec ? 'Especialista' : 'Club');
  const statusInfo = resolveStrictAffiliateStatus(
    raw?.estado,
    !fromSupabaseDb && (raw?.esClubActivo === true || raw?.activo === true)
  );
  const fallbackImage = resolveCategoryImage(disciplina, categoria);
  const portadaResolved = sanitizeImageUrl(raw?.portada_url) || fallbackImage;
  const avatarResolved =
    sanitizeImageUrl(raw?.avatar_url) || sanitizeImageUrl(raw?.logo_url) || fallbackImage;
  const imagenResolved = sanitizeImageUrl(raw?.imagen) || portadaResolved || fallbackImage;

  return {
    ...raw,
    id: String(raw?.id || `${nombre || 'af'}_${fallbackIdx}`),
    nombre,
    disciplina,
    categoria,
    es_especialista: isSpec,
    ubicacion: String(raw?.ubicacion || '').trim() || 'Ubicación Verificada',
    horarios: parsed.horarios,
    instagram: parsed.instagram,
    whatsapp: String(raw?.whatsapp || '').trim(),
    amenidades: parsed.amenidades ?? raw?.amenidades,
    portada_url: portadaResolved,
    avatar_url: avatarResolved,
    imagen: imagenResolved,
    fallbackImage,
    estado: statusInfo.estado,
    esClubActivo: statusInfo.esClubActivo,
    activo: statusInfo.activo,
    esActivo: statusInfo.esActivo,
    _fromSupabaseDb: fromSupabaseDb,
  };
};

const hydrateServicesGps = (services: any[], affiliates: any[] = []): any[] => {
  const clubByName = new Map<string, any>();
  affiliates.forEach((af) => {
    const k = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(af?.nombre);
    if (k) clubByName.set(k, af);
  });
  return (services || []).map((srv: any) => {
    const af = clubByName.get(canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(srv?.negocio_nombre));
    const rawLoc = `${srv?.ubicacion || ''} ${srv?.atributos?.ubicacion || ''} ${srv?.atributos?.maps_url || ''} ${af?.ubicacion || ''}`.trim();
    const gps = extractSyncEventGpsCoordinates(
      rawLoc,
      srv?.latitud ?? srv?.latitude ?? srv?.atributos?.latitud ?? af?.latitud ?? af?.latitude,
      srv?.longitud ?? srv?.longitude ?? srv?.atributos?.longitud ?? af?.longitud ?? af?.longitude
    );
    return { ...srv, latitud: gps.latitud, longitud: gps.longitud, latitude: gps.latitud, longitude: gps.longitud };
  });
};

export const exploreService = {
  clearRadarCache() {
    _memoryRadarSnapshot = null;
    _clubGalleryCache.clear();
  },

  // 1. CARGA DE AFILIADOS Y EXPERIENCIAS ACTIVAS DEL RADAR (SUPABASE = ÚNICA FUENTE DE VERDAD)
  async fetchRadarData(_isAdminView = false, forceFresh = false) {
    if (forceFresh) {
      _memoryRadarSnapshot = null;
    }

    const readFallbackSnapshot = async () => {
      if (_memoryRadarSnapshot?.afiliados?.length) {
        const cleanAf = _memoryRadarSnapshot.afiliados.filter((a) => !isBannedAffiliateRecord(a));
        const cleanSv = (_memoryRadarSnapshot.servicios || []).filter((s: any) => !isPurgedGhostServiceId(s?.id));
        return { afiliados: cleanAf, servicios: hydrateServicesGps(filterValidRadarServices(cleanSv), cleanAf) };
      }
      try {
        const rawSnap = await AsyncStorage.getItem(RADAR_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (rawSnap) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(rawSnap);
          const snapAfil = Array.isArray(parsed?.afiliados) ? parsed.afiliados.filter((a: any) => !isBannedAffiliateRecord(a)) : [];
          if (snapAfil.length > 0) {
            const rawSv = Array.isArray(parsed?.servicios) ? parsed.servicios.filter((s: any) => !isPurgedGhostServiceId(s?.id)) : [];
            _memoryRadarSnapshot = { afiliados: snapAfil, servicios: hydrateServicesGps(filterValidRadarServices(rawSv), snapAfil) };
            return _memoryRadarSnapshot;
          }
        }
      } catch {}
      const staticFallback = (KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES || [])
        .filter((st: any) => !isBannedAffiliateRecord(st))
        .map((st: any, idx: number) => normalizeAffiliateRecord(st, idx, false));
      return { afiliados: staticFallback, servicios: [] };
    };

    if (isSupabaseQuotaExceeded()) {
      return readFallbackSnapshot();
    }

    let afilReq: any = { data: null, error: null };
    let servReq: any = { data: null, error: null };

    try {
      [afilReq, servReq] = await Promise.all([
        supabase.from('afiliados').select(AFILIADOS_RADAR_COLS).limit(200),
        supabase
          .from('servicios')
          .select(SERVICIOS_RADAR_COLS)
          .in('estado', ['activo', 'aprobado'])
          .order('fecha', { ascending: false })
          .limit(150),
      ]);
      if (servReq?.error && !isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(servReq.error)) {
        servReq = await supabase
          .from('servicios')
          .select(SERVICIOS_RADAR_COLS_LEGACY)
          .in('estado', ['activo', 'aprobado'])
          .order('fecha', { ascending: false })
          .limit(150);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(err)) setSupabaseQuotaExceeded(true);
      return readFallbackSnapshot();
    }

    if (isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(afilReq?.error) || isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(servReq?.error)) {
      setSupabaseQuotaExceeded(true);
      return readFallbackSnapshot();
    }
    if (afilReq?.error) {
      return readFallbackSnapshot();
    }

    const rawDbRows = (Array.isArray(afilReq?.data) ? afilReq.data : []).filter(
      (row: any) => !isBannedAffiliateRecord(row)
    );
    const reconciledLiveServices = reconcileActiveLiveServicesSnapshot(servReq?.data || []);
    const approvedServices = hydrateServicesGps(filterValidRadarServices(reconciledLiveServices), rawDbRows);

    if (rawDbRows.length === 0) {
      const emptyRes = { afiliados: [], servicios: approvedServices };
      _memoryRadarSnapshot = emptyRes;
      return emptyRes;
    }

    void reconcileClubOverridesWithSupabase(rawDbRows);

    const catalogByName = new Map<string, any>();
    const catalogById = new Map<string, any>();
    (KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES || []).forEach((st: any) => {
      if (!st || isBannedAffiliateRecord(st)) return;
      const nKey = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(st.nombre);
      const idKey = normalizeRadarText(st.id);
      if (nKey) catalogByName.set(nKey, st);
      if (idKey) catalogById.set(idKey, st);
    });

    const dedupedByKey = new Map<string, any>();
    rawDbRows.forEach((dbRow: any, idx: number) => {
      const nameKey = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(dbRow?.nombre);
      const idKey = normalizeRadarText(dbRow?.id);
      const st = (idKey && catalogById.get(idKey)) || (nameKey && catalogByName.get(nameKey)) || null;
      const parsedSt = st ? extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule(st) : null;
      const parsedDb = extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule(dbRow);

      const enrichedDbRow = {
        ...dbRow,
        disciplina: dbRow.disciplina || st?.disciplina,
        categoria: dbRow.categoria || st?.categoria,
        es_especialista: dbRow.es_especialista ?? st?.es_especialista,
        ubicacion: String(dbRow.ubicacion || '').trim() || st?.ubicacion || 'Ubicación Verificada',
        horarios: parsedDb.horarios || parsedSt?.horarios || '',
        instagram: parsedDb.instagram || parsedSt?.instagram || '',
        whatsapp: String(dbRow.whatsapp || '').trim() || String(st?.whatsapp || '').trim(),
        amenidades:
          Array.isArray(parsedDb.amenidades) && parsedDb.amenidades.length > 0
            ? parsedDb.amenidades
            : st?.amenidades,
        disciplinas_secundarias:
          Array.isArray(dbRow.disciplinas_secundarias) && dbRow.disciplinas_secundarias.length > 0
            ? dbRow.disciplinas_secundarias
            : st?.disciplinas_secundarias,
        latitud: dbRow.latitud ?? dbRow.latitude ?? st?.latitude,
        longitud: dbRow.longitud ?? dbRow.longitude ?? st?.longitude,
        portada_url: sanitizeImageUrl(dbRow.portada_url) || sanitizeImageUrl(st?.portada_url),
        avatar_url:
          sanitizeImageUrl(dbRow.avatar_url) ||
          sanitizeImageUrl(dbRow.logo_url) ||
          sanitizeImageUrl(st?.avatar_url),
        galeria:
          Array.isArray(dbRow.galeria) && dbRow.galeria.length > 0
            ? dbRow.galeria
            : Array.isArray(st?.galeria)
              ? st.galeria
              : undefined,
      };

      const normalized = normalizeAffiliateRecord(enrichedDbRow, idx, true);
      const dedupeKey = nameKey || idKey || `idx_${idx}`;
      const existing = dedupedByKey.get(dedupeKey);
      if (!existing || (!existing.esClubActivo && normalized.esClubActivo)) {
        dedupedByKey.set(dedupeKey, normalized);
      }
    });

    const mergedAffiliates = Array.from(dedupedByKey.values());
    const result = { afiliados: mergedAffiliates, servicios: approvedServices };
    _memoryRadarSnapshot = result;
    AsyncStorage.setItem(RADAR_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(result)).catch(() => {});
    return result;
  },

  // 1B. CARGA BAJO DEMANDA DE GALERÍA INDIVIDUAL (SOLO AL ABRIR DETALLE DE SEDE)
  async fetchClubGalleryOnDemand(clubId?: string): Promise<{ galeria: string[]; comentarios: any[] } | null> {
    const cleanId = String(clubId || '').trim();
    if (!cleanId || !/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(cleanId)) return null;
    const cached = _clubGalleryCache.get(cleanId);
    if (cached) return cached;
    if (isSupabaseQuotaExceeded()) return null;
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('afiliados')
        .select('id, galeria, comentarios')
        .eq('id', cleanId)
        .maybeSingle();
      if (error) {
        if (isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(error)) setSupabaseQuotaExceeded(true);
        return null;
      }
      const entry = {
        galeria: Array.isArray(data?.galeria) ? data.galeria : [],
        comentarios: Array.isArray(data?.comentarios) ? data.comentarios : [],
      };
      _clubGalleryCache.set(cleanId, entry);
      return entry;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  },

  // 2. FAVORITOS PERMANENTES EN SUPABASE POR USUARIO (SIN DESCARGAR TABLA PERFILES)
  async fetchUserFavorites(userId?: string): Promise<string[]> {
    if (!userId) return [];
    const localKey = `${FAV_STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX}${userId}`;

    try {
      const rawLocal = await AsyncStorage.getItem(localKey);
      if (rawLocal) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(rawLocal);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed.map(String);
      }
      if (isSupabaseQuotaExceeded()) return [];
      const authRes = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      const metaFavs = authRes.data?.user?.user_metadata?.favoritos_ids;
      if (Array.isArray(metaFavs) && metaFavs.length > 0) {
        const clean = metaFavs.map(String);
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(localKey, JSON.stringify(clean)).catch(() => {});
        return clean;
      }
      const { data: profRow } = await supabase
        .from('perfiles')
        .select('favoritos_ids')
        .eq('id', userId)
        .maybeSingle();
      const profFavs = (profRow as any)?.favoritos_ids;
      if (Array.isArray(profFavs) && profFavs.length > 0) {
        const cleanProf = profFavs.map(String);
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(localKey, JSON.stringify(cleanProf)).catch(() => {});
        return cleanProf;
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback a caché local
      try {
        const rawLocal = await AsyncStorage.getItem(localKey);
        if (rawLocal) return JSON.parse(rawLocal);
      } catch {
        // Silencioso
      }
    }

    return [];
  },

  async saveUserFavorites(userId: string, favIds: string[]): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!userId) return false;
    const cleanIds = Array.from(new Set((favIds || []).map(String)));
    const localKey = `${FAV_STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX}${userId}`;

    try {
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(localKey, JSON.stringify(cleanIds));
    } catch {
      // Silencioso
    }

    // Persistencia permanente en la nube (Supabase Auth User Metadata + tabla perfiles)
    try {
      await Promise.allSettled([
        supabase.auth.updateUser({
          data: { favoritos_ids: cleanIds },
        }),
        supabase
          .from('perfiles')
          .update({ favoritos_ids: cleanIds })
          .eq('id', userId),
      ]);
    } catch {
      // Silencioso si la columna opcional no existe aún; user_metadata siempre persiste en Supabase
    }

    return true;
  },

  // 3. PROCESAR RESERVA PROTEGIDA 40% + ANTI-COLISIÓN SPOTS 3D + CERO DESFASE UTC + PASES 0 KC
  async procesarReservaEscrow(
    userId: string,
    servicio: any,
    montoEscrow: number,
    montoPuerta: number,
    options?: {
      fechaClase?: string | null;
      selectedDate?: string | null;
      spot?: number | null;
      spotNumber?: number | null;
      splitCount?: number | null;
      split_count?: number | null;
      esPaseMaestro?: boolean;
      es_pase_maestro?: boolean;
      lang?: string;
    } | string | null,
    spotOptional?: number | null
  ) {
    const optsObj = typeof options === 'object' && options !== null ? options : null;
    const rawDate =
      (typeof options === 'string' ? options : optsObj?.fechaClase || optsObj?.selectedDate) ||
      servicio?.fecha_clase || servicio?.selectedDate || servicio?.fecha_proyectada || servicio?.fecha;
    const exactLocalDate = normalizeLocalIsoDate(rawDate);
    const lockKey = `${String(userId || '').trim()}:${String(servicio?.id || servicio?.nombre_servicio || 's')}:${exactLocalDate}`;
    if (_inFlightBookingLocks.has(lockKey)) {
      throw new Error('Reserva en proceso. Espera un momento.');
    }
    _inFlightBookingLocks.add(lockKey);
    try {
    const jwtCheck = await reconcileRealSupabaseJwtSession(supabase, 3500);
    const authUid = String(jwtCheck.user?.id || '').trim();
    const isVerifiedAdmin = Boolean(jwtCheck.user?.email_confirmed_at && isKineticMasterAdminEmail(jwtCheck.user?.email));
    if (jwtCheck.status !== 'valid' || !authUid || (authUid !== String(userId || '').trim() && !isVerifiedAdmin)) {
      throw new Error('No autorizado para reservar a nombre de otro usuario.');
    }
    const targetUserId = isVerifiedAdmin ? userId : authUid;
    const rawSid = String(servicio?.id || '').trim();
    const serviceUuidOk = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(rawSid);
    const esPaseMaestro = Boolean(isVerifiedAdmin && (optsObj?.esPaseMaestro ?? optsObj?.es_pase_maestro ?? servicio?.es_pase_maestro));
    const splitCount = Math.min(4, Math.max(1, Number(optsObj?.splitCount ?? optsObj?.split_count ?? servicio?.split_count ?? 1) || 1));
    let negocioNombre = servicio?.negocio_nombre || 'Kinetic Oficial';
    let servicioNombre = servicio?.nombre_servicio || servicio?.titulo || 'Sesión Kinetic';
    let cupoMaximo = Math.max(1, Number(servicio?.cupo_maximo) || 12);
    let costoTotal = Math.max(0, Number(montoEscrow) || 0) + Math.max(0, Number(montoPuerta) || 0);
    if (serviceUuidOk) {
      const [{ data: dbSrv, error: dbErr }, perfRes] = await Promise.all([
        supabase.from('servicios').select('id, estado, activo, negocio_nombre, nombre_servicio, precio_kc, cupo_maximo, fecha, hora_inicio, es_venta_flash, atributos').eq('id', rawSid).maybeSingle(),
        supabase.from('perfiles').select('nivel, kc_membresia').eq('id', targetUserId).maybeSingle(),
      ]);
      if (dbErr) {
        throw new Error(dbErr.message || 'No se pudo verificar el evento en el servidor.');
      }
      if (!dbSrv || isConfirmedRetiredServiceRecord(dbSrv)) {
        purgeGhostServiceEverywhere(rawSid);
        throw new Error('La experiencia seleccionada ya no existe.');
      }
      negocioNombre = String(dbSrv.negocio_nombre || negocioNombre);
      servicioNombre = String(dbSrv.nombre_servicio || servicioNombre);
      cupoMaximo = Math.max(1, Number(dbSrv.cupo_maximo) || cupoMaximo);
      const dbPrecio = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(dbSrv.precio_kc) || 0));
      if (dbPrecio > 0) assertValidKcMultipleOfTen(dbPrecio, 'ExploreReservaEscrow');
      costoTotal = esPaseMaestro ? 0 : resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing({
        event: { ...dbSrv, fecha: exactLocalDate || dbSrv.fecha, hora_inicio: servicio?.hora_inicio || dbSrv.hora_inicio },
        vipLevel: perfRes.data?.nivel, kcMembresia: perfRes.data?.kc_membresia, isAdmin: isVerifiedAdmin,
      }).discountedTotalKc;
    } else if (!esPaseMaestro && costoTotal > 0) {
      const rawBaseKc = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(servicio?.precio_kc ?? costoTotal) || 0));
      if (rawBaseKc > 0 && rawBaseKc === costoTotal) {
        assertValidKcMultipleOfTen(costoTotal, 'ExploreReservaEscrow');
      }
    }
    const spotNum =
      spotOptional ?? optsObj?.spotNumber ?? optsObj?.spot ??
      servicio?.spot_number ?? servicio?.selectedSpot ?? servicio?.spot ?? servicio?.estacion ??
      resolveClientPickedSpot(servicio?.id, servicioNombre);

    await assertLiveSpotAvailableOrThrow({
      serviceId: servicio?.id, eventKey: servicio?.id, eventTitle: servicioNombre,
      fechaClase: exactLocalDate, spotNumber: spotNum, userId: targetUserId, maxSpots: cupoMaximo, lang: optsObj?.lang,
    });

    if (!serviceUuidOk && (costoTotal <= 0 || esPaseMaestro)) {
      return await insertZeroKcReservaDirect({
        userId: targetUserId, serviceId: servicio?.id, negocioNombre, servicioNombre,
        exactLocalDate, horaInicio: servicio?.hora_inicio, spotNumber: spotNum, cupoMaximo, lang: optsObj?.lang,
      });
    }

    const attemptStartedIso = new Date(Date.now() - 4000).toISOString();
    const { data: rpcData, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('procesar_reserva_escrow', {
      p_usuario_id: targetUserId,
      p_negocio_nombre: negocioNombre,
      p_servicio_nombre: servicioNombre,
      p_precio_kc: esPaseMaestro ? 0 : costoTotal,
      p_servicio_id: serviceUuidOk ? rawSid : null,
      p_fecha_clase: exactLocalDate || null,
      p_spot_number: spotNum ?? null,
      p_split_count: splitCount,
      p_es_pase_maestro: esPaseMaestro,
    });

    if (!rpcErr && rpcData) {
      const synced = await syncPostRpcBookingRow({
        rpcData, userId: targetUserId, serviceId: servicio?.id, servicioNombre, exactLocalDate, spotNumber: spotNum, precioTotal: costoTotal,
      });
      notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed();
      return synced;
    }

    const rawMsg = String(rpcErr?.message || '');
    const errCode = String((rpcErr as { code?: unknown } | null)?.code || '').trim();
    const isTransportDrop =
      !errCode && /network|failed to fetch|fetch|timeout|timed out|econn|abort|socket|502|503|504/i.test(rawMsg);
    if (isTransportDrop) {
      const savedJustNow = await verifyReservaSavedInDb(
        targetUserId,
        servicio?.id,
        servicioNombre,
        exactLocalDate,
        spotNum,
        attemptStartedIso
      );
      if (savedJustNow) return savedJustNow;
    }

    await verifyAndPurgeConfirmedGhostService(supabase, servicio?.id);
    const cleanMsg = rawMsg.includes('|') ? rawMsg.split('|')[1].trim() : rawMsg;
    throw new Error(cleanMsg || 'No se pudo autorizar la reserva Escrow.');
    } finally {
      _inFlightBookingLocks.delete(lockKey);
    }
  },
};

export default exploreService;

    FILE: src/services/exploreZoneHelpers.ts

    // === src/services/exploreZoneHelpers.ts === //
import { KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES } from '../constants/affiliatesCatalog';
import { normalizeClubAlias } from '../utils/externalLinks';
import { canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey } from './exploreRadarFilters';
import { resolveCanonicalVenueCoordinates } from './kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';

const normalizeZoneText = (val?: unknown): string =>
  String(val ?? '')
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');

export const CORREDOR_KEYWORDS = [
  'corredor', 'tourist corridor', 'palmilla', 'chileno', 'el tule', 'puente el tule', 'tule',
  'santa maria', 'cabo real', 'cabo del sol', 'cerro colorado', 'querencia', 'acapulquito',
];
export const SJC_KEYWORDS = [
  'san jose', 'sjc', 'fonatur', 'zacatal', 'campestre',
  'costa azul', 'chamizal', 'rosarito', 'guaymitas', 'santa rosa', 'las veredas', 'finisterra',
];
export const CSL_KEYWORDS = [
  'cabo san lucas', 'csl', 'medano', 'pedregal', 'marina',
  'tezal', 'venados', 'cangrejos', 'matamoros', 'arcos del sol', 'leona vicario', 'lazaro cardenas',
];

export const resolveLosCabosZoneKey = (
  ubicacionRaw?: string,
  lat?: number,
  lon?: number
): 'CABO SAN LUCAS' | 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO' | 'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO' => {
  const text = normalizeZoneText(ubicacionRaw);
  if (CORREDOR_KEYWORDS.some((w) => text.includes(w)) || /\bkm\.?\s*(?:[4-9]|1\d|2[0-8])\b/i.test(text)) {
    return 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO';
  }
  if (SJC_KEYWORDS.some((w) => text.includes(w))) return 'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO';
  if (CSL_KEYWORDS.some((w) => text.includes(w))) return 'CABO SAN LUCAS';
  const hasRealLat = typeof lat === 'number' && Number.isFinite(lat) && Math.abs(lat) > 1;
  const hasRealLon = typeof lon === 'number' && Number.isFinite(lon) && Math.abs(lon) > 1;
  if (hasRealLat || hasRealLon) {
    if ((hasRealLat && (lat as number) >= 23.025) || (hasRealLon && (lon as number) >= -109.715)) {
      return 'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO';
    }
    if ((hasRealLat && (lat as number) >= 22.918) || (hasRealLon && (lon as number) >= -109.875)) {
      return 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO';
    }
  }
  return 'CABO SAN LUCAS';
};

export const formatClubZoneDisplay = (
  ubicacionRaw?: string | null,
  lat?: number,
  lon?: number,
  isEn = false
): string => {
  const text = normalizeZoneText(ubicacionRaw);
  const zoneKey = resolveLosCabosZoneKey(ubicacionRaw || undefined, lat, lon);
  if (zoneKey === 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO') {
    const corrLabel = isEn ? 'Tourist Corridor' : 'Corredor Turístico';
    return text.includes('tule') ? `El Tule • ${corrLabel}` : corrLabel;
  }
  if (zoneKey === 'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO') return 'San José del Cabo';
  return 'Cabo San Lucas';
};

export const computeHaversineKm = (lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2: number, lon2: number): number => {
  if (![lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2].every(Number.isFinite)) return 0;
  const R = 6371;
  const dLat = ((lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dLon = ((lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const a =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 +
    Math.cos((lat1 * Math.PI) / 180) * Math.cos((lat2 * Math.PI) / 180) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) ** 2;
  const ca = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, a));
  return R * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(ca), Math.sqrt(1 - ca));
};

const extractExplicitMapsCoords = (rawText?: unknown): { lat: number; lon: number } | null => {
  const str = String(rawText || '');
  if (!str) return null;
  const match = str.match(/@(-?\d+\.\d+),(-?\d+\.\d+)|[?&](?:q|query|ll)=(-?\d+\.\d+),(-?\d+\.\d+)/i);
  if (!match) return null;
  const lat = Number(match[1] ?? match[3]);
  const lon = Number(match[2] ?? match[4]);
  return Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon) ? { lat, lon } : null;
};

export const hydrateServicesGpsWithClubCoords = (servicios: any[], afiliados: any[] = []): any[] => {
  if (!Array.isArray(servicios) || servicios.length === 0) return Array.isArray(servicios) ? servicios : [];
  const byId = new Map<string, any>();
  const byOwner = new Map<string, any>();
  const byAlias = new Map<string, any>();

  const parseRealCoord = (raw: unknown): number => {
    if (raw === null || raw === undefined || raw === '') return NaN;
    const n = Number(raw);
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : NaN;
  };

  const registerClub = (club: any) => {
    if (!club || typeof club !== 'object') return;
    const canon = resolveCanonicalVenueCoordinates(club);
    const enrichedClub = { ...club, latitud: canon.latitud, longitud: canon.longitud, latitude: canon.latitud, longitude: canon.longitud };
    if (club.id != null) byId.set(String(club.id).trim(), enrichedClub);
    if (club.propietario_id != null) byOwner.set(String(club.propietario_id).trim(), enrichedClub);
    const canonLookup = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(club.nombre);
    if (canonLookup) byAlias.set(canonLookup, enrichedClub);
    const alias = normalizeClubAlias(club.nombre);
    if (alias) byAlias.set(alias, enrichedClub);
    const cleanLower = String(club.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (cleanLower) byAlias.set(cleanLower, enrichedClub);
  };

  (KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES || []).forEach(registerClub);
  (afiliados || []).forEach(registerClub);

  return servicios.map((srv: any) => {
    if (!srv || typeof srv !== 'object') return srv;
    const sidClub = String(srv.afiliado_id || srv.club_id || '').trim();
    const ownerId = String(srv.propietario_id || '').trim();
    const clubNameRaw = String(srv.negocio_nombre || srv.club || '').trim();
    const clubCanon = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(clubNameRaw);
    const clubAlias = normalizeClubAlias(clubNameRaw);
    const clubMatch =
      (sidClub ? byId.get(sidClub) : null) ||
      (ownerId ? byOwner.get(ownerId) : null) ||
      (clubCanon ? byAlias.get(clubCanon) : null) ||
      (clubAlias ? byAlias.get(clubAlias) : null) ||
      (clubNameRaw ? byAlias.get(clubNameRaw.toLowerCase()) : null);
    if (!clubMatch) return srv;

    const clubLat = parseRealCoord(clubMatch.latitud ?? clubMatch.latitude);
    const clubLon = parseRealCoord(clubMatch.longitud ?? clubMatch.longitude);
    if (!Number.isFinite(clubLat) || !Number.isFinite(clubLon) || Math.abs(clubLat) < 1 || Math.abs(clubLon) < 1) return srv;

    if (srv.es_sede_externa === true) return srv;
    void extractExplicitMapsCoords;
    return {
      ...srv,
      latitud: clubLat,
      longitud: clubLon,
      latitude: clubLat,
      longitude: clubLon,
      coords: { latitude: clubLat, longitude: clubLon },
    };
  });
};

export const computeUserMarkerSafeCoordinate = (
  userCoords: { latitude?: number; longitude?: number } | null | undefined,
  clustersAndPins: Array<{ latitude?: number; longitude?: number; kind?: string; count?: number }> = [],
  regionDelta?: { latitudeDelta?: number; longitudeDelta?: number }
): { latitude: number; longitude: number; wasOffset: boolean } => {
  const rawLat = userCoords?.latitude === null || userCoords?.latitude === undefined || String(userCoords?.latitude) === '' ? NaN : Number(userCoords?.latitude);
  const rawLng = userCoords?.longitude === null || userCoords?.longitude === undefined || String(userCoords?.longitude) === '' ? NaN : Number(userCoords?.longitude);
  const uLat = Number.isFinite(rawLat) && Math.abs(rawLat) > 1 ? rawLat : 22.8905;
  const uLng = Number.isFinite(rawLng) && Math.abs(rawLng) > 1 ? rawLng : -109.9167;
  const latD = Math.max(0.004, Number(regionDelta?.latitudeDelta) || 0.08);
  const lngD = Math.max(0.004, Number(regionDelta?.longitudeDelta) || 0.08);
  const latThresh = latD * 0.085;
  const lngThresh = lngD * 0.095;

  const points = (clustersAndPins || [])
    .map((p) => ({ lat: Number(p?.latitude), lng: Number(p?.longitude) }))
    .filter((p) => Number.isFinite(p.lat) && Number.isFinite(p.lng));

  const hasCollision = points.some(
    (p) => Math.abs(uLat - p.lat) < latThresh && Math.abs(uLng - p.lng) < lngThresh
  );
  if (!hasCollision) return { latitude: uLat, longitude: uLng, wasOffset: false };

  const stepLat = latD * 0.095;
  const stepLng = lngD * 0.105;
  const dirs: Array<[number, number]> = [
    [-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1], [-0.75, 0.75], [-0.75, -0.75], [0.75, 0.75], [0.75, -0.75],
  ];
  let best = { latitude: uLat - stepLat, longitude: uLng, score: -1 };
  for (const [dLat, dLng] of dirs) {
    const candLat = uLat + dLat * stepLat;
    const candLng = uLng + dLng * stepLng;
    let minNormDist = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    for (const p of points) {
      const dist = Math.hypot((candLat - p.lat) / latD, (candLng - p.lng) / lngD);
      if (dist < minNormDist) minNormDist = dist;
    }
    if (minNormDist > best.score) best = { latitude: candLat, longitude: candLng, score: minNormDist };
  }
  return { latitude: best.latitude, longitude: best.longitude, wasOffset: true };
};

export const EXPLORE_ZONE_EDGE_PADDING = { top: 88, right: 54, bottom: 140, left: 54 };

export const padZoneCameraRegion = <
  T extends { latitude: number; longitude: number; latitudeDelta: number; longitudeDelta: number }
>(
  cam: T,
  factor = 1.18
): T => ({
  ...cam,
  latitudeDelta: Number((cam.latitudeDelta * factor).toFixed(5)),
  longitudeDelta: Number((cam.longitudeDelta * factor).toFixed(5)),
});

export const resolveZoneCameraDurationMs = (
  prevRegion?: { latitude?: number; longitude?: number } | null,
  nextRegion?: { latitude?: number; longitude?: number } | null
): number => {
  const pLat = Number(prevRegion?.latitude);
  const pLng = Number(prevRegion?.longitude);
  const nLat = Number(nextRegion?.latitude);
  const nLng = Number(nextRegion?.longitude);
  if (!Number.isFinite(pLat) || !Number.isFinite(pLng) || !Number.isFinite(nLat) || !Number.isFinite(nLng)) {
    return 180;
  }
  return computeHaversineKm(pLat, pLng, nLat, nLng) > 6 ? 160 : 320;
};

export const KINETIC_RADAR_RADIUS_KM = 10;

export type ExploreZoneFilterResolution =
  | { mode: 'NEAR_10KM' }
  | { mode: 'ALL_CABOS' }
  | { mode: 'ZONE'; zoneKey: 'CABO SAN LUCAS' | 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO' | 'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO' };

export const resolveZoneFilterMode = (selectedZone?: string): ExploreZoneFilterResolution => {
  const norm = normalizeZoneText(selectedZone);
  if (!norm) return { mode: 'NEAR_10KM' };
  if (
    norm === 'cerca' ||
    norm === 'near' ||
    norm === 'near_me' ||
    norm.includes('cerca de mi') ||
    norm.includes('near me') ||
    norm.includes('10 km') ||
    norm.includes('5 km')
  ) {
    return { mode: 'NEAR_10KM' };
  }
  if (
    norm === 'todas' ||
    norm === 'todas las zonas' ||
    norm === 'all' ||
    norm === 'all zones' ||
    norm === 'los cabos' ||
    norm === 'todo los cabos' ||
    norm.includes('todas las zonas') ||
    norm.includes('all zones') ||
    norm === 'mi gps actual' ||
    norm === 'my current gps'
  ) {
    return { mode: 'ALL_CABOS' };
  }
  if (norm === 'csl' || norm.includes('cabo san lucas')) {
    return { mode: 'ZONE', zoneKey: 'CABO SAN LUCAS' };
  }
  if (norm === 'corredor' || norm.includes('corredor') || norm.includes('tourist corridor')) {
    return { mode: 'ZONE', zoneKey: 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO' };
  }
  if (norm === 'sjc' || norm.includes('san jose')) {
    return { mode: 'ZONE', zoneKey: 'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO' };
  }
  return { mode: 'ALL_CABOS' };
};

export const computeDistanceKm = (lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2?: number | null, lon2?: number | null): number => {
  void computeExactHaversineKm;
  return computeCanonicalVenueDistanceKm(lat2, lon2, { latitude: lat1, longitude: lon1 });
};

export const toValidCoordinate = (primary: any, secondary: any, fallback: number): number => {
  const n1 = parseFloat(String(primary ?? ''));
  if (!Number.isNaN(n1) && Math.abs(n1) > 1) return n1;
  const n2 = parseFloat(String(secondary ?? ''));
  if (!Number.isNaN(n2) && Math.abs(n2) > 1) return n2;
  return fallback;
};

export const resolveAffiliateZoneKey = (ubicacionRaw?: string, lat?: number, lon?: number) =>
  resolveLosCabosZoneKey(ubicacionRaw, lat, lon);

const normalizeIgHandle = (raw?: string | null): string => {
  const core = String(raw || '').trim().replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?instagram\.com\//i, '').replace(/[/?#].*$/, '').replace(/^@+/, '').trim();
  return core ? `@${core}` : '';
};

export const stripAffiliateIgMeta = (rawHorarios?: string | null): string =>
  String(rawHorarios || '').replace(/\s*\|\|\s*ig:@?[^\s|]+/gi, '').replace(/\s*__ig:@?[^\s|]+/gi, '').trim();

export const extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule = (af: any): {
  instagram: string;
  horarios: string;
  amenidades: string[] | undefined;
} => {
  const rawHorarios = String(af?.horarios || '');
  const cleanHorarios = stripAffiliateIgMeta(rawHorarios);
  let ig = normalizeIgHandle(af?.instagram);
  const rawAm = Array.isArray(af?.amenidades) ? af.amenidades : undefined;
  if (!ig && rawAm) {
    const tag = rawAm.find((x: any) => String(x || '').trim().toLowerCase().startsWith('__ig:'));
    if (tag) ig = normalizeIgHandle(String(tag).trim().slice(5));
  }
  if (!ig && rawHorarios) {
    const m = rawHorarios.match(/\|\|\s*ig:(@?[^\s|]+)/i) || rawHorarios.match(/__ig:(@?[^\s|]+)/i);
    if (m?.[1]) ig = normalizeIgHandle(m[1]);
  }
  ig = canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram(ig, af?.nombre);
  const cleanAmenidades = rawAm
    ? rawAm.map((x: any) => String(x || '').trim()).filter((x: string) => Boolean(x) && !x.toLowerCase().startsWith('__ig:'))
    : undefined;
  return { instagram: ig, horarios: cleanHorarios, amenidades: cleanAmenidades };
};

const cleanDiscValue = (val: unknown): string => {
  const s = String(val || '').trim();
  if (!s) return '';
  const low = s.toLowerCase();
  if (low === 'club' || low === 'especialista') return '';
  return s;
};

export interface ISyncClubMediaOverride {
  portada_url?: string | null;
  avatar_url?: string | null;
  galeria?: string[];
  updatedAt?: number;
}

const _syncClubMediaMemory = new Map<string, ISyncClubMediaOverride>();
const SYNC_MEDIA_PREFIX = '@kinetic_club_media_sync_v1_';

export const setSyncClubMediaMemory = (
  keys: Array<string | null | undefined>,
  media: ISyncClubMediaOverride
): void => {
  const cleanMedia: ISyncClubMediaOverride = {
    portada_url: sanitizeImageUrl(media?.portada_url) || null,
    avatar_url: sanitizeImageUrl(media?.avatar_url) || null,
    galeria: Array.isArray(media?.galeria)
      ? media.galeria.slice(0, 4).map((u) => String(u || '').trim())
      : undefined,
    updatedAt: Date.now(),
  };
  keys
    .map((k) => String(k || '').trim().toLowerCase())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .forEach((normKey) => {
      _syncClubMediaMemory.set(normKey, cleanMedia);
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.localStorage) {
        try {
          window.localStorage.setItem(`${SYNC_MEDIA_PREFIX}${normKey}`, JSON.stringify(cleanMedia));
        } catch {}
      }
    });
};

export const readSyncLocalClubOverride = (idKey?: string | null, nameKey?: string | null): Record<string, any> | null => {
  const kId = String(idKey || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const kName = String(nameKey || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const mem = (kId && _syncClubMediaMemory.get(kId)) || (kName && _syncClubMediaMemory.get(kName)) || null;
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !window.localStorage) return mem;
  try {
    const rawExt =
      (kId && window.localStorage.getItem(`@kinetic_club_ext_v1_${kId}`)) ||
      (kName && window.localStorage.getItem(`@kinetic_club_ext_v1_${kName}`));
    const rawMedia =
      (kId && window.localStorage.getItem(`${SYNC_MEDIA_PREFIX}${kId}`)) ||
      (kName && window.localStorage.getItem(`${SYNC_MEDIA_PREFIX}${kName}`));
    const parsedExt = rawExt ? JSON.parse(rawExt) : null;
    const parsedMedia = rawMedia ? JSON.parse(rawMedia) : null;
    if (!parsedExt && !parsedMedia && !mem) return null;
    return { ...(parsedExt || {}), ...(parsedMedia || {}), ...(mem || {}) };
  } catch {
    return mem;
  }
};

const sanitizeClubDescriptionByVenue = (rawDesc: unknown, keyCanonical: string): string => {
  const str = String(rawDesc || '').trim();
  if (!str) return '';
  if (keyCanonical === 'cabo training') {
    return str
      .replace(/\s*\(seal\s*cross\s*training\)/gi, '')
      .replace(/\bseal\s*cross\s*training\b/gi, 'Cabo Training')
      .replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ')
      .trim();
  }
  return str;
};

export function buildEnrichedAffiliates(
  afiliadosRaw: any[],
  serviciosRaw: any[],
  userCoords: { latitude: number; longitude: number },
  favoriteIds: string[]
): any[] {
  const catalogByName = new Map<string, any>();
  const catalogById = new Map<string, any>();

  // Catálogo estático SOLO como diccionario de referencia visual/geográfica (PROHIBIDO re-inyectar filas)
  (KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES || []).forEach((item: any) => {
    if (!item || isBannedAffiliateRecord(item)) return;
    const nKey = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(item.nombre);
    const idKey = normalizeRadarText(item.id);
    if (nKey) catalogByName.set(nKey, item);
    if (idKey) catalogById.set(idKey, item);
  });

  const validServices = filterValidRadarServices(hydrateServicesGpsWithClubCoords(serviciosRaw || [], afiliadosRaw || []));
  const servicesByClubKey = new Map<string, any[]>();
  validServices.forEach((sv: any) => {
    const cKey = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(sv?.negocio_nombre);
    if (!cKey) return;
    const bucket = servicesByClubKey.get(cKey);
    if (bucket) bucket.push(sv);
    else servicesByClubKey.set(cKey, [sv]);
  });

  const favExactSet = new Set((favoriteIds || []).map((f) => String(f || '').trim()));
  const favLowerSet = new Set((favoriteIds || []).map((f) => String(f || '').trim().toLowerCase()));

  const uniqueByKey = new Map<string, any>();
  (afiliadosRaw || []).forEach((af: any) => {
    if (!af || isBannedAffiliateRecord(af)) return;
    const nombreRaw = String(af.nombre || '').trim();
    if (!nombreRaw) return;
    const nameKey = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(nombreRaw);
    const idKey = normalizeRadarText(af.id);
    const dedupeKey = nameKey || idKey;
    if (!dedupeKey) return;

    const existing = uniqueByKey.get(dedupeKey);
    if (existing) {
      const existingActive = normalizeRadarText(existing.estado) === 'activo';
      const nextActive = normalizeRadarText(af.estado) === 'activo';
      if (existingActive && !nextActive) return;
    }
    uniqueByKey.set(dedupeKey, af);
  });

  return Array.from(uniqueByKey.values()).map((af: any, idx: number) => {
    const rawNombre = String(af.nombre || '').trim();
    const keyCanonical = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(rawNombre);
    const nombreAf = keyCanonical === 'palesttra club' ? 'PALESTTRA CLUB' : rawNombre;
    const idStr = String(af.id || `${nombreAf}_${idx}`);
    const catalogRef =
      catalogById.get(normalizeRadarText(idStr)) || catalogByName.get(keyCanonical) || null;
    const isFromDb = Boolean(af._fromSupabaseDb);
    const syncOverride = af?._localOverride || readSyncLocalClubOverride(idStr, keyCanonical);
    const localOv = isFromDb ? null : syncOverride;
    const hasFreshMediaSync = Boolean(
      syncOverride?.updatedAt && Date.now() - Number(syncOverride.updatedAt) < 12 * 3600 * 1000
    );
    const isKineticSpec = keyCanonical.includes('kinetic especialista');
    const forceCatalogGeo = keyCanonical.includes('baja crossfit') || isKineticSpec;

    const parsedAf = extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule(af);
    const parsedCat = catalogRef ? extractAffiliateIgAndCleanSchedule(catalogRef) : null;

    const disciplina = resolveCanonicalAffiliateDiscipline(
      nombreAf,
      cleanDiscValue(af.disciplina) ||
        cleanDiscValue(localOv?.disciplina) ||
        cleanDiscValue(catalogRef?.disciplina),
      isKineticSpec
    );
    const isSpec = isKineticSpec
      ? true
      : Boolean(
          af.es_especialista ??
            String(af.categoria || catalogRef?.categoria || '').toLowerCase().includes('especialista')
        );
    const categoriaResolved = isSpec ? 'Especialista' : af.categoria || catalogRef?.categoria || 'Club';
    const clasesDelClub = servicesByClubKey.get(keyCanonical) || [];

    const allDisciplinesSet = new Set<string>([disciplina]);
    if (isKineticSpec) {
      ['Fisioterapia', 'Masaje Deportivo', 'Cold Plunge', 'Crioterapia'].forEach((d) =>
        allDisciplinesSet.add(d)
      );
    } else {
      const secondarySource =
        Array.isArray(af.disciplinas_secundarias) && af.disciplinas_secundarias.length > 0
          ? af.disciplinas_secundarias
          : Array.isArray(catalogRef?.disciplinas_secundarias)
            ? catalogRef.disciplinas_secundarias
            : [];
      secondarySource.forEach((d: string) => {
        const cleanD = cleanDiscValue(d);
        if (cleanD) allDisciplinesSet.add(cleanD);
      });
    }

    const canonCoords = resolveCanonicalVenueCoordinates(af, catalogRef, idx);
    const lat = canonCoords.latitud;
    const lon = canonCoords.longitud;
    const distanciaKm = computeCanonicalVenueDistanceKm(lat, lon, userCoords);
    void toValidCoordinate;
    void forceCatalogGeo;

    const syncedClasesDelClub = clasesDelClub.map((sv) =>
      sv?.es_sede_externa === true
        ? sv
        : {
            ...sv,
            latitud: lat,
            longitud: lon,
            latitude: lat,
            longitude: lon,
            distanciaKm,
            coords: { latitude: lat, longitude: lon },
          }
    );

    const statusInfo = resolveStrictAffiliateStatus(
      af.estado,
      !isFromDb && (af.esClubActivo === true || af.esActivo === true || af.activo === true)
    );

    const auditVals = [af.rating_limpieza, af.rating_seguridad, af.rating_equipo, af.rating_atencion, af.rating_comunidad]
      .map((v) => Number(v))
      .filter((v) => Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0);
    const auditColsAvg = auditVals.length > 0
      ? Math.round((auditVals.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / auditVals.length) * 10) / 10
      : 0;
    const localRating = Number(localOv?.rating);
    const ratingNum =
      Number.isFinite(localRating) && localRating > 0
        ? localRating
        : auditColsAvg > 0
          ? auditColsAvg
          : statusInfo.esClubActivo ? 5.0 : 4.8;

    const isFavorite =
      favExactSet.has(idStr) ||
      favExactSet.has(nombreAf) ||
      favLowerSet.has(idStr.toLowerCase()) ||
      favLowerSet.has(nombreAf.toLowerCase());

    const totalCuposReservados = clasesDelClub.reduce(
      (acc: number, c: any) => acc + Math.max(0, Number(c.cupo_actual) || 0),
      0
    );
    const trendingScore =
      clasesDelClub.length * 35 +
      totalCuposReservados * 20 +
      ratingNum * 10 +
      (TRENDING_CATEGORIES.includes(disciplina) ? 15 : 0) +
      (statusInfo.esClubActivo ? 25 : 0);
    const isTrending = Boolean(
      statusInfo.esClubActivo &&
        (clasesDelClub.length > 0 ||
          totalCuposReservados > 0 ||
          (TRENDING_CATEGORIES.includes(disciplina) && ratingNum >= 4.9))
    );
    const minPrice =
      clasesDelClub.length > 0
        ? Math.min(
            ...clasesDelClub.map((c) => {
              const p = Number(c?.precio_kc);
              return c?.precio_kc !== null && c?.precio_kc !== undefined && c?.precio_kc !== '' && Number.isFinite(p)
                ? Math.max(0, p)
                : 150;
            })
          )
        : 150;

    const rawUbicacion = String(af.ubicacion || '').trim();
    const normUbi = normalizeRadarText(rawUbicacion);
    const isGenericCaboFitnessLoc =
      !normUbi ||
      normUbi === 'ubicacion verificada' ||
      normUbi === 'verified location' ||
      normUbi === 'cabo san lucas, b.c.s.' ||
      normUbi === 'cabo san lucas, b.c.s' ||
      normUbi.includes('plaza tres virgenes') ||
      normUbi.includes('el tezal');
    const ubicacionClean =
      keyCanonical === 'cabo fitness club' && isGenericCaboFitnessLoc
        ? 'Plaza Pioneros, Blvd. Lázaro Cárdenas, Centro, Cabo San Lucas, B.C.S.'
        : (forceCatalogGeo && catalogRef?.ubicacion) ||
          rawUbicacion ||
          catalogRef?.ubicacion ||
          'Ubicación Verificada';
    const zoneKey = resolveAffiliateZoneKey(`${ubicacionClean} ${af.direccion || catalogRef?.direccion || ''}`, lat, lon);

    const fallbackImage = resolveCategoryImage(disciplina, categoriaResolved);
    const portadaResolved =
      (hasFreshMediaSync && sanitizeImageUrl(syncOverride?.portada_url)) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(af.portada_url) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(syncOverride?.portada_url) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(catalogRef?.portada_url) ||
      fallbackImage;
    const avatarResolved =
      (hasFreshMediaSync && sanitizeImageUrl(syncOverride?.avatar_url)) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(af.avatar_url) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(af.logo_url) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(syncOverride?.avatar_url) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(catalogRef?.avatar_url) ||
      fallbackImage;
    const imagenResolved = sanitizeImageUrl(af.imagen) || portadaResolved || fallbackImage;
    const galeriaResolved: string[] =
      hasFreshMediaSync && Array.isArray(syncOverride?.galeria) && syncOverride.galeria.some(Boolean)
        ? syncOverride.galeria.slice(0, 4)
        : Array.isArray(af.galeria) && af.galeria.some(Boolean)
          ? af.galeria.slice(0, 4)
          : Array.isArray(syncOverride?.galeria) && syncOverride.galeria.some(Boolean)
            ? syncOverride.galeria.slice(0, 4)
            : Array.isArray(catalogRef?.galeria)
              ? catalogRef.galeria.slice(0, 4)
              : [];
    const descripcionClean = sanitizeClubDescriptionByVenue(
      af.descripcion || catalogRef?.descripcion,
      keyCanonical
    );

    return {
      ...catalogRef,
      ...af,
      id: idStr,
      nombre: nombreAf,
      disciplina,
      allDisciplines: Array.from(allDisciplinesSet),
      es_especialista: isSpec,
      categoria: categoriaResolved,
      ubicacion: ubicacionClean,
      descripcion: descripcionClean,
      horarios: parsedAf.horarios || parsedCat?.horarios || '',
      instagram: parsedAf.instagram || parsedCat?.instagram || '',
      whatsapp: String(af.whatsapp || catalogRef?.whatsapp || '').trim(),
      amenidades: parsedAf.amenidades ?? af.amenidades ?? catalogRef?.amenidades,
      portada_url: portadaResolved,
      avatar_url: avatarResolved,
      imagen: imagenResolved,
      galeria: galeriaResolved,
      fallbackImage,
      estado: statusInfo.estado,
      esClubActivo: statusInfo.esClubActivo,
      activo: statusInfo.activo,
      esActivo: statusInfo.esActivo,
      latitude: lat,
      longitude: lon,
      coords: { latitude: lat, longitude: lon },
      distanciaKm,
      rating: ratingNum,
      experiencias: syncedClasesDelClub,
      isFavorite,
      isTrending,
      trendingScore,
      zoneKey,
      minPrice,
      coverResolved: portadaResolved,
      pinImageResolved: avatarResolved,
    };
  });
}

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { isWellnessCategory, resolveCategoryImage } from '../constants/exploreData';
import { canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey, normalizeRadarText } from '../services/exploreRadarFilters';
import { computeEventLocalLifecycle } from '../services/eventosScreenHelpers';
import { KINETIC_RADAR_RADIUS_KM, resolveZoneFilterMode } from '../services/exploreZoneHelpers';
import {
  computeCanonicalVenueDistanceKm,
  resolveCanonicalVenueCoordinates,
} from '../services/kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';
import { IMapLayerVisibility } from '../types';

export const DEFAULT_EXPLORE_ZONE_ID = 'todas';

const CABO_SJD = { latitude: 23.0591, longitude: -109.6956, zoneKey: 'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO' };
const CABO_CORREDOR = { latitude: 22.9765, longitude: -109.7995, zoneKey: 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO' };
const CABO_CSL = { latitude: 22.8905, longitude: -109.9167, zoneKey: 'CABO SAN LUCAS' };

const expandedMapsUrlCache = new Map<string, { latitude: number; longitude: number }>();
const pendingShortUrls = new Set<string>();
const INACTIVE_EVENT_STATES = new Set([
  'inactivo', 'inactiva', 'pausado', 'pausada', 'cancelado', 'cancelada',
  'eliminado', 'eliminada', 'borrado', 'borrada', 'deleted', 'inactive', 'paused', 'canceled', 'archivado',
  'pendiente', 'pending', 'borrador', 'draft', 'rechazado', 'suspendido', 'suspended',
]);

export const isAllCaboZone = (zone?: string | null): boolean => {
  const z = String(zone || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  return !z || z === 'TODAS' || z === 'ALL' || z.includes('TODAS LAS ZONAS') || z.includes('ALL LOS CABOS');
};
export const isNearMeZone = (zone?: string | null): boolean => {
  const z = String(zone || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  return Boolean(z) && (z.includes('CERCA') || z.includes('10 KM') || z.includes('5 KM') || z.includes('NEAR') || z === 'MI GPS ACTUAL');
};
export const normalizeHostClubName = (raw?: string | null): string => {
  const clean = String(raw || '').trim(), low = clean.toLowerCase();
  return !clean || low === 'terminal global kinetic' || low === 'kinetic oficial' ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : clean;
};
export const isOfficialKineticHost = (raw?: string | null): boolean => {
  const low = String(raw || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  return !low || low === 'kinetic oficial' || low === 'kinetic sports club' || low === 'terminal global kinetic';
};
export const isValidHttpMediaUri = (raw?: any): string => {
  const s = typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.trim() : '';
  return s.length >= 8 && s !== 'null' && s !== 'undefined' && /^(https?:\/\/|data:image\/|file:\/\/|blob:)/i.test(s) ? s : '';
};

export const parseGpsCoordsFromText = (rawText?: string | null): { latitude: number; longitude: number } | null => {
  const text = String(rawText || '').trim();
  if (!text) return null;
  const patterns = [
    /!3d(-?\d+\.\d+)!4d(-?\d+\.\d+)/i, /@(-?\d+\.\d+)\s*,\s*(-?\d+\.\d+)/i,
    /[?&](?:q|ll|query|daddr|destination|center)=(-?\d+\.\d+)\s*(?:,|%2C)\s*(-?\d+\.\d+)/i,
    /(-?\d{2}\.\d{3,})\s*,\s*(-?\d{2,3}\.\d{3,})/,
  ];
  for (const rx of patterns) {
    const m = text.match(rx);
    if (!m) continue;
    const lat = parseFloat(m[1]), lng = parseFloat(m[2]);
    if (Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lng) && Math.abs(lat) <= 90 && Math.abs(lng) <= 180 && Math.abs(lat) > 0.1) return { latitude: lat, longitude: lng };
  }
  return null;
};

const extractShortMapsUrl = (raw?: string | null): string | null =>
  String(raw || '').match(/https?:\/\/(?:maps\.app\.goo\.gl|goo\.gl\/maps)\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+/i)?.[0] || null;

export const expandShortMapsUrlsInBackground = (servicios: any[], onResolved: () => void): void => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' || !Array.isArray(servicios) || typeof fetch !== 'function') return;
  let count = 0;
  for (const ev of servicios) {
    if (count >= 8) break;
    const shortUrl = extractShortMapsUrl(`${ev?.ubicacion || ''} ${ev?.google_maps_url || ''}`);
    if (!shortUrl || expandedMapsUrlCache.has(shortUrl) || pendingShortUrls.has(shortUrl)) continue;
    pendingShortUrls.add(shortUrl);
    count++;
    const ctrl = typeof AbortController !== 'undefined' ? new AbortController() : null;
    const timer = ctrl ? setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 4500) : null;
    fetch(shortUrl, { method: 'GET', redirect: 'follow', signal: ctrl?.signal })
      .then(async (res) => {
        const coords = parseGpsCoordsFromText(res?.url || '') || (res?.text ? parseGpsCoordsFromText((await res.text()).slice(0, 32000)) : null);
        if (coords) { expandedMapsUrlCache.set(shortUrl, coords); onResolved(); }
      })
      .catch(() => {})
      .finally(() => {
        pendingShortUrls.delete(shortUrl);
        if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
      });
  }
};

export const resolveFallbackCaboCoords = (hint?: string | null) => {
  const norm = normalizeRadarText(String(hint || ''));
  if (/san jose|\bsjd\b|palmilla|fonatur|guaymitas|puerto los cabos/.test(norm)) return CABO_SJD;
  if (/corredor|corridor|chileno|santa maria|el tule|\btule\b/.test(norm)) return CABO_CORREDOR;
  return CABO_CSL;
};

const inferZoneKey = (lat: number, lng: number, hint?: string | null): string => {
  const norm = normalizeRadarText(String(hint || ''));
  if (/san jose|\bsjd\b|fonatur|guaymitas|puerto los cabos/.test(norm)) return 'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO';
  if (/corredor|corridor|chileno|santa maria|el tule/.test(norm)) return 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO';
  if (/cabo san lucas|\bcsl\b/.test(norm)) return 'CABO SAN LUCAS';
  if (lat >= 23.02 || lng >= -109.73) return 'SAN JOSÉ DEL CABO';
  return lng > -109.86 ? 'CORREDOR TURÍSTICO' : 'CABO SAN LUCAS';
};

export const computeRadarDistanceKm = (lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2: number, lon2: number): number => {
  if (![lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2].every(Number.isFinite)) return 0;
  return computeCanonicalVenueDistanceKm(lat2, lon2, { latitude: lat1, longitude: lon1 });
};

export const resolveEventGpsCoords = (ev: any, matchedClub?: any): { latitude: number; longitude: number; hasCustomCoords: boolean } => {
  if (matchedClub && ev?.es_sede_externa !== true) {
    const canon = resolveCanonicalVenueCoordinates(matchedClub, matchedClub);
    return { latitude: canon.latitud, longitude: canon.longitud, hasCustomCoords: false };
  }
  const rawLat = ev?.latitud ?? ev?.latitude ?? ev?.coords?.latitude ?? ev?.atributos?.latitud;
  const rawLng = ev?.longitud ?? ev?.longitude ?? ev?.coords?.longitude ?? ev?.atributos?.longitud;
  const dLat = typeof rawLat === 'boolean' || rawLat == null || rawLat === '' ? NaN : Number(rawLat);
  const dLng = typeof rawLng === 'boolean' || rawLng == null || rawLng === '' ? NaN : Number(rawLng);
  if (
    Number.isFinite(dLat) &&
    Number.isFinite(dLng) &&
    Math.abs(dLat) <= 90 &&
    Math.abs(dLng) <= 180 &&
    Math.abs(dLat) > 0.1 &&
    Math.abs(dLng) > 0.1
  ) {
    return { latitude: dLat, longitude: dLng, hasCustomCoords: true };
  }
  const locText = `${ev?.ubicacion || ''} ${ev?.google_maps_url || ''} ${ev?.direccion || ''}`;
  const shortUrl = extractShortMapsUrl(locText);
  if (shortUrl && expandedMapsUrlCache.has(shortUrl)) return { ...expandedMapsUrlCache.get(shortUrl)!, hasCustomCoords: true };
  const parsed = parseGpsCoordsFromText(locText);
  if (parsed) return { ...parsed, hasCustomCoords: true };

  const cLat = typeof matchedClub?.latitude === 'boolean' || typeof matchedClub?.latitud === 'boolean' ? NaN : Number(matchedClub?.latitude ?? matchedClub?.latitud);
  const cLng = typeof matchedClub?.longitude === 'boolean' || typeof matchedClub?.longitud === 'boolean' ? NaN : Number(matchedClub?.longitude ?? matchedClub?.longitud);
  const evNorm = normalizeRadarText(ev?.ubicacion || ''), clNorm = normalizeRadarText(matchedClub?.ubicacion || '');
  const diffZone = Boolean(evNorm && shortUrl) || (Boolean(evNorm && clNorm && evNorm !== clNorm) && /san jose|\bsjd\b|corredor|cabo san lucas/.test(evNorm));
  if (matchedClub && !diffZone && Number.isFinite(cLat) && Number.isFinite(cLng) && Math.abs(cLat) <= 90 && Math.abs(cLng) <= 180 && Math.abs(cLat) > 0.1 && Math.abs(cLng) > 0.1) {
    return { latitude: cLat, longitude: cLng, hasCustomCoords: false };
  }
  const fb = resolveFallbackCaboCoords(`${locText} ${ev?.zona || ''} ${matchedClub?.ubicacion || ''}`);
  return { latitude: fb.latitude, longitude: fb.longitude, hasCustomCoords: Boolean(evNorm) };
};

export const extractCleanZoneLabelForSearch = (entity: any): string => {
  const rawCity = `${entity?.zoneKey || ''} ${entity?.ciudad || ''} ${entity?.zona || ''}`
    .replace(/https?:\/\/\S+/gi, ' ').replace(/\bc\.?\s*p\.?\s*\d*\b/gi, ' ').replace(/\d+/g, ' ');
  const locNorm = normalizeRadarText(String(entity?.ubicacion || ''));
  const tokens = [rawCity];
  if (/san jose|\bsjd\b/.test(locNorm)) tokens.push('san jose del cabo');
  if (/corredor|corridor/.test(locNorm)) tokens.push('corredor turistico');
  if (/cabo san lucas|\bcsl\b/.test(locNorm)) tokens.push('cabo san lucas');
  if (/los cabos/.test(locNorm)) tokens.push('los cabos');
  return normalizeRadarText(tokens.join(' '));
};

export const matchesCleanExploreSearch = (af: any, normalizedQuery: string): boolean => {
  if (!normalizedQuery) return true;
  const clubDiscs: string[] = Array.isArray(af?.allDisciplines)
    ? af.allDisciplines
    : [af?.disciplina, ...(Array.isArray(af?.disciplinas_secundarias) ? af.disciplinas_secundarias : [])].filter(Boolean);
  return normalizeRadarText(`${af?.nombre || ''} ${af?.disciplina || ''} ${clubDiscs.join(' ')} ${extractCleanZoneLabelForSearch(af)}`).includes(normalizedQuery);
};

export const deduplicateAffiliatesList = (list: any[]): any[] => {
  const seenIds = new Set<string>(), seenNames = new Set<string>(), out: any[] = [];
  for (const af of list || []) {
    if (!af) continue;
    const idK = String(af.id || '').trim().toLowerCase(), nmK = normalizeRadarText(String(af.nombre || ''));
    if ((idK && seenIds.has(idK)) || (nmK && seenNames.has(nmK))) continue;
    if (idK) seenIds.add(idK);
    if (nmK) seenNames.add(nmK);
    out.push(af);
  }
  return out;
};

export const deduplicateSectionsForSearch = (sections: any[]): any[] => {
  if (!Array.isArray(sections) || sections.length === 0) return sections;
  const preferredSec = new Map<string, number>();
  const getKey = (af: any) => String(af?.id || '').trim().toLowerCase() || normalizeRadarText(String(af?.nombre || ''));
  sections.forEach((sec, sIdx) => {
    const secDisc = normalizeRadarText(String(sec?.discipline || sec?.title || ''));
    for (const af of sec?.data || sec?.items || []) {
      const k = getKey(af);
      if (k && (!preferredSec.has(k) || normalizeRadarText(String(af?.disciplina || '')) === secDisc)) preferredSec.set(k, sIdx);
    }
  });
  const emitted = new Set<string>();
  return sections
    .map((sec, sIdx) => {
      const raw = sec?.data || sec?.items || [];
      const uniq = raw.filter((af: any) => {
        const k = getKey(af);
        if (!k || emitted.has(k) || preferredSec.get(k) !== sIdx) return false;
        emitted.add(k);
        return true;
      });
      return uniq.length === raw.length ? sec : { ...sec, data: uniq, items: uniq };
    })
    .filter((sec) => (sec?.data || sec?.items || []).length > 0);
};

} from '../services/kineticStudioAndMapPureMath';

export const buildCompleteExploreMapMarkers = (params: {
  baseMarkers: any[]; serviciosRaw: any[]; afiliadosEnriquecidos: any[]; mapLayers: IMapLayerVisibility;
  selectedZone: string; hasRealUserCoords: boolean; userCoords: { latitude: number; longitude: number };
  searchQuery: string; selectedCategories: string[]; quickChip: string; isAdminView: boolean;
}): any[] => {
  const { baseMarkers, serviciosRaw, afiliadosEnriquecidos, mapLayers, selectedZone, hasRealUserCoords, userCoords, searchQuery, selectedCategories, quickChip, isAdminView } = params;
  const nonEventMarkers = (baseMarkers || []).filter((m) => String(m?.markerType || '').toLowerCase() !== 'event');
  if (!mapLayers.showEvents) return nonEventMarkers;

  const clubByName = new Map<string, any>();
  const clubById = new Map<string, any>();
  for (const af of afiliadosEnriquecidos || []) {
    const canonK = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(String(af?.nombre || ''));
    const rawK = normalizeRadarText(String(af?.nombre || ''));
    if (canonK && !clubByName.has(canonK)) clubByName.set(canonK, af);
    if (rawK && !clubByName.has(rawK)) clubByName.set(rawK, af);
    const aid = String(af?.id || '').trim();
    if (aid && !clubById.has(aid)) clubById.set(aid, af);
  }
  const q = normalizeRadarText(searchQuery);
  const zoneMode = resolveZoneFilterMode(selectedZone);
  const placed: { lat: number; lng: number }[] = nonEventMarkers.map((m) => ({ lat: Number(m.latitude), lng: Number(m.longitude) }));
  const eventMarkers: any[] = [], seenEvIds = new Set<string>();
  (serviciosRaw || []).forEach((ev: any, idx: number) => {
    if (!ev || ev.activo === false || ev?.atributos?.activo === false || INACTIVE_EVENT_STATES.has(String(ev.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase())) return;
    const evId = String(ev.id || `idx_${idx}`).trim();
    if (seenEvIds.has(evId)) return;
    const hostName = normalizeHostClubName(ev.negocio_nombre), isOfficial = isOfficialKineticHost(ev.negocio_nombre || hostName);
    const evClubId = String(ev.afiliado_id || ev.club_id || '').trim();
    const hostCanon = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(hostName);
    const hostRaw = normalizeRadarText(hostName);
    const matchedClub = isOfficial ? null : (evClubId ? clubById.get(evClubId) : null) || clubByName.get(hostCanon) || clubByName.get(hostRaw) || null;
    if (!isOfficial) {
      if (!matchedClub) { if (!isAdminView) return; }
      else if (!(matchedClub.estado === 'activo' && matchedClub.esClubActivo !== false && matchedClub.esActivo !== false)) return;
    }
    const life = computeEventLocalLifecycle(ev, matchedClub);
    if (life.isDeleted || life.isExpired || !life.isServiceApproved) return;

    const { latitude: rawLat, longitude: rawLng, hasCustomCoords } = resolveEventGpsCoords(ev, matchedClub);
    const distKm = computeRadarDistanceKm(userCoords.latitude, userCoords.longitude, rawLat, rawLng);
    const evZone = hasCustomCoords || !matchedClub?.zoneKey ? inferZoneKey(rawLat, rawLng, ev.ubicacion || matchedClub?.ubicacion) : matchedClub.zoneKey;
    if (zoneMode.mode === 'NEAR_10KM' && hasRealUserCoords && distKm > KINETIC_RADAR_RADIUS_KM) return;
    if (zoneMode.mode === 'ZONE' && normalizeRadarText(evZone) !== normalizeRadarText(zoneMode.zoneKey)) return;

    const evDisc = String(ev.categoria || ev.disciplina || matchedClub?.disciplina || 'Fuerza').trim();
    if (q) {
      const evSearch = normalizeRadarText(`${ev.nombre_servicio || ''} ${ev.titulo || ''} ${hostName} ${evDisc} ${extractCleanZoneLabelForSearch({ ubicacion: ev.ubicacion || matchedClub?.ubicacion, zoneKey: evZone })}`);
      if (!evSearch.includes(q) && !(matchedClub && matchesCleanExploreSearch(matchedClub, q))) return;
    }
    if ((quickChip === 'fitness' && (Boolean(matchedClub?.es_especialista) || isWellnessCategory(evDisc))) || (quickChip === 'wellness' && !Boolean(matchedClub?.es_especialista) && !isWellnessCategory(evDisc)) || (quickChip === 'favoritos' && !matchedClub?.isFavorite)) return;
    if (selectedCategories.length > 0 && !selectedCategories.some((s) => s.toLowerCase() === evDisc.toLowerCase())) return;

    let lat = rawLat, lng = rawLng;
    const clash = placed.filter((c) => Math.abs(c.lat - lat) < 0.00009 && Math.abs(c.lng - lng) < 0.00009).length;
    if (clash > 0) { const a = ((clash * 65 + idx * 25) * Math.PI) / 180; lat += Math.sin(a) * 0.00024; lng += Math.cos(a) * 0.00024; }
    placed.push({ lat, lng }); seenEvIds.add(evId);

    const cover = isValidHttpMediaUri(ev.portada_url || ev.imagen_url || ev.imagen);
    const customUpload = cover && !cover.includes('images.unsplash.com') ? cover : '';
    const realClubAv = isValidHttpMediaUri(matchedClub?.avatar_url || matchedClub?.logo_url);
    const clubAv = (realClubAv && !realClubAv.includes('images.unsplash.com') ? realClubAv : '') || isValidHttpMediaUri(ev.avatar_club) || realClubAv;
    const fbImg = resolveCategoryImage(evDisc, matchedClub?.categoria || hostName), evTitle = String(ev.nombre_servicio || ev.titulo || 'Evento Kinetic').trim();
    const enrichedEv = { ...ev, nombre_servicio: evTitle, titulo: evTitle, negocio_nombre: hostName, categoria: evDisc, disciplina: evDisc, fecha_original: life.fechaOriginal, fecha: life.fechaProyectada, fecha_proyectada: life.fechaProyectada, ubicacion: ev.ubicacion || matchedClub?.ubicacion || 'Los Cabos, B.C.S.', portada_url: cover || isValidHttpMediaUri(matchedClub?.portada_url) || fbImg, avatar_club: isOfficial ? '/logo.png' : (clubAv || cover || fbImg), latitude: lat, longitude: lng, distanciaKm: distKm };
    const parentOrVirtual = matchedClub || { id: `virtual_${isOfficial ? 'kinetic_oficial' : normalizeRadarText(hostName).replace(/\s+/g, '_')}_${evId}`, nombre: hostName, disciplina: evDisc, allDisciplines: [evDisc], categoria: 'Club', ubicacion: enrichedEv.ubicacion, estado: isOfficial ? 'activo' : 'prospecto', esClubActivo: isOfficial, esActivo: isOfficial, activo: isOfficial, isOfficialKinetic: isOfficial, avatar_url: clubAv || customUpload || null, portada_url: enrichedEv.portada_url, latitude: lat, longitude: lng, distanciaKm: distKm, zoneKey: evZone, rating: 5.0, experiencias: [enrichedEv], clases: [enrichedEv], eventosActivos: [enrichedEv], hasActiveEvents: true };
    eventMarkers.push({ id: `ev_${evId}`, markerType: 'event', latitude: lat, longitude: lng, title: evTitle, subtitle: hostName, negocio_nombre: hostName, disciplina: evDisc, avatar_url: isOfficial ? '' : (clubAv || cover || fbImg), portada_url: enrichedEv.portada_url, useOfficialLogo: isOfficial, isOfficialKinetic: isOfficial, pinColor: '#00FF66', haloColor: '#00FF66', distanciaKm: distKm, hasActiveEvents: true, clases: [enrichedEv], eventosActivos: [enrichedEv], rawEvent: enrichedEv, rawAffiliate: parentOrVirtual });
  });
  return [...nonEventMarkers, ...eventMarkers];
};

import {
  cleanLocationDisplay,
  formatFechaCortaEs,
  resolveCategoryImage,
} from '../constants/exploreData';

  isBannedAffiliateRecord,

const toValidMarkerCoord = (primary: any, secondary: any, fallback: number): number => {
  const n1 = parseFloat(String(primary ?? ''));
  if (!Number.isNaN(n1) && Math.abs(n1) > 1) return n1;
  const n2 = parseFloat(String(secondary ?? ''));
  if (!Number.isNaN(n2) && Math.abs(n2) > 1) return n2;
  return fallback;
};

export function buildExploreMapMarkers(
  filteredAffiliates: any[],
  mapLayers: IMapLayerVisibility,
  isAdminView = false
): any[] {
  const markers: any[] = [];
  const placedCoords: Array<{ lat: number; lng: number }> = [];
  const seenIds = new Set<string>();
  const sortedAZ = [...(filteredAffiliates || [])].sort((a, b) =>
    String(a.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b.nombre || ''), 'es')
  );

  sortedAZ.forEach((af: any, afIdx: number) => {
    if (!af || isBannedAffiliateRecord(af)) return;
    const isClubActive = af.estado === 'activo' && af.esClubActivo === true && Boolean(af.esActivo);
    if (!isClubActive) return;

    const isSpec = Boolean(af.es_especialista);
    if (isSpec && !mapLayers.showSpecialists) return;
    if (!isSpec && !mapLayers.showClubs) return;
    const markerId = `af_${af.id || afIdx}`;
    if (seenIds.has(markerId)) return;
    seenIds.add(markerId);
    let lat = toValidMarkerCoord(af.latitude, af.latitud, 22.8905);
    let lng = toValidMarkerCoord(af.longitude, af.longitud, -109.9167);
    const clash = placedCoords.filter((c) => Math.abs(c.lat - lat) < 0.00009 && Math.abs(c.lng - lng) < 0.00009).length;
    if (clash > 0) {
      const ang = ((clash * 65 + afIdx * 23) * Math.PI) / 180;
      lat += Math.sin(ang) * 0.00022;
      lng += Math.cos(ang) * 0.00022;
    }
    placedCoords.push({ lat, lng });
    const isOfficial = Boolean(af.isOfficialKinetic) || isOfficialKineticHost(af.nombre);
    const fallbackImg = af.fallbackImage || resolveCategoryImage(af.disciplina, af.categoria);
    markers.push({
      id: markerId,
      markerType: isSpec ? 'specialist' : 'club',
      pinColor: !isClubActive ? '#94A3B8' : isSpec ? '#9D4EDD' : '#00F0FF',
      latitude: lat,
      longitude: lng,
      title: af.nombre,
      disciplina: af.disciplina,
      estado: af.estado,
      esClubActivo: isClubActive,
      isHiddenVenue: !isClubActive,
      isOfficialKinetic: isOfficial,
      useOfficialLogo: isOfficial,
      avatar_url: isOfficial ? '' : sanitizeImageUrl(af.pinImageResolved) || sanitizeImageUrl(af.avatar_url) || fallbackImg,
      fallbackImage: fallbackImg,
      rawAffiliate: af,
    });
  });

  if (mapLayers.showEvents) {
    sortedAZ.forEach((af: any) => {
      if (!af || isBannedAffiliateRecord(af)) return;
      const isClubActive = af.estado === 'activo' && af.esClubActivo === true && Boolean(af.esActivo);
      if (!isAdminView && !isClubActive) return;

      (af.experiencias || []).forEach((ev: any, idx: number) => {
        const evEstado = normalizeRadarText(ev?.estado);
        if ((evEstado !== 'aprobado' && evEstado !== 'activo') || ev?.activo === false) return;
        const evMarkerId = `ev_${ev.id || `${af.id}_${idx}`}`;
        if (seenIds.has(evMarkerId)) return;
        seenIds.add(evMarkerId);
        const evLat = toValidMarkerCoord(
          ev.latitud,
          ev.latitude,
          toValidMarkerCoord(af.latitude, af.latitud, 22.8905) + (idx + 1) * 0.00055
        );
        const evLon = toValidMarkerCoord(
          ev.longitud,
          ev.longitude,
          toValidMarkerCoord(af.longitude, af.longitud, -109.9167) + (idx + 1) * -0.00055
        );
        const evDisc = ev.categoria || af.disciplina || 'Fuerza';
        const evFallback = resolveCategoryImage(evDisc, ev.nombre_servicio);
        const isEvOfficial =
          Boolean(af.isOfficialKinetic) || isOfficialKineticHost(ev.negocio_nombre || af.nombre);
        const clubRealAv =
          sanitizeImageUrl(af.avatar_url) ||
          sanitizeImageUrl(af.logo_url) ||
          sanitizeImageUrl(af.pinImageResolved);
        const nonStockClubAv = clubRealAv && !clubRealAv.includes('images.unsplash.com') ? clubRealAv : '';
        const evCoverImg =
          sanitizeImageUrl(ev.imagen_evento_url) ||
          sanitizeImageUrl(ev.imagen_url) ||
          sanitizeImageUrl(ev.portada_url) ||
          sanitizeImageUrl(Array.isArray(ev.galeria) ? ev.galeria[0] : '') ||
          evFallback;
        const evImg = isEvOfficial ? '' : (nonStockClubAv || clubRealAv || evCoverImg);
        markers.push({
          id: evMarkerId,
          markerType: 'event',
          pinColor: '#00FF66',
          latitude: evLat,
          longitude: evLon,
          title: ev.nombre_servicio || 'Evento Kinetic',
          negocio_nombre: ev.negocio_nombre || af.nombre,
          disciplina: evDisc,
          isOfficialKinetic: isEvOfficial,
          useOfficialLogo: isEvOfficial,
          avatar_url: evImg,
          fallbackImage: evFallback,
          rawEvent: {
            ...ev,
            negocio_nombre: af.nombre,
            ubicacion: ev.ubicacion || af.ubicacion,
            distanciaKm: af.distanciaKm,
            rating: af.rating,
          },
          rawAffiliate: af,
        });
      });
    });
  }
  return markers;
}

export function buildMapPreviewCardData(
  mapPinPreview: { pin: any; isEvent: boolean } | null,
  helpers: {
    tDisc: (d: string) => string;
    fmtDist: (km: number, withSuffix?: boolean) => string;
    fmtDate: (d: string, opts?: any) => string;
    tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
    language: string;
  }
) {
  if (!mapPinPreview?.pin) return null;
  const { pin, isEvent } = mapPinPreview;
  const { tDisc, fmtDist, fmtDate, tx, language } = helpers;
  const af = pin.rawAffiliate || pin;
  const ev = pin.rawEvent || pin;
  const rawDiscipline = String(
    (isEvent ? ev?.categoria : af?.disciplina) || pin?.disciplina || 'Fuerza'
  ).trim();
  const disc = tDisc(rawDiscipline).toUpperCase();

  if (isEvent) {
    const totalKc = Math.max(0, Number(ev?.precio_kc) || 150);
    const escrowKc = Math.round(totalKc * 0.4);
    const bannerUri =
      sanitizeImageUrl(ev?.imagen_evento_url) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(ev?.imagen_url) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(ev?.portada_url) ||
      sanitizeImageUrl(Array.isArray(ev?.galeria) ? ev.galeria[0] : '') ||
      resolveCategoryImage(ev?.categoria || 'Fuerza', ev?.nombre_servicio);
    const dateStr =
      language === 'en' && ev?.fecha
        ? fmtDate(ev.fecha, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })
        : formatFechaCortaEs(ev?.fecha);
    const timeStr = String(ev?.hora_inicio || '18:00').slice(0, 5);
    const durStr = `${ev?.duracion_minutos || 60}m`;
    return {
      isEvent: true,
      borderColor: COLORS.green,
      badgeColor: COLORS.green,
      badgeTextColor: '#050505',
      rawDiscipline,
      discipline: disc,
      topRightMeta: `${dateStr} • ${timeStr} (${durStr})`,
      title: ev?.nombre_servicio || pin?.title || tx('Evento Kinetic', 'Kinetic Event'),
      subLine: `📍 ${ev?.negocio_nombre || af?.nombre || 'Kinetic'} • ${fmtDist(
        af?.distanciaKm ?? ev?.distanciaKm ?? 1.2,
        false
      )}`,
      rightMainValue: `${totalKc} KC`,
      rightSubValue: `40%: ${escrowKc} KC`,
      bannerUri,
    };
  }

  const isSpec = Boolean(af?.es_especialista);
  const expCount = Array.isArray(af?.experiencias) ? af.experiencias.length : 0;
  const minKc = Math.max(0, Number(af?.minPrice) || 150);
  const escrowMin = Math.round(minKc * 0.4);
  const bannerUri =
    af?.coverResolved ||
    af?.portada_url ||
    resolveCategoryImage(af?.disciplina || 'Fuerza', af?.nombre);

  return {
    isEvent: false,
    borderColor: isSpec ? COLORS.purple : COLORS.cyan,
    badgeColor: isSpec ? COLORS.purple : COLORS.cyan,
    badgeTextColor: isSpec ? '#FFFFFF' : '#050505',
    rawDiscipline,
    discipline: disc,
    topRightMeta: `★ ${Number(af?.rating || 5.0).toFixed(1)} • ${fmtDist(
      af?.distanciaKm ?? 1.2,
      false
    )}`,
    title: af?.nombre || pin?.title || 'Kinetic',
    subLine: `📍 ${cleanLocationDisplay(af?.ubicacion, tx('Ubicación Verificada', 'Verified Location'))} • ${
      expCount === 0
        ? `🗓️ ${tx('Agenda en actualización', 'Schedule updating')}`
        : `⚡ ${expCount} ${expCount === 1 ? tx('evento activo', 'active event') : tx('eventos activos', 'active events')}`
    }`,
    rightMainValue: expCount > 0 ? `${minKc} KC` : tx('VER SEDE', 'VIEW VENUE'),
    rightSubValue:
      expCount > 0 ? `40%: ${escrowMin} KC` : tx('TOCA PARA ABRIR ›', 'TAP TO OPEN ›'),
    bannerUri,
  };
}
